“veḷvēl viṭaliyām talimakaṉoṭu tokuvaḷi muṉkiyuṭiyaḷākiya eṉ maṭanti koṇṭa naṭpu, paṟikaḷ muḻaṅka, caṅkukaḷ ārppa, nālūrk kōcaratu oṉṟu paṭṭa vāymoḻi pōla nilipeṟṟa uṇmiyāyiṟṟu”  (kuṟun. 15) eṉṟa ceviliyatu kūṟṟu. 
“uṭaṉpōy em peruntōṭ kuṟumakaḷi varintu koṇṭa viṭali avaḷi muṟpaṭa em illattukku aḻittu varuvāṉō? aṉṟi, taṉ illattiṟku iṭṭuc celvāṉō? avaṉ karuttu yātō, aṟikilēṉ”  (akanā. 195) eṉat taliviyatu kaṟpiṉ ākkattiṉkaṇ cevili karuttu nikaḻntavāṟu.  	(to. po. 115 nac.)
cevili, makaḷ neñcu valittavaḻik kūṟal -
uṭaṉpōkkiṟkut talivi maṉam tuṇintu ceṉṟamiyāl talivikkut taṉmēl aṉpu nīṅkiyatu eṉa aṟintu cevili kūṟal.
‘cevili piriviṉ eccattu.... kūṟutal’ kāṇka.
‘peyarttaṉeṉ muyaṅka’	(kuṟun. 84)
eṉṟa pāṭalākiya veṭuttukkāṭṭē īṇṭum amiyum.
(to. po. 115 nac.)
ceviliyatu cēṟal -
iravuk kuṟikkaṇ cevili etirpārā vakiyāl kuṟiyiṭattu vantu  talivaṉtaliviyākiya iruvartamatu kaḷavoḻukkam paṟṟi aṟintāḷākat tōḻi kūṟutal. itu ‘tāy aṟi vuṇarttal’ (na. a. 166) eṉṟa varivu kaṭāya kūṟṟākavum koḷḷappaṭum. (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal) 			(vī.cō. 92. urimēṟ.)
cevili varaviṉkaṇ kaṇṭōr kūṟiyami -
uṭaṉpōkkil ceṉṟa talivaṉ talivi iruvariyum tēṭikkoṇṭu cevili pālinilattukku vantapōtu, avaḷi vaḻiyiṭik kaṇṭōr kūṟutal.
“pakaṟpoḻutiṉ veppattāl cūṭēṟiya pāṟiyiṭik kiṭanta kalakkamuṟṟa cilavākiya nīrit talivaṉ taṉ kiyāl mukantu koṭuppa, ataṉik taṉ kikaḷik kuvittu vāṅki amutam pōlap parukik kaḷippu nīṅkic ceṉṟa at taṭaṅkaṇ vañcik kompaṉiyāḷip peṟṟa tāypōlum ivaḷ!” eṉṟu kaṇṭōr tammuḷ kūṟutal.
ceviliyik kaṇṭa āṭavaṉ, avaḷ talivaṉtalivi yarik kuṟittu viṉaviya iṭattu, “ammiyē! avarkaḷ num uṟaviṉarō? eṉ maṉivi attūṇṭā viḷakkup pōṉṟa naṅki nallāḷik kaṇṭāḷ. nāṉ kaḷaṅkaṉi aṉṉa niṟattiṉaṉ ākiya akkāḷiyik kaṇṭēṉ” eṉṟal.	(kōvi. 244)
ceviliyāl vaḻiyiṭi viṉavappaṭṭa antaṇar, “taṉ talivaṉ iṉitu  pārāṭṭa, kuṉṟukaḷ uyarnta immali vaḻiyik kaṭantu ceṉṟa iḷiyāḷik kaṇṭōm” (aiṅ. 387) eṉṟal. 	(to. po. 40 nac. )
cevili, veṟi vilakkiyavaḻi(t tōḻiyi) viṉaval -
veṟi vilakkiya tōḻiyic cevili, “ivaḷukku nērntatu yātō?” eṉa viṉavutal.
“peṇṇē! nī murukap perumāṉāl taliviyiṉ nōy tīrātu eṉṟataṉ kāraṇam yātu?”  (tañci. 299) eṉpatu pōṉṟa ceviliyatu viṉā.
itu variviyaluḷ ‘aṟattoṭu niṟṟal’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (na. a. 177) (to.po. 40 nac.)
ceṟippaṭa avaṇuṟṟu iraṅkal -
talivi iṟceṟikkappaṭṭa kāli  talivaṉi niṉittu varunti avaṉatu uruveḷippāṭu kaṇṭu kūṟal.
“tiṉi koyyap peṟṟataṉāl aḻinta puṉattiyum, yāṉ iḻitta kūṭaliyum, puṉattil nāṅkaḷ irunta paraṇiyum, illattuḷ  ciṟiyiruntavāṟē pārttu ēṅkik kaṇṇīr coriyum eṉatu tuyaratti aṟintu, eṉ kātalar nāṉ vikum illattiṉi vaḻi viṉavik kēṭṭaṟintu iṅku vantārā? itō eṉ etiril tōṉṟukiṟārē!” eṉṟu talivi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘pakaṟkuṟi iṭiyīṭu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatu oru kūṟṟu.  	(ampikā. 186)
ceṟippu -
‘iṟceṟippu’ nōkkuka.
ceṉṟu kiyikantu peyarttu uḷḷiyavaḻit talivaṉ kūṟiyatu -
niliniṟkap piriyum piriviṉkaṇ añciya attiṟattiṉik kiyikantu muṉṉorukāl ceṉṟu mīṭṭum anneṟi yiṉip pōka niṉintavaḻit talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum; kaṟpiyal kūṟṟu itu.
“maṉamē! niṟiya celvaṅkaḷip peṟalām eṉṟu kūṟi eṉṉi aḻittuc ceṉṟu pulliya ilikaḷiyuṭiya marāa maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya akaṉṟa neṭiya vaḻiyilē piṉtaṅki eṉṉit taṉittu aṉupputaṟku muṉ orukāl muyaṉṟāyē. ataṉi maṟantu itupōtu mīṇṭum poruḷ tēṭalām eṉṟu eṉṉit tūṇṭukiṟāyē! taliviyiṉ uruveḷittōṟṟam yāṉ cellum vaḻiyiṭit tōṉṟik kuṟukkiṭumpōtu eṉakku ēṟpaṭakkūṭiya tuyariṉi uṉ uṇmi pōṉṟa poymoḻi pōkka iyalumā? ātaliṉ immuṟi taliviyip pirintu poruḷīṭṭa yāṉ vārēṉ”  (akanā. 3) eṉṟu talivaṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟiyatu.  			(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
talivaṉ āṟṟāṉāyt tuṉiyit tīrttaṟkut taliviyi aṇukac cella, avaṉ meykkaṇ kiṭanta tavaṟukaṇṭu talivi āṟṟāḷāy nīkki niṟuttalāṉē, avaṉ oruvāṟ ṟāṉ avaḷ āṟṟāmiyic ciṟitu mīṭkiyiṉālē avaḷ kūṭak karutiya iṭattu avaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum.			(to.po. 146 nac.)
ituvum tuṉi tīrppatoru muṟi kūṟiṉār.
“emmi vañcaṉiyāl varutti, uṇmiyiṉ eṉ makaṉ mēl pācamiṉṟi, nī kūṭiya parattiyiṉ muṭiyiṉiṉṟum utirnta pūntūtu uṉ āṭiyil oṭṭiyiruppatu pulappaṭu māṟu kāṟṟiṉ etirē niṟkiṟāyē. iṅṅaṉam nillātu pō!” eṉṟu talivi kūṟiyavaḻi, talivaṉ. “ēṭi! yāṉ tavaṟu ceyyavilli eṉṟu uṟuti kūṟavum, eṉ colli nampāmal uṉ kōpam kuṟiyāmalirukkiṟāy. viruppoṭu kaṉṟu kaṭṭappaṭṭa iṭam nōkkic cellum pacuppōla eṉ piḷḷiyiyāvatu yāṉ tūkkikkoṇṭu makiḻkiṉ ṟēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟip putalvaṉit tūkkik koḷḷat talivi ūṭal nīṅkiyatu.  	(kali. 81, nac.)
ceṉṟuetir kōṭal -
iravukkuṟikkaṇ cevili etirpārāvakiyāl puḻik kaṭip puṟam vantāḷākat talivaṉtaliviyaratu kaḷavuk kāla nikaḻcciyi avaḷ kaṇṭuviṭṭatākat tōḻi kūṟik kaḷavoḻukkam tavirntu varintu koḷḷumāṟu talivaṉi vēṇṭal.	(kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal)
itu ‘tāy aṟivuṇarttal’ (na.a. 166) eṉavum paṭum. ‘ceṉ ṟetir kāṇṭal’ eṉṟiruppiṉ ciṟakkum.			(vī. cō. 92 urimēṟ.)
ceṉṟōṉ nīṭaliṉ kāmam mikka kaḻipaṭar kiḷavi -
kaḷavu oḻukkattiṉiṭiyē pirintu aṉṟu māli varuva tākak kūṟic ceṉṟa talivaṉ vārāmiyāl vēṭki mītūrntu mikka tuyarattoṭu talivi kūṟum coṟkaḷ. kaṭal, aṉṉam mutaliyavaṟṟuṭaṉ uriyāṭuvatu pōla avaḷ taṉ tuyaratti veḷippaṭutti āṟutal tēṭuvatē ikkūṟṟu.
“kaṭalē! tuṟivar emmi viṭṭuc ceṉṟapōtu mīṇṭu varum nēratti niṉakkuk kūṟiṉārō? col” (kōvi. 181) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa taliviyatu kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘oruvaḻit taṇattal’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 168)
cēṭpaṭuttal -
‘cēṭpaṭi’ kāṇka.
cēṭpaṭuttu vanta talimakaṟkut talimakaḷ colliyatu -
“kuṉṟanāṭa! nī palakālum vārātuviṭuttaliṉ niṉṉi niṉintu niṉintu ēṅki, vāṭikkāṟṟil, palarum uṟaṅkum iravil niṉ varukiyi etirpārttup puṟattē niṉṟu koṇṭirukkum emnili, uṉṉi ārat. taḻuvutaliṉ ēṟpaṭum iṉpattiṉum ciṟappāka vuḷḷatu!” (aka.nā. 58) eṉṟu talivi kuṟippāl taṉ varuttattiṉi veḷippaṭut tiyatu.
cēṭpaṭi -
talimakaḷik kuṟinayappittut taṉṉiṉ ākiya kūṭṭam kūṭṭaluṟum tōḻi, talimakaḷatu perumiyiyum taṉatu muyaṟciyiṉ arumiyiyum talivaṉ uṇartaṟkākavum, ariyavaḷākiya taliviyik kaḷavil eḷitil peṟṟu nukartal iyalātu eṉpataṉi uṭkoṇṭu talivaṉ kaḷa voḻukkatti viriviṉ nīttu maṇam ceytukoḷ ḷutali uṇartaṟkākavum, talimakaṉi nerukkamākap paḻakaviṭātu ciṟitu akaṟṟi vittal. ākavē, mutalil ciṟitu akaṟṟivittup piṉṉark kuṟimuṭippāḷ eṉpatu.			(na.a. 143)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭattu vaki paṉṉiraṇ ṭaṉuḷ oṉṟu.
cēṭpaṭiyil tuṟikaḷ -
‘taḻi koṇṭu cēṟal; 2. cantaṉattaḻi takātueṉa maṟuttal, 3. nilattu iṉmi kūṟi maṟuttal, 4. niṉivaṟivu kūṟi maṟuttal, 5. paṭittu moḻiyāṉ maṟuttal, 6. nāṇurittu maṟuttal, 7. iciyāmi kūṟi maṟuttal, 8. cevviyilaḷ eṉṟu maṟuttal, 9. kāppuṭitteṉṟu maṟuttal, 10. nīyē kūṟu eṉa maṟuttal. 11. kulamuṟi kūṟi maṟuttal, 12. nakiyāṭi maṟuttal, 13. irakkattoṭu maṟuttal, 14. ciṟappiṉmi kūṟi maṟuttal, 15.  iḷami kūṟi maṟuttal, 16. maṟittami kūṟi nakit turittal, 17. naki kaṇṭu makiḻtal, 18. aṟiyāḷ pōṉṟu niṉivu kēṭṭal, 19. avayavam kūṟal, 
20. kaṇṇayantu uyarttal, 21. taḻi etirtal, 22. kuṟippaṟital, 23. kuṟippaṟintu kūṟal, 24. vakutturittal, 25. taḻi ēṟpittal, 26. taḻi viruppurittal eṉpaṉa irupattāṟum ‘cēṭpaṭi’ eṉṉum kiḷavikkaṇ nikaḻum tuṟikaḷ. 			(kōvi. 90-115)
cēy nilikku akaṉṟōr celaviṭi iṭiccurattu makaḷir kūṟal -
talivaṉ taliviyōṭu uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻttip pāli vaḻiyē ceṉṟapōtu aṅkirunta āṟalikkaḷvarām pāli nila mākkaḷ avarkaṭkut tīṅku ceyya niṉitta pōtu, avarkaḷuṭiya maṉiviyar talivikkut tīṅku vārāmal taṭuttut talivaṉōṭu avaḷi aṉuppiviṭa vēṇṭum eṉat tammuḷ kūṟic ceyaṟpaṭa muṉital.
“maṭakkaṇṇaḷāy, takarac cāntu pūciya kūntalaḷāy, paṇit tōḷiyāy, cērntu ceṟitoṭiyaḷāy, taḻiyuṭi yuṭuttu, vārntu ilaṅku veṇpaṟkaḷ muṟuvalāl ciṟitu tōṉṟa, vēṟu cuṟṟat tuṇiyiṉṟi, nam viḻavukkaḷam poṟ puṟumāṟu ivaḷ iṅku vantu niṉṟāḷ. iṅku naṉmiyē nikaḻumāṟu, ivaḷik kavarntu koḷḷutali niṉikkum nam koḻunari nām atu ceyyātavāṟu taṭuppōm. (naṟ. 170)” eṉa iṭiccurattuk kuṟumpiṉil uḷḷa maṟavar peṇṭirtam kūṟṟup pōlvaṉa. 	(to. po. 40 nac.)
cēy nilikku akaṉṟōr celaviṭik kaṇṭōr kūṟutal -
talivaṉ taliviyōṭu uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻttip pāli vaḻiyē neṭuntūram ceṉṟapōtu, avviruvariyum kaṇṭa aṅkirunta makkaḷ, “mūṅkil niṟinta ikkāṭṭakattuc cellum ivvillēntiya iḷiñaṉ kālkaḷil kaḻalkaḷ aṇiyappaṭṭuḷa; toṭi aṇinta naṅki ivaḷuṭiya melliya aṭikaḷil cilampukaḷ aṇiyappaṭṭuḷa. ittakiya celvarkaḷ ikkoṭiya pāli vaḻiyē uṭaṉpōkkut tuṇinta ceyal iraṅkattakkatu. ivarkaḷ yāvarō?”(kuṟu. 7) eṉṟāṟ pōlat tammuḷ kūṟik koḷḷutal.  			(to. po. 40 nac.)
cēyōṉ -
cenniṟamuṭiya kaṭavuḷākiya murukaṉ; kuṟiñcinilakkaṭavuḷ maliyum mali cārnta iṭamumākiya kuṟiñcinila makka ḷākiya kuṟavar mutaliyōr kūṭi veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻttaṟku vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷik koṇṭu veṟiyāṭṭu eṭuppavē, āṇṭu avarkaṭku murukaṉ aruḷ veḷippaṭum. avaṉaru ḷāl maḻivaḷam tavaṟātu eytum.
kuṟiñcinila makkaṭkuc cūraramakaḷirum vaḻipaṭutaṟkuriyar. 
(to. po. 5 nac.)
cēyōṉukku mali urimiyātal -
malippakutiyākiya kuṟiñci nilattukkuriya kuṟavar mutaliyōr kuḻīi veṟiyāṭal nikaḻttaṟku vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷi muṟiyākat tokuttukkoṇṭu veṟi yāṭṭu nikaḻttavē, āṇṭu atu kaṇṭu makiḻntu murukaṉ avarkaṭku aruḷ ceyvāṉ. ātaliṉ mali avaṉukku urimi eytiṟṟu. 	(to. po. 5 nac.)
cēri cellum tāy -
talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻttiyati maṟunāḷ viṭiyaṟkāliyil uṇarnta naṟṟāy taṉ makaḷit tāṉvāḻum cērimuḻutum illamtōṟum ceṉṟu tēṭi varuvāḷ. 	(to. po. 37 nac.)
cēripparatti -
poruḷiṭattu viruppattāṉ oruvaṉukkē urimi pūṇātu palariyum kūṭit tiriyum iyalpuṭiya parattiyiṉattic cērntavaḷ.  	(na. a. 60)
‘col avaṭ cārttaliṉ pulliya vaki’ -
talivaṉ tāṉ irantu kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟit tōḻi talivi yic cārtti moḻitaliṉ, puṟkeṉṟa neñcattaṉākiya vakiyil avaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻttuvāṉ.
“nīyē taliviyiṭattuc ceṉṟu niṉkuṟi muṭittuk koḷka” eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟiyaviṭattu, talivaṉ ātaravaṟṟa vaṉāyp puṟkeṉṟa neñcattaṉāyk kalaṅkik kūṟṟu nikaḻttum.	(to. kaḷa. 11 ca.pāla.)
‘col etir peṟāaṉ colli iṉpuṟutal’ -
kāmattiṟkuriya paruvam nirampāta ciṟumipāl kāmam koṇṭa āṇṭu mūtta talivaṉ taṉ viṉāviṟku avaḷ viṭi kūṟāta niliyiṉkaṇṇum tāṉ avaḷip paṟṟip pēcutalilēyē iṉpuṟuvatu. itu kikkiḷiyuḷ ciṟantatu.
(kāmakkuṟippiṟku amiyāmi eṉpatu : peṇmip paruvak kuṟikaḷ ākiya tōḷum nakilum peruttal, kaṇpiṟaḻtal - mutaliyaṉa tōṉṟiyum, pūppeytutaṟkuriya āṇṭukaḷ nirampiyum aḵtu eytāmal iruttal; pūppeytiya piṉṉarum kāma uṇarcci tōṉṟāmaliruttal; pūppeytu taṟkuriya āṇṭu nirampāta niliyilum pūppeytiyavaḷip pōla uṭal vaṉappuk koṇṭiruttal eṉa ivi.)
ittakiya kāmakkuṟippiṟku amitiyillāta peṇkaḷuḷ oruttipāl kātal koṇṭa oruvaṉ taṉ kuṟippiṟku etirkkuṟippu avaḷ nikaḻttāmal celvāḷāyiṉ, ataṉ kāraṇam aṟiyātu, “ivaḷ ivvāṟu celvatu ēṉ? eṉ kuṟippiṉi aṟiyāḷō?” eṉṟu niṉintu taṉ kātali aṭakka iyalāta niliyil peruntuṉpam uṟuvāṉ. attuṉpattiṟkup parikāram etuvum kiṭṭātātaliṉ aḵtu ‘ēmam cālā iṭumpi’ ākiya maruntu piṟitillāp peruntuyarākum. attakiya tuṉpuṟum niliyil,
“aṉṉattip pōlavum, peṇ taṉmiyuṭiya āṇ mayilip pōlavum, puṟāvip pōlavum aminta niṉ pēraḻaku niṉṉik kaṇṭavarkaḷi mayakkum eṉpataṉi nī aṟitiyō? aṟiyāyō?
“mūṅkil pōlavum taliyaṇi pōlavum kāmakkaṭali nīntap payaṉpaṭum teppam pōlavum aminta niṉ parutta melliya tōḷkaḷ uṉṉik kāṇpavarkaḷi varuttum eṉpati aṟitiyō? aṟiyāyō?
“kōṅka moṭṭup pōlavum, muṟṟiya kurumpi pōlavum nīr mokkuḷ pōlavum amintu  perutta niṉ iḷaṅ koṅkikaḷ niṉṉik kāṇpār uyiri vāṅkum eṉpataṉi nī aṟitiyō? aṟiyāyō?
“uṉmēl tavaṟilli; uṉṉi veḷiyē puṟappaṭaviṭṭa uṉ uṟaviṉar mēlum tavaṟilli; matayāṉiyi nīrāṭṭa aḻittuc cellumpōtu, ‘ellōrum katavukaḷi aṭittuk koṇṭu maṉikkaṇṇēyē iruttal vēṇ ṭum’ eṉṟu ūr muḻutum paṟiyaṟintu cāṟṟac ceyyum aracaṉ, nī puṟappaṭṭu veḷiyē varukiyil paṟiyaṟintu em pōlvār illattiṉuḷ aṭittiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu paṟi cāṟṟuvittirukka vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉam ceyyāta aracaṉē tavaṟuṭiyāṉ” (kalit. 56) eṉṟāṟ pōla, naṉmi yum tīmiyum muṟiyē taṉṉiṭattum avaḷiṭattum poruttic collum kūṟṟu.
ittakiya kūṟṟukkut talivi ētum maṟumāṟṟam koṭāta niliyilum kikkiḷit talivaṉ iṅṅaṉam avaḷip paṟṟip pēcuvatiyē iṉpamākak koṇṭu amitiyuṟum.
(to. po. 50 nac.)
col kēṭka virumputal -
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyaṉṟu, nāṇattāl yātum kūṟāmal niṉṟa taliviyiṉ aḻakiya vāyip pukaḻntu kūṟiya tali vaṉ, avaḷatu colliṉimiyiyum tuykka virumputal.
itaṉik ‘kiḷavi vēṭṭal’ eṉṉum tirukkōviyār (10). iḵtu ‘iyaṟki puṇarcci’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇ aminta oruvakiyākiya teyvappuṇarcciyuḷ nikaḻum oru kūṟṟu.			(i. vi. 493 uri)
col kēḷāta ayarvu nīṅkiyatu -
iyaṟkippuṇarcci iṟutikkaṇ, taliviyiṉ collik kēḷāmiyāl tāṉ uṟṟa cōrviṉit talivaṉ nīṅkutal. itaṉi nalampuṉinturittal (11) eṉṉum tirukkō viyār.
iḵtu ‘iyaṟkippuṇarcci’ eṉṉum kiḷavikkaṇ aminta oruvakiyākiya teyvap puṇarcciyuḷ nikaḻum orukūṟṟu.
(i. vi. 493 uri)
col pala urittal -
pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ tōḻi talivaṉiṭam appakaṟ kuṟiyāl viḷiyum ētaṅkaḷip palavāka eṭuttuk kūṟutal. itu ‘muṉṉiṉṟuṇarttal’ (na.a. 154) eṉavumpaṭum. (kuṟiñcinaṭi yiyal)	(vī. cō. 92 urimēṟ.)
collātēkal -
kaḷavuk kālattil variporuṭkup pirinta talivaṉ “yāṉ nēril kūṟiṉ talivi eṉ pirivukku uṭaṉpaṭātu varuntu vāḷ ātaliṉ, eṉ piriviṉi avaṭku nīyē kūṟu. yāṉ virivil variviṟkuriya poruḷtēṭi mīṇṭu varuvēṉ” eṉṟu tōḻiyiṭam kūṟit taliviyiṭam kūṟātu pirital.
itu ‘variporuṭ pirital’ eṉṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru ceytiyām.  	(kōvi. 270)
‘colliya nukarcci vallē peṟṟuḻit tīrāt tēṟṟam -
teyvap puṇarcciyākiya iṉpa nukarcciyip pālatu āṇiyāl talimakkaḷ virintu peṟṟaviṭattu, talivi “itu māyamō, maruṭkiyō, kaṉavō, naṉavō!” eṉat tikittu iṉi evvāṟāṅkolō?” eṉak kalaṅkuvāḷ. ataṉi ōrntuṇarnta talivaṉ taṉatu tīrāk kātaṉmiyi nayamuṟak kūṟi, “eññāṉṟum piriyēṉ” eṉac cūḷuri kūṟit taliviyit tēṟṟit teḷivittal eṉṉum kiḷavi.
(to. kaḷa. 11 ca. pāla.)
colluna pōlavum kēṭkuna pōlavum amivaṉa -
ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, aṟivu, nāṇam, kaṭal, kāṉal, vilaṅku, maram, poḻutu, paṟavi, neñcu mutaliyaṉa kēṭkuna pōla vum kiḷakkuna pōlavum pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam koḷḷappaṭum. 
(to.po. 512 pērā.)
colvaḻippaṭuttal -
taṉiyiṭattut taliviyi etirppaṭṭa talivaṉ, taṉ perumiyum aṟivum nīṅki vēṭki mītūrap puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉāṉāyiṉum taliviyiṉuṭiya accam maṭam nāṇ ivaṟṟi nīkkutaṟkuk kaṇṇāl kūṟiyati viṭuttu moḻiyāṉum kuṟippiṭum eḻuvakic ceytikaḷuḷ iḵtu iraṇṭāvatu.
colvaḻippaṭuttalāvatu, tāṉ collukiṉṟa colliṉ vaḻi avaḷ niṟkumāṟu paṭuttukkūṟutal. (to. po. 98 iḷa.)
“yāṉ oṉṟu colliṉ ataṉik kēṭkum cevvi koḷḷāyāy niṉ tirumukam kuṉintu nāṇam uṟukiṉṟāy. virivil kāmam elli kaṭappiṉ ataṉit tāṅkutal eḷitō? kaṭimaṇi civanta niṉ kaṇkaḷē aṉṟi, karumpu eḻutap peṟṟa niṉtōḷkaḷum eṉṉit tuyar ceykiṉ ṟaṉa” (naṟ. 39) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu. (iḷa.)
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ piṉ piritalum pirital nimittamu māyt talivaṉukku uriya eṉappaṭṭa ceytikaḷ ēḻaṉuḷ mutal mūṉṟu nayappiṉ kūṟu. avaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭāvatu colvaḻippaṭuttal. aḵtāvatu vārtti collum iyalpu illāta vaṇṭu neñcu mutaliyaṉa  maṟumoḻi colluvaṉa pōla avaṟṟiṭam kūṟal. 	(101 nac.)
‘koṅkutēr vāḻkki’ eṉṉum (kuṟun. 2) pāṭaluḷ, añciṟit tumpi eṉṟu viḷittu, “nī meyyeṉṟu kaṇṭa taṉi moḻivāyāka” eṉṟatu naṉṉayam urittal. (nac.)
coṟporuḷ -
iḵtu akapporuḷ uri irupattēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu. 			(vī. cō. 90).
taṉiccol poruḷi viḷakkutalum, oru toṭar moḻiyiluḷḷa iraṇṭu mutaliya coṟkaḷ oṉṟu cērntu tam poruḷi viḷakkutalum, oru toṭarmoḻiyuḷ muṉmoḻi poruḷ ciṟantu viḷakkutalum, piṉmoḻi poruḷ ciṟantu viḷakkutalum eṉa moḻipporuḷi viḷakkutal nālvakittākum. 	(vī. cō. 96; urimēṟ.)
cōtiṭam kēṭṭal -
cevili uṭaṉpōkkuc ceṉṟa taliviyit tēṭi mīṭṭu varap puṟappaṭa, naṟṟāy cōtiṭarkaḷi aṇuki virivil talivaṉum taliviyum mīṇṭuvantu tamvīṭṭil tirumaṇam ceytu koḷvārkaḷā eṉṟa ceytiyi avarkaḷi viṉavutal.
itaṉi ‘uyttu uṇarvōri urimiṉ eṉṟal’ (i.vi. 538 uri) eṉṟum kūṟuvar; ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇ aṭakkum.
ikkūṟṟu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatu. 
(kōvi. 236)
ña
‘ñāṅkark kiḷanta mūṉṟu’ -
muṟpaṭak kūṟappaṭṭa ōtal, paki, tūtu eṉṉum mūṉṟu poruḷum paṟṟiya pirivukaḷ. karaṇamoṭu puṇarat talivaṉ taliviyi varintu illaṟakkiḻami pūṇṭallatu mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟum paṟṟip pirintu cellutal avaṉukku illi. ātaliṉ immūṉṟuporuḷ paṟṟiya pirivukaḷ talivaṟkuk kaḷavuk kālattil illi  	(to.kaḷa. 51 ca.pāla.)
ta
takutiyatu amiti oṉṟāmi -
poruṭ celvam nilittu niṟpatoṉṟu aṉṟu eṉṟu uṇarntōrkkup poruḷmēl kātal takātu eṉṟu uṇarum uṇarcci poruntāmi.	(kali. 8, 12.)
taki pāṭutal -
iyaṟkip puṇarcciiṟuti mutaliya kaḷavu oḻukkak kālattum kaṟpuk kālattum talivaṉ taliviyiṉ aḻaku paṇpu mutaliyavaṟṟip pārāṭṭik kūṟutal.	(cīvaka. 1379)
takiyaṇaṅkuṟuttal (1) -
tōḻi taliviyiṉpāl talivaṉatu kūṭṭattāl kāṇap paṭṭa putiya aḻakukaḷik koṇṭu avaḷi oru teyva makaḷākac colli nāṇa vittal. 
taṉṉi vantaṭinta taliviyiṭam tōḻi, “maliyil uṟiyum teyvamē! eṅkaḷ taliviyum uṉṉip pōlavē iruppāḷ. āṉāl avaḷ utaṭukaḷ civantirukkum; kaṇkaḷ karumiyavāyirukkum; nīyō utaṭu veḷuttuk kaṇ civantu kāṇappaṭukiṟāy” (tañci. kō : 67) eṉṟu kūṟutal.		(na. a. 139 uri)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ pāṅki matiyuṭaṉpāṭṭil ‘muṉṉuṟa vuṇartal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇ uḷḷatoru kūṟṟu.
takiyaṇaṅkuṟuttal (2) -
talimakaḷaḻaku taṉṉi varuttum ceytiyit talivaṉ kūṟal. itu kaḷavoḻukkattil talivaṉ taliviyi mutaṉmutal cantikkumpōtu nikaḻvatu.			(kuṟaḷ. ati. 109 tōṟṟu. parimē.)
taṇkaṭaṟ cērppaṉ -
neytal nilat talivaṉ.	(piṅ. 602)
‘taṇtaḷir mātu taṉakkuri māṟṟam’ -
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ iṭiccurattut talivaṉ talivi yiṭam pakivarkaḷ etirttāl avarkaḷit tāṉ aḻittu viṭuvatākavum, avaḷtamar piṉ toṭarntu vantu etirt tāl avarkaḷukku ūṟu ceyyāmal tāṉ maṟintiruppa tākavum kūṟutal. (pāli naṭiyiyal)			(vī.cō. 93 uri mēṟ.)
taṇappiṭar oḻittal -
pirintavaḻit tuṉpatti nīkkutal. talivaṉ kaḷavu veḷip paṭṭapiṉ oruvaḻit taṇantaviṭattē akkaṟpuk kālattut talivi talivaṉatu pirivu kuṟittu varuntavē, tōḻi, “mikum aṉpiṉarākiya talivar nī pirintu vāṭa neṭunāḷ tammiṭattut taṅkār; ātaliṉ nī varuntaṟka” (kōvi. 274) eṉpatu pōṉṟa coṟkaḷāl vāṭṭam taṇittal. taṇappu - pirivu.
itu ‘variporuṭku ēkal’ eṉṟum tokutikkaṇ uḷḷa mūṉṟu kūṟṟiṉuḷ iṟutiyāvatu.	(ka. kā.pak. 133)
tattiyoṭu pulampal -
talivi uṭaṉpōyiṉami aṟinta naṟṟāy avaḷ vaḷartta kiḷiyiṭam varuntik kūṟal.
“kiḷiyē! eṉ makaḷ koṭiya pāli vaḻiyi evvāṟu kaṭantu celvāḷ? ‘talivi eṉṉi viṭuttuc ceṉṟu viṭṭataṉāl eṉṉip pēṇuvār irār; ataṉāl paruntu eṉṉik koṉṟuviṭum’ eṉṟu nī kūvum coṟkaḷ vēṟu eṉṉi mikavum varuttukiṉṟaṉa.” (kōvi. 231) eṉṟāṟ pōla varum naṟṟāy kūṟṟu.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘kiḷimoḻikku iraṅkal’ eṉṉum.
variviyaluḷ ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu itu.	(i. vi. 538 uri)
‘tanta teyvam tarum eṉac cēṟal’ -
“muṉṉar iyaṟkip puṇarcciyil avaḷi eṉṉoṭu kūṭṭuvitta teyvam iṉṉum avvāṟē ceyyum” eṉṟa uṟutiyuṭaṉ talivaṉ muṉpu talippeyta atē iṭat tiṟkut taliviyik kāṇac cellutal.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘poḻiliṭic cēṟal’ eṉṉum (49).
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘iṭantalippāṭu’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 135)
tannili urittal -
taṉiyiṭattut taliviyi etirppaṭṭa talivaṉ taṉ perumiyum aṟivum nīṅki vēṭki mītūrap puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉāṉāyiṉum, taliviyiṉuṭiya accam maṭam nāṇ ivaṟṟi nīkkutaṟkuk kaṇṇāl kūṟiyati viṭuttu moḻiyāṉum kuṟippiṭum eḻuvakic ceytikaḷuḷ iḵtu āṟāvatu.	(to. po. 101 nac.)
taṉṉili urittalāvatu taṉṉuṭiya puṟattu uṟuppuk kaḷiṉ polivu aḻiviṉik kaṇṭa taliviyiṭattut talivaṉ taṉṉuḷḷattil vēṭki mikkiruttali vali yuṟuttic kūṟutal.	(98 iḷa.)
e-ṭu :	talivaṉ taliviyatu peruvaṉappiṉip pukaḻntu kūṟiya aḷavil avaḷ nāṇattāl taṉ kaṇkaḷip pottik koḷḷa, avaḷi nōkki, “kāmam mikuva tāyiṉ ataṉi eṉṉāl taṭuttuk koḷḷal iyalātu. kaṭicivanta niṉ kaṇkaḷē eṉṉik kōpikkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu avaṟṟip putittuk koṇ ṭāy; karumpu vaṭivil toyyiṟ koṭi eḻutap peṟṟa uṉ tōḷkaḷum eṉakkut tuṉpam tarukiṉṟaṉa” eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal.	(naṟ. 39)
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ piṉṉart talivaṉ taliviyi nōkki, “uṉakkum eṉakkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa toṭarpu eḻupiṟappum toṭarntu varukiṟatu. ituvari iruvarākit taṉittu vāḻnta nām ikkūṭṭattiṉpiṉ taṉittaṉip pirintu vāḻum tuyaram nīṅkap pirivu aritākiya kāmat tuṭaṉēyē iruvarkkum uyir pōvatākuka!” eṉṟu virumpukiṟēṉ eṉak kūṟutal.	(kuṟun. 57)
ivi muṟiyē nacciṉārkkiṉiyar iḷampūraṇar eṉa iruvarālum kāṭṭappeṟṟa kūṟṟukkaḷ.
tam nili urittalāvatu “niṉṉoṭu poruntiya toṭarcci toṭarntu eḻumiyum varukiṉṟatu” eṉat talivaṉ tamatu aṉpu niliyiṉik kūṟutal.	(nac)
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉpiṉ piritalum pirital nimittamu mākap poruntuvaṉavākiya talivaṟkuriya ēḻaṉuḷ iḵtu āṟāvatu (nac.)
tammuḷ nēr eṉṟal -
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ iṭiccurattut talivaṉiyum taliviyiyum kaṇṭavarkaḷ “ivarkaḷukku nikar ivarē” eṉṟu avarkaḷuṭiya vaṭivaḻakiṉiyum kātaṉmiyiṉi yum viyantu kūṟutal. (pāli naṭiyiyal).			(vī. cō. 93 uri mēṟ.)
tammoṭu tāmē cāṟṟal -
talivaṉ talivi mutaliyōr piṟariṭam uriyāṭit tam karuttik kūṟutalēyaṉṟi, tammuḷ tāmē kūṟic koḷḷu talum uṇṭu. itaṉ payaṉ mūṭiniṉṟa vēkiṉṟa koḷkalam mūṭiyit tiṟantavaḻi āvi veḷippaṭutalāl veppam ciṟitu kuṟivatu pōlap piṟar kēṭpār illātavaḻiyum tammoṭu tāmē kūṟik koḷvataṉāl maṉattiluḷḷa cumi ciṟitu kuṟiyum. iḵtu akappāṭṭuṟup pākiya kūṟṟiṉuḷ aṭaṅkuvatu.	(na. a. 224)
tamar niṉivurittu varivu kaṭātal -
pakaṟkuṟi iṟutikkaṇ tōḻi talivaṉit taṉiyē kaṇṭu iṟceṟivu aṟivuṟutti varivu kaṭāviyatoṭu, taliviyatu paruvavaṉappuk kaṇṭu uṟaviṉar avaḷi iṟceṟittataṉi aṟintu ēṉiyār avaḷi makaṭpēca niṉikkum ceytiyi yum aṟivittu, avaḷi virivil variyumāṟu vēṇṭutal.
itaṉi ‘alar aṟivuṟuttal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa. (na. a. 166, i. vi. 523) itu pakaṟkuṟi eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 134)
tamar piṉ cēṟalik kaṇṭōr iraṅkal -
uṭaṉpōya taliviyi mīṭṭuvarat talivitamar avariru variyum piṉtoṭartalik kaṇṭavar iraṅkik kūṟutal.
“taliviyi aḻittuc cellum talivaṉ nalla vīraṉ tāṉ. āyiṉ avaṉi etirkkap palar varukiṟārkaḷē! iruva rukkum arukē pōy niṉṟu, pōr nikaḻāmal taṭukkak kūṭiyavar yāvar iruttal kūṭum?” (ampikā. 423) eṉa vaḻiyiṭik kaṇṭōr kūṟal.
itu variviyalil, ‘uṭaṉpōkku iṭiyīṭu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(i. vi. 547)
tamar piṉ vantuḻi nikaḻntami kūṟal -
uṭaṉpōkkut taṭippaṭavē, taṉ cuṟṟattāriṉ piṉṉē cellukiṉṟa talivi, talivaṉ ceyali eṇṇi varuntik kūṟutal.
“iṭiccurattil eṉ kātalaṉ maṟintu niṉṟa kuṉṟu, mikak koṭiya pōr ceypavarkaḷāṉa eṉ tanti mutaliyōrk kuc cāṉṟu kūṟuvatupōla, eṉ uyirkkātalaṉik kāṭṭik koṭuttu viṭumō eṉṟu eṉṉuyir mikavum ēṅkukiṟatu” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu variviyalil, ‘uṭaṉpōkku iṭiyīṭu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ampikā. 427)
tamariṟ peṟutal -
taliviyiṉ uṟaviṉarāl avaḷi aṭital. 
talivaṉum taliviyum nikaḻttum kaḷavoḻukkam aṟattoṭu niliyāl veḷippaṭṭa piṉ, talivaṉ tali viyi avaḷuṭiya peṟṟōr makiḻvuṭaṉ maṇam ceytu koṭuppattāṉ avaḷik karaṇattoṭu maṇattalē tamariṟ peṟutalām.	(tol. po.499 pērā.)
tirumaṇam kaṟpoḻukkattic cārntatu.
‘tamaruṭaṉ celpavaḷ avaṉpuṟam nōkkik kavaṉṟu araṟṟal’ -
uṭaṉpōkkil taliviyi mīṭkum nōkkuṭaṉ avaḷ cuṟṟattār varuvatikkaṇṭa talimakaṉ, avarkaḷoṭu pōriṭṭu avarkaḷik kollavō puṇpaṭuttavō kūṭāteṉṟu karutit taliviyi ōriṭattil niṟuttiviṭṭu akaṉṟu cellavē, cuṟṟattār avaḷi mīṭṭu aḻittup pōmpōtu, taṉakkākat talivaṉ puṟaṅkāṭṭiyami uṇarntu urukum talivi avaṉ tērppakkam pārttuk kavaliyoṭu kūṟutal.
“nāṉ talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻttiyapōtu eṉ tamiyaṉmār piṉ toṭarntuvara, avarkaḷukku ūṟu ceytal ākātu eṉṟu eṉakkākap puṟaṅkoṭuttup pōṉa eṉ talivaṉuṭiya tēril pūṭṭappaṭṭa kutirikaḷ eṉ tuyariṉip pōkkiyamiyāl, cūriyaṉ tēril pūṭṭappaṭṭa kutirikaḷ pōla nīṭuvāḻka!” (tañci. 365) eṉṟu talivi vāḻttuvatu.
itu variviyalil ‘uṭaṉpōkku iṭiyīṭu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na.a. 198)
tamiyar kāṇṭal -
talivaṉum taliviyum tam uṇarviṉar allarāka oruvari oruvar kāṇutal.	(iṟi. a. 2.)
tamiyar ātal -
tam uṇarviṉar allarātal. talivaṟku aṟivu, niṟi, ōrppu, kaṭippiṭi eṉpaṉa paṇpukaḷ; talivikku nāṇam, maṭam, accam, payirppu eṉpaṉa paṇpukaḷ.
aṟivāvatu epporuḷāyiṉum apporuṭkaṇ niṉṟu ammeymmiyi aṟital. niṟiyāvatu kāppaṉa kāttuk kaṭivaṉa kaṭintoḻukum oḻukkam. ōrppāvatu oru poruḷi ārāyntu uṇartal. kaṭippiṭiyāvatu koṇṭa poruḷ maṟavāmi.
nāṇ eṉpatu peṇṭirkku iyalpāka uḷḷatoru taṉmi. maṭamāvatu koḷuttak koṇṭu koṇṭatu viṭāmi. accamāvatu putupporuḷ kaṇṭaviṭattu añcuvatu. payirppāvatu payilāta poruṭkaṇ aruvaruttu niṟkum nilimi.
talivaṉ talivi ivaruṭiya paṇpukaḷum puṉal ōṭum vaḻiyil pul cāyntāṟ pōla, vēṭkiyāl mītūrappaṭṭuc cāyntu kiṭattalē tamiyarātalām.	(iṟi. a. 2)
tamiḻiyal vaḻakku -
paṇṭiya tamiḻnūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum aintiṇiyākiya kāmak kūṭṭam; ‘tamiḻiyal vaḻakkiṉaṉ taṇappumikap perukki’   (peruṅ. vattava. 17 : 67)
talittāḷ kaḻaṟal -
talivaṉ muṉ niṉṟu avaṉit talivi iṭitturittal. talivaṉāl tamakku ēṟpaṭṭa tuyarattip parattiyar taliviyiṭam vantu kūṟikkoḷvatuṇṭu. avarkaḷatu kūṟṟil uṇmiyum irukkalām; puṉinturiyum irukka lām. parattiyar talivaṉip paṟṟik kūṟum koṭumi kaḷit talivi uṇmiyākavē nampuvāḷ; taṉṉip pōlavē talivaṉ parattiyariyum varuttiyataṟku makiḻcci koḷvāḷ; taṉṉip pōlavē urimi pārāṭ ṭip parattiyarum talivaṉtoṭarpu paṟṟip pēcukiṟār kaḷē eṉṟu ūṭalum koḷvāḷ. ūṭalilliyēl, talivaṉ puṟattoḻukkam karuti maṉam veṟuttiruppāḷ; parattiyar irukkumpōtu talivaṉi iṭittu uri yāḷ; avarkaḷ tam kuṟi kūṟic ceṉṟa piṉṉart taṉimi yil talivaṉi yaṭintu avaṉ puṟattoḻukkam paṟṟi iṭittuk kūṟuvāḷ.	(to. po. 235 nac.)
talittāḷ - tirumuṉ eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa mariyātic col. talivi parattiyar kūṟiyatit talivaṉiṭam pulaviyilum, kalavi iṟutiyilum kūṟuvāḷ.	(231 iḷa.)
taliniṉṟu oḻukappaṭāniṉṟa paratti talimakaṉi neruṅkip pāṇarkku urittatu -
talimi koṇṭu ciṟantu vāḻum parattioruttiyit talivaṉ kalantu piṉ avaḷik kiviṭṭu vēṟoru parattipāṟ ceṉṟāṉ. avaṉi am mutaṟparatti iṭit tup pēca, avaḷatu vekuḷiyit taṇikkumpaṭi avaṉ pāṇaṉi viṭuttāṉ. apparatti appāṇaṉi nōkki, “pāṇaṉē! num talivaṉ em cēriyil vantu paricapporuḷ tantu eṉatu neñcatti vacappaṭuttip piṉ eṉṉik kiviṭṭa tīviṉi avaṉi viṭātu. em aṉṉi ciṉam uṭiyavaḷ. avaḷ ciṟitum iraṅkāḷ ātaliṉ, avaḷāl talivaṉ oṟukkappaṭutalum kūṭum. iṉit talivaṉ yāva rālum nakittaṟku uriyaṉāvāṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiyami.(naṟ. 150)
talippāṭu -
itu tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟkum vaki ēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu. “talivaṉ talivi ākiya iruvarum tāmē etirp paṭṭār; yāṉ aṟintilēṉ” eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟutal.			(to. po. 207 nac.)
talippeyal marapu iraṇṭu -
iyaṟkip puṇarccikkup piṉ talivaṉ taliviyit taṉittuk kāṇṭaṟkuriya vāyppu iraṇṭu. teyvam iṭiniṟpap pāṉmiyāl uykkappaṭṭu yārum illāta ōriṭattil talimakaḷik kaṇṇuṟṟa talivaṉ, maṟunāḷ taṉ pāṅkaṉ utaviyāl kuṟiyiṭattu avaḷik kāṇṭalum, pāṅkaṉi iṉṟit tāṉē taliviyit taṉittu niṟkum iṭattil kāṇṭalum eṉa iruvakic cantippil, ētēṉum oṉṟē nikaḻum. perumpālum pāṅkaṉāl ākum eṉṟu cintippāṉ; ciṟupāṉmi vitiyiṉāl ākum eṉṟu cintippāṉ. ātaliṉ pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam nikaḻiṉ iṭantalip pāṭu nikaḻātu; iṭantalippāṭu nikaḻiṉ pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam nikaḻātu. taliviyik kāṇṭal eḷitaṉṟu ātaliṉ, ivviraṇṭaṉuḷ oṉṟē nikaḻum eṉpatu.	(iṟi. a. 3)
talimakkaḷ oḻukalāṟukaḷuḷ kūṟṟu vakiyāṉ amivaṉa -
tām urikkap pukuvaṉavaṟṟik kēṭṭaṟkuriya irutiṇip poruḷkaḷiyum viḷittu muṉṉiliyākkik koḷḷu tal,
muṉṉiliyākkik koṇṭa māntariyum neñcu mutalāya vaṟṟiyum tam karuttiṉvaḻiyē oḻuka vēṇṭutal,
tamatu ārāta kātal pulappaṭa itaṉic ceytal aṟattāṟu ākum eṉat tam viruppiṉi iṉiteṭuttu iyamputal,
kūṭṭattiṟku ētuvākum muṟuvaṟkuṟippu mikkut tōṉ ṟāmal aṭakki niṟkum taliviyatu peṇmiyuṇarvit talivaṉ aṟintukōṭal,
taṇattalum, varivu nīṭṭittalum, alareḻutalum, tamar varivu maṟuttalum piṟavum ākiyavaṟṟāṉ nērnta varuttat tik kēṭpōr uḷaṅkoḷak kūṟal,
kaḷavoḻukkattiṉkaṇ nikaḻum nikaḻccikaṭku ēṟpat talivaṉ, talivi, tōḻi mutalāṉōr tam niliyiṉi eṭuttuk kūṟal,
oruvar koṇṭa aiyamum accamum varuttamum nīṅkat tam pēraṉpiṉiyum piriyiṉ āṟṟāta kēṇmiyiṉiyum cūḷuṟavoṭu kūṟit teḷivikkat teḷital - ivi ēḻum, ‘mey toṭṭup payiṟal’ (kaḷa.11) mutal ‘kaḷam cuṭṭukkiḷavi’ (30) īṟākak kūṟappeṟum talimakkaḷ oḻukalāṟukaḷuḷ kūṟṟuvakiyāṉ amiyum poruḷkaḷ.			(to.kaḷa. 10 ca.pāla.)
talimakkaḷāvār -
piṟari ēvik koḷḷum toḻil tamakku uriyarākiya aracar antaṇar vaṇikar ākiya makkaḷum; avarkaḷip pōlap piṟari ēvikkoḷḷum niliyiṉarākiya kuṟunila maṉṉar kaḷum, aivaki nilattiṟkum uriya talimakkaḷum urip poruḷ talivarātaṟkuriyar.	(to. po. 21, 22, 24 nac.)
mulli mutaliya aivaki nilattiṟkum uriya talimakkaḷ pōlap piṟarkku aṭimiyāyuḷḷavarum, aṭimiyar allāk kammiyar pōṉṟa toḻilāḷarkaḷum, taṉikkuṭi ēvalarkaḷum, ēṉiya ēval marapiṉarum ākiya kīḻōr nālvarum akattiṇikku urimiyuṭi yavarkaḷ.			(to. po. 21-24 pārati; kuḻa.)
talimakaḷ -
‘talivi’ kāṇka.
talimakaḷ, āṟṟāmiyit tāy talivaṉukkuc collutal -
“uṭal tuṭittu āṭi āṭi maṉamuruki uṉ peyari ‘naraciṅkā! vaḷḷalē! kaṇṇaṉē! eṉṟāṟ pōlac colliccolli aḻittu, ivaḷ peritum tuṉpamuṟṟu vāṭukiṉṟāḷ. ivaḷi aruḷātu viṭṭirukkum nī ivaḷi eṉṉatāṉ ceyyappōkiṟāy?” eṉṟa tāy kūṟṟu.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvāy. 2 - 4 - 1)
talimakaḷ taṉṉip puṟaṅkūṟiṉāḷākak kēṭṭa paratti avaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpac colliyatu -
“nam talivaṉuṭiya  maṉivi avaṉi nammoṭu kūṭṭi veṟuttup pēcukiṟāḷ. nām ataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa ceyal oṉṟum ceytilam. āṉāl avaḷ kūṟum paḻiyi el lōrum nampiviṭuvar. ātalāl pāṇaṉ mattaḷatti aṭippatu pōlat talivi taṉvayiṟṟil aṭittuk koṇṭu varuntumpaṭi ciṟitu nēram avaḷ teruviṉ pakkam ceṉṟu ulāvi varuvōm” eṉṟu paratti taṉ tōḻi yiṭam kūṟutal.	(akanā. 106)
talimakaḷ tūtuviṭutal -
nāyaki eṉṉum niliyaṭinta nammāḻvār nāriyit tam nāyakaṉāṉa tirumāliṭam tūtu pōkumpaṭi kēṭṭuk koḷvatāka aminta kiḷavi.
“aḻakiya ciṟakukaḷip peṟṟuḷḷa nāriyē! nīyum uṉ cēvalumākak kūṭit tirumāliṭam ceṉṟu avarmītu koṇṭa kātalāl nāṉ paṭum pāṭṭic colluka. avar ataṟkāka uṅkaḷip puṟakkaṇittāl kūṭa oṉṟum paḻutilli” eṉṟa kūṟṟu.
iṅṅaṉamē kuyil aṉṉam makaṉṟil kiḷi pūvi vāṭi neñcam eṉpaṉavaṟṟiyum parāṅkuca nāyaki tūtu viṭu kiṟāḷ.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvāy. 1 - 4 - 1)
talimakaḷ nilikaṇṭu tāy iraṅkal -
talivi kātalāl mayaṅkic ceyalaṟum niliyik kūṟit tāy varuntutal.
“tirumālē! uṉpāl tīrāta kātal koṇṭu uṉ perumikaḷiyē niṉintu niṉintu mayaluṟum eṉ makaḷ, niṉivi iḻantu, uṉ tiruttuḻāy māli vēṇ ṭum eṉṟu vāy pitaṟṟukiṟāḷē. ivaḷ ippaṭit tavikkum vaki nāṉ ceyta pāvantāṉ eṉṉavō?” eṉṟa tāy kūṟṟu.	(tiruvāy. 4 - 2 - 1)
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉpāṟpaṭum.
(talimakaḷ) nilimi kūṟal -
talivi uḷḷattu nilimiyit talivaṉ taṉṉuḷ collik koṇṭatu.
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattē, pakaṟkuṟiyil taliviyik kūṭi avaḷuṭiya kulinta āṭiaṇikaḷi avaḷ kavali yuṟātapaṭi tōḻi aṇivikkumāṟē aṇivittu avaḷatu peruvaṉappiṉik kaṇṭu avaḷip pukaḻum talivaṉ, “yāṉ virumpip pukaḻum pukaḻuri kēṭṭu ivaḷ peruvaṉap piṉaḷākak kāṭciyaḷippati nōkka, ivaḷ poykiyil tōṉṟiya tāmarip pūvākavum yāṉ ataṉi malarvikkum katiravaṉākavum iruppatāka niṉikkiṟēṉ” eṉṟāṟ pōlat taṉṉuḷ kūṟikkoḷḷutal.(ta.ne.vi. pak. 24; pā. 55)
itu tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam  (pakaṟkuṟi) eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu. itaṉi ‘uḷ makiḻnturittal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.	(ka. kā. pak. 76)
talimakaṟku avaṉuṭiya naṟṟāy colliyatu (1) -
(1) taliviyoṭu curattiṭi uṭaṉpōyt tāṉ talivi yoṭu koṇṭa toṭarpu ūril uṟuti ceyyappaṭṭa piṉ mīṇṭa talivaṉ taliviyoṭu taṉmaṉi vantāṉāka, avaṉi īṉṟa tāy avaṉiṭam, “makaṉē! maṉam iṭintu tuyarkkaṭalil mūḻkum uṉ maṉiviyiṉ tāykku uṉ maṉiviyik kāṭṭip piṟaku iṅku varuka!” eṉṟu kūṟutal.
itu kaṟpiṉuḷ ‘uṭaṉ celavu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(ta. ne. vi. pak. 46; pā. 134)
talimakaṟku avaṉuṭiya naṟṟāy colliyatu (2) -
uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻttit taliviyit taṉṉūril varintu koṇṭāṉ talivaṉ. avaṉuṭiya naṟṟāy, avarka ḷuṭiya tirumaṇak kōlattit taliviyiṉ naṟṟāykkum ceṉṟu kāṭṭumāṟu avaṉiṭam kūṟutal.
“naṉ minta! nīyum ivaḷum kūṭip piṉ cēymikkaṇ veñcuram kaṭantu vanta nāḷmutal, taṉ makaḷik kāṇātu varuntik kavalippaṭum ivaḷ naṟṟāykkum aṟam vaḻuvāta ivaḷatu maṅkala nāṇiyum ittirumaṇak kōlattiyum ceṉṟu kāṭṭuvāyāka!”
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku iṭiyīṭu’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ampikā. 429)
talimakaṟkuk kuṟi nērnta tōḻi avaṉ nilimi talimakaṭkuk kūṟi, avaḷ uritta māṟṟattāl avaḷ kuṟippaṟintu “ivvakiyāṉa talivaṟkuk kūṟu” eṉa avaḷuṭaṉ nakiyāṭik kūṭṭamuṇmi tāṉ aṟintami tōṟṟuvittatu -
“talivi! talivaṉ nām pārttāl nammi nōkkit toḻukiṟāṉ; caṟṟu vilakic ceṉṟāl varunti niṟkiṟāṉ. avaṉi iṉi aṭiyoṭu vilakka muṭiyavilli. eṉṉa ceyyalām?
“talivi! uṉṉit talivaṉiṭam nāṉ nērāka aḻittuc cellavilli. avaṉiṭam, ‘eṉ tōḷmēl karumpu eḻutit taruka’ eṉṟu vēṇṭuvatu pōla yāṉ colkiṟēṉ nī eṉṉit tēṭi vantavaḷip pōla avaṉi aṭitaṟ kuriya kuṟiyiṭam cel. avaṉ uṉṉaṭimītu viḻuntu uṉṉi vēṇṭuvāṉ!”
“tōḻi! iṉimēl itupōlap pēcuvatilli eṉṟu eṉṉit toṭṭuc cūḷurippāyāka!”
“talivi!  itil eṉṉa tavaṟu? uṉatu kaḷavoḻukki nāṉ pēcumpōtu nī nāṇi nilam kiḷittu eṉpakkal niṟpatu pōlat talivaṉpakkal niṉṟu nāṇi nilam kiḷippatu tāṉē nī ceyya vēṇṭuvatu?”	(kali. 63)
eṉṟāṟ pōlat tōḻi taliviyiṭam  uriyāṭal.
talimakaṟkuk kuṟi nērnta tōḻi talimakaṉōṭu uṟaḻntu colli, avaṉatu nīkkattukkaṇ avaṉkuṟi maṟāmikku ēṟpaṉa collit talimakaḷik kuṟi nayappittatu -
“taliva! nī ētō etirpārttu vantiruppavaṉip pōla emmi niḻalpōlat toṭarntu varukiṟāy!  nī vēṇṭuvatu yātu!”
“naṅkāy! kēṭṭatu kiṭṭāviṭṭāl yāṉ uyir viṭutalum kūṭum.”
“aiya! ivaḷ tanti peruvaḷḷal; yāvarukkum vēṇṭiyatu vaḻaṅkuvāṉ.  nī vēṇṭuvatu yātu?”
“pētāy! poruṭkuṟi illi. uṉ talivi eṉakku aruḷ ceyya vēṇṭum.”
“aṉṉiyō! perumakaṉukku oru peṇ aruḷuvatu yātuḷatu?”
“talivi! ittalivaṉ ciṟitum nāṇam illātavaṉ. nām eḷḷi nakiyāṭiṉum varukiṟāṉ. kaḷvar pōl nammi nōkkukiṟāṉ. taṉ viruppiṉi īṭēṟṟiyē celvāṉ pōlum! nām avaṉukku utavi ceyyavilliyeṉiṉ maṭalēṟuvāṉpōlum!” (kali. 61) eṉṟāṟ pōla aminta uriyāṭal.
talimakaṟkut tōḻi teyvam iṟiñciyamiyic colliyatu -
iruvarum maṇam ceytukoṇṭu illaṟam naṭattum pōtu, tōḻi, avaṉ taliviyi maṇam ceytukoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉat tāṉum taliviyum kaṭavuḷi vēṇṭik koṇṭa kaḷavukkālac ceytiyik kūṟutal.
“taliva! em cuṟṟattār intat tirumaṇattiṟku icintu ivaḷi uṉakkē taruka eṉṟum, nīyum taliviyiṉpāl koṇṭa niliyāṉa kātali maṟavāmal ivaḷiyē varintu koḷvāyāka eṉṟum yāṉum ivaḷum. iṟivaṉi iṟiñciṉōm” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.			(ampikā. 437)
iḵtu ilvāḻkki eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatorukūṟṟu.
talimakaṉ -
‘talivaṉ’ kāṇka.
talimakaṉ iṭiccurattu niṉṟu colliyatu -
“neñcē! vāṉattil iruḷparakkum. ikkoṭiya māli nērattē āṟali kaḷvar koḷḷiyiṭutaṟku vāyppiṉṟi muṭaṅkik kiṭakkum veppamikka koṭiya pālivaḻiyil eṉṉi aḻittu vantu niṟutti nam taliviyiṉ kuṇatti īṇṭu niṉittu mīṇṭupōkak karutukiṟāyē! iḵtu aḻakitā?” eṉṟu talivaṉ neñciṉik kaḻaṟutal.			(akanā. 365)
talimakaṉ uruveḻil uritta talimakaḷ vārtti -
“eṉ perumāṉuṭiya kaṇṇum kiyum kālum centāmari pōl eḻil mikkaṉa. avaṉ tirumēṉi nīlamali pōlvatu. avaṉuṭiya aḻakup polivu evvulakattavarālum niṉik kavum iyalāta perumiyuṭiyatu”.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvi. 43)
talimakaṉ talimakaḷoṭu puṉalāṭiṉāṉ eṉakkēṭṭu iṉpuṟṟu cevilittāy tōḻiyi, “nīṅkaḷ āṭiya puṉal aṇi iṉpam kūṟuka” eṉṟāṭku, avaḷ appuṉalaṇi iṉpamum palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa iṉpamum talimakaṉ kātaṉmiyum kūṟi, “eṉṟum inta nīraṇi iṉpam peṟuka yām!” eṉṟatu -
mēkam kaṭali mukantu maḻi poḻiyavē maliccikaraṅ kaḷiṉiṉṟu  aruvinīr peruki, maṟṟiya puṉaloṭum kalantu kalavimaṇam pala uṭkoṇṭu, viyiyil putuppuṉa lākap perukiyatu. kaṭal ūric cūḻntatu eṉap palarum araṟṟa, veḷḷanīr vayaṟ parappuḷ pukuntatu. malipōṉṟa karikaḷiṭiyē viriyum viyip putuppuṉal, mintar kaṇṇi, makaḷir vaḷi mutaliyavaṟṟik kavarntu pūvum nuri yum pōrppa varuvataṉkaṇ, puṉalāṭṭu ayarvārāyiṉar, makaḷirum mintarum. puṉalāṭṭut tīrntu uṭampiṉil kuḷir nīkki veppamuṇṭākkat talivi matu ēntiṉāḷ. muṉpu neytalmalar pōṉṟirunta avaḷkaṇkaḷ matu māntiyami yāl naṟavampūp pōlac civantaṉa. avaḷip pārāṭṭiya vāṟē talivaṉ avaḷic cārntāṉ; ūṭaluṟṟa avaḷip paṇintāṉ. paṇinta piṉṉarum ūṭal taṇin tilaḷ avaḷ!” eṉṟa kūṟṟu.	(paripā. 7)
talimakaṉ pirintavaḻi avaṉ kuṟitta paruvavaraviṉkaṇ tōḻi aṟivarik kaṇṭu viṉāviyatu -
“kuṟṟamaṟṟa teruvilē nāyē illāta maṉimukappilē, cennel cōṟṟuruṇṭi veḷḷiya ney ākiyavaṟṟāl ōrillattil iṭum picciyip peṟṟut tiruptiyōṭu uṇṭu, paṉikkālattukkup parukutaṟkēṟṟa vennīri um kamaṇṭalattil peṟṟukkoṇṭu, aṟiva! vāṭikkālam eppoḻutu toṭaṅkum eṉpatik kūṟuka. ēṉeṉil akkālattu em talivar viṉi muṭittu mīḷvar” eṉṟu tōḻi aṟivari viṉavutal.	(kuṟun. 277)
talimakaṉ pirintaviṭattuk kaṉākkaṇṭu colliyatu -
“kaṉavē! pātirimalariṉ tuyyip pōṉṟa melliya mayir eḻuntu paṭarnta aḻaku oḻukum māmiyiyum nuṇṇiya aṇikalaṉkaḷiyum uṭiya eṉ taliviyik koṇarntu koṭuttāyip pōla iṉiya uṟakkattiṉiṉṟum eḻuppu kiṟāy. tam tuṇiviyarip pirintavarkaḷ niṉṉi ikaḻār. ciṟitu nēram taliviyik kāṇum vāypput tanta nī vāḻka!” eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(kuṟun. 147)
talimakaṉ maṟuttal -
“maṇantu kōṭalē ulaka iyal” eṉṟuritta tōḻikkut talivaṉ maṟutturittal.
“nī kūṟum ulakiyali yāṉum aṟivēṉ. ivaḷi nāṉ varintukoḷḷap pōvatum uṟuti. āyiṉ, itupōtu niṉ utavi peṟṟut taliviyuṭaṉ kūṭappeṟṟilēṉ eṉil, nāṉ uyir vāḻtalē iyalātu. ātaliṉ ippoḻutu aruḷ ceyka!” (tañci. 95) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 144)
talimakaṉ maṉivayiṉ pōkak karutiṉāṉ eṉpatu collit taṉ tōḻikkup paratti kūṟiyatu -
“tōḻi! nam talivaṉ taṉ colli nampi vāḻpavarkaḷ maṉaniliyip paṟṟik kavalippaṭupavaṉ allaṉ. taḷiri otta mēṉiyiyum, toṭiyi aṇinta muṉkikaḷiyum uṭiya nammit taṉ piriviṉāl aḻacceytu pirital avaṉukku iyalpē” (aiṅ. 38) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟutal.
talimakaṉ vāyil peṟṟup pukuntatu aṟiyātu vanta pāṇaṟkut talimakaḷ nakiyāṭic colliyatu -
“pāṇa! nī paṇpaṟṟavaṉ. nī aṉpaṟṟa coṟkaḷiyum kaṭumi yāṉa coṟkaḷiyum pēcak kaṟṟuḷḷāyē aṉṟit tali vaṉip parattiyariṭamiruntu mīṭṭu eṉakkut tarum āṟṟal uṭiyi alli” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(aiṅ. 138)
talimakaṉ vāyil peṟṟup pukuntu nīṅkiṉaviṭattum avaṉ ceyta paṇṭiya tīṅku niṉintu vēṟupaṭṭirunta talivi, viṉaviya pāṇaṟkuc colliyatu -
“pāṇa! num talivaṉik kūṭi aḻakiḻanta makaḷir eṉṟā vatu tam paḻiya aḻakiṉi mīṇṭum peṟṟuc ciṟantiruppa taṉik kaṇṭirukkiṉṟāyā?” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu. (aiṅ. 137)
talimakaṉukku vāyil nērnta tōḻi, talimakaḷiṭattup poṟāmi kaṇṭu colliyatu -
parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉi etirēṟṟu upacaritta tōḻi taliviyiṭam ceṉṟu avaṉ varuki paṟṟik kūṟutalum, avaḷ ciṉam mikuntu uṭaṉpaṭāmiyiṉ, “talivaṉ iṉipparattiyariṭamē viruppamuṭiyavaṉāy maṉivaḻiyē varamāṭṭāṉ; appaṭi variṉum nam talivi tāṉ pulavi nīṅkamāṭṭāḷ. ivviruvaruṭiya maṉa māṟupāṭum. yāṉ iṟantāltāṉ tīrumpōlum!” eṉṟu tōḻi nontu kūṟutal.	(naṟ. 180)
talimakaṉip paratti nontu colliyatu -
parattiyiṉ pirinta talivaṉ mīṇṭu talivipāl varuta lum, avaḷ ciṉakkavē, talivaṉ taṭumāṟa, avaṉuṭiya iḷami mutirnta kāmakkiḻatti talivi vekuḷātapaṭi kūṟi uṭaṉpaṭuttat toṭaṅki, “tuṟivaṉē! paruvam ceṉṟapiṉ aṉṟō, nī taliviyi emmip pōlak kiviṭat tuṇiyalām? nī oḻuṅkāka ārāyntu kuṟṟam vārātu naṭavāyeṉiṉ, niṉṉi virumpiyavarkaḷ tīyiṭippaṭṭa malarpōl vāṭivataṅkiviṭuvar” eṉṟu kaṭintu kūṟi ūṭal tīrttu iruvariyum kūṭṭiyatu.	(naṟ. 315)
talimakaṉoṭu tāṉ puṇarnta kalavi karutit talivi pulattal -
puṇarccikkaṇṇum talivi ūṭal koḷḷutal.
talivaṉuṭiya puṟattoḻukkam paṟṟi maṉattiṟ puḻukkam koṇṭa talivi paṭukkikkaṇ talivaṉoṭu viki iruvarumāka atarapāṉam parukiyapiṉṉar, iruvarum oṉ ṟākak kalakkum kalavi muṭiyum niliyil. ittakiya iṉpattit taṉakku māttiramē nalkātu parattiyarkkum talivaṉ nalkuvataṉi niṉintu avaṉpāl pulavi koṇ ṭami.
iḵtu ‘uṇarvatōṭu uṇarā ūṭal’ eṉṉum tokutiyil uḷḷa kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu; ‘piṟavum’ eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.	(mā. akap. 104 uri)
talivaṟku vāyil nērāta talivi koṭumiyi avaṉ kūṟak kēṭṭa tōḻi avaṟkuc colliyatu -
‘talivaṉ taṉakku vāyilnērāta taliviyatu........... col liyatu’ - kāṇka.
talivaṉ -
‘tolkāppiyam kuṟippiṭum talivaṉ’ kāṇka.
talivaṉ añcavanta urimikkaṇ kūṟiyatu -
talivaṉ, tāṉum piṟarum añcumpaṭiyākat talivimāṭ ṭuḷatākiya kaṟpākiya urimiyiṭattuk kūṟiyatu.			(to. po. 144 iḷa, 146 nac.)
aḵtu illaṟam nikaḻttumāṟu taṉmaṉattāl palavāṟu kāṇalum, piṟarkkut tāṉ koṭuttalum, kaṟpuc ciṟattalu mām.	(nac.)
talivi illaṟappakutiyi nikaḻttumāṟu palavakiyākak kāṇum taṉmi, uṇarvuṭiyōṉ ōtiya nūl viriyumāṟu pōla viriyāniṉṟatu eṉavum, ivaḷ koṭinalam vaḷḷaṉmi pūṇṭāṉ poruḷi ottatu eṉavum, ivaḷ kaṟpucciṟappup piṟarkku accam ceytaliṉ vāḷiṉi ottatu eṉavum talivaṉ avaḷ urimikaḷi viyantu kūṟiyatu.			(nālaṭi. 386)
talivaṉ ‘antaṇar tiṟattum cāṉṟōr tēettum, antamil ciṟappiṉ piṟarpiṟar tiṟattiṉum, oḻukkam kāṭṭiya kuṟippiṉum’ kūṟal -
vēṭpitta āciriyaṉum kaṟpitta āciriyaṉumākiya pārppār kaṇṇum, muṟṟu uṇarntu aimpoṟiyiyum aṭakkiyōr kaṇṇum, muṭivilāc ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya tēvarkaṇṇum oḻukum oḻukkattiṉit tāṉ toḻutu kāṭṭiya kuṟippiṉ kaṇṇum talivaṉ talivikkuk kūṟutal.
‘piṟarpiṟar’ eṉṟār, tēvar mūvar eṉpatu paṟṟi. taṉṉi yaṉṟit teyvam toḻātāḷi ittaṉmiyōrit toḻa vēṇṭum eṉṟu toḻutu kāṭṭiṉāṉ.			(to. po. 146 nac.)
tuṟavikaḷākiya antaṇarkaḷiṭattilum, periyōrkaḷiṭatti lum mikka ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya peṟṟōr cuṟṟam mutali yōriṭattilum naṭantukoḷḷa vēṇṭiya muṟimiyik kūṟumiṭattum.
cāṉṟōr - ūrp periyōrkaḷ, tuṟavātōr.
piṟarpiṟar - māmaṉ māmi pāṭṭaṉ mutaliyōr.			(po. 194 kuḻa.)
talivaṉ, ‘antarattu eḻutiya eḻuttiṉ māṉa, vanta kuṟṟam vaḻi keṭa oḻukutaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
kaḷavukkālattu oḻukiya oḻukkak kuṟipāṭṭāṉ nikaḻnta kuṟṟatti ākāyatteḻuttup pōla vaḻikeṭa oḻuku taṟkaṇ kūṟutal.
kaḷavukkālattu uṇṭākiya pāvam ākāyattileḻutiya eḻuttu vaḻi keṭumāṟu pōla vaḻikeṭumpaṭi pirāyac cittam ceytu oḻukutaṟkaṇ kūṟutal. atu muṉpu pōlak kuṟṟam cāṉṟa poruḷi vaḻuvamittuk koḷḷātu, kuṟṟameṉṟē karutik kaṭitalām.
“meypporuḷ uṇarntōri niṉintu avaric cārntu avariṭattuk kaṟṟa poyyaṟṟa nūṟkēḷvikaḷālē maṉattil mācaṟṟa virataṅkaḷit taṅkac ceytal” eṉṟu talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam kūṟiyatu. (kali. 15)	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ, kaḷavukkālattup pakaliṉum maṟiviṉum kūṭi oḻukiya tuṉpam vāṉatteḻutiya eḻuttuppōla nīṅkat takunta nērattil, kūṭi vāḻtalik kūṟuvaṉ; muṉ pakaliṉum accattoṭum virivoṭum kūṭiyatu pōlaṉṟit takka nērattil amitiyākak kūṭutalik kūṟuvaṉ.			(to. po. 194 kuḻanti)
talivaṉ, ‘aruntoḻil muṭitta cemmaṟkāli, viruntoṭu nallavi vēṇṭaṟkaṇ’ kūṟutal -
ariya viṉi muṭitta talimik kālattu viruntiṉaroṭu kūṭa nallaṉavaṟṟik kūṟi viruppamuṟutaṟkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟutal.	(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
ceyaṟkaritākiya viṉiyi muṭitta talimiyi eytiya kālattē, talivi viruntetir kōṭalōṭē nīrāṭik kōlam ceytal mutaliyavaṟṟik kāṇṭal vēṇṭiya iṭattum talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum.
‘paral niṟinta vaḻiyil, uppuvaṇikar mikka kaḷarnilattil kuṭiyoṭu cellum putiya māntarkāḷ! ituvari eṉ pirivāl varuntik kaṇṇīr vaṭittukkoṇṭirunta eṉ kātali ippoḻutu nīṇṭa kūntali vārip piṉṉi aṇi kalaṉkaḷ aṇintu tāṉē camiyalaṟikkuc ceṉṟu uṇavu aṭum nili itaṟkumuṉ eṉakkuk kāṇum vāyppilli. iṉṟu aḵtu eytiyuḷḷatu” eṉṟu talivaṉ makiḻntu kūṟutal. (naṟ. 374) 	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ allakuṟippaṭṭu varumpōtu neñciṟkuk kūṟutal -
talivaṉ taṉ mikka talimippāṭṭiṉāṉ poḻutaṟintu vārāmiyiṉ avaṉ ceyyakkūṭiya kuṟikaḷ avaṉāṉaṉṟi iyaṟkiyāka nikaḻntaviṭattē, taliviyum tōḻiyum iravukkuṟiyiṭattu vantu avaṉik kāṇātu mīṇṭu pōṉa piṉ, avaṉ vantu kuṟiyiṭattē taliviyik kāṇātu mīḷumpōtu,
“neñcē! aḻakiyāḷākiya taliviyi virumpi ik koṭiya vaḻikaḷik kaṭakkac ceytu eṉṉi allaṟpaṭuttu kiṟāy. kiṭṭutaṟku eḷiyaḷ allāta taliviyik karuti eṉakkut tīrāta tuṉpam tarum nī uṉmārpakattu vēl eyyappaṭa valiyiḻantu varuntuvāyāka!” (akanā. 212) eṉṟāṟ pōlat taṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟutal.	(to. po. 134 nac.)
talivaṉ, ‘allal tīra ārvamoṭu aḷiic, colluṟu poruḷiṉkaṇ’ kūṟiyatu -
talivi taṉ kaḷavukkālat tuṉpam tīra ārvattoṭu poruntac collappaṭṭa poruṇmikkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟal.	(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
tirumaṇattiṉ piṉṉar mūṉṟunāḷ kūṭṭamiṉmikkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa eṉṟu talivi maṉattilirunta varuttam tīrumāṟu talivaṉ vētavitiyit talivikku vakuttuk kūṟutal.	(to. po. 146 nac.)
“eṉ tāyum niṉtāyum itaṟku muṉ oruvari oruvar aṟintavarallar; eṉ tantiyum niṉtantiyum itaṟkumuṉ uṟaviṉarum allar; yāṉum nīyum itaṟkumuṉ oruvari oruvar aṟintōmallōm. cemmaṇnilattil vīḻnta maḻinīr maṇṇiṉ cenniṟamum ataṉ cuviyum pirikka iyalāta vakiyiṟ peṟṟāṟpōla, aṉpāṉ piṇikkap paṭṭa nam iru neñcaṅkaḷum piṇintaṉa” (kuṟun. 40) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟil avaṉ kātal mikuti tōṉṟak kūṟutal peṟappaṭṭatu.	(iḷa.)
“varinta kālattu mūṉṟunāḷ kūṭṭam iṉmikkuk kāraṇam eṉ?” eṉṟu talivi maṉattu nikaḻāniṉṟa varuttam tīrumpaṭi, mikka vēṭkiyoṭu kūṭiyiruntu vētam collutaluṟṟa poruḷiṉkaṇ talivaṉ virittu viḷaṅkak kūṟal. atu “mutal nāḷ taṇkatirc celvaṟkum, iṭināḷ kantaruvarukkum, piṉṉāḷ aṅkiyaṅ kaṭavuṭkum aḷittu nāṉkām nāḷ aṅkiyaṅkaṭavuḷ eṉakku niṉṉi aḷippa yāṉ nukara vēṇṭiṟṟu” eṉṟu talivaṉ talivikku viḷaṅkak kūṟal.	(nac.)
varinta piṉṉarum talivivaṉappuk kuṉṟik kāṭṭa atu paṟṟit talivaṉ tōḻiyi viṉāyavaḻit tōḻi, “pakkalēyē iruntu karuṇi ceyyiṉum, ciṟitaḷavu muyakkam kinekiḻiṉum kaṇpacakkum talivikku ōramaḷikkaṇṇē tuyilappeṟṟum vētaviti paṟṟik kūṭṭam nikaḻāmiyiṉ piṟanta vēṟupāṭaṉṟō itu! itaṉi nīyē muyaṅki aṟivikkumāṟaṉṟi yāṉ ceyaṟpālatu yātu?” eṉṟa karuttuppaṭak kūṟa, atu kēṭṭa talivaṉ talivikkuk kūṟutal. 	(naṟ. 35)
talivaṉ avvaki viṉātal -
taliviyum tōḻiyum cērntu irukkum cevvi yaṟintu vanta talivaṉ, muṉ pōlavē, ūrum pērum keṭutiyum pōṉṟavaṟṟi viṉavutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkimati uṭaṉpāṭu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 141)
talivaṉ, ‘avvaḻip (pirintavaḻi) perukiya ciṟappiṉkaṇ’ kūṟal -
pirinta talivaṉ tāṉ peṟṟa perukkam eytiya ciṟap piṉkaṇ kūṟṟu nikaḻttutal. 	(to. po. 146 nac.)
“kāntaḷ moṭṭu, mullippū, kuvaḷippū, ivaṟṟit toṭuttu aḻakākak kaṭṭiya pūmāli pōla naṟumaṇam kamaḻum taliviyiṉ uṭal taḷiriṉum mellitu; taḻuvaṟkum iṉitu” eṉṟu talivaṉ pirintuḻip perukiya ciṟappiṉkaṇ kūṟutal.	(kuṟun. 62) (144 iḷa.)
“kēḷir tuyarip pōkkavum, uṟaviṉar makiḻvāka nukaravum, pakivarkaḷum nammoṭu naṭpuc ceyyavum, poruḷ utavum eṉṟu karuti īṭṭivanta perumporuḷāṉ makiḻvōṭu uḷḷēṉ” (akanā. 93) eṉavum,
“vāṉamē! eṭutta viṉiyi naṉku muṭitta talimiyiṉi yuṭiya ciṟappōṭu yāṉ mīṇṭuvantu taliviyiṉ kūnta laṇiyil uṟaṅkum vāyppup peṟṟuviṭṭēṉātaliṉ, iṉi nī naṉku miṉṉi iṭittu maḻi poḻika!” 			(kuṟun. 270) eṉavum,
“maṉamē! taliviyip piriyumpoḻutu viṉimuṭitta aṉṟē mīṇṭuvaruvatākak kūṟiyuḷḷōm; avaḷ paṭum tuṉpam ellām tīra, iṭiyaṉ cūṭiya mullippū maṇaṅ kamaḻum ikkārkāla māliyil,  nam varaviṉi nam illat tirukkum talivikkup palli olittut terivikkuṅ kollō?” (naṟ. 169) eṉavum mīṇṭuvaruvōṉ makiḻ voṭu kūṟutal.	(nac.)
talivaṉ avaṭpeṟṟu malintu kūṟal -
avaṭpeṟṟu malitalāvatu talivaṉ pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟi ivaṟṟukkut tōḻiyiṉ uṭaṉpāṭu peṟṟu makiḻntu kūṟutal eṉavum, taliviyit talivaṉ iravukkuṟi pakaṟkuṟi iraṇṭaṉkaṇṇum peṟṟu makiḻntu kūṟutal eṉavum iruvakip poruḷpaṭum.
pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟikaḷukkut tōḻiyiṉ icivu peṟṟa talivaṉ, “maṭanti! uṉ naṟpaṇpu palavum eṉakkup payaṉpaṭum vakiyil  eṉakku virumpik karuṇi ceyta nī, mūṅkil pōṉṟa tōḷkaḷiyum naṟiya neṟṟiyiṉiyum uṭiya taliviyoṭu mella naṭantu nī kuṟitta iṭattiṟku varuka!” (aiṅ. 175) eṉṟu tōḻiyiṭam makiḻntu kūṟutal.			(to. po. 103 nac.)
talivaṉ (kiḻavōṉ) avaḷ nāṭṭu aṇiyiyal viṉātal -
taṉṉit taṉ nāṭṭiṉ pū mutaliyavaṟṟi viṉaviya tōḻi yiṭam, talivināṭṭiṉ pū mutalāṉavi yāviyeṉa viṉavutal. kuṟippaṟinta talivaṉ, taliviyiṉ nāṭṭic cārntavaṉ pōlavē iravukkuṟiyiṭam vantu cērum nōkkuṭaṉ ivaṟṟik kēṭṭaṟiya virumpuvāṉ.
itaṉi ‘uṭkoṇṭu viṉātal’ eṉṉum tirukkōviyār.		(153)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 158)
talivaṉ, ‘aḻiyal añcal eṉṟu āyiru poruḷiṉkaṇ kūṟal’-
“vanta kuṟṟam niṉakku uḷḷatu eṉṟu aḻiyaṟka” eṉavum, “eṉakku uḷḷatu eṉṟu añcaṟka” eṉavum collappaṭum ivviruporuṇmikkaṇṇum talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“cemmaṇnilattil peyta nīr pirikka iyalātavaki cenniṟamum cuviyum peṟṟāṟ pōla, aṉpuṭi neñcam iraṇṭum pirippu aṟap piṇintatāl ēṟpaṭṭa kalappu ātaliṉ itu teyvattāṉ āyiṟṟu” eṉat teruṭṭutal. (kuṟun. 40) 	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ taliviyi nōkki, “varuntaṟka, añcaṟka” eṉṟu tāṉ avaḷi eṉṟum piriyumāṟu illi eṉṟu kūṟiya iru karuttiṉiyum tavaṟip puṟappeṇṭirmāṭṭup pirintavaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻttutal.	(144 iḷa.)
“pāṇa! naṭivaṇṭi uruṭṭum eṉ ciṟuvaṉit tūkkik koṇṭēṉ. avaṉ vāyiṉiṉṟu oḻukiya eccil yāṉ pūci yirunta cantaṉattic citittatu. ataṉōṭu eṉ kātaliyit taḻuvac ceṉṟēṉāka. avaḷ ataṉit tavaṟāka uṇarntu añciya māṉpiṇi pōla otuṅki niṉṟāḷ; yāṉ puṟattoḻukkamuṭiyēṉ eṉṟu taṉakku iṭar vantapōtu talivi varuntāmalum añcāmalum iruttal vēṇṭum” eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal.	(194 kuḻa.)
talivaṉ āyattoṭu pōkiṉṟāḷik kaṇṭu kūṟal -
“uyir kaṭpulaṉākātatu eṉṟu ituvari kūṟivanta makkaḷ poyyurittuviṭṭaṉar. iṅṅaṉam poy pēcaṟku avarkaḷ ciṟitum nāṇam koḷḷavilliyē! eṉ āruyir kāṭcikkup pulaṉāka iruttali nāṉ kāṇkiṟēṉē! aḵtu aḻakākap pēcukiṟatu; meṉmiyāka naṭakkiṟatu; tiraṇṭa muṉkālkaḷiyum melliya iṭiyiyum kuḷircci tarum kaṇkaḷiyum virumpattakka mūṅkil pōṉṟa periya tōḷkaḷiyum uṭiyatu” eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivaṉ taliviyi vaṭivueṭuttuc cellum taṉ uyirākavē kūṟutal. (iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘payiṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 101. nac.)
talivaṉ (kiḻavōṉ) āṟṟal -
pāṅki, tāṉ koṭutta kiyuṟiyi ēṟṟuk kūṟiya coṟkaḷ potuppaṭa - taṉakkuc cārpākavō cārpiṉṟiyō - iruppiṉum, kuṟippāl avi taṉakkuk kuṟi nērvittatu makiḻcciyit tarum eṉṟu talivaṉ āṟutal koḷḷutal.
“eṉ talivikku eṉatu uḷḷak kiṭakkiyit terivittu eṅkaḷ kātal ciṟakkac ceyya eṉ kiyuṟiyit talivikkup peṟṟuc ceṉṟa ittōḻikku yāṉ eṉṉa kimmāṟu ceyya vallēṉ!’ (ampikā. 133) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 146)
talivaṉ āṟṟuvittirunta arumi viṉātal -
taṉ pirivukkālattil tōḻi taliviyi varuntāmal kātta tiṟattit talivaṉ viṉavutal.
“perumpālāṉa nēram eṉṉuṭaṉēyē iruntu ciṟitu pirintālum appirivit tāṅkātu tavikkum taliviyi yāṉ poruḷvayiṟ pirintirunta pala nāḷkaḷum yāṅkaṉam amitiyuṟumāṟu ceytuvittiruntāy?” (ampikā. 327) eṉṟu talivaṉ tōḻiyi viṉātal.
itu ‘variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(i. vi. 527 uri)
talivaṉ iṭampeṟṟut taḻuvutaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ nikaḻvatu eṉavum, iṭantalip pāṭṭiṉkaṇ nikaḻvatu eṉavum irutiṟatta vākiya karuttukkaḷ uḷa.
poy pārāṭṭal kāraṇamākat taliviyiṭam nerukkam peṟṟu avaḷit tāṉ taḻuvak karutiya ceytiyik kūṟal (to. po. 99 iḷa.) eṉavum, meytoṭṭup payiṟal poy pārāṭṭal ivviraṇṭiṉāṉum taliviyi vacīkaritta talivaṉ avaḷ pārtta pārviyiṉ uṭkaruttit taṉṉiṭattuc cērttukkoṇṭu kūṟal (102 nac.) eṉavum poruḷ kūṟupa.
iṭampeṟṟut taḻuvaṟkaṇ talivaṉ, “peṇṇē! yāṉi yiṉ veḷḷiya tantaṅkaḷiyum pulittōliyum tiṟi yākap peṟum valimi cāṉṟa niṉ tamiyaṉmār pakivar kaḷi ampu oṉṟaṉālēyē eytu uyirip pōkkuvar. āyiṉ, nīyō yāṉ ivviṭattiviṭṭup pōkātirukku māṟu iraṇṭu ampiṉāṉ eytu eṉṉit taṭuttuḷḷāy” (tiṇimāli. 22) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟutal.	(nac.)
talivaṉ iṭiyūṟu kiḷattaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
talivaṉ taliviyi iṭam peṟṟut taḻuviyavaḻi, nāṇa mum maṭaṉum uṭiya talivi aṟivunalaṉ iḻantu oṉṟum aṟiyātu uyirttaṉaḷ. ituvari illāta vakiyil putitāka āṭavaṉoruvaṉ mēṉi taṉ mēṉiyil paṭṭavaḻi avaḷ aṟiviḻattalum perumūcceṟitalum kūṭum. annili yil puliyaṉ toṭṭa tīmpāl viruppum veṟuppum orē kālattil payappatu pōlat talivi kātalum nāṇamum orucēra amiyak kaṇkaḷik kikaḷāṉ pottikkoḷ ḷutal, oru kompiyō koṭiyiyō paṟṟukkōṭākac cārtal mutaliyaṉa ceytāḷākat talivaṉ, talivi nikaḻttuvaṉa tāṉ karutum ūṟṟiṉpattiṟku iṭiyūṟu ākumāṟṟik kūṟutal.
iḵtu iyaṟkippuṇarccikku muṉ nikaḻum eṉavum iṭan talippāṭṭukku muṉ nikaḻum eṉavum irutiṟak karuttukkaḷ uḷa.
inniliyil talivaṉ taliviyi nōkki, “yāṉ niṉṉit taḻuvikkoṇṭu cila coṟkaḷ pēcavum, avaṟṟiṟku maṟu moḻi tārāmal mukattik kaviḻttuk koṇṭu nāṇattāṉ niṉ kaṇkaḷ iraṇṭaṉiyum niṉ kikaḷāl pottik koṇṭuḷḷāy. eṉakkuk kāmam elli kaṭantu pōyiṉ. ataṉit taṭuttu niṟuttutal attuṇi eḷiya ceyalākātu. kaṭi civanta niṉ kaṇmāttiramā eṉṉit tuṉpuṟuttu kiṉṟaṉa? toyyilākak karumpu eḻutappaṭṭa niṉ tōḷ kaḷum eṉakkut tuṉpam tarukiṉṟaṉa. kaṇkaḷip putittatu pōlavē. tōḷkaḷiyum putittukkoḷ” (naṟ. 39) eṉṟal pōlat tāṉ karutum puṇarccikku iṭiyūṟāṉa vaṟṟik kūṟutal.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ iravukkuṟikkaṇ parivuṟṟuk kūṟal -
talivaṉ iravukkuṟi tāmatamāyiṉavaḻi varuntik kūṟutal.
“kaṭalum oli aṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. cāraṟ kāṟṟu vīcum kaṭaṟkaric cōliyum poliviḻantu viṭṭatu. kūki taṉ kurālōṭu (pēṭiyōṭu) ūril uḷḷa nāṟcantikaḷil accam uṇṭāka olikkiṟatu. pēykaḷum veḷippaṭṭu ulaviniṟkum. oruvari oruvar  aṟitaṟku iyalāta vakiyil iruḷ ceṟin tuḷḷatu. ittakiya cūḻniliyil taliviyit taḻuvak karuti, mīṉkaḷum uṟaṅkum nērattilum yāṉ uṟaṅkātu taṭu māṟukiṉṟēṉ. ātaliṉ, eṉatu nili yātāy muṭiyumō?” (naṟ. 319) eṉavum,
“maḻiyiṉ varukiyi aṟintu mayilkaḷ āṭum pakkamali kaḷi aṭutta nallūriluḷḷa talivi eṉakkuk karuṇi ceyya virumpāviṭiṉum, yāṉ avaḷiyē eppoḻutum niṉittukkoṇṭu iruppatāl uṟakkam eṉpataṉiyē maṟantuviṭṭēṉ” (aiṅ. 298) eṉavum palavāṟu iravukkuṟi iṭiyīṭṭil talivaṉ pulampik kūṟutal.			(to. po. 103 nac.)
talivaṉ (iṟiyōṉ) iruṭkuṟi vēṇṭal -
talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam maṉippuṟattil taliviyuṭaṉ iravil kūṭum kuṟiyiṭam kūṟumāṟu vēṇṭal.
“uṅkaḷ cīṟūrkku iṉṟiravu yāṉ viruntiṉaṉāka vara lāmā?” (tañci. kō. 163) eṉṟāṟ pōla viṉavit talivaṉ iravukkuṟi vēṇṭal.
kaḷaviyaluḷ iḵtu ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 158)
talivaṉ, ‘iruvakik kuṟi(yum) piḻippākiya iṭattu’t tōḻikkuk kūṟutal -
pakaṟkuṟiyilō iravukkuṟiyilō talivi varutaṟkut tavaṟiyavaḻit talivaṉ tōḻiyi nōkkik kūṟutal.
“maḻiyiṉ varukiyi aṟintu mayilkaḷ āṭum maliyiṭi aminta nallūriluḷḷa talivi eṉ varukiyi aṟinta aḷavil kūṭutaṟku makiḻntu varavēṇṭiyavaḷ. avaḷukku eṉpāl aruḷillātu pōyiṟṟu eṉiṉum, yāṉ oru pōtum maṟavātu avaḷ niṉippākavē uḷḷēṉ” (aiṅ. 298) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 105 iḷa., 103 nac.)
talivaṉ ‘iruvakik kuṟi(yum) piḻippākiya iṭattu, neñciṟkuk kūṟutal -
pakaṟkuṟiyilō iravukkuṟiyilō taliviyit tāṉ cantikka vāyppillātavaḻit talivaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟutal.
“neñcē! nam kātali mika nallaḷ eṉpataṉiyē nī niṉin tirukkiṟāyaṉṟi, avaḷ namakkuk kiṭṭutaṟku ariyaḷ eṉpataṉi aṟintāyalli. kiyil celvamillātavaṉ iṉpanukarcciyi virumputal niṟivēṟātatu pōla, niṉ niṉippum niṟivēṟuvatāka illi” (kuṟun. 120) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 105 iḷa.)
talivaṉ iruvakik kuṟiyiṉum ‘kāṇā vakiyiṉ poḻutu naṉi ikappa’k kūṟal -
pakaṟkuṟiyilō iravukkuṟiyilō talivaṉ taliviyik kāṇappeṟāmal nēram vīṇēkaḻiyumpōtu kūṟutal.
“muḷ pōṉṟa viḻunteḻunta kūriya paṟkaḷiyum, amutam ūṟum cevvāyiṉiyum, cantaṉap pukiyiṉ maṇam kamaḻum karumaṇal pōṉṟa kūntaliyum, periya mukattiṟkup poruntiya kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiyum uṭiya taliviyiṉ puṉmuṟuvaliyum cerukkiya pārviyiyum niṉittu maṉakkāṭcikkuk koṇṭuvantu makiḻvatutāṉ iṉṟu nikaḻumpōlum!” (kuṟun. 286) eṉṟu taṉṉuḷ kūṟikkoḷ ḷum kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 105 iḷa.)
(ippāṭal, iruvarum uḷvaḻi vanta talivaṉ talivi taṉmi kūṟavē, “ivaḷkaṇṇatu ivaṉ vēṭki” eṉṟu tōḻi kuṟippāṉ uṇara avaṉ kūṟiya kūṟṟu eṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttu.)	(to. po. 102)
piṉṉāḷ neṭuntaki kuṟivayiṉ nīṭu ceṉṟu iraṅkiyatu eṉṟa tuṟikku eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ikkuṟuntoki 286ām pāṭali nampi akapporuḷ uri koḷḷum. 	(cū. 156)
talivaṉ illattu aḻuṅkal -
taliviyiṉ varutta mikutiyik kaṇṭa talivaṉ tāṉ celvatit tavirttu maṉikkaṇṇēyē taṅkiviṭutal.
“ivaḷi aṉpuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭṭu  māli cūṭṭit toyyil eḻutit taḻuvi makiḻntirukkumvēḷiyil ivaḷ eṉ pirivik kuṟippāl aṟintu aḻutu kaṇcivantāḷ; pacali yum paraviṟṟu. ivaḷi viṭṭup piriyiṉ ivaḷ uyirukkē ūṟu nērum. ākavē, yāṉ pirintu cellēṉ” eṉṟu talivaṉ taṉ maṉattuḷ eṇṇic cellātu aḻuṅkutal.		(ampikā. 548)
itu ‘poruḷvayiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
talivaṉ ukanta poruḷkoṇṭu taṉ kātalnōy taṇikkumāṟu talivi vēṇṭutal -
“kaṇṇaṉmītu kātal koṇṭataṉ viḷivāṉa nōyāl cōrntu kiṭakkum eṉṉiṭam palapaṭap pēci ēṉ eṉṉit tuṉpuṟuttukiṉṟīr? tōḻimīr! peṇṇiṉ varuttamaṟiyāta avaṉi aḻittu vantu eṉṉuṭaṉ kūṭṭa iyalātu pōṉāl, avaṉ uṭuttuk kaḷinta pītāmparattiyāvatu koṇarntu eṉmītu ataṉ kāṟṟuppaṭa viciṟi eṉ tāpattit taṇiyuṅkaḷ” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉpāṟpaṭum.	(nācci. tiru. 13 - 1)
talivaṉ uṭkōḷ cāṟṟal -
taliviyum tōḻiyum uṭaṉirukkiyil talivaṉ vantu taṉkuṟiyik kūṟutal.
“taṇmi mikkavarkaḷē! uṅkaḷukkāka nāṉ etiyum ceyvēṉ; kuṟṟēvalum purivēṉ; vāṉattup poruḷik kēṭpiṉum koṇarntu aḷippēṉ. nīṅkaḷ iruvīrum eṉpāl aruḷkūrtal vēṇṭum.” (tañci. kō. 81) eṉṟal pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 144)
talivaṉ, ‘uṭaṉcēṟal ceykiyōṭu aṉṉavi piṟavum, maṭampaṭa vanta tōḻikkaṇ’ kūṟal -
talivi uṭaṉpōka vēṇṭum eṉṟu niṉittavaḻiyum mēṟcollappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟilum maṭamippaṭa vanta tōḻi māṭṭut talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻttutal.
“talivi eṉṉoṭu variṉ, melliya poruḷkaḷilēyē naṭantu paḻakiya tāmari pōṉṟa avaḷuṭiya aṭikaḷ pālivaṉap parukkik kaṟkaḷil paṭumpōtu tāmariyiṉ akavitaḻkaḷ cātiliṅkam tōyttāṟ pōlak kaṟuttu viṭumē” (kali. 13) eṉṟum,
“nammiṭam irappavarkku īyāmaliruppatu iḻivākum eṉṟu karutit taliviyip pirintu nīr tēṭak karutum poruḷ uṇmiyiṟ ciṟanta poruḷā?” (kali. 21) eṉṟu tōḻi viṉaviyavaḻi, “iṉpatti virumpāmal tiṟampaṭac ceyaṟ paṭutali virumpuvōṉē taṉ uṟaviṉartuyarkaḷit tuṭittu avarit tāṅkum tūṇ” (kuṟaḷ 615) eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal.	(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
“nī kaḷavil tēṟṟiya teḷivakappaṭuttalum tīrāt tēṟṟamum poyyām. avi poyyākātapaṭi ceykikaḷōṭē ivaḷi uṭaṉkoṇṭu celka” eṉṟu taṉ aṟiyāmi tōṉṟat tōḻi kūṟumiṭattut talivaṉkūṟṟu nikaḻum.
uṭaṉkoṇṭu pōtal muṟimiyaṉṟu eṉṟu aṟiyāmal kūṟaliṉ ‘maṭampaṭa’ eṉṟār. ceykikaḷāvaṉa: piriyak karutiya talivaṉ tāṉ koṇṭupōkum vilnāṇiṉit teṟittup pārttavaḻi avaṉpirivu karutit taliviyiṉ oḷi poruntiya mukattil pacali paṭartalum, avaṉ ampu kaḷip poṟukkic cērttavaḻi avaṉpirivu karuti varuntiya tāl avaḷkaṇkaḷil nīr nillāmal coritalum piṟavumām. (kali. 7)
“tāmarip poykiyuḷ nīr nīttavaḻit tāmari vāṭi vataṅkuvatu pōla ivaḷvaruntiyiruppāḷallaḷ; nī pirinta aṉṟē ivaḷ uyir pōm” (kali. 5) eṉṟu kūṟiya tōḻi “taliviyiyum uṭaṉkoṇṭu cērmiṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiyatu kēṭṭut talivaṉ, “ivaḷi uṭaṉkoṇṭu pōtal evvāṟṟāṉum muṟimiyaṉṟu” eṉṟu, “eṉ neñcattil iṭampeṟṟa taliviyi niṉittāl eṉakku varuttamākavuḷḷatu. avaḷ yāṉ poruḷvayiṉ piriyum pōtu tāṉum uṭaṉvaruvatākak kūṟiṉāḷ. pālinilattil cennāy nīrvēṭkiyāl tōṇṭi uṇṭu mītiyirukkum nīril kāṭṭumallikippū viḻuntu aḻukik kalantu kiṭakkum. annīri nīrvēṭkiyāl kuṭikka nēriṭum. attakiya tuṉpa nilikku ivaḷ eṉṉuṭaṉ varukiṟēṉ eṉkiṟāḷē!” (kuṟun. 56) eṉat tōḻi kēṭkumāṟum,
“nammoṭu, kaṭuṅkāṟṟaṭippatum mucukkūṭṭaṅkaḷ mikkirup patum ākiya kāṭṭuvaḻiyē koṭiya vēṉiṟkālattil marāmarattu niḻalil taṅki vaḻinaṭantu varuvatākat talivi kūṟuvatu eṉakku avaḷatu maṭam kāraṇamāka nakiyiyē payakkiṟatu” (akanā. 121) eṉṟu neñciṭamum kūṟutal.			(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ (kiḻavōṉ) ulakiṉmēl vittu (maṭalē poruḷeṉa)p pāṅkikku urittal -
pāṅki taṉkuṟiyi niṟivēṟṟātatāl varunti maṭalēṟuta lē vaḻiyeṉat tuṇinta talivaṉ ataṉi ulakiṉmēl vittuk kūṟutal.
“tāṉ koṇṭa kātal niṟivēṟāviṭṭāl, kātal koṇṭavaṉ maṭalēṟuvatum, erukkampū aṇintu teru vil niṉṟu tāṉ kātalittavaḷip paṭattil eḻutik kiyil koḷvatum, peyar colvatum pōṉṟa ceyalkaḷic ceyyavum tuṇivāṉ!” (kōvi. 74) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 145)
talivaṉ uḷḷatu urittal -
talivaṉ taṉakku nērnta kāmanōyit tōḻaṉukkuk kūṟutal.
“eṉṉuyirt tōḻa! oru peṇṇaṇaṅku tanta kāmanōyāl varuntukiṉṟēṉ. innōyit tantu eṉṉi varuttum avaḷē innōykku maruntum āvāḷ. itu tavira vēṟu maruntum illi” eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(kuṟaḷ. 1102)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 137)
talivaṉ, ‘uṟal aruṅku uṇmiyiṉ ūṭal mikuttōḷip, piṟapiṟa peṇṭiriṉ peyarttaṟkaṇ’ kūṟal -
talivaṟkuc cāntu aḻivērum kuṟi peṟṟār kūntal tukaḷum uḷḷamiyāl, avaṉik kūṭutal arumi yiṉāṉē, ūṭal mikutta taliviyi, piṟapiṟa peṇṭir ētuvākat talivaṉ ūṭal uṇarttumiṭattuk kūṟutal; eṉṟatu, ulakattut talivaroṭu kūṭiya taliviyar maṉiyaṟattu ivvāṟu oḻukuvar eṉa avaroḻukkam kāṭṭi aṟattuṟippaṭuttalām.
parattiyiṟ pirintuvanta talivaṉ vāyil peṟātu āṟṟāmi vāyilākap pukkut taliviyi nayappittal kāraṇamāka, “teyva makaḷir poytal ayarvatāka oru kaṉāk kaṇṭēṉ. aḵtu uṇmiyākumpaṭi vēṉiṟparuvam vantuviṭṭatu. kuyilkaḷ kūvukiṉṟaṉa. avi ‘kūṭiyavar piriyātīr! pirintavar virintuvantu kūṭumiṉ!’ eṉṟu icikkiṉṟaṉa. iḷavēṉilil kāmaṉukku viḻāk koṇ ṭāṭa maturi nakarattu makaḷirum mintarum cōliyil kūṭi viḷiyāṭappōkiṉṟaṉar” (kali. 92) eṉṟu kūṟit talivi yatu ūṭalip pōkkutal.	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ ūṭal nīkkik kūṭit taṉṉuḷ kūṟiyatu -
“nammiṭam tavaṟu illiyāyiṉum, tavaṟu nampāl uḷatāka eṇṇi nam kātali ūṭi akaṉṟālum, ataṉ muṭivil mikap periyatōr iṉpam uṇṭu” eṉṟu talivaṉ eṇṇiyatu.		(kuṟaḷ 1325)
talivaṉ ūṭali virumpik kūṟutal -
“iṉip puṇarcci nīṭṭikkumō nīṭṭiyātō eṉṟu karuta lāl, iṉpattiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta ūṭaliṉ, kaṇṇēyum oru tuṉpam uṇṭu” eṉavum, (ūṭutal kūṭaṟkaṇ viri vikkum eṉpatu kuṟippu) (kuṟaḷ. 1307)
“ivaḷ nammōṭu ūṭuvāḷāka! aṅṅaṉam avaḷ ūṭi niṟṟaṟkum ataṉi uṇarttutaṟporuṭṭu  yām irantu niṟṟaṟkum kālam peṟum vaki, ivviravu viṭiyātu nīṭṭittal vēṇṭum” eṉavum (kuṟaḷ. 1329) nikaḻum talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu, cūttirattuḷ ‘paṇṇami pakuti’yuḷ aṭaṅkuvatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ ūr viṉātal -
tōḻiyi irantu piṉṉiṟṟaṟku muṭivu ceyta talivaṉ, taliviyum tōḻiyum uṭaṉirunta iṭattō tōḻi taṉittu niṉṟa iṭattō vantu, “ivaṉ viṉavuvatu vēṟōr uṭkaruttuṭiyatu pōlum!” eṉṟu tōḻi karutumāṟu, “vilpōṉṟa puruvatti aṭutta kaṇkaḷāl karuṇiyoṭu pārkkum makaḷirē! ikkal ceṟinta vaḻiyil vantamiyāl kālkaḷ nōkiṉṟaṉa. katiravaṉ veppam vāṭṭukiṟatu. ituvō numatu ūr?” eṉṟāṟpōla viṉavutal.			(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ, ūrum piṟavum viṉātal -
“nīṇṭa tōḷkaḷiyuṭiya makaḷirē! aruvikaḷ olikkum periya malippakutiyil, kaṉṟukaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭa palāmarattu aṭiyiṉkaṇ vēril toṅkum palāppaḻattiṉik kaṉṟi ṉiyuṭiya cenniṟappacu uṇṭu kuṟuṅkāṭṭil nīr parukum iyaṟki vaḷam mikka uṅkaḷ ciṟukuṭiyiṉ peyar yātu eṉṟu viṉavavum, viṭi kūṟa maṟukkiṉṟīr. ittiṉip puṉaṅkāval uṅkaḷiṭam oppaṭikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatā eṉpatiyāvatu kūṟumiṉ” (naṟ. 213) eṉṟāṟ pōla, tōḻiyi irantu piṉṉiṟṟaṟku muṭivu ceyta talivaṉ, tōḻiyum taliviyum cērntirukkum iṭattiṟku vantu, “ivaṉ viṉavuvatu vēṟōr uṭkaruttiyuṭiyatu pōlum” eṉṟu tōḻi karutumāṟu avarkaḷiṭam ūrum piṟavum viṉavutal.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ, ‘eñcā makiḻcci iṟantuvaru paruvattu’k kūṟiyatu -
tirumaṇattiṉpiṉ oḻiyāta makiḻcci palvēṟu vakiyāṉa nukarccikkaṇ putitāka vanta kālattut talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“erukkampūviyum āvirampūviyum cūṭi maṭalūrum inta maṭavōṉ iṟantuviṭuvāṉō’ eṉṟu maṟṟa peṇṭir iraṅkiṉum, tāṉ iraṅkāḷ ivaḷ. ūrp potuviṭattu maṭalēṟi ivaḷ nalatti yāṉ pāṭiṉum ivaḷ aṟiyāḷ. iṉi, ‘varipāyntu ivaḷip peṟuval’ eṉṟu iṭiyiṉ veppattiṉum mikka kāmakkāyccalāl yāṉ paṭṭa tuyari nōkki ittaliviyi eṉakku varintukōṭaṟkut tanta nalviṉiyi yāṉ palapaṭiyākap pārāṭṭukiṟēṉ” eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu (kuṇanāṟpatu), avaṉukkut tirumaṇattāl makiḻcci mikka ceytiyi aṟivuṟuttukiṟatu.
	(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
tirumaṇattiṉ piṉṉar oḻiyāta makiḻcci palvēṟu vakiya vākiya  nukarccikkaṇ putitāka vanta kālattē talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“poruḷkaḷi uṇmiyāka uṇarnta iṉpatti aṟiyun tōṟum avaṟṟi muṉṉar ivvāṟu viḷaṅka uṇarāta aṟiviṉmiyi vēṟupaṭuttuk kaṇṭāṟpōlum, ic cēyiḻimāṭṭuc ceṟiyuntōṟum talittalic ciṟappap peṟukiṉṟa kāmatti muṉṉar aṟiyappeṟṟilēm” (kuṟaḷ 1110) eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal.	(146 nac.)
talivaṉ, ‘ēṉi vāyil etiru’ṅkālik kūṟal -
peṇṭirum makkaḷum allāta vāyilkaḷāyiṉār etir kūṟum kūṟṟum talivaṉmāṭṭu nikaḻum.
iviyellām kāmapporuḷākat tōṉṟā; avar ceyal poruḷākat tōṉṟum.	(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
ciṟanta moḻiyi oḻintuniṉṟa vāyilkaṭku etirē talivaṉ kūṟal. avarkaḷ tōḻi nīṅkalāṉa pāṇaṉ mutalāyiṉār.
“pāṇa! teruvil viḷiyāṭiya ciṟuvaṉi yāṉ tūkkik koḷḷa, avaṉ vāyiṉiṉṟu oḻukiya eccil yāṉ pūciya cantaṉatti aḻikka, ataṉoṭu nāṉ talivipāl ceṉṟē ṉāka, ataṉi avaḷ ētō eṉ puṟattoḻukkattāl cantaṉam aḻintatākak karuti añci niṟkum peṇmāṉ pōla maruṇṭu, ‘iṉi nīvir eṉakku eṉṉa uṟavu?” eṉa vilaki niṉṟa ceytiyi ippoḻutu niṉippiṉum naki varu kiṉṟatu!’ (naṟ. 250) eṉṟāṟ pōla (ēṉi vāyilkaḷiṭam) talivaṉ kūṟutal.	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ, ‘oḻukkattuk, kaḷaviṉuḷ nikaḻnta arumiyip pulampi, alamaral uḷḷamoṭu aḷaviya iṭattu’k kūṟal -
kaḷavoḻukkattil kūṭṭattiṟkēṟpaṭṭa iṭiyūṟukaḷi eṇṇit taṉ cuḻalutaliyuṭiya uḷḷattoṭu viṉaviya pōtu talivaṉ kūṟutal.	(to. po. 146 nac.)
kaṟpu oḻukkattilum, kaḷavuk kālattu nikaḻnta arumiyit taṉittuc cuḻaṉṟa uḷḷattōṭu ucāviya iṭattut talivaṉ kūṟutal.	(144 iḷa.)
“talivi, tāyppacuvik kāṇa vēṇṭum eṉṟa viruppam koṇṭa kaṉṟi ottavaḷ; taḻuvuvatil virivuṭiyavaḷ; viruppam tarum vaṉappiṉaḷ; kuvinta melliya koṅkiyaḷ; nīṇṭa kūntalaḷ.” (kuṟun. 132) eṉṟu taliviyiṉ kaḷavuk kāla niliyit talivaṉ pāṅkaṉiṭam kūṟiyatik kaṟpuk kālattu niṉittal.	(nac.)
kaḷavoḻukkattil kūṭṭattiṟkēṟpaṭṭa iṭiyūṟukaḷi eṇṇit talivi maṉaccuḻaṟciyoṭu viṉaviyapōtu - aḵtāvatu ‘nummip pārkkavum toṭavumkūṭap peru nāṇ aṭintēṉē, ataṉ kāraṇam yātu?” eṉṟu talivi viṉaviyapōtu - talivaṉ “paṉinīr iravil toṭa uṭal naṭukkuṟum; toṭattoṭat kuḷir akaṉṟu iṉpam taruvatu pōla,  mutalil avvāṟiruntu paḻakappaḻaka nāṇ akaṉṟatu” eṉṟal.	(po. 194 kuḻa.)
talivaṉ kalakkam kaṇṭu pāṅkaṉ viṉātal -
“eṉṉoṭu piṉṉiya naṭpiṉiyuṭiya taliva! nī muṉ pellām ivvāṟu poliviḻantu kāṇappaṭuvāy alli. niṉ maṉatti varuttum tuyaram oṉṟu iruppatāka aṟikiṟēṉ. putiyiruḷ iriyal pōkap poṉtāḻ aruvi utayam eṉṉum maṇivari ucciyilniṉṟu ulaku tuyil peyartta muḷi iḷañāyiṟṟiṉ eḻil vaṉappu aḻitta niṉ tirumukattil iṉṟu vāṭṭam kāṇappaṭuvataṉ kāraṇam yātu?” eṉṟu pāṅkaṉ talivaṉuṭiya kalakkam paṟṟi viṉavutal.
iccūttirattuḷ ‘kalaṅkalum’ eṉṟataṉāl akkalakkattāl nikaḻvaṉa vellām koḷḷappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu ik kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ (kiḻavōṉ) kaḻaṟṟu etirmaṟuttal -
talivaṉ taṉpāṅkaṉ iṭitturittati etirmaṟut turittal.
“tōḻa! iviyāvum veṟumiyāṉa vāyvīramē! eṉ uyiriyē pōlvāḷām avaḷ pēraḻaki nī pārttili; pārttiruppiṉ, ivvāṟu kūṟamāṭṭāy” (kōvi. 23) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 137)
talivaṉ kaḷavilum ūrtiyil varutal -
talivaṉ taliviyirukkum iṭattiṉiṉṟu tolivilirup patāl avaḷik kāṇa varuvataṟkut tēr yāṉi civiki attiri ivaṟṟi ūrtiyākap payaṉpaṭutti varuvāṉ.			(to. po. 159 kuḻa.)
talivaṉ, kaṟpil ūṭiyatu -
“‘empāl tīṅku illi’ eṉṟu yām teḷivuṟuttavum, avvuriyik kikaṭantu, ciṟitum emmiṭattut tuṉitta niliyiṉiṉṟu mīṭciyilli yāyiṉ.....................” (kali. 81) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu, avaṉ kaṟpukkālattil ūṭutal.			(to. po. 156 nac.)
talivaṉ, kaṟpiṟ pulantatu -
“evvi eṉṟa vaḷḷali iḻantamiyāṉ vaṟumiyuṟṟa yāḻppāṇarkaḷtam pūccūṭappeṟāta poliviḻanta vaṟuntaliyip pōla, neñcē, nī poliviḻantu varuntuka! varuntip payaṉ peṟuvatu yātu? kāṭṭumalliki maṇam kamaḻum kūntali yuṭiyāḷākiya avaḷ (-talivi) namakku evvuṟaviṉaḷ?” (kuṟun. 19) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu, avaṉ kaṟpiṟ pulantami.	(to. po. 156 nac.)
talivaṉ, ‘kāmak kiḻatti maṉiyōḷ eṉṟivar ēmuṟu kiḷavi colliya etiri’ṉkaṇ kūṟutal -
kāmakkiḻattiyum maṉiyāḷum eṉṟu collum iruvarum pātukāvalākak kūṟiya kūṟṟiṉ etir talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum.
ivviruvarum illuṟi makaḷir ātaliṉ talivaṉmāṭṭu  nikaḻumavi iruvarukkum okkum. aḵtāvatu, “vaḻivantavāṟu eṉṉi?” eṉavum, “varuttamuṟṟīr!” eṉavum innikaraṉa pala kūṟal.
“neruppu erivatupōṉṟa veppamuṭiya vaḻi nīṇṭu aritāka iruppiṉum, māṉ nōkkiyākiya uṉṉi niṉintavāṟē, uṉṉit taḻuva virumpiya neñcattoṭu, virintu cellum kutiri pūṭṭiya tēriṉi iyakki vantamiyāṉ, vaḻiyi eḷitākak kaṭantu vantuviṭṭēṉ!” (aiṅ. 360) eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal.	(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
iṟparatti, talivi eṉṟu kūṟiya iruvar colliya varuttamuṟṟa kiḷaviyiṉ etiriṭattum talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻttum. avi “aruñcurattu varutta muṟṟīrē” eṉavum, “emmi maṟantīrē” eṉavum kūṟuvaṉavum, piṟavum ām.
“‘uṟcākam celutta eṭutta kāriyatti muṭikkum ārvat tuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭu, ‘niṉṉi niṉiyātu kaḻiyum nāḷ eṉakku vīḻnāḷ’ eṉṟu kūṟic ceṉṟati maṟantuviṭṭīrpōlum!’ eṉṟu varuttattoṭu viṉavum taliviyē! koṭiya kāṭṭuvaḻiyilē, piṟar ‘tirumpi vantuviṭṭāṉ’ eṉṟu ikaḻātavāṟu poruḷ tēṭi varavēṇṭum eṉṟa viruppat tāṉ ceṉṟu taṅkiyapōtu uṭamputāṉ vēṟṟu nāṭṭil iruntatē oḻiya, eṉ neñcam niṉṉiṭattil tāṉ iruntatu” (akanā. 29) eṉṟu, “maṟantīrpōlum” eṉṟataṟkut talivaṉ maṟumoḻi kūṟutal.
“marāmarattiṉatu kiḷiyit toṭṭup perukiya veḷḷam kiyāṉ iṟittu uṇṇum aḷaviṟkuk kuṟintatu pōl, eṉ kāmam iṅku vanta aḷavil kuṟiyumpaṭi niṉṉiyē niṉittu, niṉṉiyē peritum karuti ivvulakattiṉ talivaṉtaliviyariṭam uḷḷa pācam ippaṭi irukkiṟatē eṉṟu viyantē, vēṟṟiṭattil kālam kaḻittēṉ” (kuṟun. 99) eṉat taliviyip piriviṭi niṉittavāṟṟit talivaṉ kūṟutal.	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ, kāmattiṉ valiyi uṭkoṇṭavaḻik kūṟal -
poruḷiṉum kāmam valiyuṭittu eṉṟu uṭkoṇṭa vaḻi, “ittaliviyi orunāḷ pirintu ceyaṟpaṭutaṟku ūtiyamāka ivvulakum tēvarulakumē kiṭṭuva āyiṉum, avi talivimāṭṭu orunāḷ nukarum iṉpattiṟku oppu ākā” (kuṟun. 101) eṉṟu talivaṉ poruḷiṉum kāmam valiyuṭittāmāṟṟik kūṟutal.	(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
kāmam ciṟattaliṉ, āṟṟāmi vāyilākac ceṉṟu talivaṉ valintu pukkut taliviyi neruṅkik kūṭumiṭattu, “eṉ kūntalit toṭa ivaṉukku eṉṉa urimi uḷatu? maṉiviyi uṇmiyil virumpāmal ūrār taṉṉi nallavaṉ eṉṟu namputaṟkuc ceyyum poycceyal itu!” eṉṟu talivi kūṟa, “uṭalum uyirum oṉṟāyt tali iraṇṭākiya paṟaviyiṉ talikaḷ iraṇṭaṉuḷ oṉṟu maṟṟoṉṟoṭu pōriṭuvatu pōla, nī ikkoṭumikaḷik kūṟip pulantāl payaṉ eṉ? eṉ ariya uyir niliniṟkum vaḻiyic col” (kali. 89) eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal.			(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ kiḷḷiyi vāḻttiyatu -
puṉaṅkāvalukkut talivi vārāviṭiṉ, talivaṉ avaḷik kaṇṭu kāmuṟṟuk kūṭutal nikaḻntirātu ātaliṉ, taliviyip puṉaṅkāval ceyyumāṟu tiṉikkatirkaḷik koytu ceṉṟa kiḷikaḷi avaṉ vāḻttiyami.
“aimpālākap pakutta kūntaliyuṭiya ikkuṟiñcinilap peṇ ittiṉippuṉattik kāval ceyyumāṟu, tiṉik katirkaḷik koytu cellum kiḷikaḷ vāyppiṉi yuṇṭākkiviṭṭamiyāṉ, ikkiḷikaḷ ellām veḷḷam eṉṉum pēreṇ aḷaviṟṟākiya ūḻikkālattik kaṭantu ataṉpiṉṉarum vāḻvaṉavākuka!” (aiṅ. 281) eṉṟu talivaṉ vāḻttiyami.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘colliya’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷap paṭṭa oru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ kuṟi piḻittavaḻi ūṭiyatu -
“neñcē! mutumiyāṉ ciṟakukaḷ utirappeṟṟa kīḻik kaṭaṟkari nāri mēlik kaṭaliṉ ayiri mīṉākiya peṟaṟkariya uṇavip peṟutalporuṭṭut taliyi mēlē eṭuttāṟpōla, cēymiyiluḷḷavaḷum peṟaṟku ariyavaḷumākiya taliviyi nī peṟutaṟku niṉintāy. ātaliṉ, nī varuttattiyuṭiyāy; tuṉpamuṟum tīviṉi yuṭiyāy!” (kuṟun. 128) eṉṟu alla kuṟippaṭṭu mīḷum talivaṉ taṉ neñcōṭu ūṭiyavāṟu.			(to. po. 156 nac.)
talivaṉ kuṟi piḻittuḻip pulantatu -
“taliva! nī ivaḷik kalantamiyāṉ viḷinta kāmanōy ivaḷatu oḻukkatti mikutippaṭuttuvatāl atu kaṇṭu yāṉum kaṇ tuyilēṉ āyiṉēṉ; eṉṉiṭattē nīyum veṟuttāyum āyiṉi. niṉ mali etirolippatu pōla, nī kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟiyē kūṟumavaḷ ivaḷ. ivaḷatu nōyiṉi, ivaḷ āṟṟumpaṭiyāka oru poymmoḻi kūṟiyāyiṉum velvāy. nī urittatē ivaṭku uriyām” (kali. 46) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭattut taliviyatu āṟṟāmi kūṟi varivu kaṭāya ikkūṟṟiṉ kaṇ, kuṟi tavaṟip pōṉa viṭattut talivaṉ pulantami pulappaṭum.	(to. po. 156 nac.)
talivaṉ, kuṟivaḻic ceṉṟa pāṅkaṉ avviṭattuk kāṇuṅkol eṉṟu aiyuṟṟatu -
talivaṉ taliviyiṉ iyal iṭam kūṟit taṉ pāṅkaṉi avaḷirukkum iṭam nāṭi varuvataṟku viṭuttapiṉ, “eṉ ṉuṭiya mikka tuyariṉit tīrttaṟkut taṉ contak kāriyam ceypavaṉip pōlap poṟuppuṭaṉ cellum eṉ pāṅkaṉ, taṉ pāṅkiyar kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum pirintu taṉiyē eṉ varavu nōkki niṟkum taliviyik kāṇkiṟāṉō allaṉō” (pāratam) eṉṟu aiyuṟṟut taṉṉuḷ kūṟik koḷḷutal.
iccūttirattuḷ ‘kuṟṟam kāṭṭiya vāyil peṭpiṉum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭa oru kūṟṟu itu.			(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ kūṟutaluṟutaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
iyaṟkip puṇarccikku muṉṉarō, iṭantalip pāṭṭukku muṉṉarō taṉ nāṇam kaṇṭu nīṭu niṉintu iraṅkiya talivaṉatu varuttam tīrat talivi taṉ nāṇam virivil nīṅkap peṟutaliṉ talivaṉ avaḷi nukarat toṭaṅ kutaṟkaṇ,
“taṉ vaḷiyalkaḷ olikkumāṟu naṇṭukaḷi alittu viḷiyāṭikkoṇṭirukkum talivi taṉ mukatti maṟitta kūntaloṭu tali kuṉintu eṉ karuttukku uṭaṉpaṭṭut taṉimit tuṉpam uṟukiṉṟa eṉatu kāma mayakkam tīrumāṟu taṉ aḻakiya mārpiṉi eṉakkut taruvāḷ” (aiṅ. 197) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟutal. 	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ kūṟum uḷḷuṟi uvamam -
talivaṉ mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal pāli eṉṉum ellā nilak karupporuḷkaḷiyum koṇṭu taṉ aṟi vuṭimi tōṉṟa uḷḷuṟi uvamam kūṟuvāṉ.			(to. po. 305 pērā.)
talivaṉ keṭuti viṉātal -
tōḻiyi irantu kuṟiyuṟutaṟku muṭivu ceyta talivaṉ, taliviyum tōḻiyum taṉittu niṟkum cūḻali eyti, “ivaṉ viṉavuvatu vēṟōr uṭkaruttiyuṭiyatupōlum” eṉṟu tōḻi karutumāṟu, “naṟumaṇam kamaḻum cantaṉa maraṅkaḷi veṭṭi nilattic camaṉ ceytu maḻi peyta aḷavil vititta tiṉi muṟṟiyatāka, akkolliyik kāval ceytuvarum, piṟiyi aṇinta tāmari pōṉṟa mukatti yum tāḻnta kūntaliyumuṭiya makaḷirē! eṉ ampupaṭṭa māṉkaḷ ippakkam vantaṉavā?” (tiṇimāli - 1) eṉavum,
“tattam kāriyaṅkaḷil tīviramāka īṭupaṭṭiruppavarum, tammiṭam vantu viṉavuvāriṭattil viṭi kūṟātu tam kāriyattilēyē īṭupaṭṭirukka māṭṭārkaḷ. ātaliṉ, mūṅkil pōṉṟa tōḷkaḷi uṭiya makaḷirē! eṉṉāl turattappaṭṭa paṉṟi ivvaḻiyē ceṉṟatā eṉṟu kūṟuṅkaḷ” eṉavum,
“tiṉiyiṉ kiḷikaḷik kaṭiyum iḷiyīr! taṉ iṉattiṉ nīṅkit taṉ uṭalit tuḷitta ampoṭu vāṭiyavāṟu puḷḷimāṉ oṉṟu pōṉavaḻi numakkut teriyumāyiṉ kūṟuṅkaḷ” eṉavum palavāṟu avarkaḷ maṟumoḻi kūṟu tali etirpārātu viṉavutal.
keṭutiyāvaṉa : “yāṉi puli mutaliyaṉavum neñcum uṇarvum iḻantēṉ; avi kaṇṭīrō?” eṉa viṉavuvaṉa palavumām.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ kiyuṟi ēntivarutal -
taliviyum pāṅkiyum uṭaṉirukkiyil, ac cevvi aṟintu talivaṉ, taṉ kātalukku aṭiyāḷamākat taḻiyum māliyum pōṉṟa kiyuṟip poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ varutal.
itaṉik ‘karuttaṟivittal’ eṉṉum tirukkōviyār(58).
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkimati uṭaṉpāṭu’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 141)
talivaṉ kiyuṟi koṇṭuvantu kūṟal -
taliviyum tōḻiyum oruṅkirunta cevvikkaṇ talivaṉ kiyuṟiyākiya pavaḻakkoṭiyoṭum muttoṭum ceṉṟu avarkaḷi aṇuki, “matam poruntiya yāṉi ciṅkak kuṭṭimuṉ niṟkamāṭṭātu naṭuṅkuvatu pōla, eṉ kiyil uṅkaḷukkup paricāka vittirukkum pavaḻakkoṭiyum muttum ittaliviyiṉ vāyitaḻiyum paṟkaḷiyum kaṇṭa aḷavil eṉkiyil nillātu kīḻē patuṅkukiṉṟaṉa’ (tiṇimāli. 42) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟutal.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘taṇṭātu irappiṉum’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ kiyuṟi pukaḻtal -
talivaṉ, talivikkākat tāṉ koṇṭu vantirunta kiyuṟiyi, “umiyāḷ iṟivaṉ payilum kayilāyatti lum, tēvar uṟiyum imiyācalattum ittaḻikku nikarāṉa taḻi illi” (tañci kō. 97) eṉṟāṟ pōla uyarttuc collutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 144)
talivaṉ, ‘kiviṭiṉ (vanta) acca’ttiṉkaṇ kūṟal -
taliviyik kiviṭṭavaḻi avaḷatu uyirpporuṭṭākat tāṉ añcutaṟkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟutal. “orunāḷ nāṉ taliviyi mikutiyāka nalam pārāṭṭa ataṉi avaḷ nāṉ pirivataṟkuc cūḻnta cūḻcciyeṉa uṭkoṇṭu, taṉ putal vaṉi mārpil aṇittu vantu. kaṇṇiṉ pāviyik kaṇṇīr maṟikka, ‘nīṅkaḷ eṉṉip pirintu pōvatāyiṉ, ‘niṉṉiṉ piriyēṉ, piriyiṉ āṟṟēṉ’ eṉṟu coṉṉa uṟutimoḻi yātāvatu?’ eṉṟu kuṟippāl mukattiṉ mūlam kāṭṭip perumūcceṟiya, am mūcciṉ veppattāl putalvaṉ cūṭiya malar karukiviṭṭatu. nāṉ pakkalil irukkum pōtē ippaṭi varuntum talivi nāṉ piriyiṉ uyir vāḻāḷ” (akanā. 5) eṉṟu talivaṉ taṉṉuḷ kūṟik koṇṭavāṟu.	(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
talivi, tāṉ uṇarttavum uṇarāmal, taṉṉik ki viṭṭup piriyil tāṉ avaḷi nīṅkutaṟkut talivaṉ añciya accattiṉkaṇṇum talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum. aḵtu uṇarppuvayiṉ vārā ūṭalām.
“neñcē! mulli cūṭiya talivi ippoḻutu paṇṭu pōlvāḷ allaḷ; nammi ayalavarāka niṉikkiṟāḷ. avaḷi niṉikkum nī evvi eṉṟa vaḷḷali iḻanta pāṇarkaḷuṭiya pūvillāta vaṟiya taliyip pōlap poliviḻantu varuntuvāyāka!” (kuṟun. 19) eṉṟu talivaṉ taṉṉuḷ kūṟikkoṇṭavāṟu.	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ cellum  tiṟaṉ -
talivaṉ taliviyip pirintu kaṟpuk kālattu nāṭu iṭiyiṭṭum kāṭu iṭiyiṭṭum celluṅkāl kālāl naṭantu cellutalum, vaṇṭi mutaliya ūrtikaḷil cellu talum vaḻakkam. āyiṉ antaṇarkaḷākiya tali. makkaḷ kalattil cellum marapu iṉṟu. taliviyi aḻittuk koṇṭu cellumpōtum, attalimakaṉ kaṭal kaṭantu cellakkaṭava iṭaṅkaṭku avaḷi uṭaṉkoṇṭu cēṟal poruntātu. pakivayiṟ cellumpōtu tali makkaḷ tam kulamakaḷiruṭaṉ pācaṟiyil taṅkutal kūṭātu.			(na. a. 83 - 85)
talivaṉ celavu aḻuṅkal -
poruḷvayiṉ pirintu cellum talivaṉ ciṟitu tolivu ceṉṟu piṉ yātō kāraṇam kūṟikkoṇṭu taṉ celavit tavirttu viṭutal.
“pāka! nām cellumvaḻi iḵtu aṉṟupōl tōṉṟu kiṟatu. pati māṟi vantuviṭṭōm; tērit tiruppu; nammūrkkē celvōm” eṉṟu kūṟit talivaṉ cela vaḻuṅkutal.
itu ‘poruḷvayiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutiyil nikaḻum kūṟṟu.	(ampikā. 549)
talivaṉ celavu aḻuṅkutal -
kaṟpuk kālattil taliviyip pirintu ōtal kāval tūtu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkup piriyak karutum talivaṉ, taliviyip pirivukuṟittut tēṟṟuvataṟku illattilum, taṉmaṉattip pirivu kuṟittut tēṟṟutaṟku naṭuvaḻiyilum caṟṟut tayaṅku vāṉēyaṉṟi, maṉattuk koṇṭa ceyali muṭittaṟkup piriyum pirivit tavaṟātu mēṟkoḷvāṉ. ōtaṟkup piriyum talivaṉ taṉ ceyal pāti muṭinta niliyil ūr tirumpu vataṟkō, taliviyi niṉintu varuntuvataṟkō iṭam koṭāṉ. tūtiṟpirivu, tuṇivayiṟ pirivu ivi kuṟittup pirintōṉ, acceyal tāṉ kuṟippiṭṭa kālavari yaṟiyik kaṭantu toṭarumāyiṉ, taliviyi niṉittu varuntutalum uṇṭu.	(na. a. 86, 87)
talivaṉ, ‘ceṉṟa tēettu uḻappu naṉi viḷakki, iṉṟic ceṉṟa taṉnili kiḷa’kkumiṭattuk kūṟal -
tāṉ ceṉṟa tēyattu varuttatti mika viḷakkit taliviyi viṭuttuc ceṉṟa taṉ niliyik kiḷakkumiṭattut talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻtal.	(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
talivi kāmakkiḻatti ākiya iruvarkkum tāṉ ceṉṟa tēyattiṉ varuttatti mikavum viḷaṅkak kūṟi, naṉaviṉāṉ cēṟaliṉṟik kaṉaviṉāṉ kaṭattiṭic ceṉṟa tammuṭiya niliyik kūṟumiṭattut talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻtal.	(146 nac.)
“nallāy! ‘peṇṭiri varuntaviṭalākātu eṉṟa aṟanūṟ potuvitiyi viṭuttup pirintu ceṉṟa  nīr emmi eppoḻutāvatu niṉintatuṇṭā?’ eṉṟu varunti viṉavaṟka. niṉ aḻakiṉi yāṉ maṟakka iyalumō? nāṉ ceṉṟa kāṭṭil kāṭṭuttīp parava vaḻikeṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷum vaṇikarkaḷum ōṭa, appoḻutu katiravaṉ maṟiya, ‘iṉi naṭattal iyalātu’ eṉṟu yāṉ ōriṭattil paṭukka, aṅku nī vantu niṟka, ‘uṉṉiṭam ivvaḷavu aṉpōṭu yāṉiruppavum, eṉṉiṭam nī kōpippatu eṉpaṟṟi?’ eṉṟu niṉ vaḷinta puruvattiyum neṟṟiyiyum taṭavit talimayiriyum nāṉ kōtiya nērattil, yāṉ kaṇviḻittu atu naṉavallāk kaṉavu eṉpati uṇarntaaḷavil yāṉpaṭṭa varuttatti nī aṟiyāyātaliṉ, iṅṅaṉam eṉṉiṭam pulantu pēcu kiṟāy!” (akanā. 39) eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal.	
	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ, ‘ceṉṟu kiyikantu peyarttu uḷḷiyavaḻik’ kūṟal -
celavu aḻuṅkiyati viṭuttu muṉṉorukāl talivi yip pirintu ceṉṟu poruḷ muṟṟiya talivaṉ, mīṭṭum anneṟiyiṉip pōka niṉintavaḻik kūṟutal.
“maṉamē! niṟiyac celvaṅkaḷip peṟalām eṉṟu kūṟi eṉṉi aḻittuc ceṉṟu pulliya ilikaḷiyuṭiya marāmaraṅkaḷi yuṭiya akaṉṟa neṭiya vaḻiyilē piṉtaṅki eṉṉit taṉittu aṉuppuvataṟku muṉṉorukāl muyaṉ ṟāyē! ataṉi maṟantu ippoḻutu mīṇṭum poruḷ tēṭalām eṉṟu eṉṉit tūṇṭukiṟāyē! taliviyiṉ uruveḷittōṟṟam yāṉ cellum vaḻiyiṭit tōṉṟik kuṟukkiṭumpōtu eṉakku ēṟpaṭakkūṭiya tuyariṉi uṉ uṇmiyip pōṉṟa poymmoḻi pōkka iyalumō? ātaliṉ immuṟi ivaḷip pirintu poruḷīṭṭa yāṉ vārēṉ” (akanā. 3) eṉṟu talivaṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟiyatu.	(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
talivaṉ āṟṟāṉāyt tuṉiyit tīrttaṟkut taliviyi aṇukacceṉṟu, avaṉ meykkaṇ kiṭanta tavaṟukaṇṭu talivi āṟṟāḷāy nīkkiniṟuttalāṉē, avaṉ oruvāṟ ṟāṉ avaḷ āṟṟāmiyic ciṟitu mīṭkiyiṉālē, avaḷ kūṭak karutiya iṭattum talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum.
“emmi vañcaṉiyāṉ varutti uṇmiyil eṉmakaṉmēl pācamiṉṟi, nī kūṭiya parattiyiṉ muṭiyiṉiṉṟum utirnta pūntātu niṉ āṭiyil oṭṭiyiruppatu pulappaṭumāṟu kāṟṟil etirē niṟkiṟāyē! iṅṅaṉam nillātu pō” eṉṟu talivi kūṟiyavaḻi, talivaṉ” ēṭī! yāṉ tavaṟuceyya  villi eṉṟu uṟuti kūṟavum, eṉ colli nampāmal niṉ kōpam kuṟiyāmalirukkiṟāy. viruppoṭu kaṉṟu kaṭṭap paṭṭa iṭam nōkkic cellum pacuppōla, eṉ piḷḷiyi yāvatu nāṉ tūkkikkoṇṭu makiḻkiṉṟēṉ” (kali. 81) eṉṟu kūṟip putalvaṉit tūkkikkoḷḷat talivi ūṭal nīṅkiyatu.	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ cēṟal -
talivaṉ vēṇṭiyavāṟē pāṅkaṉ taliviyic ceṉṟu kaṇṭu viyantu mīṇṭu avaṟku avaḷnili aṟivikkavē avaṉum avaḷik kāṇṭaṟporuṭṭuk kuṟivaḻiyē cellutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 137)
talivaṉ ‘col avaṭ cārttaliṉ pulliya vakiyiṉ’kaṇ kūṟal -
talivaṉ āṟṟāmiyoṭu taliviyic cārttik kūṟiya kūṟṟi uṭkoṇṭu, avaṉ āṟṟāmiyik karuti, tōḻi uṇarāmal talivi tāṉē kūṭiya pakutikkaṇ, talivaṉ tōḻiyi irantu kūṟutal.
“cēraṉuṭiya kollippāviyip pōṉṟa talivi yōṭu yāṉ nukarntuvanta kaḷavuppuṇarcci kāvalmikuti yāṉ iṭiyīṭu paṭṭatāka, eṉ āṟṟāmi kaṇṭu, talivi orunāḷ tāṉē vantu kāviriyil nīrāṭupavaḷip pōṉṟa makiḻvuṭaṉ pirivuttuṉpattil ēṟpaṭṭa eṉ tuyar tīrat taḻuvi eṉ mārpiṉic cārntāḷ” (akanā. 62) eṉavum,
“cevvari paranta miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷiyum aḻakoḻukum mēṉiyiyum uṭiya talivi, kuṟikaḷ tavaṟiyamiyāṉ āṟṟāmi mītūrntu varuntiya eṉporuṭṭu, maṇam vīcum cōliyil tāṉē kuṟiyamittu eṉṉik kūṭi iṉpuṟut tiṉāḷ” (aiṅ. 174) eṉavum talivi taṉakkuc ceyta karuṇi yit talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam kūṟit tam kaḷavoḻukkam nīṭṭikka avaḷ utaviyi nāṭutal.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ, ‘colliya nukarcci vallē peṟṟuḻi’k kūṟal -
iyaṟkip puṇarccikku muṉṉarō, iṭantalippāṭṭukku muṉṉarō talivaṉ taliviyi meytoṭṭup payiṉṟu, poypārāṭṭi iṭam peṟṟut taḻuvi, iṭiyūṟu kiḷantu, nīṭu niṉintu iraṅkik kūṟutaluṟṟut tāṉvirumpiya nukarcciyi avaḷmāṭṭup peṟṟaviṭattuk kūṟutal.
ittaliviyiṉ tōḷkaḷ, oruvar virumpiyapōtu av virumpiya poruḷkaḷ eytiṉāl viḷiyak kūṭiya makiḻc ciyit tāmē eṉakku nalki varukiṉṟaṉa” (kuṟaḷ. 1105) eṉṟāṟ pōlat taṉ puṇarcciyiṉpattip pukaḻntu kūṟum talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ ‘colleṉa, ēṉatu cuvippiṉum nī kitoṭṭatu, vāṉōr amutam puriyumāl emakkeṉa, aṭicilum pūvum toṭutaṟkaṇ’ kūṟiyatu -
“yātāṉum oṉṟi nukariṉum, nī kiyāl toṭṭatu vāṉōr amutam okkum; itaṟkuk kāraṇam colluka” eṉṟu aṭicil toṭutaṟkaṇṇum pūttoṭuttaṟkaṇṇum talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“talivi, akkālattil vēppaṅkāyi uṇṇak koṭuppi ṉum ataṉi iṉiya tēṉkaṭṭi eṉṟu uṇṭīr” (kuṟun. 196) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟil mēṟkūṟiya ceyti uṇarappaṭum.			(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
“amutiṟku māṟāṉa nañciṉi nukariṉum nī kiyāl tīṇṭiṉa poruḷ emakku uṟutiyit tarutaliṉ tēvarkaḷuṭiya amutatti okkum, emakku” eṉap puṉinturittuḻi, “itaṟkuk kāraṇam kūṟu” eṉṟu aṭicilum pūvum talivi toṭutaṟkaṇ talivaṉatu akkūṟṟu nikaḻum.	(146 nac.)
talivaṉ (kiḻavōṉ) tañcam peṟātu neñcoṭu kiḷattal -
pāṅki iṟceṟippu urittu ōmpaṭi kūṟic cellumāṟu viṭiyum tantuviṭṭatik kēṭṭa talivaṉ, taṉakku iṉip pukal illiyē eṉat taṉ maṉattiṟkuḷ kūṟik koḷḷutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘orucār pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 154)
talivaṉ taṇṭātu irattal -
iṭantalippāṭu mutaliya kaḷavukkālattu kūṭṭattāṉ maṉam niṟivuṟātu piṉṉum tōḻiyit talivaṉ pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟi nērvikkumāṟu vēṇṭutal.
talivaṉ tōḻipāl kuṟikūṟutali avaḷ maṟuttāḷāka, atu paṟṟi varuntiya taṉ maṉattiṟkut talivaṉ, “āyattā roṭu pantu viḷiyāṭac cellum talivi namakku aruḷ ceyyiṉum ceyyāviṭiṉum, nī varuntit tōḻiyi irantu vēṇṭutali taviraṟka. yāṉ uṟṟa ipperuntuṉpamākiya nōyip pōkka avaḷaṉṟi maruntu piṟitilli. avaḷ tōḻiyariṭiyē cellutalāṉ nampāl aruḷ illāta vaḷ pōla naṭittāḷē yaṉṟi uṇmiyil nammi aruḷvāḷ” (naṟ. 140) eṉṟu kūṟi, tōḻiyi mīṇṭum pakaṟkuṟi vēṇṭi irattal.
“maṉamē! ittōḻiyiṉ piṉṉēyē ceṉṟu, ivaḷukku aṭumpamalar tāḻimalar neytalmalar ivaṟṟip paṟittuk koṭuttuk kuṟṟēval ceytē nām ivaḷiṭattil kālam pōkkuvatu paṟṟit talivi eṉṉa niṉikkiṉṟāḷō? avaḷ yātu niṉippiṉum avaḷi, aṭiyum vāyil ivaḷātalāṉ ivaḷit toṭarntu irappōm” (naṟ. 349) eṉṟu taṉ neñciṭam kūṟit talivaṉ ceyaṟpaṭutal.
“pūṅ koṭikaḷ paṭarnta malinel viḷiyum viḷipulat tik kāval ceyyum ittaliviyiṉ kaṇkaḷ eṉ neñcip piḷantu eṉ uyirik kuṭittalik kāṇavēṇṭiyō tōḻiyākiya nī eṉakku ivaḷi aṭiyum vāyppiṉit tarutaṟkuk kālam tāḻkkiṉṟāy?” (tiṇamāli. 18) eṉṟu talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam vēṇṭutal.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ tam ūr cārntami cāṟṟal -
uṭaṉpōkkiṉiṉṟu mīṇṭa talivaṉ taliviyiṭam tām tamatu ūr (aḵtāvatu taliviyatu ūr) vantucērntu viṭṭamiyik kūṟutal.
“talivi! niṉ ūrvantuviṭṭatu. nī eppōtum taṅkum iḷa marac cōliyum kuḷikkum kuḷamum itō kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟaṉa” (ampikā. 410) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu variviyaluḷ ‘mīṭci’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 191)
talivaṉ, talivi “ivaṉ pirivaṉ” eṉṟu karutiya kuṟippuṇarntu kūṟutal -
