“niṉtāyum eṉtāyum, niṉ tantiyum eṉtantiyum ic cantippiṟku muṉṉar uṟaviṉar allar; yāṉum nīyum oruvariyoruvar aṟiyātavarākavē itaṟku muṉṉar iruntuḷḷōm. cemmaṇ nilattiṟ peyta maḻinīr nilattiṉ cemmiyum nīriṉ taṉmiyum pirikkamuṭiyāta aḷavil iyintāṟ pōla, pirikkamuṭiyāta niliyil iruvēm neñcamum kalantu oṉṟāṉa piṉṉar, yāṉ niṉṉip pirintu maṟantuviṭuvēṉ eṉṟa kavaliyē niṉakku vēṇṭā” (kuṟun. 40) eṉṟu talivaṉ taliviyatu kuṟip puṇarntu vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟutal.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘teḷivakappaṭuttal’ eṉpataṉkaṇ cārvatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 101 nac.)
talivaṉ talivi uru veḷippaṭṭami kaṇṭu kūṟutal -
talivi paṟṟiya niṉivilēyē irukkum talivaṉ, vēṭki mikutiyāṉ taliviyi nēriṟ kāṇpatu pōla uṇarkiṟāṉ (iḵtu uruvuveḷippāṭu). anniliyil taṉporuṭṭākak kuṟiyiṭam ceṉṟuḷḷa tōḻaṉ avaḷik kāṇpāṉō māṭṭāṉō eṉṟu aiyamum kavaliyum koṇṭu kūṟutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uriyil koḷḷappaṭṭatu.			(i. vi. 505 uri)
talivaṉ, “talivi ūṭaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉ?” eṉṟu tōḻi viṉāyavaḻik kūṟutal -
“kātal mikutiyāṉ, immiyākiya piṟappiṉkaṇ yām piriyēm eṉṟu coṉṉēṉāka, ataṉāl, ēṉi maṟumi yākiya piṟappiṉkaṇ pirivēṉ eṉṉum kuṟippiṉiyuṭi yēṉākak karuti, avaḷ taṉ kaṇ niṟiya nīriṉik koṇṭāḷ” 	(kuṟaḷ 1315)
“taṉṉi yāṉ paṇintu ūṭal uṇarttiṉēṉāyiṉum, piṟamakaḷirkkum nīr avarkaḷ ūṭiyapōtu ivvāṟē paṇintu uṇarttum nīrmiyi uṭiyīr ākutir eṉṟu vekuḷkiṉṟāḷ” (kuṟaḷ 1319) eṉṟāṟpōla varum talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘paṇṇami pakuti’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭum kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ talivikkut taṉnakar kāṭṭal -
uṭaṉpōkkil vanta talivikkut talivaṉ taṉ ūrik kāṭṭutal.
“naṅkāy! koṭikaḷ aciya, matilkaḷ uyaramākak kāṭci vaḻaṅka, vāṉaḷāviya māḷikikaḷil uṉ naṭiyi otta aṉṉaṅkaḷ viḷiyāṭum cūḻaliyuṭiya nam nakar atō tōṉṟukiṟatu, pār!” (kōvi. 222) eṉṟu talivikkut talivaṉ taṉ ūric cuṭṭik kāṭṭutal.
itu variviyaluḷ kaḷavuveḷippāṭṭil ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uriyiṟ kōṭal.		(i. vi. 536 uri)
talivaṉ talivikkup paṇiyumiṭaṅkaḷ -
talivaṉi eppoḻutum vaḻipaṭṭoḻukuvatu talivikku illaṟattoṭu kūṭiya paṇpu. talivaṉ tavaṟu kaṇṭu talivi pulappatu uṇṭu. taṉ tavaṟu ciṟitāyavaḻit taliva ṉum taliviyip pulappāṉ. talivaṉ taṉ tavaṟu peri tākiyiṭattut taliviyiṭam paṇintu pēcuvāṉ. potu vākak kāmam elli mīṟiyavaḻit taliviyit taṉvacap paṭuttuvataṟkut talivaṉ paṇinta moḻikaḷ pēcuvatu vaḻakkam. itaṉip pulavi ūṭal eṉṉum iraṇṭaṉ kaṇṇum kāṇalām.	(to. po. 160, 227 nac.)
talivaṉ talivitaṉṉi uyarttal -
talivaṉ taliviyiṉ perumiyi uyarttik kūṟutal.
“tēvar ulakattārum taṉakku nikarilli eṉṉumāṟu ayaṉāl paṭikkappaṭṭa ittaliviyi, avaḷ irukkum cūḻali maṉam koṇṭu kuṟiñci nilappeṇ eṉṟu kūṟuvatu takātu” (ampikā. 99) eṉṟāṟpōlat talivaṉ tōḻi yiṭam kūṟuvatu.
‘varivuṭampaṭātu mikuttuk kūṟal’ (kōvi. 129) eṉpatu pōlvatu ikkūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 144)
talivaṉ talivi taṉṉi; varuttiya vaṇṇam urittal -
iḷamit taṉmiyuṭiyavaḷ, kātaliyum ataṉviḷi vāṉa tuṉpattiyum aṟiya iyalātavaḷ eṉṟu kūṟiya tōḻikkut talivaṉ, ataṉi maṟuttu taliviyiṉ kaṇkaḷāl tāṉ puṇpaṭṭamiyic collutal.
“tōḻi! pāmpum ciṅkamum tīyum aracum iḷiya nili yilum kūṭap piṟarik kollum āṟṟaluṭiyaṉa. niṉtalivi peṇṇaracu. avaḷ eṉṉi varuttiya taṉmi collukkaṭaṅkātatu” (ampikā. 126) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 146)
talivaṉ talivinōkkiṉ vāci (ciṟappuk) kaṇṭu kuṟippaṟintu kūṟal -
“iḷamāṉiṉ kaṇkaḷip pōṉṟu kātaḷavu nīṇṭu pāyntu ōṭum ivaḷuṭiya kaṇkaḷ, potunōkkāka allāmal kuṟippuṭi nōkkāka amintu eṉṉik kolvaṉavē pōl uḷḷaṉa” eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu; pāṅkaṉoṭu kūṟiṟṟu.	
itu pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam.	(tiruvi. 65)
talivaṉ talivipaṟṟi iṭiccurattu muṉ nikaḻntatu ētuvāy niṉittal -
talivaṉ, muṉṉorukāl pirintu ceṉṟavaḻi naṭuppāli vaḻiyil taliviyatu niṉippu mēliṭṭut tāṉ varuntiyati niṉiviṟ koṇṭu, mīṇṭum avaḷip pirintu cellutal attuṇi eḷiya ceyal aṉṟu eṉṟu niṉittal.
muṉṉorukāl poruḷvayiṟ pirintu mīṇṭa talivaṉ, mīṇṭum poruḷtēṭa eṇṇum neñcatti nōkki, “poruḷ tēṭutaliṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyik kuṟippiṭṭuk koṭiya pālivaḻiyil eṉṉi muṉṉorukāl aḻittuc ceṉṟāyē. appoḻutu pōkum vaḻiyil muḷmuruṅkippūviṉ itaḻip pōṉṟa cevvāyiṉiyum, iṉiya coṟkaḷiyum, aḻakiya aṇikaḷiyum uṭiya eṉ taliviyiṉ uruveḷit tōṟṟam eṉ kaṇmuṉṉē tōṉṟi appālivaḻiyil eṉ celavit taṭutta appoḻutu uṉṉāl eṉtuṉpattip pōkka muṭintilatu. ātaliṉ eṉ taliviyip pirintu nāṉ orupōtum puṟappaṭa māṭṭēṉ” (akanā.  3) eṉṟu kūṟutal.	(to. po. 43. nac.)
talivaṉ, talivipāl nikaḻntatu ētuvāy niṉittal -
taliviyiṭam nikaḻnta vēṟupāṭukaḷik kaṇṭu avaḷ taṉ pirivut tuyarit tāṅkātu iṟantupaṭutalum kūṭumeṉat talivaṉ karututal.
talivaṉuṭiya aḷavukaṭanta aṉpuc ceyalkaḷip poṟātu māṟupaṭṭa mukattoṭu, talivi avaṉ piritaṟkuk karuti yiruppataṉi nīkkum eṇṇattōṭu, avaṉirukkum iṭat tiṟkut taṉputalvaṉit tāṅkiyavāṟē mella naṭantuvantu poyppuṉṉakiyuṭaṉ, “nīvir eṉṉip pirintu pāli nilattik kaṭantu cellak karutuvīrāyiṉ muṉpu ‘niṉṉiṟpiriyēṉ, piriyiṉ āṟṟēṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟiya num uṟutimoḻi yātām?” eṉṟu kūṟi uyirtta perumūcci ṉāl putalvaṉ aṇintirunta malar karukiyatik kaṇṭa talivaṉ, tāṉ pakkalil irukkumpōtē talivikku  ittakiya varuttam nikaḻumāyiṉ, tāṉ pirintāl avaḷ orutaliyāka uyirvāḻāḷ eṉṟu muṭivu ceytu, taṉ piriviṉi nīkka vēṇṭum eṉṟu niṉittal (akanā. 5) pōlvatu.	(to. po. 43. nac.)
talivaṉ, talivi pulavi taṇiyāḷāka ūṭal -
talivaṉ tāṉē paṇintum, tōḻi taṇivittum talivi ūṭal taṇiyātavaḷāy iruntakāli, atu poṟāta talivaṉum ciṟuciṉam koḷḷutal.
“varavara, uṉakku eṉpāl aṉpu kuṟikiṉṟatu. muṉpu nam iruvariṭiyēyum irunta antap piṇippukkuk kuṟi nērvati eṉṉāl poṟukka iyalātu; nāṉum itō vēṟu eṅkēṉum ceṉṟu viṭuvēṉ!”“maṉamē! kaḷavuk kālattil namakkākap pala iṭiyūṟukaḷiyum tāṅkip pakaṟkuṟiyilum iravuk kuṟiyilum namakku iṉpam nalku vatiyē taṉatu perumpēṟāk karutiya peṇṇamirtam ivaḷ allaḷ- iḵtu ivaḷ vaṭivil vantirukkum piṟitoru vaṭivam!” (kōvi 394) eṉpaṉa pōla varum talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu tirukkōviyāril ‘ūṭal nīṭa vāṭi urittal’ eṉṉum kūṟṟu (394).
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉapataṉkaṇ ‘uṇartta uṇarā ūṭal’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 206)
talivaṉ, talivi maṇamporuṭṭāka aṇaṅkip parānili kaṇṭu makiḻtal -
talivi taṉ tirumaṇam talivaṉōṭu iṉitu naṭipeṟa aruḷ ceyyumāṟu teyvatti vaḻipaṭuvatik kaṇṭa talivaṉ taṉ neñci nōkki, “neñcē! talivi teyvat tiṟku malar tūvit taṉ tirumaṇam iṉitiṉ īṭēṟuvataṟkāka vaḻipaṭum aṉpik kaṇṭu, avaḷ eṉṉōṭu koṇṭuḷḷa naṭpu vañcaṉiyāṉa taṉṟu eṉpatit teḷintu aṟivā yāka!” (ampikā. 335) eṉṟu kūṟi makiḻtal.
itu variviyaluḷ ‘varivu malital’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru  kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 174)
talivaṉ, talivimuṉ pāṅkiyip pukaḻtal -
tirumaṇam muṭiyappeṟavē ilvāḻkki toṭaṅkiya talivaṉ, taliviyiṉ muṉṉiliyil, kaḷavuk kālattum piṉṉarum taṉakkup peritum tuṇiniṉṟa tōḻiyip pārāṭṭutal.
‘tōḻi!’ kaḷavoḻukkaṅkaḷāl viḷinta tuṉpam tīra yāṅkaḷ piṇintuviṭṭōm. pakivarkaḷ tanta koṭiya nōykaḷuk kellām iṉiya maruntāmāṟu talivikkum eṉakkum iṭiyē niṉṟu ituvari nī muyaṉṟa muyaṟci ittakiya naṟpayaṉ tantuḷḷatu” (ampikā. 435) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ ilvāḻkki’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 203)
talivaṉ talivi mūtaṟivuṭimi moḻital -
“talivi pēti allaḷ;aṉpum aruḷum kāṭṭum mūtaṟivu paṭittavaḷē” eṉṟu tōḻikkut talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“naṅkāy! niṉ talivi nī kūṟuvatu pōṉṟu pētimi yuṭiyaḷ allaḷ; eṉ varuttam aṟintu, taṉ kaṭikkaṇ ṇāl eṉṉik kuḷirap pārttuk kalaviyiṉpamum tantu eṉakku āṟutal nalkiya avaḷ nalla mutirnta aṟivuṭiya vaḷē” (tañci. kō.91) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 144)
talivaṉ, talivimēl (iṟivaṉ iṟivimēl) kuṟipiḻippu ēṟṟal -
kuṟiyiṭattiṟku vārāmal taṉṉi varuttiya piḻi talivi yuṭiyatē eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“maḻi varaviṉi aṟintu mayilkaḷ āṭum pakkamalikaṭku iṭippaṭṭa nalla ūriluḷḷa melliyal vāynta talivi tāṉ eṉṉi maṟantāḷāyiṉum, yāṉ avaḷiyē iṭi viṭātu niṉittukkoṇṭiruttaliṉ, orupōtum maṟantaṟiyēṉ” (aiṅ.  298) eṉṟal pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘iravukkuṟi iṭiyīṭu’ eṉṉum toku tikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na.  a.  160)
talivaṉ taliviyiṭam “aṇittu em iṭam” eṉṟal -
“aḻakiya melliya mayirmuṭiyiṉi yuṭiyāy! yāṉ niṉṉip pirintu pōkiṉṟēṉē eṉṟu varunti aḻēṟka. emmaliyil tiṉiyi vitippataṟku, vaḷarntuḷḷa maraṅkaḷi aḻittu nilattic camaṉ ceyvatu uṇṭu. appoḻutu aḻikkappaṭum cantaṉa marattiṉ taḻikaḷiyē num ūravar taḻiyuṭiyākap puṉintu payaṉpaṭuttuvar. attakiya aṇmiyiluḷḷatu emmūr. nāḷtōṟum palamuṟi vantu eṉ tuyarum niṉtuyarum tīra niṉṉuṭaṉ aḷavaḷāvuvēṉ” eṉṟāṟ pōlat taliviyi āṟṟu vikku mukattāṉ talivaṉ taṉatu ūr aṇimikkaṇ uḷḷamiyik kūṟutal.
iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ piṉṉar nikaḻvatu. ‘teḷi vakappaṭuttal’ eṉṉum pakutippaṭa nikaḻvatu ikkūṟṟu.	
(to. po. 101 nac.)
talivaṉ taliviyiṭam nayappum pirivaccamum vaṉpuṟiyum kūṟal -
talivaṉ taliviyiṭam tāṉ avaḷi virumputaliyum, pirintu pōtaṟkaṇ avaḷukku ēṟpaṭum tuṉpam paṟṟit tāṉ añcutaliyum kūṟittaṉ naṭpiṉ nilipēṟu paṟṟi aiyuṟal vēṇṭā eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttal.
“kuvaḷippū maṇakkum kūntaliyum, āmpaṟpūp pōla maṇam vīcum vāyiṉiyum, tāmarittātu pōṉṟa pacali yiyum uṭiya māayōyē! nī ‘nāṉ añcēl’ eṉṟu kūṟiya eṉ  collik koṇṭē uṉṉip pirintu maṟantu viṭuvēṉō eṉṟu añcātiru. niṉṉip pirivataṉāl eṉakku inta ulakamē paricākak kiṭippatā yiruppiṉum uṉṉuṭiya naṭpik kiviṭamāṭṭēṉ” (kuṟun. 300) eṉṟāṟpōla, avaḷ aḻaki nayantu pārāṭṭi, avaḷatu accattic cuṭṭi, avaḷit tāṉ orukālum piriyā miyi vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟutal. iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarcci yiṉ piṉṉart ‘teḷivakappaṭuttal’ eṉṉum pakutikkaṇ nikaḻvatu.	(to. po. 101. nac.)
talivaṉ taliviyatu acivu aṟintu iruttal -
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ, naṭantuvarum kaḷippāl talivi ayarvuṟṟami kaṇṭa talivaṉ, avaḷic ciṟupōtu amarac ceytu tāṉum iruntu iḷippāṟutal.
“naṅkāy! ikkoṭiya pāliyiṉ aram pōṉṟa kūriya kaṟkaḷi uṉ aṭimalarkaḷ tāṅkum āṟṟaluṭiyaṉa alla. ātaliṉ, pāliyiṉ veppam nīṅkumaḷavum iccōliyil taṅki iḷippāṟuvāyāka” (tañci kō. 317) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
ituvariviyaluḷ kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭil ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatorukūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 182)
talivaṉ taliviyiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmi iyampal -
tāṉ uyirvāḻat taliviyit tāṉ aṭital iṉṟi yamiyātatu eṉat talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal. 
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum kiḷavik kaṇṇatoru tuṟi. itaṉi ‘āṟṟātu urittal’ eṉṉum tirukkōviyār. (73)	(na. a. 144)
talivaṉ taliviyiṉ cevvi eḷimi kūṟal -
“cevvi aritu” eṉṟu kūṟiya tōḻikkut talivaṉ “talivi cevvi eḷitē” eṉak kūṟutal.
“tōḻi!niṉ taliviyiṭam ceṉṟu nāṉ vantiruppatic col. atupōtu uṭaṉē avaḷ uṉṉit taḻuvi makiḻ vāḷ. avaḷ maṉanili attakiyatākattāṉ irukkum!” 	(tañci. kō.111) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 146)
talivaṉ taliviyiṉ naṉṉayam urittal-
talivaṉ vaṇṭu, maṉam mutaliyavaṟṟit taṉṉuṭaṉ uri yāṭuvaṉavākak koṇṭu avaṟṟiṟkut tāṉ taliviyiṭam koṇṭuḷḷa pēraṉpiṉik kūṟupavaṉāyt taṉ virup piṉi eṭuttukkūṟal.	(to. po. 101 nac.)
aḵṟiṇip poruḷi muṉṉiliyākkic colvaḻip paṭuttiya talivaṉ, avaḷ aḻakiṉip puṉinturippā ṉāka,  aṉṉap paṟaviyi nōkki, “aṉṉamē! inneytaṟ pakutiyil naṭantu cellum ippeṇṇiṉ piṉṉē cel laṟka. ivaḷ naṭiyaḻaku uṉakku orupōtum vārātu. ivaḷit toṭarntu ceṉṟāl ivaḷ naṭiyaḻakiṉmuṉ nī naṭiyaḻaku tōṟṟu nāṇuvāy!” (cilap. kāṉal. 23) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟutal.	(98 iḷa.)
talivaṉ taliviyiṉ peṟṟōri vāḻttal -
“kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiyum taḻiyāṭi uṭutta ceyaṟki vaṉappiyum uṭiya ivaḻakiyāḷ eṉṉuḷḷattik kavarntavaḷ. ivaḷ tanti vāḻka! nelvaḷam poruntiya toṇṭi eṉṟa ūriṉi otta celvatti, innaṅkinal lāḷi eṉakku īṉṟu aḷitta tāy peṟuvāḷāka!” (naṟ. 8) eṉpatu pōla, iṭantalippāṭṭiṉ iṟutikkaṇṇō iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ iṟutikkaṇṇō talimakaḷi āyattu uytta talimakaṉ avaḷ peṟṟōrit taṉ ṉuḷḷē vāḻttik kūṟal.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘colliyanukarcci’ eṉpuḻic ‘colliya’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ oru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ, taliviyum tāṉum orē maliyiṉar ātalit taṉ kuṟippāṟ   kūṟutal -
talivaṉ taliviyiṉ vaṉappip pukaḻntu kūṟum niliyil vaṇṭiṉi nōkki, “pūmaṇattiṉi ārāytaliyē vāḻkkit toḻilāka vuṭiya aḻakiya ciṟakukaḷiyuṭiya tumpī! nī eṉ nilattu vaṇṭu ākiyāl eṉakkākak kūṟātu uṇmiyik kūṟu. mayil pōṉṟa cāyaliyum, payiṉṟatu eḻumiyum poruntiya naṭpiṉiyum, ceṟinta paṟkaḷiyum uṭiya ittaliviyatu kūntaliṉ maṇattiviṭa uṉṉāl aṟiyappaṭum pūkkaḷil maṇamuṭiyavum uḷavō?” (kuṟun.2) eṉṟu kūṟutaṟkaṇ, taliviyum taṉmaliyic cārntavaḷ eṉpataṉik kuṟippāṉ veḷiyiṭṭavāṟu. iṅṅaṉam kūṟutal iyaṟkip puṇarccikku muṉṉar eṉavum, piṉṉar eṉavum iruvakik karuttukkaḷ nilavuvaṉa.			(to. po. 93. nac.)
talivaṉ taliviyi aṭiyac cākkāṭu kuṟittal -
talivaṉ tāṉ taliviyip peṟutaṟkākat taṉṉuyiri viṭavum  tuṇintuviṭṭa ceytiyit tōḻikkuk kūṟutal.
tōḻiyāṉ cēṭpaṭukkappaṭṭa talivaṉ tōḻiyi nōkki, ”kāmam elli kaṭakkumāyiṉ kutiriyi ivartal pōlap paṉimaṭalāṉ amikkappaṭṭa kutiri ēṟavum tuṇivar; naṟumpūkkaḷi muṭippatu pōla erukkampūvic cūṭavum muṟpaṭuvar; ūriluḷḷavar pēcum ēḷaṉac coṟ kaḷiyum nāṇātu ēṟṟuk koḷvar; variyiṉiṉṟu kīḻē pāyntu uyirviṭavum tuṇivar ”(kuṟun. 17) eṉṟu talivaṉ tāṉ varipāytaṟkum oruppaṭṭa ceytiyit tōḻikkuk kūṟutal.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘maṭalmāk kūṟum iṭaṉumār uṇṭē’ eṉpuḻi ummiyāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 102  nac.)
talivaṉ taliviyi avaḷatu mēṉmi kaṇṭu viyattal -
talivaṉ petumpip paruvattu oruttiyi maṇantu koṇṭu illiṟku aḻittuvarukiyil, antap peṇṇi etirkoṇṭu varavēṟṟa taliviyiṉ uyarkuṇatti avaṉ pukaḻnturittal.
“nāṉ petumpippeṇ oruttiyi mikavirumpi maṇantu maṅkalak kōlattuṭaṉ vantapōtu ivaḷ avaḷi etir koṇṭu aḻittu upacarittāḷē! itaṟkuk kāraṇam, ivaḷukku eṉmēl uḷḷa kātalā? aṉṟi, ivaḷatu aruntatik kaṟpiṉ uyarvā? eṉ eṉṟu ivaḷip pukaḻvēṉ” eṉṉum talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu poruḷvayiṉpirivu eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ampikā. 561)
talivaṉ (iṟiyōṉ) taliviyi iṭattetirppaṭutal -
talivi niṟkum kuṟiyiṭattiṟkut talivaṉ vantu, avaḷik kāṇutal.
“tirumakaḷē! immalip pakutiyi āticēṭaṉākiya paṭukki eṉṟum eṉṉit tirumāl eṉṟum karutiyō, eṉ maṉa mākiya tāmariyil taṅkalām eṉṟu viruppam koṇṭō, ippoḻiṟku nī vantuḷḷāy?” (kōvi. 120) eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivaṉ kūṟi eytutal.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘uvanturittal’ eṉṉum (120)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 149)
talivaṉ taliviyi (iṟiviyi) ilvayiṉ viṭuttal -
iravukkuṟip puṇarccikkup piṉṉar, talivaṉ taliviyi avaḷatu maṉikkaṭṭaṭa ellikkaṇ koṇṭu viṭutal.
itaṉip ‘paḷḷiyiṭattu uyttal’ eṉṉum tirukkōvi yār (167).
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 158)
talivaṉ taliviyik kūṭiyapiṉ taṉṉūr mīṭal - 
‘tirumakaṭ puṇarntavaṉ cēṟal’ eṉṉum tuṟi. “talivi eṉakku  uruveḷiyāka eppoḻutum kāṭci vaḻaṅkuvatāl avaḷ kātukaḷil aṇintuḷḷa kuḻikaḷ tarum oḷiyi lēyē yāṉ eḷitil ūr pōyc cērntuviṭuvēṉ” (tañci kō.189) eṉṟu talivaṉ iravukkuṟi nikaḻntapiṉ tōḻiyiṭam kūṟic cēṟal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ’iravukkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 158)
talivaṉ taliviyic curattu uyttal -
talivaṉ taliviyip pālivaḻiyil vaḻinaṭakkum kaḷipput teriyāmal iṉiya coṟkaḷip pēci mella mella aḻittuc cellutal.
“eṉṉuyir aṉiyāy! kāṉyāṟum veṇmaṇalum pōṉṟavaṟṟip pārttukkoṇṭē mella naṭantuvā” (tañci. kō. 316) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘mellak koṇṭēkal’ eṉṉum (215).
itu variviyaluḷ kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭil ‘uṭaṉ pōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 182)
talivaṉ taliviyic colvaḻippaṭuttuk kūṟal -
talivaṉ talivimuṉ uriyāṭa iyalāta vaṇṭu, neñcu mutaliyavaṟṟit taṉṉiṭam uriyāṭuvaṉa pōlap pēcutal.
(to. po. 101 nac.)
talivaṉ tāṉ colkiṉṟa colliṉvaḻi avaḷ niṟku māṟu amittuk kūṟutal.	(98 iḷa.)
iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ muṉ nikaḻvatu eṉavum, iraṇṭām kūṭṭattu nikaḻvatu eṉavum iru tiṟamākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
talivaṉ, nāṇattālum accattālum vēṭkiyi maṟittu niṉṟa taliviyi muṉṉiliyākkic cila kūṟa, avaḷ taṉ vēṭkiyi veḷippaṭuttum vakiyil muṟuval ceytu uvakikkaṇṇīr tuḷittapiṉ nāṇattāṉ taṉ kaṇkaḷip pottik koḷḷat talivaṉ avaḷi nōkki, “nāṉ kūṟuvataṉi nī etirēṟṟuk koḷḷātu taliyik kaviḻttukkoṇṭu nāṇukiṟāy. kāmam elliyik kaṭakkumāyiṉ aḵtu eṉṉāṉ tāṅkutaṟku ariyatu. uṉ kaṇkaḷ eṉṉi varuttukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu karutik kaṭi civanta uṉ kaṇkaḷip pottikkoṇṭāy. karumpiṉ vaṭivam toyyilāka eḻutappaṭṭa uṉ tōḷ kaḷum eṉakku varuttam tarukiṉṟaṉa” (naṟ. 39) eṉpatu pōlvaṉa kūṟit taliviyi meyyuṟu puṇarccikku uṭaṉ paṭac ceytal.	(98. iḷa.)
talivaṉ (puravalaṉ) taliviyit tēṟṟal -
varum vaḻiyil taṉakku ettakiya tuṉpamum nērātu eṉṟu talivaṉ talivikku āṟutal kūṟutal. “eṉkiyil uṉ kaṇ pōṉṟa koṭiya vēl uṇṭu. ataṉ oḷi iruḷip paruka, nāṉ yātōr accamum  iṉṟi vantēṉ; iṉiyum varuvēṉ. nī kavalaṟka” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 1581)
talivaṉ taliviyippuṇarntu nīṅkumpōtu āṟṟātu kūṟal - 
“eṉ neñcattuḷḷa kāmackāyccalukku maruntākiya taṉ koṅkikaḷāl, pūciya cantaṉam aḻiyumāṟu eṉṉit taḻuvum ciṟukuṭiyi luḷḷa kāṉavaṉmakaḷ eṉakku eṉṟum iṉimi ceypavaḷāyiṉum, pirintu pōkuṅkāl eṉakku eṉṟum koṭiyavaḷāka irukkiṟāḷ” eṉṟu talivaṉ taliviyatu pirivu āṟṟātu kūṟutal.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘parivuṟṟu meliyiṉum’ eṉṟatāl koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.	(to. po. 103 nac.)
talivaṉ taliviyip peṟṟavaḻi makiḻntu kūṟal -
iṭantalippāṭṭilum, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattilum talivi yip puṇarntu nīṅkum talivaṉ, “neyppiṉiyuṭiya kūntaliyum oḷi poruntiya neṟṟiyiyumuṭiya ik kuṟumakaḷ maṇattiyum taṇmiyiyum uṭiyavaḷ. āyiṉum, pirinta kālattup poṟuttaṟku ariya varuttat tit tarupavaḷ. avaḷ taṉmikaḷi eṉ collāl pukaḻntu kūṟum āṟṟal eṉakku illi. cila coṟkaḷiyē pēcukiṟāḷ. aviyum mikameṉmiyākavuḷḷaṉa. yāṉ avaḷit taḻuvumpōtu pañcaṇiyip pōṉṟu meṉ miyi yuṭiyavaḷāy irukkiṟāḷ” (kuṟun.  70) eṉavum,
“kāntaḷ, mulli, kuvaḷiivaṟṟit toṭuttuk kaṭṭap paṭṭa pūmāli pōṉṟu naṟumaṇam kamaḻum ittaliviyiṉ mēṉi taḷirik kāṭṭilum meṉmiyum niṟamum poruntit taḻuvutaṟku iṉitāyuḷḷatu”.  (kuṟun  62) eṉavum
taliviyiṭam tāṉ peṟṟa aimpoṟi iṉpattit talivaṉ makiḻntu taṉṉuḷ kūṟiyatu.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ taliviyi muṉṉiliyākkik kūṟal -
taliviyiṉ vēṭkik kuṟippiṉi avaḷkaṇkaḷāṉ uṇarntavaḻiyum, kulattiṉ vaḻivanta iyaṟki vaṉmi yāṉ nāṇamum accamum mītūrak kāmakkuṟippu illā tāḷip pōla avaḷ  niṉṟaviṭattut talivaṉ avaḷi muṉṉilippaṭutti,
“‘maṭantāy! tōḻiyaroṭu kūṭiviṉiyāṭāmalum ney tal pūkkaḷi māliyākat toṭukkāmalum ikkaṭaṟkaric cōliyiṉ oru pakkattē niṟkum pēraḻakiyākiya nī, ikkaṭaṟ parappil virumpit taṅkiyirukkum nīr aramakaḷō? allatu inta uppaṅkaḻi arukē taṅki vāḻum māṉuṭap peṇṇō? colluka’ eṉṟu paṇivāṉa coṟkaḷil yāṉ viṉaviyatik kēṭṭu, avaḷ uvakik kaṇṇīr tuḷittup puṉmuṟuval ceyta kuṟippiṉāṉ avaḷit taḻuvutaṟku eṉakku icivu kiṭṭiviṭṭatu” (naṟ. 155) eṉṟu taṉṉuḷ kūṟik koṇṭatu.	(to. po. 98. iḷa.)
iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ muṉ nikaḻvatu eṉavum, iraṇṭām kūṭṭattu nikaḻvatu eṉavum iru tiṟamākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
talivaṉ talivimuṉ taṉakku muṉṉar vantu uriyāṭāta vaṇṭu, neñcu mutaliyavaṟṟi muṉṉilikkaṇ, kaṟpaṉi yil koṇṭuvantu kūṟal 101 nac.
talivaṉ taliviyi viṭuttal -
talivaṉ taliviyik kūṭi makiḻntapiṉ āyattuṭaṉ cēra aṉupputal.
“vāṉamākiya nīrniliyil viṇmīṉkaḷākiya moṭṭuk kaḷukkum, mēkaṅkaḷākiya ilikaḷukkum iṭiyē cantiraṉākiya tāmari aḻakuṟa viḷaṅkuvatu pōlac cōli yil uṉ tōḻiyarkūṭṭam polivu peṟumāṟu nī avar kaḷic ceṉṟu aṭivāyāka” (kōvi. 124) eṉṟu talivaṉ taliviyi āyattu uyttal.

itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. iḵtu iṭantalippāṭṭilum ‘āyattu uyttal’ eṉṉum kiḷaviyāka varum. ikkūṟṟu ‘pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkum uriyatu.	(na. a. 149)
talivaṉ taliviyi (talimakaḷ taṉṉi) viṭuttal (2) -
uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻumkāli taliviyi mīṭkum nōk kuṭaṉ avaḷ cuṟṟattār piṉ toṭarntu varuvatit talivi talivaṟkuk kūṟa, uṭaṉē avaṉ avaḷi viṭṭu appāṟ cellutal.
taliviyiṉ uṟaviṉar paṭikkaruvikaḷuṭaṉ varuvatik kaṇṇuṟṟa talivaṉ, “eṉ uyirē aṉiyāy! nampiṉṉē varuvōr āyutam tāṅkiya āṟalikaḷvar eṉiṉ, am poṉṟiṉālēyē aṉivariyum koṉṟu kuvippēṉ; āyiṉ, varuvōr niṉ uṟaviṉar ātaliṉ, avarkaḷip puṇpaṭuttutal takkataṉṟu eṉa viṭuttēṉ. nī eṉṉuṭaṉ varutal vēṇṭā; iṅkēyē nil; ivarkaḷ niṉṉi mīṭṭuk koṇṭu celka. virivil nāṉ vantu varintu koḷ vēṉ” (tañci. 364) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘aṭaleṭutturittal’ eṉṉum (216). itu variviyaluḷ ‘uṭaṉpōkku iṭiyīṭu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 198)
talivaṉ taliviyoṭu taṉ nakar cārtal -
uṭaṉpōṉa talivaṉ taliviyuṭaṉ taṉṉūric ceṉ ṟaṭital. nakar - illamum ām.
“caṅkukaḷ muḻaṅka, muracaṅkaḷ ārppat talivaṉuṭiya uṟaviṉarkaḷ etirkoḷḷap polivu peṟum māḷiki yit talivaṉum taliviyum tirumālum tirumakaḷum pōlap piriyā aṉpoṭu vantaṭintaṉar” (ampikā. 384) eṉpatu pōṉṟa kavikūṟṟu.
itu variviyaluḷ kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭil ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uriyiṟ koṇ ṭatu. 	(i. vi.536 uri)
talivaṉ talivi (kuṟuntoṭi) vāḻum ūr nōkki mati mayaṅkal -
talivi vikum ūrip pārttut talivaṉ ēṅki niṉittal.
“avaḷip peṟutal aritāyiṟṟu; avaḷ piṉṉē  ceṉṟuviṭṭa eṉ neñcatti mīṭpatu ataṉiṉum aritā yiṟṟu. eṉ ceyvēṉ? iṉi avaḷ vāḻum ūriṉic ceṉṟu uṟutalum aritām. eṉṉuyir cōrvuṟukiṉṟatu”. (tañci. kō. 162) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itaṉip ‘patinōkki varuntal’ eṉṉum tirukkōviyār (147). itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pakaṟkuṟi iṭiyīṭu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 156)
talivaṉ taḻi koṇṭu cēṟal -
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattil talivaṉukku uṭaṉēyē taliviyiṉ uṭaṉpāṭṭiṉik kūṟiṉ avaḷi avaṉ eḷiyaḷākak karutuvāṉ eṉṟu cēṭpaṭi kūṟat tuṇinta tōḻiyiṭam talivaṉ ceṉṟu, avaḷ kuṟippaṟintu, piṉṉum maṟuttaṟku iṭamiṉṟi, “num karuṇi vēṇṭi vantuḷḷa eṉ kiyi luḷḷa ittaḻiyuṭiyik kiyuṟiyāka ēṟṟu eṉkuṟi muṭittaruḷuvīr” eṉṟu kūṟa vēṇṭic cantaṉattaḻi koṇṭu cellutal.	(kōvi. 90)
itu ‘cēṭpaṭi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
talivaṉ taḷarntōḷ ompal -
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ talivi naṭimelintavaḻit talivaṉ avaḷukku ōyvaḷittu iṉitiṉ uriyāṭi makiḻvittu aḻittuc cēṟal. (iḵtu ‘acivu aṟintu iruttal’ (na. a. 182) eṉavum paṭum.) itu pāli naṭiyiyal.			(vī. cō. 93 urimēṟ.)
talivaṉ taḷarvakaṉ ṟurittal -
iravukkuṟiyiṭattu vanta taliviyit talivaṉ etirp paṭṭut tirumakaḷākiya avaḷ taṉ iruppiṭam ākiya tāmariyi iravil tāṉē valiyat tiṟantukoṇṭu poḻilukku vanta ceyti taṉakku makiḻcci taruvatākak kūṟiyatu.
itaṉi ‘vaṇṭuṟi tārōṉ vantetirppaṭutal’ (na. a. 158) eṉavum kūṟupa.
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 164)
talivaṉ taṉkaṭami kūṟal -
uṭaṉpōkkikkaṇ talivaṉ, taliviyi aḻittuk koṇṭu pālivaṉattiṭic cellumpōtu, taṉṉi nampi vanta avaḷi orupōtum kiviṭāta taṉ kaṭami yuṇarcciyi avaḷiṭam kūṟikkoṇṭu celvāṉ.
talivaṉ taṉ tuṇipu urittal -
taliviyip peṟa niṉitta talivaṉ taliviyum tōḻi yum cērntirunta iṭatti aṭintu tāṉ maṭal ēṟiyāvatu taliviyip peṟa muṭivu ceyta ceytiyit tōḻiyiṭam kuṟippiṭutal.
itaṉi ‘ maṭalēṟṟiṉit talivaṉ taṉmēl vittuc cāṟṟal’ eṉpar. 	(na. a. 145)
itu tirukkōviyāril ‘maṭaṟṟiṟam’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 75)
talivaṉ taṉ nili urittal -
taliviyiṉ maṉattaḷarvāṉ ēṟpaṭṭa polivu aḻi viṉik kaṇṭa talivaṉ, taliviyiṭam tāṉ koṇṭa pēraṉpiṉi avaḷmaṉattil nilipeṟumāṟu kūṟal			(to. po. 98 iḷa.)
talimakaḷmāṭṭut talivaṉ taṉṉuḷḷattil mēliṭṭu niṉṟa vēṭki mikutiyiṉi iyaṟkippuṇarcciyiṉ muṉṉark kuṟippiṭutal. aḵtāvatu, talivaṉ taliviyiṉ peruvaṉappiṉip pukaḻntu kūṟiya aḷavil avaḷ nāṇat tāl kaṇkaḷip pottikkoḷḷa, avaḷi nōkki, “kāmam mikuvatāyiṉ ataṉi eṉṉāl tāṅkutal iyalātu. kaṭi civanta niṉ kaṇkaḷ eṉṉik kōpikkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu avaṟṟip putittukkoṇṭāy. uṉṉuṭiya karumpu vaṭivil toyyiṟkoṭi eḻutappeṟṟa tōḷkaḷum eṉakkut tuṉpam tarukiṉṟaṉa” (naṟ.39) eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa kūṟutal.	(iḷa.)
tam niliyurittal āvatu iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ piṉṉart talivaṉ taliviyiṭam, “uṉakkum eṉakku miṭiyēyuḷḷa toṭarpu eḻupiṟappum toṭarntu varukiṟatu. itu vari iruvarākit taṉittu vāḻnta nām ikkūṭṭattiṉ piṉ taṉittaṉip pirintu vāḻum tuyaram nīṅkap peṟuvōm. pirivu aritākiya kāmattuṭaṉēyē iruvarukkum orucēruyir pōvatāka eṉṟu virumpu kiṟēṉ” (kuṟun. 57) eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa kūṟutal.	(101 nac.)
talivaṉ taṉ nili cāṟṟal -
tōḻi utavi ceyya maṟuttāl tāṉ taliviyik kūṭutal iyalātu eṉpatit talivaṉ avaḷukkuk kūṟutal.
“tōḻi! niṉ utaviyiṉṟi yāṉ taliviyip pala pārāṭṭi aṇukuvatum kūṭuvatum iyalā. avi iyalāviṭiṉ, yāṉ uyir vāḻtalum iyalātu” (aiṅ. 178) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 144)
talivaṉ taṉpati aṭintami talivikku uṇarttal -
uṭaṉpōkkil curattiṭi naṭantu taḷarntu uṭaṉvarum talivikkut taṉ ūr arukē vantuviṭṭami talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“naṅkāy!atō ceykuṉṟukaḷ uḷa. ivi aḻakiya malar ōṭikaḷ. aṅkut terivaṉa uyarmarac cōlikaḷ. appakkam vētiyar vētam ōtum iṭaṅkaḷ. ivviṭam iṟivaṉ tirukkōyil” (kōvi. 223) eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivaṉ taṉpatikku vantu cērnta ceytiyit talivikkuk kūṟal.
itu variviyaluḷ kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭil ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 182)
talivaṉ taṉpatikku akaṟci cāṟṟal -
talivaṉ taṉ ūrkkut tāṉ ceṉṟu varal vēṇṭumeṉṟu tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal. kaḷavoḻukkam toṭarntu nikaḻum pōtu itu nērkiṟatu.
“tōḻi!yāṉ eṉatu ūr ceṉṟu virivil mīḷvēṉ. atuvari taliviyit tēṟṟuvittal niṉ kaṭami” (tañci. kō. 248) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘oruvaḻit taṇattal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 168)
talivaṉ, taṉ piriviṟkut talivi añciya kuṟippi nāṇ paṟṟi veḷiyiṭātavaḻi, tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu -
“eṉ kaṇ niṟinta aḻakiyāṉa niṉ talivikkup peṇ ṇiṟku iyalpāy niṟinta maṭami elli mikkuḷḷatu” eṉṟu tāṉ piriyātapōtē pirintuviṭakkūṭum eṉṟu añciya taliviyatu kuṟippuk kaṇṭu talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam kūṟiyavāṟu. 	(kuṟaḷ 1272)
talivaṉ taṉmaṉattu uvaki kūrtal -
parattiyiṟ pirintu ceṉṟu taliviyiṉ makappēṟṟi aṟintu maṉikkut talivaṉ mīṇṭapōtu, tōḻi talivi putalvaṉi īṉṟu neyyāṭiya ceyti kūṟiyapōtu, avaṉ taliviyatu uyarviyum putiya aḻakiyum niṉintu uvattal.
“eṉ taliviyiṉ miyuṇṭa kuvaḷimalar pōlum kaṇkaḷiyum, maṉamakiḻcciyiyum, kuḻantiyi ēntiya kikaḷiyum pāl paraviyuḷḷa taṉaṅkaḷiyum neyyāṭip pacantu oḷi vīcum uṭampiyum, kuṇaṅkaḷiyum kaṇṭu makiḻvēṉ.” (ampikā. 465) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu. (‘yāṇarik kāṇa virumpal’ eṉpatu ikkōvik kūṟṟu.)
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. iḵtu uṇartta uṇarā ūṭal.			(na. a. 206)
talivaṉ taṉmikaḷit taṉṉuṭiyavākak koṇṭu pēcum taliviyatu nili kaṇṭa tāy “āvēcamō” eṉṟu nontu kūṟutal -
‘kaṭal ñālam’ eṉṟa pāṭal-
“ulakattip paṭittatum, ulakamāyp parantiruppatum, ulakattik kālāl aḷantukoṇṭatum, aṉittum yāṉē eṉṟellām pēcukiṉṟāḷ eṉ makaḷ. iḵtu ivaḷiṭattilē tirumāl āvēcittu iruppatāltāṉō, eṉṉavō!” eṉṉum tāy kūṟṟu.	(tiruvāy. 5-6-1)
talivaṉ (talimakaṉ) taṉṉit tāṉē pukaḻtal -
taliviyiṉ uruvatti ōviyattil variya oṇṇātu eṉṟa pāṅkikkut talivaṉ, taṉṉāl avaḷi ōviyattil eḻuta iyalum eṉṟu taṉ āṟṟalik kūṟik koḷḷutal- itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 145)
talivaṉ taṉṉit tōḻi paṇpiṟ peyarttavaḻik kūṟal -
tōḻi, taliviyiṉ iḷami mutaliya iyalpiṉi eṭuttuk kūṟit talivaṉuṭiya vēṭkiyi māṟṟak karutiya viṭattē talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“tōḻi! kuṉṟak kuṟavaṉuṭiya aṉpukkuriya iḷiya makaḷ vaṇṭukaḷ moykkum kūntaliyum, taḻi uṭiyiyum, vaḷilkaḷ aṇinta kikaḷiyum, viḻuntu muḷitta veḷḷiya paṟkaḷiyum, uṭiyavaḷ. avaḷ iḷiyaḷāyt tōṟṟam vaḻaṅkiṉum, eṉṉit taṉ vaṉappiṉāl peritum varuttiviṭṭāḷ” (aiṅ.256) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 103 nac.)
talivaṉ tōḻiyiṟ puṇarccikkaṇ taṉ nili koḷīik kūṟiyatu.
talivaṉ taṉṉi nōkki makiḻnta talimakaḷik kaṇṭu kūṟiyatu -
“tōḻi eṉṉi akaṟṟum coṟkaḷik kūṟiyakāli nāṉ kuḻintu kūṟiyakāli nōkkiyapoḻutu, talivi tāṉum iraṅki nōkkit taṉakkuḷḷē nakukiṉṟāḷ;avaḷuṭiya an nakikkaṇ oru naṉmik kuṟippuṇṭu” eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal. 	(kuṟaḷ 1098)
talivaṉ taṉṉip pukaḻtal -
talivaṉ talivi muṉṉart tāṉ ceṉṟu kaṟṟut teḷiyap pōvatākavum, poruḷ peritum īṭṭi vantu iravalarkkuk koṭuppatākavum, aṟam ceyvatākavum, taṉ naṇpaṉukku utavappōvatākavum kūṟit taṉ āṟṟalip pukaḻntu kūṟit taliviyit taṉ potuttoṇṭukku utavum vakiyil pirivukku uṭaṉpaṭumāṟu ceytal.	(to. po. 230 kuḻa.)
talivaṉ taṉakku vāyil nērāta taliviyatu koṭumiyik kūṟak kēṭṭa tōḻi, avaṟkuc colliyatu -
“kuḷattil nīrāṭum erumi naṉku piṇikkappaṭṭa ōṭattip pōlat tōṉṟum ūraṉē! (nīrāṭum erumi palarum ēṟutaṟkuriya ampi pōlat tōṉṟuvatu pōla,nīyum palarukkumurimiyāvāypōlum!) ivaḷ, nī tavaṟu ceytavaḻi, uṉ peṟṟōr pōla uṉṉik kaṭintu koḷḷa urimiyuṭiyavaḷ eṉpataṉi maṟantāyō?” eṉṟāṟ pōlat tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal.	(aiṅ. 98)
talivaṉ taṉittup puṉalāṭutal kaṇṭa paratti avaṉarukil tāṉum taṉittup puṉalāṭat talivaṉ avaḷik kippaṟṟiyavaḻi avaḷ tōḻi kūṟiyatu-
“putuppuṉalil nīrāṭip pōric ceyyum civanta kaṇkaḷi yuṭiya immakaḷ yāvaḷ?”eṉṟu kūṟit talivaṉ taṉ parattiyiṉ kikaḷip paṟṟiṉāṉāka, avaḷ tōḻi, “ivaḷ yāruṭiya makaḷāyiruntāl umakku eṉṉa?em kikaḷip paṟṟutalukku urimi koṇṭāṭum nīr yāruṭiya makaṉ?” eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟutal.	(aiṅ.79)
talivaṉ taṉinili kaṇṭu taḷarvakaṉṟu urittal-
talivi āyattuṭaṉ cērātu taṉiyē iruppatu kaṇṭa talivaṉ kavali nīṅka makiḻntu urippatu.
“atō eṉa uyiraṉiyāḷ āyattoṭu kūṭi malar paṟikkā malum, cuṉiyāṭāmalum, pantu āṭāmalum taṉiyē niṟkiṟāḷ. eṉ vēṭki payaṉ peṟum” (kōvi. 40)eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal 
itaṉik kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘iṭantalippāṭu’ eṉum toku tikkaṇṇum (i.vi)pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉum tokutik kaṇṇum (kōvi) aṭakkupa. (i.vi. 503 uri; kōvi. 40)
talivaṉ tāḷāṇetirum piriviṉkaṇ kūṟal -
kaḷavuk kālattut talivaṉ neṭuntolivu taliviyip pirintu cellāmal pakkattu ūrukkut taṉmuyaṟciyāṉ ceyaṟpaṭap piriyumiṭattuk kūṟutal.
“pāka!iṉṟē viṉi kuṟittup puṟappaṭṭu, nāḷi, maliyi liruntu viḻum aruviyip pōla nam tēr virivil taṉ cakkaraṅkaḷāṉ putarkaḷi aṟuttukkoṇṭu virintu vara, taliviyiṉ mēṉiyi maṇantu uvakkum vakiyil mīḷutal vēṇṭum”(kuṟun. 189) eṉṟāṟ pōlap pirital arumi karutit talivaṉ kūṟutal.
pirintavaḻi vāṭik kāṟṟi nōkki, “aruvikaḷiyuṭiya maliyiṉ  ucciyil māṉkaḷ nellikkāyit tiṉṉum muṉṉiṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya   kuṭicikaḷ niṟinta ciṟukuṭikkaṇ vikum talivi varuntātavāṟu avaḷik kāppāyāka!” (kuṟun. 235) eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutalumuṇṭu.			(to. po. 105iḷa.)
ittuṟiyit talivi kūṟṟākak koḷḷuvar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar. avar ‘vāḷāṇ etirum piriviṉāṉum’ eṉṟu pāṭam ōtuvar.	(107 nac.)
talivaṉ tāḷāṇpakkamum takutiyatu amitiyum oṉṟāmikkaṇ kūṟutal -
‘maṉikkaṇ cōmpiyiruttali viṭuttu muyaṟciyāl poruḷ tēṭa vēṇṭum’ eṉṉum eṇṇatti, “taliviyi maṉiyil iruntukoṇṭu tuyaruṟātu pēṇa vēṇṭum” eṉṉum eṇṇamum, “eppaṭiyāvatu poruḷīṭṭal vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇam kūṭātu;tattam takutikkēṟṟa toḻil ceytē poruḷīṭṭal vēṇṭum; ataṟku. niṉitta aḷavilēyē vāyppuk kiṭṭātu” eṉṉum eṇṇamum māṟākap poruntit taṭuttaṟkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟal. 	(to. po. 41. nac.)
talivaṉ, ‘tāṉ akam pukāaṉ peyartal iṉmiyiṉ, kāṭci āciyiṉ kaḷampukkuk kalaṅki, vēṭkiyiṉ mayaṅkik kiyaṟu poḻutiṉ’kaṇ kūṟal -
pakaṟkuṟiyilō iravukkuṟiyilō talivaṉ taliviyik kāṇa iyalāta niliyil poḻutu vīṇē kaḻintavaḻi, avaḷik kāṇum vēṭkiyāṉ kuṟiyiṭattuc ceṉṟu āṇṭuk kāṇātu kalaṅki āciyāl mayakkamuṟṟuc ceyalaṟṟu niṟkum kālattut talivaṉ kūṟutal.			(to. po. 105 iḷa.)
“ikkāntaḷ malaril yāṉ ceruki vikkum mōtiram eṉ varukiyit talivikku urippatākuka” eṉṟu kūṟit taṉ mōtiratti ataṉkaṇ cerukic cellutal, mōtiram māli muttam mutaliyavaṟṟik kōṭṭiṉum koṭiyiṉum iṭṭuvittut taṉṉuṭ kūṟutal pōlvaṉa.	(107 nac.)
icceytiyit talivi kūṟṟākak koḷvar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar. 
kāṇāvakiyiṉ poḻutu naṉi ikantu talivi kuṟi tappiyakkālum, talivaṉ kuṟiyiṭam pukuntallatu peyarāṉ eṉpatu tāṉ aṟiyum ātaliṉ, āṇṭup pukuntavaṉ tāṉ vantu nīṅkiṉami aṟitaṟku ōr kuṟi ceytaṉṟi vāḷātu peyarāṉ aṉṟē? akkuṟi kāṇum kāṭci viruppiṉāṉ talivi piṟṟiñāṉṟu viṭiyaliṟ ceṉṟu āṇṭiya kuṟi kaṇṭu kalaṅki avaṉi etirppaṭutal vēṭkiyaḷākic ceytal aṟiyātu mayakkattoṭu kiyaṟavu eytum poḻu tiṉkaṇ kūṟṟu nikaḻttuvāḷ.
kuṟi- mōtiram māli muttam mutaliyaṉa kōṭṭiṉum koṭiyiṉum, iṭṭu vittaṉavām. ivi varuttattukku ētuvām. (nac.)
“tōḻi! ikkāntaḷ meṉmukiyil vaṇṭu moykka villi. veṟpar vantu ceṉṟataṟku aṭiyāḷamāka nīlakkal mōtiram aṇivikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu” eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu. (nac.)
talivaṉ tāṉ avaṭ piḻitta niliyiṉkaṇ kūṟal -
“niṉṉiṉ piriyēṉ” eṉṟu taliviyip piriyāmikku uṟuti moḻi kūṟiya talivaṉ talivikkuc coṉṉa collip piḻitta niliyiṉik koṇṭavaḻit taṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟutal. (piḻittal- pirital)	(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
“neñcē! iṉṟu aḻakiya illattil pūmpaṭukkiyil talivayōṭu iṉimiyākak kaḻittuviṭṭōm. nāḷi ivaḷi viṭuttup poruḷvayiṉ pirintu cellumpōtu, pāḻiṭattē ciṟṟūril ampalattil kaṟiyāṉ ēṟiya kūrikkaṭiyil meḻukāt tiṇṇiyil taṉimit tuyarāṉ uṟakkamum nīttut taṅkiyiruttaliyā nī virumpukiṟāy?” (akanā. 167) eṉṟu talivaṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟutal. 	(iḷa.)
iyaṟkip puṇarcci toṭaṅkip palakālum “piriyēṉ” eṉṟu teḷivittataṉit tappit talivaṉ piḻitta piriviṉkaṉ kūṟutal.	(146 nac.)
“neñcē! aṉpum maṭaṉum cāyalum iyalpum neñci nekiḻvikkum coṟkaḷumuṭiya taliviyōṭu oṉṟi avaḷit taḻuvi iṉṟu poḻutu kaḻitta nām, nāḷi tēkkumaraṅkaḷ ōṅkiya ariya kāṭṭu vaḻiyil ciṟiya ūrilē nam taliviyiṉ aṟivu kalaṅkiya pārviyiyum pulaviyi yum niṉittavāṟu taṉittut taṅkiyiruttal iyalumā?” (akanā.225) eṉṟu talivaṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟutal.	(nac.)
talivaṉ tāṉ avaṭ piḻitta paruvam uṇarttumiṭattuk kūṟal -
“kaḷavukkālattu oḻukiya oḻukkak kuṟipāṭṭāṉ nikaḻnta kuṟṟam teyvattāṉ āyiṟṟēṉum kuṟṟamēyaṉṟō?” eṉa uṭkoṇṭa talivikku, “yāṉ kātal mikutiyāl puṇarcci vēṇṭa, eṉ kuṟippiṟ kēṟpa oḻukiṉi ātaliṉ niṉakkoru kuṟṟamum iṉṟu” eṉṟu talivaṉ tāṉ piḻitta paruvam uṇarttumiṭattuk kūṟṟu nikaḻttutal.
“tōḻi! ‘naṉṉāḷ vēṇṭum’ eṉṉātu kūṭiya kūṭṭat tuḷ taṅki nāṇuc curuṅki vēṭki perukiya nammiṉum āṟṟārāyiṉār pōla nām kuṟittuḻi vantoḻukiya talivar, tām kuṟittaṉavē ceytaṉam nām eṉa namakkut tavaṟiṉmi kūṟiṉār” (paḻam pāṭal) eṉat talivi tōḻi yiṭam kūṟiya kūṟṟil, talivaṉ tāṉ avaṭ piḻittami kuṟittatic cuṭṭiyavāṟu.	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ talivikkut tavaṟu ceyta kālattum kūṟuvaṉ; itu muṉṉark kaḷavil kuṟipiḻittatit talivi kēṭka, atu tāṉ ceyta tavaṟē eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal.(194 kuḻa.)
talivaṉ, tīrāt tēṟṟattiṉkaṇ kūṟal -
tīrāt tēṟṟam iyaṟkippuṇarcciyiṉ piṉṉarum iṭan talippāṭṭiṉ piṉṉarum nikaḻvatām. iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarcciyuṭaṉ niṟivaṭiyāta teḷivu eṉṟu oru cārār poruḷ kūṟi, iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉkaṇ taliviyik kūṭi iṉpuṟṟa talivaṉ, “kaṇṭu kēṭṭu uṇṭu uyirttu uṟṟu aṟiyum aimpula iṉpaṅkaḷum ittaliviyiṭattu oru cērap peṟṟēṉ” (kuṟaḷ 1101) eṉṟu kūṟuvataṉoṭu niṟi vuṟāmal iṭantalippāṭṭu iṟutiyil, “ittaliviyiṉ tōḷkaḷ yāṉ virumpukiṟa ovvoru poruḷākavum iruntu eṉakku iṉpam tarukiṉṟaṉa” (kuṟaḷ. 1105) eṉṟāṟ pōla, mīṇṭum puṉinturittaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa maṉa nilikkaṇ kūṟuvataṉi ittuṟikku eṭuttuk kāṭṭākat taruvar.	(to. po. 99 iḷa.)
maṟṟoru cārār, tīrāt tēṟṟam eṉpataṟkut taliviyik kūṭiya talimakaṉ avaḷ maṉam teḷiyumāṟu avaḷi oru pōtum tāṉ piriyāmikku maliyiluḷḷa teyvaṅka ḷoṭu poruttic cūḷuṟutal eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, iḵtu iṭantalippāṭṭiṉ iṟutiyil nikaḻvatu eṉpa.	(102 nac.)
talivaṉ taliviyi maṇappataṟkuk kālam tāḻttiyavaḻit tōḻi avaṉi nōkki, “makiḻna! puṉkampū vīḻntu kiṭakkum veṇmaṇal, vēlaṉāṉ neṟpori citaṟappaṭṭa veṟi yāṭu kaḷatti nikarkkum emmūr āṟṟaṅkariyil aminta maṇalmēṭṭil taliviyiṉ kiyip paṟṟik koṇṭu teyvat taṉmi poruntiya aramakaḷir cāṉṟāka, ‘uṉṉi virivil maṇappēṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟiya cūḷuriyi nekiḻa viṭṭuk koṇṭiruppatāl, accūrara makaḷir niṉakkut tuṉpam viḷipparō eṉṟa niṉippu eṅkaḷukku varuttat tit tarukiṟatu” (kuṟun. 53) eṉṟu kūṟiya kūṟṟil, talivaṉ taliviyi orupōtum pirivatilli eṉṟu teyvatti muṉṉilic cāṉṟākak koṇṭu uṟutimoḻi kūṟiyami aṟiyappaṭum.	(nac.)
talivaṉ, tutāyc ceṉṟa pāṅkaṉukkuk kūṟiyatu -
“muṉpu taliviyik kūṭiyiruntapōtu peṟṟa iṉpam pirintirukkum ippōtu niṉippiṉum, ippoḻutum ataṉiyē peṟṟāṟ pōla makiḻvu peṟac ceyvatāl, kaḷḷiṉum kāmam iṉpam payappatu- kaḷ uṇṭālaṉṟi makiḻvikkātaṉṟō!” eṉṟu talivaṉ pāṅkaṉiṭam kūṟutal.		(kuṟaḷ 1201)

talivaṉ tūtiṟ pirital -
tutiṟ piriviṉit talivaṉ talivikkut tōḻi vāyilāka aṟivuṟuttalum, talivi pirivuṭaṉpaṭāmiyum, talivik kut talivaṉum pāṅkiyum appiriviṉi iṉṟiyami yāmi aṟivuṟutti uṭaṉpaṭuvittalum, talivi piriviṟku uṭaṉpaṭutalum, tūtiṟ pirintuḻit talivaṉ (muṉpaṉip) paruvam kaṇṭu varuntalum, avvāṟē apparuvam kaṇṭu varuntiya talimakaḷit tōḻi āṟṟuvittalum, ‘talivaṉ tūtiṟpirital’ eṉṉum tokutikkuriya kūṟṟām. pirivuṭaṉ paṭuttal, pirivuṭaṉpaṭutal, vaṉpoṟi (-talivi talivaṉ pirivi aritāka āṟṟiyiruttal), talivaṉ varuvaḻik kalaṅkal, vantuḻi (iruvartam) makiḻcci eṉpaṉavum ēṟṟa peṟṟi koḷḷappaṭum. ikkūṟṟukkaḷ ēṉiya kalvip pirivu, kāval pirivu, tuṇivayiṉ pirivu, poruḷvayiṉ pirivu eṉṉum nāṉkaṟkum poruntum. avvat talippi ṉuḷḷum aṭakkikkoḷka.	(na. a. 209;i. vi. 558 uri)
talivaṉ, ‘tūtu iṭiyiṭṭa vaki’yiṉkaṇ kūṟutal -
iruperu vēntar pōr ceyvatu kuṟittavaḻi, avviruvari yum cantu ceyvittal aracar antaṇar vaṇikar kuṟunila maṉṉar mutaliyōruṭiya kaṭami ātaliṉ, pakiyaracar iruvaric cantu ceyvittalēyaṉṟi, mīṇṭum pōr paṟṟiya eṇṇam nikaḻātavāṟu appakiyaracartam tuṇivar mutalāyiṉārip pirittal mutaliyaṉa karutit talivaṉ taliviyip pirintu cellak karutiyavaḻik kūṟutal.
(to. po. 41 nac.)
talivaṉ teyvattiṟam pēcal -
tammic cērttu vitta teyvattiṉ ceyalit talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“peṇṇē! nam iruvariyum kūṭṭivittatuūḻviṉi. teyvat taṉmi vāynta ikkātali yārālum citittal iyalātu!” (tañci. kō. 22) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘aruṭ kuṇam urittal’ eṉ ṉum (14). itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘vaṉpuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 129)
talivaṉ teyvatti makiḻtal -
taliviyik kaṇṭa talivaṉ avaḷ māṉuṭamakaḷē eṉpatu tuṇintu, piṉ avaḷ taṉṉiṭattuk kātal koṇ ṭuḷḷa kuṟippiyum avaḷ kaṇkaḷāl aṟintu, it taliviyit tāṉ kāṇa vitta teyvattiṉ tiruvaruḷi niṉittu makiḻtal.
itu ‘teyvam makiḻnturittal’ eṉavumpaṭum (i. vi. 491 uri). tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇ oru kūṟṟu iḵtu.	(kōvi. 6)
talivaṉ teyvap puṇarcci tuṇital -
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyaṉṟu tāṉ kaṇṭa taliviyuṭaṉ kūṭi makiḻat talivaṉ muṭivu ceytal 
itu kikkiḷiyiṉkaṇṇatoru nikaḻcci.	(i. vi. 491)
itaṉip ‘puṇarcci tuṇital’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.	(kōvi.  7)
talivaṉ teyvam tuṇiyākak koṇṭu āṟṟip peyartal -
taliviyi etirpārāta vakiyil aḷitta teyvam mīṇṭum avaḷik kāṇat tuṇi ceyyum eṉṟu eṇṇaṅ koṇṭu, avaḷ piriviṟku maṉattit tēṟṟik koṇṭu talivaṉ cēṟal.
teyvattiruvaruḷāl, etirpārā vakiyāl taṉiyaṉāy vanta talivaṉ, taṉiyaḷāyc cōliyil niṉṟa taliviyi etirppaṭṭu, avaḷ kuṟippaṟintu kūṭi avaḷit taṉ uyirākavē matittu, uyirip pirintu uṭal celvatu pōla avaḷippirintu pōtaṟku varunti, avaḷi aṉṟu kūṭṭuvitta teyvam toṭarntu kūṭṭuvikkum eṉṉum uṟutiyiyē tuṇiyākak koṇṭu maṉam tēṟi avaḷi viṭuttu nīṅkuvatu.
iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarcci eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(ta. ne. vi. pak. 11)
talivaṉ teyvam makiḻnturittal -
“eḻil vāynta taliviyit tāṉ kāṇa nērntami teyvac ceyalē” eṉṟu talivaṉ niṉittu makiḻtal. iyaṟkip puṇarcci muṉ nikaḻvatu itu.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘teyvatti makiḻtal’ eṉṉum (6).
itu kikkiḷik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(i. vi. 491)
talivaṉ teḷivu akappaṭuttuk kūṟal -
talivaṉ ‘muṉṉiliyākkal’ mutaliyaṉa kūṟi iyaṟkip puṇarcciyi viḻintu niṟkum niliyil, talimakaḷ paṇpiṉit tāṉ aṟintu appaṇpiṉit taṉ uḷḷattuk koṇṭu taliviyiṉ maṉaniliyit teḷivāka aṟital.
“niṉtāyum eṉtāyum muṉṉar aṟiyātavarkaḷ; niṉ tanti yum eṉ tantiyum itaṟkumuṉ uṟaviṉar allar; nīyum nāṉum itaṟku muṉ oruvari oruvar aṟiyōm. otta aṉpuṭiya iruvēm neñcamum, cemmaṇ nilattil peyta maḻiyiṉ nīril nīriṉ taṉmiyum maṇṇiṉ cemmi yum pirikka muṭiyātavāṟu pōla iruvar neñcamum kalantu viṭṭaṉa” (kuṟun. 40) eṉṟāṟ pōla iruvar uḷḷamum otta paṇpiṉit talivaṉ kūṟal. iḵtu oru karuttu.			(to. po. 98 iḷa.)
iṉi ēṉiya karuttu:
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ piṉ talivaṉ taliviyiṭattē, “niṉṉiṟ piriyēṉ; piriyiṉ āṟṟēṉ;piriyiṉ aṟaṉallatu ceyvēṉāvēṉ” eṉṟu avaḷ maṉattil avaṉ eṉṟum taṉṉip piriyāṉ eṉṟa teḷivuṇṭāmāṟu kūṟal.
“melliyal makaḷē! niṉ nalla neñcam taṉimiyāl varunta niṉṉip pirintu ceṉṟu, ceṉṟa iṭattē maṉam poruntiyiruppēṉāyiṉ, eṉṉi nāṭip palanāḷkaḷ iravalar vārātoḻika! iravalarkku īyum vāyppiṉi yāṉ iḻappēṉ ākuka!” (kuṟun. 137) eṉṟāṟ pōlat taliviyiṭam taṉ piriyāmi paṟṟik kūṟi avaḷit teḷivu peṟac ceytal.	(to. po. 101 nac.)
talivaṉ tōḻaṟku ākkam ceppal -
ākkam ceppal - taṉ neñcil varuttam mikukiṉṟapaṭiyip piṟarkku urittal. varuttamāvaṉa uṟaṅkāmi mutalāyiṉa.
“talivi eṉ uḷḷattik kippaṟṟik koṇṭataṉāṉ toṇṭi eṉṉum ūriṉ kaṭal tuṟiyiluḷḷa ali kaḷip pōla yāṉum iravilum uṟakkattit tuṟantu viṭṭēṉ” (aiṅ. 172) eṉṟu taṉ melivu kuṟittu viṉaviya tōḻaṉukkut talivaṉ taṉakku nērnta vēṟupāṭṭi veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟiyatu.	(to. po. 97 iḷa.)
talivaṉ tōḻi aṉpuṟṟu nakkavaḻik kūṟal -
tōḻi taṉṉi irantu kuṟiyuṟṟa talivaṉi aṉpu tōṉṟa nōkkik kuṟi maṟuttu nakkavaḻit talivaṉ kūṟutal
“kuṟumakaḷ! ciṟitum uṟavu maṉappāṉmiyiṉṟi uyirkaḷip pāmpu kaṭittu varuttuvatu pōla, nī eṉṉi nakittu varuttuvatu takkataṉṟu. taliviyatu ampu pōṉṟa pārvi yil tuṉpuṟuttappaṭṭa eṉ neñcam āṟutali uṟumpaṭi cila kūṟātu eṉṉi nakitturippatu eṉakkuk kalakkam tarukiṉṟatu” (naṟ. 75) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 103 nac.)
talivaṉ, tōḻi iṟceṟippu aṟivuṟuppa varuntik kūṟal -
taliviyit tamar illatti viṭṭu veḷiyē vārātu kāval ceytuviṭṭa ceytiyit tōḻi kūṟat talivaṉ varuntik kūṟutal-
“maṉamē! talivi iṟceṟikkappaṭṭuviṭṭāḷ.  iṉi avaḷik kāṇpatu aritu. avaḷ pirivu nammāl tāṅka iyalātatu. nām talivipāl koṇṭa naṭpiṉiyuṭiya ikkāmanōy maṟumi yulakattum nammiṭam toṭarntiruk kum. ātaliṉ taliviyi ivvulakil iṉip peṟutal iyalātu pōyiṉum maṟumiyulakil nām tavaṟātu peṟṟu makiḻalām. ātaliṉ varuntaṟka” (kuṟun. 199) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
iḵtu iccūttirattil ‘parivuṟṟu meliyiṉum’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.	(to. po. 103 nac.)
talivaṉ, tōḻi cēṭpaṭuttatu poṟātu avaḷiṭam maṭalmāk kūṟal -
tōḻi talivaṉ kuṟiyi niṟivēṟṟutaṟkaṇ kālatāmatam ceytataṉit tāṅkāmal, talivaṉ, tāṉ maṭalēṟiyāvatu taliviyi eytat tuṇinta tiṟatti avaḷiṭam eṭuttuk kūṟal.
“curapuṉṉimalar maṇam vīcum kūntaliyuṭiya talivi taṉ mārpiṉi yāṉ taḻuvumāṟu koṭukkum vari, elumpi māliyākac cūṭi maṭalmāmēl ēṟi, numteruvil nāḷiyē varuvatāka muṭivu ceytuviṭṭēṉ! iṉi eṉakku vēṟu muṭivu illi”  (tiṇimāli. 16) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ, tōḻi cēṭpaṭuttatu poṟātu neñciṭam maṭalmāk kūṟal -
tōḻi taṉṉuṭiya kuṟiyik kēṭṭut taliviyit taṉṉoṭu kūṭṭuvikkak kālam peritum pāṇittami kaṇṭu, talivaṉ taṉ neñciṭam taliviyi eytum vaḻi maṭalēṟutal aṉṟip piṟitoṉṟum iṉṟu eṉṟu kūṟutal.
“neñcē! talivi nammāṭṭu neñcam nekiḻntilaḷ. paṉimaṭalāl ceyta kutirikku maṇikaḷ aṇintu periya māliyippūṭṭi, nāṉ veḷḷiya eṉpumāli pūṇṭu, piṟar ikaḻumāṟu ammaṭal māmēl ēṟi, nāṇatti viṭṭu ivaḷ teruvaḻiyē varuvatutāṉ ivaḷukkut tirumaṇam kuṟittu nām viṭukiṟa tūtākum!” (kuṟun. 182) eṉṟal pōlat talivaṉ tāṉ maṭalēṟum karuttiṉi neñciṭam kūṟutal.
(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ, ‘tōḻi nīkkaliṉ ākiya nilimik’kaṇ kūṟal -
tōḻiyāṉ iravukkuṟi maṟukkappaṭṭa talivaṉ mīṇṭum kaḷavuk kūṭṭatti virumpiya taṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟutal.
iravukkuṟi maṟukkappaṭṭa talivaṉ piṉṉum kaḷaviṉāṉ taliviyik kūṭutaṟku avāviya neñciṉi nōkki, “neñcē! nalla ceytikaḷi nīkkip payaṉaṟṟa kaṟpaṉikaḷi mēṟkoṇṭu, maḻinīri ēṟṟa cuṭappaṭāta maṭkalam pōla, poṟukka muṭiyāta āciveḷḷattil nīntip pērācippaṭukiṟāy. tāyit taḻuvum kuraṅkuk kuṭṭi pōla niṉ viruppiṉi ēṟṟukkoṇṭu niṟivēṟṟuvār uḷarāyiṉ niṉ āci takkatē; āyiṉ, aḵtu illiyē!” (kuṟun. 29) eṉṟu kūṟutal.
tōḻi taṉkuṟiyi maṟukkāmal uṭaṉpaṭum vakiyil potuvākat talivaṉ, “ittalivikku nāṉ pāvi ceytu koṭuttuḷḷēṉ; ivaḷ mārpil toyyil eḻuti aṇi ceytuḷḷēṉ. ivaṟṟi yellām ivaḷik kāppatākac colpavarkaḷ aṟintatākat teriyavilli. ivaṟṟic cuṭṭi aracaviyil nīti vēṇṭi nāṉ taliviyi viṉaviṉ avaḷ nili yātām? iviyellām niṉiyā mal makkaḷ iruttal aṟiyāmiyākum. innili iraṅkat takkatu” (kuṟun. 276) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟuvaṉavum koḷḷap paṭum.
“tōḻi! cōlimalarkaḷiṉ maṇam kamaḻum talivi tōḷkaḷi viṭuttuc cellumāṟu kūṟukiṟāy. ataṉāl eṉṉuyir nīṅkiviṭum. ivvāṟu nī collutal niṉakkup perumiyāmō eṉpataṉic colluti” (ciṟṟaṭṭakam) eṉṟāṟ pōl kūṟutalum uṇṭu. 	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ tōḻiyiṟ puṇarccikkaṇ taṉnili koḷīik kūṟiyatu -
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattil talivaṉ taliviyiṭam taṉ neñciṟ kiṭanta varuttattiyum elliyaṟṟa viruppattiyum eṭutturittu viḷakkiyami.
“koṭicci! murukaṉoṭu vaḷḷi piṉ toṭarntu ceṉṟami pōla, nī em ciṟukuṭikku eṉṉoṭu puṟappaṭṭu varukiṉ ṟāyā!”(naṟ. 82) eṉṟal pōla uḷḷam melintu talivaṉ kūṟutal. 
itu ‘parivuṟṟu meliyiṉum’eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. 	(to. po. 103 nac.)
talivaṉ ‘tōṉṟal cāṉṟa māṟṟōr mēṉmi’ kūṟip piritaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
tāṉi yāṉi kutiri eṉṟa paṭimikuti, viṉivali taṉvali tuṇivali eṉṟa ivaṟṟiṉ mikuti, iyaṟkiyum ceyaṟkiyumākiya araṇvalimi - ivaṟṟāṉ taṉ pakivar taṉṉiṉ mikka pukaḻiṉar āyiṉār eṉṟu kūṟakkēṭṭa aracaṉākiya talivaṉ aḻukkāṟu koṇṭu, pakivar taṉakku  vaḻimoḻintu oḻukumāṟu avari aṭakkutaṟkup pakivayiṉ piriyumiṭattē taliviyiṭam kūṟutal. 			(to. po. 44 iḷa., 41nac.)
talivaṉ ‘naki naṉi uṟāatu annili aṟital’ -
talivi makiḻcci peritum eytāmal, puṇarccikku iṉa māṉa pirivunili kūṟi, avaḷ piriviṉi āṟṟum taṉmi yit talivaṉ aṟital. iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarccikkup piṉ nikaḻvatu.
“āṇum peṇṇumākap piriyātu uṭaṉuṟikiṉṟa makaṉṟil eṉṟa paṟaviyiṉ kūṭṭam pōla, oruvari oruvar pirital aṟiyāta immikka kāmaniliyil kūṭṭattiṟku iṭiyū ṟākap pūppu nikaḻntu taṉittirukkum munnāḷ elliyum ōr āṇṭukkālam pōla namakkut tōṉṟukiṟatu. muṟpiṟappil nām iruvarum kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyāka iruntōm. ip piṟappil nām vevvēṟāy uḷḷa attuṉpattip pōkkiyatu namkāmam. nām itupōtu mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya ciṟupiriviyum, niṉi yāṉ virivil varintu koḷḷappōvatāl ikkāmam nīkkiviṭum. attakiya kuṟiyāk kāmattoṭu nam iruvarukkum uyir cēra nīṅkuva tākuka!” (kuṟun. 57) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu. 			(to. po. 101nac.)
iṉi, talivaṉ taliviyiṉ aḻakiṉip puṉinturitta vaḻit taliviyiṉ uḷḷattu makiḻcci muṟuval kuṟippāṉ puṟattē tōṉṟa, ataṉi avaṉ aṟintu urittal eṉṟum poruḷ kūṟupa. 	(98 iḷa.)
talivaṉ taṉ neñciṭam,“maṉamē!ciṟanta aṇikalaṉkaḷi yum maṇam kamaḻum kūntaliyum uṭiyaḷāy, immali yakattē uṟiyum aramakaḷip pōṉṟu kāṭcitanta it talivi nammiṭam aṉpiṉṟip potunōkku nōkkukiṟāḷ eṉṟu kavalippaṭṭāyē!ippōtu ivaḷ muṟuvaṟ kuṟippi aṟintukoḷ!” eṉṟu kūṟutal. iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarccikku muṉ nikaḻntatu eṉpa.	(iḷa.)
talivaṉ, ‘naṉṉeṟip paṭarum tolnalap poruḷil’ kūṟiyatu -
naṉṉeṟiyāvatu aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vaḻuvātaneṟi. talivaṉ ciṟapput toṉṟutoṭṭu varutaliṉ kuṭinalat tit ‘toṉṉalap poruḷ’ eṉṟār. itaṉāṟ col liyatu, aṟam poruḷ iṉpaṅkaḷi vaḻāmal taṉ kulattiṟ kēṟṟa maṉivāḻkkiyit talimakaḷ naṭattutaṟkaṇ talimakaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum eṉṟavāṟu.
“talivi camiyal ceytu vittu eṉṉiṭamuḷḷa pulavi yāl camiyalaṟiyilēyē uḷḷāḷ. ippōtu viruntiṉar yārēṉum variṉ, ivaḷ pulavi nīṅkip puṉmuṟuval pūkkum mukattiṉi nām kāṇalām!” (naṟ. 120) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟiṉkaṇ, ūṭaṟkuṟippiṉaḷ ākiya talivi, maṉi vāḻkki aṟamākiya viruntu puṟantarutal viruppiṉaḷ ātaliṉ ‘naṉṉeṟip paṭartal’ kūṟappaṭṭatu. 			(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
illattiṟku ōtiya neṟiyiṉkaṇ talivi kallāmal pākampaṭa oḻukum toṉṉalañ cāṉṟa poruḷiṉkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum. poruḷ varuvāy illāta kālamum illaṟam nikaḻttutal iyalpāy iruttaṟkut ‘toṉṉalam’ eṉṟār.
“orukuṭam nīrāl cōṟamittu uṇṇumāṟu miṭippaṭṭa kālattum maṉikku māṭciyuṭiyāḷ kaṭalnīri vaṟṟa uṇṇum kēḷir variṉum  illaṟam nikaḻttutali neṟi yākak koḷvāḷ” (nālaṭi. 382.) eṉṟu talivaṉ viyantu kūṟutal	(146 nac.)
talivaṉ ‘nāṭṭam iraṇṭum aṟivu uṭampaṭuttaṟkuk kūṭṭiyurikkum kuṟippuri’ yātalik kūṟal -
nāṭṭam iraṇṭum- talimakaṉ kaṇkaḷum, talivi kaṇ kaḷum;
aṟivu uṭampaṭuttal - oruvar vēṭkipōla iruvar vēṭkiyum ottiruttal;
kūṭṭi urikkum kuṟippuri ātal- tam vēṭkiyoṭu kūṭṭi oruvar oruvarukkuk kāmakkuṟippiṉip pulappaṭuttal.
tāṉ taṉ vēṭkiyit taṉ kaṇkaḷāṉ pulappaṭutta, avaḷum otta vēṭkiyit taṉ kaṇkaḷāṉ pulappaṭut tiyavaḻi, avaḷ uḷḷak kuṟippi aṟinta talivaṉ makiḻntu,
“neytaṟpūkkaḷiyuṭiya uppaṅkaḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu taṉ tamiyaṉmārpiṭittuvanta mīṉkaḷ ularttappaṭṭavaṟṟip paṟavikaḷ kavarātavāṟu kāvalāka vikkappaṭṭa it taliviyiṉ pārvikaḷ mīṉvaṟṟalip pātukāvāviṭiṉum eṉṉiyē pātukākkiṉṟaṉa!” (tiṇimāli. 32) eṉṟu kūṟutal.	(to. po. 93 iḷa.)
talivaṉ, ‘nāṇu neñcu alippa viṭuttaṟkaṇ’ kūṟal -
nāṇam taṉ neñciṉi varuttat tam kaḷavoḻukkam ūravar aṟiya ākum eṉṟu kalaṅkum taliviyi nōkkit talivaṉ “ūrār aṟiyum kuvi nallatu aṉṟō? ataṉik kuṟṟamākak koḷḷātu peṟātu peṟṟa nīrmit tāka nallateṉṟu koḷ. ūravar alar tuṟṟuvatik kēṭṭāl num tamar virivil nam maṇattiṟku uṭaṉpaṭuvar” eṉṟa kūṟṟu. (kuṟaḷ 1143)	(to. po. 105 iḷa.)
itaṉit talivi kūṟṟu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. talivikku iṉṟiyamiyāta nāṇuttāṉ avaḷ neñciṉi alittaliṉ avaḷ annāṇiṉik kiviṭutaṟkaṇ kūṟṟu nikaḻttuvāḷ. aḵtu uṭaṉpōkkiṉum varivu kaṭāvum vaḻiyum vēṭki mītūrntu nāṇ tuṟantu urittal pōlvaṉa.	(to. po. 107. nac.)
“nāṇam iraṅkat takkatu. nammoṭu pallāṇṭukaḷ uṭaṉ uṟintu tuṉpuṟṟuḷḷatu. ippoḻutu karumpup pāttiyiṉ maṇaṟkari pāttikkut taṇṇīr virintu varutalāṉ karintu aḻivatu pōlak kāmam tākkutalāṉ nammiṭatti viṭṭu nīṅkukiṟatu!” (kuṟun. 149) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(nac.)
talivaṉ nāṇu vari iṟattaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
kaḷavuk kālattil vēṭki mikutiyāṉ talivaṉ nāṇattiṉ elliyik kaṭantu taṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟutal.			(to. po. 100 nac.)
“neñcē! oṉṟu kāmatti viṭṭuviṭu; iṉṟēl, tavaṟāṉa ceyalkaḷ ceyyumvaḻi veṭkuṟum nāṇatti viṭṭuviṭu. iru vēṟupaṭṭa paṇpukaḷākiya ivviraṇṭaṉiyum yāṉ oru cērat tāṅkum āṟṟal uṭiyēṉ allēṉ’ (kuṟaḷ 1247) eṉat talivaṉ kūṟutal.	(97 iḷa.)
talivaṉ, ‘nāmak kālattu uṇṭeṉat tōḻi, ēḻuṟu kaṭavuḷ ēttiya maruṅkiṉ’ kūṟiyatu -
accakkālattu namakkut tuṇi āyiṟṟeṉat tōḻi ēmuṟu kaṭavuḷi ēttutaṟkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum.			(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
tōḻi, “iṉṉatu viḷiyum eṉṟu aṟiyātu añcutali yuṭiya kaḷavuk kālattē yāṉum taliviyum varuntātirut taṟkuk kāraṇamāya toru kaṭavuḷ uṇṭu” eṉak kūṟi ataṉip peritum ēttiyiṭattu, talivaṉ, “vatuvi kāṟum ētamiṉṟākak kātta teyvam iṉṟum kākkum” eṉṟu ēttutal.	(146 nac.)
virivil maṇam vāykka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kaḷavuk kālattut tōḻiyum taliviyum kuraviyāṭi vaḻipaṭṭa teyvattiṉi (kali. 39),tōḻi kaṟpuk kālattup paravukkaṭaṉ koṭuttaṟku ēttiyavaḻit talivaṉ,
“tāṉ cēmittu vittirunta tēṉi vanta viruntiṉarkku  utavum nāṭaṉ niṉ tanti ātaliṉ, niṉ nōykku iyaṟṟiya veṟi, eṉakkup payaṉ koṭukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu tōḻi vaḻipaṭṭataṟku ēṟpa, niṉṉi yāṉ maṇattaṟku utaviyatu” eṉṟu taliviyiṭam kūṟiyatu. 	(nac.)
talivaṉ, ‘niṟpavi niṉii uri’ttal -
talivaṉ, kāmanukarcci oṉṟiyē niṉikkāmal, talivi yi maṇantu koṇṭu avaḷālēyē taṉakku illaṟam iṉitu naṭattal vēṇṭum eṉṉum uṟuti maṉattuk koṇṭu kūṟutal; kāma nukarcci koṇṭu kalaṅkum maṉattik kaḻaṟi yurittal.
iṭantalippāṭum pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭamum nikaḻntapiṉ talivaṉ taṉ maṉatti nōkki, “taliviyik kaṇṭa aḷavil kalaṅki niṟkiṟāyē! iṅṅaṉam vitivayattāṉ kaṇṭa taliviyik kaṇṭu kalakka muṟutalum ulakiyal eṉṉum eṇṇattāṉ nāṉ niṉ kalakkattip poruṭ paṭuttavilli. nī katiravaṉ oḷi maḻuṅkiṉum, matiyam vemmiyoṭu tiriyiṉum, ticikaḷē naṭuṅkiṉum, neṟi muṟiyiṉiṉṟum tavaṟāta nam kulattiṟkuriya kōṭpāṭ ṭoṭu nērmivaḻiyil niṟkum nāṭṭam uṭiyāy. kāma nukarcci oṉṟaṉiyum niṉiyātu, ivaḷāṉē uṉakku illaṟam naṭattal vēṇṭum eṉpataṉiyum uṭ koṇṭu, virivil ivaḷi maṇattaṟku āvaṉa ceyvāy” eṉṟāṟ pōlat taṉ neñciṉik kaḻaṟik kūṟutal.			(to. po. 99 iḷa.)
pēti orutti poruṭṭāka maṉam kātalvayappaṭṭa talivaṉi nōkkip pāṅkaṉ ulakattu niliniṟkiṉṟa naṟkuṇaṅkaḷi niṉippittu kaḻaṟik kūṟutal.	(102 nac.)
“appētiyik kaṇṭa aḷavil uḷḷam kalaṅkukiṟāy! ñāyiṟu taṉ veṅkatir maḻuṅkiṉum, tiṅkaḷ tīrāvemmi yoṭu tiriyiṉum, tici naṭuṅkuṟiṉum, peyarāta taṉmit tām cīrcāṉṟa naṟkulattil tōṉṟi ataṟkēṟṟa tiriyāk koḷkiyum nalattiṉiṉṟu tiriyāta nalla eṇṇamum uṭiya nīyē, avaḷik kaṇṭa aḷavil kalaṅkuvāy eṉil, ivvulakam taṉatu nilitiriyumaṉ!” eṉṟa pāṅkaṉatu kaḻaṟṟuri.	(nac.)
talivaṉ ‘niṉṟu naṉi piriviṉ añciya piyuḷiṉkaṇ kūṟal -
nili niṟka mikap piriyum piriviṉkaṇ añciya nōyiṉkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“maṉamē!vēṉiṟ kālattē, nīṇṭa mūṅkil uyarnta paranta veḷiyi aritiṉ kaṭantu ceṉṟu poruḷ tēṭuvativiṭa, taliviyik kūṭi illaṟam naṭattutalē ciṟantatu eṉpa taṉi niṉippāyāka” (aka nā.51) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
muṉ nillātu oru ciṟi pōy niṉṟu nīṭṭittup pirivi ṉāl talivaṉ añciya nōyiṉkaṇ kūṟutal.	(146 nac.)
“matiluḷ iruppavar kaḷvaṉik kāṇāmalēyē avaṉāl varum tuṉpattik karutik ‘kaḷvaṉik kaṇṭōm’ eṉpa varip pōla, muṉ nillātu oru pakkam pōyniṉṟu yāṉ ceyyāta tavaṟukaḷi eṉmēl ēṟṟik kūṟātē;niṉ āṇivaḻi naṭappēṉē aṉṟi, niṉ āṇiyi nāṉ kaṭappatuṇṭō?” (kali. 81) eṉṟu talivaṉ taliviyiṭam kūṟutal.	(nac.)
talivaṉ, “niṉṉāl collappaṭṭāḷi aṟiyēṉ” eṉṟa tōḻikku avaḷ aṟiya urittatu -
“maṭavāy!yāṉ kūṟuval, kēḷ. eṉ talivi ciṟitu naṭantu celliṉum avaḷ nuṇṇiṭi oṭintu viṭumō eṉṟu añci, eṉ uḷḷam avaḷ alaṭciyam ceytālum poruṭpaṭuttātu, avaḷ taḷarntu naṭakkumtōṟum piṉ ceṉṟu taḷartaluṟum. attakiya melliya iṭiyiṉaḷ eṉ talivi” (tiṇi māli. 17) eṉṟa talivaṉ tōḻikkut talivi paṟṟiya aṭiyāḷam kūṟutal.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘taṇṭātu (tavirātu) irappiṉum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ vēṟupaṭa varum kūṟṟukkaḷākat taḻuvik koṇṭavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ niṉintami ceppal -
kaḷavuk kālattē poruḷvayiṉ pirintu ceṉṟu mīṇṭa talivaṉ, tōḻiyiṭam, tāṉ orukaṇamum taliviyi niṉiyāmal iruntatilli eṉṟu, avaḷ “niṉintīrō?” eṉṟu viṉaviya viṉāviṟku maṟumoḻi kūṟutal.
taliviyip pirintu ceṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷilellām nuṅkaḷ iḷamarakkāvum, ceykuṉṟum, pāviyum, uṅkaḷ vaṭivum, naṟumaṇam kamaḻum cōliyum, viḷiyāṭṭiṭamumē eṉ kaṇmuṉ uruveḷiyākak kāṭci vaḻaṅkiṉa” (ampikā. 325) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 170)
talivaṉ nīṅkal vēṇṭal -
kaḷavoḻukkam toṭarntu nikaḻuṅkāli, iṭiyē talivaṉ taṉ ūrukkuc cellutali aṟivittapōtu, pāṅki atit taṭutturikkiyil, tāṉ celvatu mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatākiṟatu eṉṟum, virivil mīṇṭu viṭuvatākavum kūṟit tōḻiyiṭam talivaṉ vaṟpuṟutti icivu vēṇṭutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘oruvaḻit taṇattal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 168)
talivaṉ, nīṅkiyavaḻip piṟanta varuttam kūṟutal -
talivaṉ taliviyip pirintavaḻi avaḷpirivāṉ taṉakku viḷinta varuttattit talivaṉ taṉṉuḷ kūṟik koḷ ḷutal.
“ulakil uḷḷa tī taṉṉi neruṅkiṉ neruṅkiyavarkaḷic cuṭum; vilakiṉ avveppam avarkaḷit tākkātu. āyiṉ, ittaliviyiṭattuḷḷa tīyō, avaḷi viṭṭuvilakiṉ cuṭṭerikkiṟatu; neruṅkiṉ taṇṇeṉṟu uṭaluḷḷaṅ kaḷik kuḷirvikkiṟatu. ittakiya putu āṟṟal uṭiya tīyiṉi ivaḷ evviṭattiṉiṉṟu peṟṟāḷ?” (kuṟaḷ 1104) eṉṟal pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
iccūttirattuḷ ‘colliya (nukarcci)’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, taḻuvik koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu itu.			(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ nīṭat talivi varuntal -
kaḷavu eṉṉum kikōḷ nikaḻumiṭattu maṟunāḷē varuva tākak kūṟic ceṉṟa talivaṉ vārāmal tāmatam ceyyavē, talivi varuntutal.
“nēṟṟu ñāyiṟu maṟiyumuṉ ceṉṟa eṉ talivar iṉṟu eṉ āyattār eṉṉit tēṭikkoṇṭu ivviṭam varuvataṟku muṉ vārāviṭil yāṉ eṉ ceyvēṉ?” (tañci. kō. 144) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘oru cār pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 154)
talivaṉ nīṭu niṉintu iraṅkaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
talivi nāṇattāṉ taṉ kaṇkaḷip pottikkoḷḷu tal, oru koṭiyiyō kompiyō  cārntu niṟṟal mutaliya ceyalkaḷic ceytavaḻi, avaṟṟāṉ puṇarcci nikaḻātu kālam vīṇē kaḻitaṟku varuntit taṉ varuttam pulappaṭumāṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“eṉ coṟkaḷukkellām maṟumoḻi etuvum tārātu nī mukam kaviḻttu nāṇikkoṇṭiruppatāl eṉ kāmam elli kaṭantu perukukiṟatu. ataṉit taṭuttu ellik kaṇ niṟuttal eḷiya ceyalaṉṟu” (naṟ. 39) eṉṟāṟ pōlat taṉvaruttak kuṟippiṉit talivaṉ taliviyiṭam veḷip paṭuttal.
iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarccikku muṉ nikaḻvatu eṉavum, iṭantalip pāṭṭiṟku muṉnikaḻvatu eṉavum iru tiṟattuk karuttum uḷa.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ, “nīvīr ēvuvaṉa yāṉ ceyvēṉ” eṉal -
taliviyum tōḻiyum oruṅkēyiruntaviṭatti aṭinta talivaṉ, “cevvalariyiyum neytaliyum iṇittut talimāliyākac cūṭiya paratavar makaḷē! orukāl niṉṉit taḻuvum vāyppu eṉakku ēṟpaṭumāyiṉ, niṉ tamiyaṉmāroṭu valikaḷikkoṇṭu kaṭalilmīṉ piṭittu, tāḻimaraṅkaḷi vēliyākak koṇṭa ivvūril koṇarntu cērppēṉ” eṉṟal pōlvaṉa kūṟutal.
iccūttirattuḷ ‘taṇṭātu - (tavirāmal) irappiṉum’ eṉṟatāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu itu.			(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ neñcoṭu viṉātal -
talivaṉ taliviyip paṟṟi niṉintu taṉ maṉattiṟkuk kūṟi viṉavutal.
“talivi, ‘nam naṭpu nīṭikkumā? avar  āyattiṭi eṉṉit tēṭuvārā? vantu ceṉṟu viṭuvārā? anta vāyppu iṉṉum kūṭumā? avvāyppi viti kūṭṭumā?’ eṉṟu eṇṇit taṉ uḷḷam vāṭuvāḷō? aṟiyēṉ” eṉṟu talivaṉ taṉ neñcattiṭam uriyāṭutal. 	(ampikā. 26)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘iṭantalippāṭu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu, ‘tanta teyvam tarumeṉac cēṟal’ eṟum kūṟṟiṉi aṭuttu uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.			(i. vi. 503 uri)
talivaṉ (iṟiyōṉ) neṟiyiṉatu eḷimi kūṟal -
iravil accamum tuṉpamum viḷivikkum vaḻiyil varutal takātu eṉṟu kūṟum tōḻikkut talivaṉ taṉakku avvaḻi varutal eḷitē eṉak kūṟutal.
“tōḻī! eṉ uyirukkum uyirāṉa taliviyiṉ muyakkiṉip peṟa virumpum eṉakku, ippuli ciṅkam pēy aintali nākam pōṉṟa yāvumē accam viḷikkamāṭṭā” (tañci.kō. 165) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 158)
talivaṉ ‘nōṉmiyum perumiyum meykoḷa aruḷi(ya) paṉṉal cāṉṟa vāyiloṭu porunti, taṉṉiṉ ākiya takutikkaṇ’ kūṟiyatu -
poṟimiyum perumiyum meyyeṉak koḷḷumāṟu aruḷi ārāytal aminta vāyiloṭu poruntit taṉṉāṉ ākiya takutikkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum.
talivaṉ taṉ tavaṟṟip poṟuttal vēṇṭum eṉṟavaḻit talivi, “ciṟumi ceytal kuṟṟam” eṉak kūṟavē, tōḻi, “nī eṉ ceytaṉi? ivaḷ vekuṭaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉi?” eṉṟavaḻit talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“kāma nukarccikkuriyār iruvar āyiṉār yāvariṉum yām mikka kātaliyuṭiyēm’ eṉpatu karuti ‘yāriṉum kātalam’ eṉṟēṉāka, niṉ tōḻi atu karutātu, ‘eṉṉāṟ kātalik kappaṭṭa makaḷir palaruḷḷum niṉkaṇ mikukātaluṭiyēṉ’ eṉṟēṉākak karuti, ‘am makaḷir yāryāriṉum eṉkaṇ kātaluṭiyīrāyiṉīr?’ eṉṟu collip pulantāṉ”			(kuṟaḷ 1314)
“kātal mikutiyāṉ ‘immiyākiya piṟappiṉkaṇ yām piriyēm’ eṉṟu coṉṉēṉāka, ataṉāl ēṉi maṟumi yākiya piṟappiṉkaṇ pirivēṉ eṉṉum kuṟippuṭiyē ṉākak karuti, avaḷ taṉ kaṇ niṟinta nīriṉik koṇ ṭāḷ”	(kuṟaḷ 1315)
(pulavi nuṇukkam eṉṉum atikārattu ēṉiya 3, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10ām kuṟaḷkaḷum aṉṉa)	(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
talivaṉāṉ uḷatākiya poṟiyiyum kalvi mutaliya perumiyiyum uṭiya makavit talivi taṉ vayiṟṟakattē koḷkiyiṉāṉē, vētatti ārāytalaminta antaṇa roṭu kūṭiyiruntu ammakaviṟkuc ceyyattakum caṭaṅku kaḷic ceyta takutippāṭṭiṉkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟutal.
taṉṉiṉ ākiya mey - karuppam. avippali koḷḷum aṅkiyaṅ kaṭavuḷukkum atu koṭukkum talivaṟkum iṭiyē niṉṟu koṭuppittaliṉ antaṇari ‘vāyil’ eṉṟār.
“muṟkālattil nām kēṭpa namakkuk kūṟiya muṉivar col uṟutiyākumpaṭi, talivarum cuṟṟattārum nōṟṟataṉ payaṉāka niṉ vayiṟṟil karuppam taṅkiyatu kaṇṭu ataṟ kēṟpac caṭaṅku ceytum eṉṟār” eṉpatu tōḻi kūṟṟu. (talivaṉ karuttum itaṉkaṇ oruvāṟu aṭaṅkum)(nac. 146)
talivi karuvuṟṟatu kaṇṭu makiḻnta talivaṉ aṟivarik kalantu taṉ takutikkēṟpac cuṟṟattār naṇpar mutalāyi ṉārkku viruntu ceytal.	(po. 194 kuḻa.)
talivaṉ pakaṟkuṟiyil parivuṟṟuk kūṟal - 
pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ cēṭpaṭutta tōḻiyi nōkkit talivaṉ varuntik kūṟal.
“orupōtum nīṅkāta kāmanōyiṉāṉē eṉṉi varuttum talivi paṟṟiya niṉippik koṇṭu kalaṅkik kāmam mikukiyiṉāṉē yāṉ ceyalaṟṟu varuntum tuṉpam kaṇṭum, nī eṉakku aruḷavilli yāyiṉ, kollimali yil teyvam eḻutiya pāviyip pōṉṟa taliviyāṉ yāṉ kollappaṭum nili eṉakku niṉṉāṉ vantataṉṟu,  eṉ ūḻviṉiyāṉ vantatu eṉṟu ataṉi nōvēṉ” (naṟ. 185) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 103 nac.)
talivaṉ tuyarukku varuntit tōḻi, “avaṉtuyar tīrkkat talivi uṭaṉpaṭuvatāka illiyē” eṉṟāṟ pōla varuntiyavaḻi, talivaṉ avaḷi nōkkik kūṟutal.
“maṭalēṟi māliyil yāṉ varuntum tuyarit talivi eṉakku aḷittuḷḷāḷ. ataṟku nī ēṉ vīṇāka varuntu kiṟāy?” (kuṟaḷ. 1135) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟutal.	(100 iḷa.)
talivaṉ ‘payaṅkeḻu tuṇiaṇi pullip pullātu, uyaṅkuvaḷ kiṭanta kiḻattiyik kuṟukip, pulku eṉa muṉṉiya niṟi aḻi poḻutiṉ, melleṉ cīṟaṭi pulliya iraviṉ’ (irattaliṉ) kaṇ kūṟiyatu -
taṉatu āṟṟāmi mikutiyāl taḻuvi āṟṟutaṟkuk kuḷirnta payaṉ koṭuttal poruntiya pala aṇikaḷit taḻuvit taṉṉip pullutal peṟātē varuntik kiṭanta taliviyi aṇukit tāṉ kūṭutalik karutiṉa niṟi aḻinta kālattē, avaḷatu metteṉṟa ciṟiya aṭiyit tīṇṭiya iratta liṉkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟiyatu.
makap peṟutaṟku muṉ attuṇi āṟṟāmi eytiṟṟilaṉ, ippirivu kāraṇattāṉ talivaṉum niṟiaḻivāṉ eṉṟār.
“nī evvaḷavu periya kuṟikaḷic ceytālum ikkuṟi kaḷi nī ceykiṉṟāy eṉṟu kūṟuvār illāta iṭattē, aṭimuṉṉē paṇintu eṉ ūṭalip pōkka varuvāy” (kali. 73) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟil, talivaṉ taliviyiṉ cīṟaṭi pulli irattal kūṟappaṭṭatu.	(to. po. 146 nac.)
“iṉip puṇarcci nīṭṭikkumō nīṭṭiyātō eṉṟu karututalāl, iṉpattiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta ūṭaliṉkaṇ ṇēyum oru tuṉpam nikaḻum” 	(kuṟaḷ 1307)
“ūṭal kāraṇamākat tōṉṟukiṉṟa ciṟiya tuṉiyiṉāl nalla taliyaḷi vāṭumāyiṉum, piṉṉarp perumi eytum” 		(kuṟaḷ 1322)
“talivi iṉṉum nammōṭu ūṭuvāḷāka! aṅṅaṉam avaḷ ūṭi niṟṟaṟkum ataṉi uṇarttutaṟ poruṭṭu yām irantu niṟṟaṟkum kālam peṟumvaki ivviravu viṭiyātu nīṭṭittal vēṇṭum”	(kuṟaḷ 1329)
ivaṟṟit talivaṉ kūṟṟukku iḷampūraṇar mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭuvar. 	(po. 144)
talivaṉ parattami nīṅkum iṭam -
talivaṉ, tāṉ piṉṉar maṇantu koṇṭa maṉiviyi mutal maṉivi viḷakku mutaliya maṅkalaṅkaḷik koṇṭu etirēṟṟuk koḷḷum ciṟappi nōkkiyum, mutal maṉivi taṉ makaṉik kōlam ceytu piṉmuṟi maṉiviyiṭam aḻittuc cellumpōtu ap piṉmuṟi maṉivi ap putalvaṉiṭam uṇmiyāṉa aṉpu celuttum tiṟatti nōkkiyum, tāṉ mutal maṉiviyiṉ putalvaṉi vāyilākak koṇṭu piṉmuṟi maṉiviyiṭam aḻittuc cellum pōtu avaḷ mukamalarntu varavēṟṟalik kaṇṭum, iṅṅaṉam paṇpāl uyarnta  taliviyar iruvariyum kiviṭṭup putalvaṉukkut tantiyākiya poṟuppuṇarcci yuṭiya tāṉ parattiyar toṭarpu kōṭal takkataṉṟu eṉṟu uṇarntu, tāṉ parattiyiṭam koṇṭa kātali mīṭṭum taliviyariṭam koḷḷuvāṉ.	(to. po. 172 nac.)
talivaṉ paḷḷiyiṭattu vantiruntu kūṟutal -
“mēkamē!nī naṉṟāka miṉṉi iṭittu maḻi peyka. eṉakku iṉik kavali illi. yāṉ ceṉṟa kāriyatti veṟṟi yoṭu muṭitta talimiyōṭu ittaliviyi aṭintu  ivaḷ kūntal aṇiyil uṟaṅkukiṉṟēṉ” (kuṟun. 270) eṉṟu viṉimuṟṟiya talivaṉ taliviyoṭu paṭukki yiṭattu uvantu iruntu kūṟutal.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘avvaḻip perukiya ciṟappiṉkaṇ’ varum kūṟṟu. pirintu ceṉṟaviṭattu, tāṉ peṟṟa perukkam eytiya ciṟappiṉkaṇ mīṇṭu vantōṉ kūṟiyatu. 			(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ paḷḷiyiṉ aḻuṅkal -
taliviyi iṉṟit taṉittup paḷḷiyil  paṭuttirunta talivaṉ varuntal. talivi talivaṉ parattamiyāl ūṭal koṇṭu taṉittut tuyila, talivaṉ, taliviyiṉ tōḷ kaḷum nakilkaḷum taṉ ciṟanta paṭukkiyil taṉakkup payaṉ paṭātu puṟattē iruntamikku varuntutal.	(ampikā. 472)
itu ‘parattiyiṟpirital’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; ‘uṇartta uṇarā ūṭal’ eṉum uṭpiriviṉ kaṇṇatu.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘piṟa’ eṉṟataṉāl koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu. 	(i. vi. 555)
talivaṉ pāṅkaṉiṭam nikaḻpavi urittal -
taṉṉi ulakattu niliniṟkiṉṟa naṟkuṇaṅkaḷi niṉip pittu iṭitturitta pāṅkaṉiṭam talivaṉ taṉ neñcil nikaḻum varuttaṅkaḷi eṭutturittal.
“eṉṉiṭam vēṭṭiyiṉiṉṟu tappi ōṭiya māṉ ceṉṟa vaḻiyē ceṉṟu cōli oṉṟil ammāṉip paṟṟi yāṉ viṉaviṉaviṭattu, aṅkut tōḻiyar naṭuvē irunta naṟumalar aṇinta kūntalāḷ orutti eṉ āṟṟalik kippaṟṟi eṉ uḷḷattit taṉakku iruppiṭamākak koṇṭa  māyattāl taṭumāṟukiṉṟēṉ” (tiṇimāli. 9) eṉavum,
“ēṭa! ariya naṇpa! pulavarkkut tōḻa!  karuṅkaṭal naṭuvē eṭṭām nāṭkali niṟinta piṟimati tōṉṟiṉāṟpōla, pēraḻakiyām ciṟumi yoruttiyiṉ karuṅkūntal ayalē tōṉṟum ciṟuneṟṟi, putitākak koḷḷappeṟṟa yāṉi pōla, eṉṉic ciṟi ceytuviṭṭatu” (kuṟun. 58) eṉavum,
“ipperuvaṉappiṉaḷākiya taliviyiṉ ari paranta maḻik kaṇkaḷi yāṉ kāṇāmuṉṉar, nayaṉum naṇpum peru nāṇum payaṉum paṇpum pāṭaṟintu oḻukalum ākiya paṇpu nalaṉkaḷinummiviṭa uṭiyaṉāy iruntēṉ maṉ!” (naṟ. 160) eṉavum talivaṉ tāṉ uṟṟa niliyit taṉ uyirp pāṅkaṉiṭam urittal.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ pāṅkaṉiṭam, “yāṉ kaṇṭa uruvu ivviyalpiṟṟu, ivviṭattatu” eṉal -
talivaṉ taliviyiṭam koṇṭa īṭupāṭu mikutiyāṉatu eṉpati aṟinta pāṅkaṉ attalivi paṟṟi viṉāvat talivaṉ,
“kēḷir! vāḻi! colluvatik kēṇmiṉ. eṉ neñ ciṉip piṇitta anta amcilōtinaṅkiyiṉ nāṇamikunta ammelliya uṭampiṉi orunāḷ yāṉ mēvappeṟiṉ, ataṟku mēlē arināḷ uyirvāḻvum virumpēṉ!” (kuṟun. 280) eṉavum,
“taliviyiṉ koṅkikaḷ arumpiviṭṭaṉa; talimayirum piṭariyil toṅkumaḷaviṟku nīṇṭuviṭṭatu; paṟkaḷ viḻuntu muḷittu viṭṭaṉa; mārpil cuṇaṅkum paṭarntuviṭṭatu. avaḷ eṉṉi varuttutali nāṉ aṟivēṉ. āyiṉ, peruñcelvariṉ appeṇ tāṉ aṟintāḷallaḷ” (kuṟun. 337) eṉavum,
“kaḻikkūtti āṭiya kayiṟṟiṉmēl kuraṅkukkuṭṭi toṅki viḷiyāṭutalik kaṇṭu kuṟacciṟuvar āravāram ceyyum kuṉṟiṉuḷ aminta ciṟiya ūrilē uḷḷa maṇam nāṟum kūntali uṭiya kuṟappeṇṇiṉ kiyil eṉ neñcu akappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. avaḷē iraṅki ataṉi viṭuvittā laṉṟip piṟarāl atu viṭuvittaṟkariyatu” (naṟ. 95) eṉavum taliviyiṉ iyal iṭaṅkaḷik kūṟutal.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘kuṟṟam kāṭṭiya vāyil peṭpiṉum’ eṉṟataṉāl, ataṉ pakutiyākak koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟuk kaḷuḷ oṉṟu. 	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ pāṅkaṉic cārtal -
iyaṟkip puṇarcci iṭantalippāṭu eṉṟa iraṇṭāṉum taliviyi eytik kātalāl varuntiya talivaṉ taṉ tōḻaṉic ceṉṟaṭital.
‘pāṅkaṉi niṉital’ eṉavum (kōvi. 19) kūṟupa.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 137)
talivaṉ pāṅkaṉi “nī talivi uḷḷa iṭam nāṭic cēṟal vēṇṭum” eṉal -
talivaṉ, taliviyiṉ iyaliyum avaḷ irukkumiṭat tiyum pāṅkaṟkuc cuṭṭi, “kāyāmalariṉ maṇam kāṟṟiṉāl maṉi mukappil eppoḻutum vīcappaṭum iyalpiṟṟu, maliyakattu ūrākiya ciṟukuṭi. acciṟukuṭiyil vāḻvār pacukkaḷik koḷḷiyaṭittaliyē tam toḻilākak koḷpavar. nī ciṟukuṭiyil eṉ talivi irukkum iṭattik kāṇac celliṉ, āṇṭut taṭuppār oruvarum irār” eṉṟu kūṟutal.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ, ‘kuṟṟam kāṭṭiya vāyil peṭ piṉum’ eṉṟataṉ pakutiyākak koḷḷappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ oru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ pāṅkiyiṭam, “yāṉ varintami numarkku iyampu, ceṉṟu” eṉṟal -
uṭaṉpōkkiṟkup piṉ taliviyatu ūrkkē avaḷoṭu mīṇṭu vanta talivaṉ, tōḻiyiṭam taliviyit tāṉ taṉ maṉikkaṇ tirumaṇam ceytukoṇṭa ceytiyic cuṟṟattārkkuc collumāṟu kūṟutal.
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘taṉmaṉi varital’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 194)
talivaṉ pāṅkiyoṭu nontu viṉātal -
kaḷavoḻukkattiṉiṭiyē varitalukku muṉ oruvaḻit taṇantapiṉ (oruvaḻit taṇattalāvatu, kāṭiṭiyiṭṭum nāṭiṭiyiṭṭum poruḷvayiṉ pirital pōlaṉṟi, ōr ūriṉkaṇṇum oru nāṭṭiṉkaṇṇum pirital), mīṇṭu vanta talivaṉ, tāṉ nīṭṭittamikku nontu, taṉ pirivit tōḻiyum taliviyum eṅṅaṉam āṟṟiyiruntaṉar eṉa(t tōḻiyi) viṉavutal.
“tōḻi! vīṇ kavalikaḷi uṇṭākkikkoṇṭu, nīṅkaḷ iruvīrum aintali nākattiṭam akappaṭṭārip pōlap peruntuṉpappaṭṭu eṉ pōl evvāṟu varunti iruntīr kaḷō?” (tañci. kō. 258) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘oruvaḻittaṇattal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 168)
talivaṉ pācaṟikkaṇ pulampal -
tūtuviṉi kālam nīṭṭittavaḻiyum, naṇpaṟku uṟṟuḻi utavac ceṉṟu kālam nīṭṭittavaḻiyum, tāṉē taṉ pakimēṟ ceṉṟavaḻik kālam nīṭṭittavaḻiyum, tāṉ pācaṟikkaṇ iruntu taṉakku veṟṟi tōṉṟiyavaḻiyum, tāṉ talivikku mīṇṭu varuvatākak kūṟiya paruvam vantavaḻiyum, talivi viṭutta tūtu kaṇṭavaḻiyum talivaṉ taliviyiṉ taṉimiyi niṉittu varuntip palavāṟu kūṟal.			(to. po. 41 nac., 44 iḷa.)
‘talivaṉ pācaṟip pulampal’ (558 uri) eṉpatu ilakkaṇa viḷakkattuḷ uriyiṟ kaṇṭa kūṟṟu.
talivaṉ, pirinta kālattu “ivaḷi maṟantavāṟu eṉ?” eṉṟa tōḻikkuk kūṟutal -
“tōḻi! vēṟṟu nāṭṭukkup poruḷ tēṭac ceṉṟa viṭattu, neruppup paṟṟi erivatu pōṉṟa veppamikka nilattil ciṟitaḷavu kaṇ ayarntālum, kaṉavil, ceṟinta iruḷil aḻakiya illattil eṉ niṉippil vikum iṉiya maṇam poruntiya kūntaliyuṭiya taliviyik kāṇpēṉ. ivvāṟē nāḷik kaṭattiṉēṉ” (aiṅ. 324) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘paṇṇami pakuti’ eṉṟataṉāl koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ, pirintavaḻik kalaṅkaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
iṭantalippāṭṭiṉkaṇ talivaṉ taliviyip puṇarntu pirintaviṭattu, aṉpu mikutiyāl maṟintu niṉṟu kāṇuṅ kāl, “iṉi ivaḷik kūṭutal aritu” eṉṟu kalaṅkiya niliyil maṉattiṭam kūṟutal.
“neñcē! kīḻik kaṭaṟkariyil taṅkiyirukkum ciṟaku nīṅkiya āṇṭu mūtta nāri, mēlik kaṭaṟkariyiluḷḷa toṇṭi eṉṟa ūri aṭutta kaṭaliluḷḷa ayirimīṉi uṇṇa ācippaṭuvatu pōla, neṭun tolivil uḷḷavaḷum kiṭṭutaṟku ariyavaḷum ākiya taliviyip peṟutaṟku niṉikkum nītuṉpuṟuttum ūḻviṉiyi uṭiyi!” (kuṟun. 128) eṉavum,
“neñcē! poruḷillātavaṉ iṉpanukarcciyi virumpu vatu pōla, nī kiṭṭutaṟkariya poruḷi virumpukiṉṟāy. niṉ kātali mika iṉiyaḷ eṉpati aṟintuḷḷa nī, avaḷ kiṭṭutaṟku ariyaḷ eṉpataṉi aṟiyātirukkiṉṟāy!” (kuṟun. 120) eṉavum,
“eṉakku  ēṟpaṭṭa kāmak kāyccalukku maruntāka amintu, eṉṉāl pūcappaṭṭa cāntu citiyumāṟu taḻuvappeṟum koṅkikaḷi yuṭiya kāṉavaṉ maṭamakaḷ, eṉakkuk kūṭuṅkāl eṉṟum iṉiyaḷāy iruppiṉum,piriyuṅkāl tīṅku tarupavaḷāka uḷḷāḷē!” eṉavum palavāṟākat taṉ neñciṭam kūṟutal.
itu pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattiṉkaṇṇum, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉ- kaṇṇum nikaḻum.	(to. po. 102 nac., 99 iḷa.)
talivaṉ, “piriyuṅkol!” eṉa aiyuṟṟu uṭaṉpaṭāmi mēṟkoṇṭu oḻukukiṉṟa talimakaṭkut tāṉ pirivu oḻintataṟkuk kāraṇam kūṟit tēṟṟiyatu -
“talivi! ‘kārkālat toṭakkam vantuviṭṭatu; iṉip pirintu cellutal kūṭātu’ eṉṟu taṭuttuviṭṭāy. ‘ikkālattil nammaṉṉaṉ pōr ceyyum viruppoṭu pāṭivīṭṭiṟku vārāṉ’ eṉṟu tuṇintu yāṉum niṉṉip pirintu cellutali nīkkiviṭṭēṉ” eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(aiṅ. 427)
talivaṉ, ‘piriviṉ eccattup pulampiya iruvarip, piriviṉ nīkkiya pakutikkaṇ’ kūṟal -
talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintu taliviyi viṭuttu irunta vaḻit talivaṉiyum taliviyiyum taṉatu aruḷiṉāl, pirintu varuntum oruvaṉ vāyilāka amintu, cērttu vittaṟkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟutal 	(to. po. 146 nac.)
pirivu nimittamāka varuntiya maṉiviyiyum kāmakkiḻattiyi yum avvaruttattiṉiṉṟum nīkkiya pakutikkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟutal. ‘parivu nīkkiya’ eṉpatu iḷampūraṇar pāṭam.
“maṭanti! niṉ mēṉi poṉ pōṉṟatu; niṉ kūntal maṇi pōṉṟatu; niṉ kaṇkaḷ kuvaḷi pōṉṟaṉa; niṉ tōḷkaḷ vēy pōṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟik kaṇṭē yāṉ niṟi vuṟukiṉṟēṉ. nam putalvaṉum viḷiyāṭak kaṟṟukkoṇṭi rukkiṟāṉ. uṅkaḷik kaṇṭu makiḻvativiṭa eṉakku vēṟṟuppaṇi illi. uṅkaḷiṭam eṉakkuḷḷa aṉpu kaṭaliṉum peritu. ātaliṉ nummip piriyēṉ” (naṟ. 166) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(144 iḷa.)
talivaṉum taliviyum taṉimiyuṟṟavaḻi iruvariyum  piriviṉ nīkkit tōḻi cērttu vittal. atupōtu talivaṉatu kūṟṟu nikaḻum. 	(po. 194 kuḻa.)
talivaṉ, piriviṉkaṇ talivi uṭaṉvarutali maṟuttuk kūṟutal -
talivaṉ pālivaḻiyē ceṉṟu poruḷīṭṭutaṟkut taliviyip piriyak karutiyavaḻit taliviyum uṭaṉ varutali virumpiṉaḷāka, avaṉ, “nī eṉṉoṭu variṉ, melliya aṉiccamalar mutaliyavaṟṟil naṭantu paḻakiya niṉ ciṟiya aṭittāmarikaḷ, pāli nilattuḷḷa kaṟkaḷil pāvi naṭakkumpōtu, tāmariyiṉ aḻakiya uḷ itaḻkaḷ iṅkulikam tōyntāṟ pōṉṟu kaṟuttup poliviḻantu viṭum” (kali. 13) eṉṟal pōlvaṉa kūṟi,  avaḷ uṭaṉ varutali maṟuttal. itu pālittiṇikku viravum poruḷām. 	(to. po. 45. nac.)
talivaṉ ‘piṟavum’ viṉātal - 
tōḻiyi irantu kuṟiyuṟa muṭivuceyta talivaṉ, taliviyum tōḻiyum oruṅkē irukkum iṭam nōkki vantu, “ivaṉ viṉavuvatu vēṟōr uṭkaruttiṉiyuṭiyatu pōlum” eṉṟu tōḻi karutumāṟu, ētilar pōla ūriṉi mutaṟkaṇ viṉavi, uṟavu tōṉṟap peyariṉip piṉ viṉavip piṉṉar, ” yāṉum melliya ilikaḷip parappi num viruntiṉaṉāka nīr koṇṭuvantirukkum uṇavip pakirn tuṇṭu numatu ciṟukuṭiyil vantu taṅkuvataṟkut taṭiyuḷ ḷatō?” (akanā.110) eṉavum, “makaḷirē! eṉṉoṭu kūṭi nīṅkaḷ illaṟam naṭattuvatāyiṉ ataṉkaṇ tavaṟuṇṭō?” eṉavum, taṉ uḷḷakkiṭakki  pulappaṭumāṟu avarkaḷi viṉātal. ‘ūrum pērum keṭutiyum piṟavum’ eṉṉumaṭik kaṇ ‘piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāl itu koḷḷappaṭṭatu.			(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ, ‘pukāakkāli pukku etirppaṭṭuḻip pakāa viruntiṉ pakutikkaṇ’ kūṟal -
iravukkuṟiyil taliviyik kāṇātu āṟṟāmi mikka talivaṉ avaḷik kāṇum viruppiṉāl avaḷ teru viṭik kāliyil vantuḻi, tāṉ viruntiṉaṉākap pukutaṟkuriya takutiyākiya naṇpakaṟ poḻutiviṭuttu viṭiyaṟ kālattukkaṇ vantapōtum, (ammaṉiyavarāl) nīkkappaṭāta viruntiṉ pakutiyaṉāka ēṟṟukkoḷḷappaṭṭa vaḻit taliviyik kaṇṭu kūṟutal.
“eṉ kātali iruvēṟu tiṟamum aṟinta kaḷviyāvāḷ. muḷḷūrk kāṭṭiṉip pōlat taṉmēṉi naṟumaṇam vīca vantu naṭuiravil eṉṉoṭu kalantu uriyāṭukiṟāḷ. viṭiyaṟ kāliyil yāṉ iravu cūṭṭiya malarkaḷi utirt tut talimuṭiyil eṇṇey taṭavik kūntali muṭintu koṇṭu eṉṉi aṉpu tōṉṟap pārttalit tavirntu notumalar pōla nōkkit taṉ uṟaviṉarkaḷ eṉṉiṭam naṭantu koḷḷumāṟupōla naṭantu koḷkiṟāḷ” (kuṟun. 312) eṉṟal pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 105. iḷa.)
ikkiḷaviyit talivi kūṟṟākak koḷvar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar. uṇṭikkālattut talivi illattut talivaṉ pukku etirppaṭṭavaḻi, nīkki niṟuttāta viruntu ēṟṟuk koḷḷum pakutikkaṇ talivi kūṟṟu nikaḻum.
eṉavē, maṉiyakam pukutaṟku ovvāta talimi mikka talivaṉ pukuntāl, “iḵtu oṉṟu uṭittu” eṉat tērātu, tāy avaṉi viruntēṟṟup piṉ nīkkiviṭum pakutiyum taḻīiyiṉavāṟāyiṟṟu.
viṭiyaṟkālamāyiṉ talivaṉ pukāṉ; pukāak kālattu (uṇavuk kālattu)ppukka poḻutāyiṉ avar viruntēṟṟuk kōṭal orutali eṉṟu pukum. talivi kāṭciāciyil kalaṅkutaṟkēṟpat talivaṟkum kāṭci āci uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
“tōḻī! talivaṉ ‘uṇṇunīr vēṭṭēṉ’ eṉak kūṟat tāy, ‘uṇṇunīr ūṭṭivā’ eṉa, ataṟku vanta eṉṉi avaṉ, vaḷi muṉki paṟṟi naliya nāṉ, “aṉṉāy!” eṉṟu aḻikkat tāy vantavuṭaṉ, ‘uṇṇunīr vikkiṉāṉ’ eṉṟu yāṉ kūṟa, tāy avaṉi mutukuppuṟam taṭavik koṭukka, avaṉ eṉṉik kolvāṉ nōkkutal pōl kaṭikkaṇ ṇāl nōkkic cirittāṉ” (kali. 51) eṉap pukāak kāliyil pukkāṉi viruntēṟṟuk koṇṭatit talivi piṉ oru kālattut tōḻikkuk kūṟiyavāṟu.
“enti kaṭalil mīṉ piṭikkac cellavum, talivaṉ em il vantu tēri niṟutti, ‘yāṉ num viruntiṉaṉ’ eṉṉa, tāy avaḷi ‘viruntiṉaṉākat taṅku’ eṉṟāḷ. attakiya aṉṉi vāḻi!” eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa.
“oru nāḷ iravu talivaṉ ‘viruntiṉaṉ’ eṉṟu vara, avaṉi viruntu ēlātu tāy iravu muṟṟum tuṅkāmal iruntāḷ. avaḷ narakam pukuka!” (kuṟun. 292) eṉṟu tali vaṉi viruntu ēlāmikkut talivi muṉintu kūṟiyavāṟu.			(to. po. 107 nac.)
talivaṉ, ‘putalvaṟ payanta puṉiṟu cēr poḻutiṉ, neyyaṇi mayakkam purintōḷ nōkki, aiyar pāṅkiṉum amararc cuṭṭiyum, ceyperuñ ciṟappoṭu cērtaṟkaṇ’kūṟal -
putalvaṉip peṟṟa īṉṟaṇimi cērnta poḻutiṉkaṇ cuṟṟak kuḻāttuṭaṉē tūymiyiṉmi nīṅkutal poruṭṭāka eṇṇeyāṭum mayakkatti virumpiya taliviyi mukama ṉākak kūṟutalik kuṟittu, muṉivarmāṭṭum tēvarkaḷ putalvaṉip pātukāttalik karutiyum akkālattuc ceyyum periya ciṟappukkaḷik kuṟitta maṉattōṭē ceṉṟu cārtaṟkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟutal.
ciṟappāvaṉa piṟanta putalvaṉi mukam kāṇṭalum, aimpaṭi pūṭṭalum, peyariṭutalum mutaliyaṉavum, ellā muṉivark kum tēvarkkum antaṇarkkum koṭuttalum ām.
‘cērtal’ kūṟavē, karuppam mutirnta kālattut talivaṟkup piṟaroṭu kūṭṭamuṇmiyum kūṟiṟṟām.
“pāṇa! nam kuṭikku ōr āṇmakavip payantu, pēy mutaliyaṉa tīṇṭātirukka neyyoṭu veṇciṟukaṭukappiya nam illattil pāyoṭu paṭuttiruntāḷ talivi. ‘makap peṟṟa mutu peṇṭāki uṟaṅkutiyō!’ eṉṟu avaḷ vayiṟṟil kuvaḷi malarāl oṟṟi yāṉ niṟka, avaḷukkuc cirippu vara, nāṇattāl kaṇkaḷi muṭik koṇṭa ceyal eṉakku niṉikkuntōṟum ciripput tarukiṟatu”  (naṟ. 370) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟil, avaṉ makap peṟṟa taliviyik kaṇṭu pēciyami kuṟikkappaṭṭavāṟu.	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ putalvaṉik kāṇṭal -
makaṉ piṟantami kēṭṭa talivaṉ avaṉi vantu kāṇṭal. talivaṉ antaṇarukku āvum poṉṉum tāṉamāka vaḻaṅkit tēvarukkup pūvum pukiyum vaḻaṅki, nalla nērattil taṉ kulattōṉṟalākiya makaṉik kiyil eṭuttāṉ eṉpatu. 	(ampikā. 468)
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; ‘uṇartta uṇarā ūṭal’ eṉṟa uṭ piriviṉkaṇṇatu, uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.			(i. vi. 555 uri)
talivaṉ ‘puripaṭa vanta maṟuttaṟ’ kaṇ kūṟiyatu -
talivaṉ taliviyi maṇattaṟku ēṟpāṭu ceyyat talivi tamar kuṟṟamuṇṭākumāṟu maṟuttaviṭattut talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“ittalivi ciṟanta poruḷum aṇikalaṉkaḷum paricap poruḷākak koṭuppiṉum, peṟutaṟku ariyaḷākiviṭ ṭāḷ. iṉi, muttukkaḷik kaṭaliliruntu koṇarntu pakuttuk koḷḷum neytal talivaṉākiya avaḷtanti yuṭaṉ kūṭi, uppaḷattil uppup potiyuṭaṉ tirintu varunti yum, kaṭalil paṭakuṭaṉ pōkiyum avaṉiṭṭa paṇikaḷic ceytu avaṉvaḻic cārntiruntāltāṉ taliviyi namakku maṇam muṭippār pōlum!” (akanā. 280) eṉṟu neñciṉi nōkkit talivaṉ kūṟutal.	(to. po. 105 iḷa.)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ikkiḷaviyit talivi kūṟṟu ākkuvar. 
talivi, taṉ kulappiṟappum kaṟpum mutaliya uyarccikku ēṟpa, “piṟar varivu maṟuttut talivaṉ variyumāṟu nī kūṟu” eṉat tōḻikkuk kūṟutal pōlvaṉa. icceyti kali.114 il kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
“maḷḷar kuḻīiya viḻaviṉkaṇṇum makaḷir kuḻīiya tuṇaṅkikkaṇṇum eṉ talivaṉik kāṇa iyala villi. yāṉum ōr āṭukaḷa makaḷ; eṉ kiyilaṇinta caṅkuvaḷiyalkaḷi nekiḻac ceyta avaṉum ōr āṭukaḷa makaṉē” (kuṟun. 31) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟum koḷka.	(107 nac.)
talivaṉ, pulantu pōtal -
‘pulantu avaṉ pōtal’ eṉpatu kiḷavi. taṉṉik kuṟiyiṭat til vantu kāṇātirunta taliviyi nontum ciṉantum talivaṉ taṉatu iruppiṭam mīḷutal.
“neñcē! talivikku māḷiki tāmariyē ātaliṉ, aḵtu iravil tiṟantu avaḷi veḷiviṭātu eṉpati aṟintu vittum,  vīṇē eṉṉit tuṉpuṟutti, yāṉikaḷ tiriyum ivviravil eṉṉi iṅkuk koṇṭuvantāyē!” (ampikā. 223) eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivaṉ neñciṭam varuntik kūṟip pōtal.	(na. a. 160)
talivaṉ (iṟimakaṉ) puṟattoḻukkut talivi (iṟimakaḷ) uṇarttal -
talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintu ceṉṟatāl, talivi iravel lām aḻutu kaṇkalaṅkiyiruntami maṟunāṭ kāli kaṇṭa tōḻi, ataṟkuk kāraṇam kēṭpavē, avaḷ talivaṉatu appuṟattu oḻukkattik kūṟutal (tañci. kō. 380)
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ ‘parattiyiṟpirivu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. iḵtu  ‘uṇartta uṇarum ūṭal.’			(na. a. 205)
talivaṉ ‘peṭṭa vāyil peṟṟu iravu valiyuṟut’tatu -
taliviyāṉ peritum virumpappaṭṭa tōḻiyi vāyi lākak koṇṭu avaḷi vēṇṭit taliviyip peṟat talivaṉ uṟuti koṇṭatu. iravu valiyuṟuttal- vēṇṭik kēṭkumāṟu neñci valiyuṟuttal.
“aciyum koṭi pōṉṟa iyalpiṉiyuṭiyāy!kaḷ kuṭiyātataṉāṉ varum tuṉpam aḵtu uṇṭapiṉṉarē tīrum. ataṉi maruntālō, teyva maṇikaḷālō, tavattālō nīkkutal eṉpatu iyalātu. atupōlavē, niṉṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa nōykku niṉṉit tavira vēṟu maruntu illi. ‘niṉ aruḷ vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu kūṟi apparatavar makaḷi vēṇṭutaṟku muṟpaṭuvāyāka, neñcē!” eṉṟu talivaṉ neñciṭam kūṟiyavāṟu. (kuṇanāṟpatu.)	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ peṭṭavāyil peṟṟuk kūṟutal -
talivaṉ taliviyāṉ virumpappaṭṭavaḷākiya, taṉakku vāyilākum taṉmiyuṭiya, tōḻiyi maṟiya niṉṟu kaṇṭuṇarntu “ivaḷi irantu taliviyi eytalām” eṉṟu kūṟutal.
talivaṉ pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattiṉpiṉ talivi taṉṉik kāṇāta vakiyil tāṉ avaḷik kāṇpatōr aṇi mikkaṇ maṟintu niṉṟāṉāka, appoḻutu talivi taṉ tōḻiyoṭu cuṉikkaṇ nīrāṭa, atu kaṇṭa talivaṉ, “tōḻi teppattiṉ talippakutiyip piṭittuk koṇṭu nīntiṉāl, taliviyum attalippakkattiyē piṭittuk koṇṭu nīntukiṟāḷ. tōḻi teppattiṉ kaṭip pakutiyip piṭittukkoṇṭu nīntiṉāl, taliviyum ak kaṭip pakkattiyē piṭittukkoṇṭunīntukiṟāḷ. teppattik kiviṭṭut tōḻi nīrvaḻic ceṉṟālum, talivi avaḷip piṉtoṭarntu celvāḷ pōlum!” (kuṟun. 222) eṉat tuṇintu, “iṉit taliviyāṉ virumpap paṭum tōḻiyiyē eṉakku vāyilākak koṇṭu ikkaḷa voḻukkattit toṭarvēṉ” eṉṟu taṉṉuḷ kūṟutal.
(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ peyarkaḷ -
kuṟiñcinilat talivaṉ - cilampaṉ, poruppaṉ, veṟpaṉ
pālinilattalivaṉ - viṭali, kāḷi, mīḷi
mulli nilattalivaṉ - kuṟumpoṟi nāṭaṉ, (tōṉṟal)
marutanilattalivaṉ - ūraṉ, makiḻnaṉ.
neytalnilat talivaṉ - cērppaṉ, koṇkaṉ, tuṟivaṉ, (pulampaṉ)
‘tiṇi nilip peyar’ kāṇka.	(iṟi. a. 1 uri)
talivaṉ peyar viṉātal -
tōḻiyi irantu piṉṉiṟṟaṟku muṭivu ceyta talivaṉ, taliviyum tōḻiyum oruṅkirunta iṭam nōkkic ceṉṟu “ivaṉ viḻivatu vēṟōr uṭkarutti yuṭiyatu pōlum!” eṉṟu tōḻi karutumāṟu, “mūṅkil pōṉṟa melliya tōḷkaḷi yuṭiyīrē! tiṉikkolliyil kiḷikaḷi ōṭṭum toḻilil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa num peyar eṉṉa eṉpataṉi yāṉ aṟiyalāmā?” eṉṟu viṉavutal.			(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ, ‘peṟṟa tēettup perumiyiṉ nilii(a)k kuṟṟam cāṉṟa poruḷ eṭutturittaṟkaṇ kūṟiyatu -
varintu poruḷ peṟṟavaḻit taliviyip perumi yiṉkaṇ niṟuttik kaḷavuk kālattuk kuṟṟam niṟinta poruḷi eṭuttuk kūṟiyavaḻit talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“vantavarkkut tēṉi viruntup poruḷāka vaḻaṅkum uṉ tantiyiṉ nāṭṭil, uṉ nōyit tīrttaṟku murukaṉukkuc ceyta veṟiyāṭṭil uṉtōḻi eṉ tuyaramum tīrtaṟku vēṇṭikkoṇṭamiyiṉ, kaḷavil pala nāḷum uṟṟa tuyaram tīra uṉtōḷi iṉṟu maṇakkum vāyppiṉip peṟṟēṉ” eṉat talivaṉ kūṟṟāka vanta ip pāṭaṟkaṇ, taliviyiṉ illattup perumiyum taṉ kuṟṟaṅ kuṟikaḷ tīrat tōḻi murukaṉi vēṇṭiyamiyum cuṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. 			(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
taliviaṅṅaṉam urimi cāṉṟa iṭattu avaḷip perumiyiṉ kaṇṇē niṟuttik kuṟṟam aminta kaḷavo ḻukkatti vaḻuvi yaminta poruḷākak kēḷirkkāyiṉum piṟarkkāyiṉum urittaṟkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟutal.
atu kaḷavoḻukkattiyum, tīya ōriyuḷḷum akalātu ōḻukiya kuṟṟattiyum uṭkoṇṭu, ataṉi veḷiyiṭutal taṉ perumikkut takātu eṉṟu karutāmal veḷiyiṭutal.
“maḻiyil oḻukum ciṟiya illamāyiṉum, ūrārum pukaḻum kaṟpiṉ nallāḷ irukkum illamē illam” (nālaṭi. 382) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟil, maṉivi amintu niṉṟa in niliyē illaṟamāvatu eṉavē, taṉ kaḷavoḻukkac ceyal ikkaṟpuṭi illattukkut tuṇiyāka utaviya taṉi uṭkoṇṭu coṉṉāṉ eṉpatu. 	(146 nac.)
talivaṉ, ‘pērici ūrtip pākar pāṅkiṉil’ kūṟal -
talivaṉ tāṉ uṟṟa iṉpattiṉip periya pukaḻiyuṭiya tēriṉiyuṭiya pākaṟkuk kūṟal.
“pāka! muṉpu yāṉ poruḷvayiṉ pirintu kārkālattē mīṇṭēṉāka, paḷḷaṅkaḷil taṅkiya nīrilē tavaḷikaḷ mikutiyāka olittalāṉ talivi eṉ tēroli kēḷāḷ ātaliṉ, ‘nīṅkaḷ muṉ ceṉṟu avaṭku eṉ mīṭciyit teriviyuṅkaḷ’ eṉṟu iḷiyōri muṉṉar viṭuppa, avarkaḷum avvāṟē aṟivittaṉarāka, avaḷ naṉku nīrāṭip pūccūṭiya aḷavil yāṉ pukuta, appaṭiyē vantu eṉṉit taḻuviya antak kāṭci maṟattaṟ kariyatu” (naṟ. 42) eṉṟu talivaṉ pākaṉiṭam kūṟutal.	(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
acciṟappukkaḷi eytiya talivaṉ periya pukaḻi yuṭit tākiya tēriṉiyuṭiya pākaṉiṭam kūṟṟu nikaḻttutal.
avaṉatu ciṟappuṇarttutaṟkup ‘pākar’ eṉap paṉmiyāṉ kūṟiṉār.
“pāka! nī vāḻka! aracaṉ pōrttoḻil muṭitta piṉṉar nī colliyapaṭi tēril ēṟiyatallatu, nī mulli nilattuc cīṟūril em maṉivāyil tēri niṟutti ‘iṟaṅkumiṉ!’ eṉṟu kūṟiyatu kēṭṭu viyantu iṟaṅkiṉēṉē allāl, iṭivaḻiyil etaṉiyum kāṇavilli. tēril kāṟṟiṉip pūṭṭiṉāyā, aṉṟi niṉ maṉattiyē pūṭṭiṉāyā?” (akanā. 384) eṉṟu talivaṉ tērppākaṉi viyantu kūṟutal.
talivaṉ tāṉuṟṟa iṉpattiṉip pākaṟkuk kūṟiyatu.	(naṟ. 42)
“pāka! niṉ piṉ varum ūrtikaḷip piṉvarac ceytu, pariyi muṭukkit tēri muṉṉē celuttu” (akanā. 44) eṉṟu taliviyik kāṇṭaṟkaṇ uḷḷa ārvattāṉ talivaṉ pākaṉiṭam kūṟutal. 	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ poy pārāṭṭik kūṟal -
taliviyi meytoṭṭup payiṉṟa talivaṉ avaḷip palavāṟu puṉinturiyāl pukaḻtal.
iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarccikku muṉnikaḻum eṉavum, iṭan talippāṭṭukku muṉ nikaḻum eṉavum irutiṟak karuttukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
taliviyit tīṇṭiya talivaṉ avaḷ kuṟippaṟintu, avaḷ talimayir pirintiruntavaṟṟit taṭavip paṭiya vippatu pōla, pirintirāta niliyilum avaḷ kūntaliyum neṟṟiyiyum taṭavi avaḷi nōkki, ” niṉ tamiyaṉmā rākiya mīṉavarkaḷ kaṭalil vēṭṭam pōy kaṭalvāḻ uyirkaḷik koṉṟu tam vāḻkkiyi naṭattukiṉṟaṉar. nīyō, neñcuvaḻiyāka eṉ uṭaliṉuḷ pukuntu eṉ uyiriṉit tuṉpuṟutti vāḻkiṟāy. paruttu, ellikku aṭaṅkāta niṉ koṅkikaḷiṉ pārattāṉ varuntit taṭumāṟum melliya iṭi ōṭintu pōkum niliyiṉi uṇṭākkik koḷḷātē!” (cilap. kāṉal. 17) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kaṟpaṉiyāṉ palapaṭiyāka avaḷ aḻakip pukaḻntu kūṟutal.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ poruṭpirivil ceṉṟa kāṭṭatu kaṭumi niṉintu āṟṟāḷākiya talivi, “avar poruṭṭāka nām ivvakippaṭṭa teyvaṅkaḷip paravutal nam kaṟpukku iyivatō?” eṉṉa, kēṭṭa tōḻi “avvāṟṟāṉē mīṇṭaṉar; nī kavala vēṇṭā” eṉak kūṟiyatu -
“‘talivar eṉṉip pirintu poruḷ tēṭap pālivaḻip pōyuḷḷār; ‘avar cellum vaḻiyil veppam nīṅka maḻi peyka’ eṉṟu mēkattiṟkuriya teyvamākiya ñāyiṟṟi vaḻipaṭutal namakku ēṟkumā? ‘marakkiḷikaḷ vāṭum veppam taṇintu taṇṇiya katirkaḷip parappuka’ eṉṟu katiravaṉi vaḻipaṭutal namakku ēṟkumā? ‘veppattip pōkkik kuḷir kāṟṟu vīcuka’ eṉṟu kāṟṟukkuriya kaṭavuḷākiya ñāyiṟṟi vaḻipaṭutal namakku ēṟkumā? ivvāṟellām piṟa teyvaṅ kaḷi vaḻipaṭutal ēṟkumā? ēlātā? eṉṟu maṉam cuḻala vēṇṭā. talivar mīṇṭuviṭṭār” eṉṟu tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟutal. (maḻiyiyum kāṟṟiyum veppat toṭu taruvatu ñāyiṟē eṉṟaṟika. avaṉē pālikkut teyvam eṉpār oru cārār.)	(kali. 16)
talivaṉ poruḷ kuṟittup piriyaluṟutalik kaṇṭōr kūṟiyatu -
talivaṉ poruḷvayiṉ pirintu pōkiṉṟavaṉi vaḻi yiṭik kaṇṭōr tammuḷ kūṟik koṇṭatu.
“aṟivuṭiyīrē! muyaṟci koṇṭu oru ceyalic ciṟappuṟac ceytu muṭippataṉi eḷitu eṉṟu niṉiyaṉmiṉ. poruḷ īṭṭum muyaṟciyāṉ taliviyi viṭuttuk kāṭṭuvaḻiyil, talivaṉ taṉ neñcam illattukku mīḷvatu karutuva taṉiyum ēṟkāmal, pirintu pōyirukkiṟāṉ. vaḷam cāṉṟa maṉikkaṇ talivaṉip pirintu amitiyākat taṅkiyiruk kum iḷiya taliviyiṉ taṉimit tuṉpam tāṅka oṇṇā tatu. kulamakaḷir nāṇattāṉ tam kaṇavar piriyumpōtu tāmum uṭaṉ varuvatākak kūṟāmal irukkiṉṟaṉar. at takiya nāṇam peritum iraṅkutaṟkuriyatu!” eṉṟa  kūṟṟu. iviyellām pālit tiṇikku viravum poruḷām.
