(to. po. 45 nac.)
talivaṉ pōkku uṭaṉpaṭutal -
talivaṉ maṟuttum, tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatāl avaṉ taliviyi uṭaṉkoṇṭu cella icital.
“pañcum malarum muḷḷeṉa nōkum meṉ cīṟaṭikaḷi yuṭiya ivaḷi, nañcō aḻalō eṉa varuttic cuṭum curattil, vaḻi naṭattik koṇṭēkat tuṇinta eṉ neñcam koṭitē! (tañci kō. 308) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 182)
talivaṉ, “maṭalēṟutal periyōrkkut takātu” eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟiyavaḻi, ataṉi maṟuttuk kūṟutal -
talivaṉtōḻikkuk kūṟuvatāka nikaḻum kuṟaḷ 1133-aik aṭuttuvarum talippil kāṇka.
“periyōrkaḷukku uriya nāṇattiyum nallāṇmiyi yum paṇṭu uṭiyēṉāyiruntēṉ. iṉṟō kāmam kāḻk koḷpavar tamakkup pukalākak koṇṭa maṭalēṟutaliyē virumpukiṉṟēṉ” (kuṟaḷ 1133) eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa...
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘maṭalmākkūṟum iṭaṉumār uṇṭē’ eṉpuḻi, ‘iṭaṉ’ eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭa toru kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ maṭal ēṟuvatākak kūṟak kēṭṭa tōḻi, avaṉ nāṇ uṭiyavaṉātaliṉ atu muṭiyātu eṉṟu kūṟiya viṭattē, avaṉ kūṟiyatu -
“kātal niṟivuṟāta tuṉpattip poṟukka muṭiyāta eṉ uṭampum uyirum, maṭalēṟutaṟku iṭiyūṟāka uḷḷa nāṇatti  nīkkit tamakku nalam tarum maṭalēṟutali niṉik kiṉṟaṉa” eṉṟu talivaṉ tāṉ nāṇam nīkkiyatik kūṟutal.		(kuṟaḷ 1132)
talivaṉ maṭal ēṟuvatākak kūṟak kēṭṭa tōḻi, avaṉ nāṇ māttiramē yaṉṟi nallāṇmiyum uṭiyaṉātaliṉ atu muṭiyātu eṉṟu kūṟiya viṭattē, avaṉ kūṟiyatu -
“nī kūṟum nāṇamum nallāṇmiyum eṉakku muṉpu iruntaṉa. talivipāl kāmuṟṟapiṉ ippōtu avaṟṟi iḻantuviṭṭēṉ. itupōtu eṉṉiṭamuḷḷatu, kāmam mikuntavarkkup pukal ākiya maṭaloṉṟumē” eṉṟu tali vaṉ tāṉ nāṇattiyum nallāṇmiyiyum nīkki maṭal ēṟat tuṇintamiyit tōḻikkuk kūṟutal.	(kuṟaḷ 1133)
talivaṉ maṇṭilattu arumi kūṟip pirital-
talivaṉ poruḷ varuvāy tarum vēṟṟu nāṭukaḷiṉ ciṟappiṉik kūṟi, avaṟṟik kippaṟṟat tāṉ piriyu miṭattuk kūṟutal 	(to. po. 41. nac.)
talivaṉ, pakivaruṭiya nāṭṭiṉ ciṟappiṉik kūṟi ataṉik kippaṟṟap piriyumiṭattuk kūṟutal.	(44. iḷa)
a) talivaṉ poruḷ tēṭa vēṟṟuppulaṅkaḷukkuc cellu miṭattu avviṭaṅkaḷ taliviyum uṭaṉ vantu taṅkum iyalpiṉa alla eṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ vacativāyppu iṉmi kūṟip pirital.
ā) taṉvali tuṇivali viṉivali ivaṟṟāl tam pakivar nāṭukaḷit taṉ pakivar kippaṟṟiyami kūṟi, avarkaḷ toṭarntu nāṭukaḷi veṉṟuvara vāyppaḷikkāmal, avarkaḷi aṭakkutaṟkut tāṉ piriyum piriviṉkaṇ taliviyiṭattuk kūṟal	(aruṇācalam piḷḷi)
i) iyaṟkiyum ceyaṟkiyumākiya araṇkaḷik koṇṭa pakivartam nāṭṭiṉ arumippāṭṭik kūṟi, ataṉik kippaṟṟat tāṉ piriyum ceytiyik kūṟutal.(po.225 kuḻa.)
itaṉit ’talivaṉ pakivayiṉ pirital’ eṉpār pāratiyār.
talivaṉ matiyuṭampaṭuttal- 
talivaṉ tōḻiyiṉ aṟivit taṉ pakkalil uṟutip paṭuttik koḷḷutal.
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ piṉ taliviyiṉ uṭal vēṟupāṭu mutaliya kaṇṭu aiyuṟṟa tōḻi, talivaṉ, taḻiyum kaṇṇi yum koṇṭu taliviyum tāṉum oruṅkēyirukkum iṭam nōkki vantu patiyum peyarum piṟavum viṉavi, annilattu vantaṟiyātāṉum avari muṉpu kaṇṭaṟiyātāṉum pōla, annilattiṟkum avari viṉātaṟkum poruttamāṉa vaṟṟic colliya aḷavilē, “ivaḷ vēṟupaṭṭatu ivaṉ kāraṇamākap pōlum! ivaṉ irantu piṉniṉṟatu ivaḷ kāraṇamākap pōlum!” eṉat tōḻi taṉ maṉattuḷ uṟuti ceyyumāṟu, talivaṉ tōḻiyiṉ aṟivi oruppaṭuttal.			(iṟi. a. 6. uri)
talivaṉ (perumakaṉ) mayaṅkal -
tōḻi, “iravukkuṟi vāraṟka” eṉat taṭuttataṉik kēṭṭut talivaṉ tikittu varuntutal.
“tirumakaḷ ittaliviyuruvil eṉakkuk kiṭṭiruppatu karutiyē eṉṉuyir nilittuḷḷatu. ivaḷi yāṉ evvāṟu pirintu variyumaḷavum āṟṟiyiruttal kūṭum?” (kōvi. 169) eṉṟāṟ pōlak karutit talivaṉ mayaṅkutal.
itaṉi ‘āṟṟātu urittal’ eṉṉum tirukkōviyār (169). itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 158)
talivaṉ (uṭaṉ pōkkiṉi) maṟuttal -
kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭapiṉ, tōḻi taliviyi uṭaṉkoṇṭu pōmāṟu talivaṉiṭam kūṟiyaviṭattu, avaṉ ataṉi maṟuttu urittal.
“tōḻī! talivitamar ippār muḻutumē paricapporu ḷākak kēṭpārkaḷō? mēlum, eṉ ūr arukilum illi. kāṭṭup pālivaḻiyil ivaḷ eṉṉuṭaṉ taṉiyē varutalum iyalāta tākumē!” (tañci. kō. 306) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘arumi urittal’ eṉṉum. (201) ikkūṟṟu variviyaluḷ ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatu.	(na. a.182)
talivaṉ, ‘māli ēntiya peṇṭirum makkaḷum, kēḷir oḻukkattup pukaṟcik kaṇ’ kūṟal -
talivaṉi etirkoṇṭu maṅkalamāka māli ēntiniṉṟa peṇṭirum makkaḷum kēḷirum oḻukum oḻukkattu virup piṉkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum. īṇṭu oḻukkamāvatu, “collātu peyarntīr” eṉavum, “iḷamiyum kāmamum nōkkātu peyarntīr” eṉavum kūṟi “itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉi?” eṉat talivaṉ vantuḻi, avar nikaḻttum nikaḻcci.
“aḷavu kaṭanta pācattāl nuṅkaḷiyē niṉittu niṉittu urukumāṟu ivvulakattiṉ paṇpu ippaṭip pācatti vaḷarttup piriviṭi vāṭṭukiṟatē eṉṟu ulaka vāḻvi niṉittu mayaṅkiyē kālattik kaṭattiṉēṉ” (kuṟun. 99) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
akkēḷir ceyyum etirkōṭal oḻukkattiṉkaṇ talivaṉ uḷḷam makiḻnturittal; peṇṭirum makkaḷumākiya kēḷir eṉka.
“pāka! talivi irukkum ūrkku namtēr vantuviṭṭatu. kiḷiyōr nalvaravu kūṟa, nam illattu etirē kūṭi viṭṭaṉar. tāyarum putalvarum tām muṉpu aṟiyāta aḷavu kaṭanta uvaki mika namakkuc ciṟappuc ceytalik kāṇpōm. nam kutirikaḷum ōyntuviṭṭaṉa”eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 146. nac.)
“talivaṉ mika aṉpu ceyka” eṉṟu teyvattukkup parāvutal -
“uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ, talivaṉ taliviyi aṉpāka naṭatta vēṇṭum” eṉṟu naṟṟāy teyvatti vēṇṭutal.
“eṉ makaḷik kaṭumiyāṉa pālivaḻiyē aḻittuc ceṉṟa talivaṉ, avaḷiṭam maṉattē aṉpu koṇṭu iṉcol pēci, taṉ mārpiṉiyē taliyaṇiyākak koṭuttu avaḷi uṟaṅkac ceyvāṉāka! ataṟku nī aruḷal vēṇṭum!” (akanā. 35) eṉṟu naṟṟāy teyvatti vēṇṭutal.
itu variviyaluḷ ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭ ṭatu.	(i. vi. 538 uri)
talivaṉ ‘mīṭṭu varavu āynta vakiyiṉkaṇ’ kūṟal (1) -
pirinta talivaṉ, iṭiccurattut taliviyiṉ uruvu veḷippaṭṭuḻiyum maṉam vēṟupaṭṭuḻiyum mīṇṭu varutali ārāynta kūṟupāṭṭiṉkaṇ kūṟutal.
poruḷvayiṉ pirinta talivaṉ iṭiccurattu āṟṟā ṉākit taliviyiṉ uruveḷit tōṟṟam nōkki, “muṉpu yāṉ viṭipeṟum poḻutu kūntali virittu ataṉuḷ maṟintu aḻintēṅkit tuṉpuṟṟu niṉṟa nam kātaliyiṉ varuntiya nōkkam ipperuṅkāṭṭiṭik kaṭantum eṉ etiril eyta vantatē! iḵtu eṉṉē!” (naṟ. 113) eṉṟu aḻuṅkik kūṟal.
poruḷīṭṭat taliviyip pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ, “kaḷiṟu niṉṟa iṭattuc ciṟunīr kaḻittup peyarnta īrattil vayiṟṟip poruttip paṭuttuk kiṭanta cennāyppiṇi paciyāl muṭaṅkik kiṭakka, vēṭṭimēṟ ceṉṟa cennāy iri peṟāmiyāl taṉatu piṇavi niṉittu varuntum kāṭṭilē, nām ippoḻutu varuntik kiṭakkiṟōm. toṭarntu celvōmā? aṉṟi, ūrkku mīḷvōmā? neñcē! iraṇṭaṉuḷ oṉṟu col. uṉmuṭivē eṉ muṭivu” (naṟ. 103) eṉṟu taliviyi niṉittu maṉam vēṟupaṭṭut talivaṉ mīṭṭu varavu āyntatu.
“talivi nampirivāl vāṭi uyirpōkum niliyiliruppāḷ eṉṟu avaḷiyē niṉittu varuntikkoṇṭirukkum namvaliya neñcam, āḷ vaḻakkaṟṟa koṭiya pāliyi nīntip poruḷ tēṭip piṟaku nammoṭu mīṇṭu varuvatākat teriyavilli!”(aiṅ. 329) eṉa mīḷaluṟṟa neñciṉi niṉintu talivaṉ uḻiyarkku urittal.
“muṉpu taliviyip pirintu poruḷīṭṭac ceṉṟapōtu mūṅkil kāyumāṟu veyiliṉ veppamikka pāli, ip poḻutu taliviyi niṉittu mīḷkiyāl kuḷircciyāka eṉakkut tōṉṟukiṟatu!” (aiṅ. 322) eṉṟu mīḷkiṉṟavaṉ iṭiccurattut talivi kuṇam niṉintu iraṅkutal.
‘iraṭṭuṟa moḻital’ eṉpataṉāṉ talivaṉ ceyviṉi muṟṟi mīṇṭu varuṅkāl varunti neñciṭam, “vaḷamali nāṭaṉ iḷamakaḷākiya eṉ taliviyi makiḻvoṭu kūṭum nāḷ ennāḷō!” eṉavum, “aḻakiya kuṭimakkaḷiyuṭiya cīṟūrkaḷ niṟinta kuṉṟukaḷik kaṭantu katiravaṉ maṟiyi ṉum, ūr cēymittu eṉṟu karutātu, kutiriyik kaṭukac celutti mīṇṭu varum eṉṉiviṭa eṉ neñcam virintu ūrukkuc ceṉṟuviṭṭatu. atu, kaṉṟukaḷ tāyppacuvik kaṇṭu pāl aruntum māliyil, palli col kēṭṭu naṉṉimittam kaṇṭu niṟkum taliviyiṉ kaṇkaḷip putittu avaḷ kūntalit tīṇṭi avaḷ tōḷkaḷit taḻuvi makiḻntu koṇṭirukkum!” (akanā. 9) eṉavum kūṟuvaṉavum, piṟavum ellām koḷḷappaṭum.	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ ‘mīṭṭuvara vāynta vakiyiṉkaṇ’ kūṟal (2) -
pirinta talivaṉ mīṇṭu vantavaḻi eṭuttukkūṟal.
“vāṉamē! nī miṉṉi iṭittu naṉṟāka maḻiyip poḻika! yāṉ eṭutta viṉiyi muṭitta talimiyāṉa uḷḷat tōṭu eṉ taliviyi eytiavaḷ kūntaliyē mel laṇiyākak koṇṭu makiḻkiṉṟēṉ” (kuṟun. 270) eṉṟu mīṇṭu vanta talivaṉ kūṟal...	(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
talivaṉ mulliyi nōkkik kūṟal -
poruḷvayiṉ pirintu ceṉṟu kār kālattil mīḷum talivaṉ taṉ pākaṉiṭam, talivi taṉṉic ciṉantu kūṟuvāḷ eṉṟu collit tēri viriviṟ celuttumāṟu vēṇṭiyapōtu, mulli nilattik kaṇṭu, “talivi ciṉattaṟkum varuntutaṟkum kāraṇam mēkaṅkaḷ cūl koṇṭu eḻuntami māttiram aṉṟu; mulli nilattuc cēvaṟkōḻi. taṉ pēṭikku uṇavit tāṉ taṉ kālka ḷāl kiḷaṟik kāṭṭum kāṭciyi avaḷ kāṇpatēyām” eṉṟu kūṟutal.
itu ‘variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ampikā. 321)
talivaṉ mūṉṟaṉ pakuti kūṟip pirital -
a.	talivaṉ taliviyiṭam, “aṟattiṉāṉ poruḷākki apporuḷiṉāṉ kāmam nukartaṟku viḻikiṉṟēṉ. ātaliṉ, apporuḷīṭṭutal karutip pirival” eṉṟu kūṟip pirital. (41 nac.)
ā.	‘viṉivaliyum taṉvaliyum māṟṟāṉ valiyum.
		tuṇivaliyum tūkkic ceyal’ (kuṟaḷ 471) eṉpatu aṟi vuṭimi ātaliṉ, talivaṉ taṉ valimi, tuṇi valimi, viṉivalimi ivaṟṟic cīrtukkip pakivari ṉum, valimi mikkāṉāyavaḻiyē pakivarmēṟ cēṟaṟkaṇ, acceytiyit talivikkuk kūṟip pirital.	(44. iḷa.)
i.	poruḷ tēṭutal, ōtutal, tūtupōtal ākiyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṉit talivikkuk kūṟi ataṟkākap pirital	(41 pārati)
ī.	tāṉi, yāṉi, kutiri eṉṟa muppaṭikaḷiṉ valimiyit talivikku eṭuttuk kūṟit talivaṉ māṟṟārip paṇittaṟkup pirital.			(aruṇācalampiḷḷi.)
talivaṉ meytoṭṭup payiṟaṟkaṇ kūṟiyatu -
taliviyiṉ  meyyiṉit talivaṉ tīṇṭip paḻakutalākiya mey toṭṭup payiṟal iyaṟkip puṇarccikku muṉ nikaḻum eṉpar oru cārār; iṭantalippāṭṭiṉ muṉ nikaḻum eṉpar oru cārār,
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ piṉṉarp perunāṇiṉaḷ ākiya talivi maṟunāḷum mutal nāḷ cantitta iṭattil talivaṉ varukiyi etir nōkki niṟpāḷō eṉiṉ, tāṉ piṟanta kuṭikkuriya ciṟanta oḻukkattiṟkut takātatu ceytāḷātaliṉ, vētavitippaṭi vāḻum antaṇaṉic cantikkum vēta neṟiyi vaḻuviya antaṇaṉuṭiya maṉaniliyum, vēṭki mikutiyāṉ cūṭāṉa poruḷi viḻuṅki ataṉāl toṇṭi mutaliyaṉa puṇpaṭṭavar maṉaniliyum koṇṭu, neñcum maṉaaṭakkamum taṭumāṟi, “iṉic ceyyat takkatu yātu?” eṉa niṉittu, talivaṉ taṉ tōḻiyar kūṭiyirukkum iṭattiṟku varuvāṉō eṉṟa accamum, vārātu maṟantu viṭuvāṉō eṉṟa kātalum mika, puliyaṉ tīmpāl pōla (pāl virumpattakkatāyiṉum, ataṉik koṇarntavaṉ iṭattuḷḷa aruvaruppāl pālum vēṇṭātatu pōla, kāma iṉpam cuviyāṉateṉiṉum ataṉi muṟiyāka maṇantu piṉ nukarātu kaḷavāṉ nukarvatāl aḵtu aru varupput tarukiṟatu.) maṉam koḷḷāmal kalakkam koṇṭa maṉaniliyuṭiyavaḷāy, nāṇatti maṟantu, kātalāl untappaṭṭu, mutalnāḷ talivaṉic cantitta avviṭat tiṟkē celvāḷ. aṅṅaṉam ceṉṟu niṉṟavaḷit talivaṉ, ivvoḻukkam puṟattār ikaḻumāṟu veḷippaṭa ataṉāl vēṟupaṭṭāḷkollō eṉavum, maṟi veḷippaṭṭu viṭṭatāyiṉ iṉimēl maṇantu koḷḷutali virumpu vāḷē yaṉṟi ikkaḷavoḻukkattiṟku uṭaṉpaṭāḷē eṉavum, karutumāṟu meyyuṟat tīṇṭiniṉṟu kuṟippaṟitaṟkaṇ kūṟiyatu.
“eṉ ki ittaliviyit tīṇṭumtōṟum eṉ uyir taḷirttalāl, ippētiyiṉ tōḷkaḷ amiḻtattāl amikkappaṭṭaṉa. pōlum!” (kuṟaḷ 1106) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭu tal pōlvaṉa meytoṭṭup payiṟalām.			(to. po. 102 nac.)
talivaṉ melivu viḷakkuṟuttuk kūṟal -
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ muṉ talivaṉ taṉ uḷḷattil mikkuk kiṭakkum kāmanōyāṉ puṟattu nikaḻum  taḷarviṉit talivikkuk ‘kāmam kimmikiṉ tāṅkutal eḷitō” (naṟ. 39) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa coṟkaḷāṉ eṭuttuk kūṟutal eṉpatu ōr uri.	(to. po. 98 iḷa.)
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉpiṉ pirivāl taṉakkuḷḷa varut tattit talivi maṉam koḷḷumāṟu, avaḷ karuṅkuḻal kaṟṟi maruṅku tirutti, aḷakamum nutalum takipeṟa nīvi, ākamum tōḷum aṇi peṟat tivantu, kuḷirppak kūṟit taḷirppa  muyaṅkik kuṟippākap pirivu aṟivitta talivaṉ, avaḷ varuttattik kuṟippāṉ uṇarntu, “niṉṉiṟ piriyēṉ, piriyiṉ āṟṟēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟutal.			(iṟi. a. 2 uri)
taliviyip pirivatāl taṉakkuḷḷa varuttattit talivi maṉam koḷḷak kūṟutalum, talivi varuttam kuṟippāṉ uṇarntu atu tīrak kūṟutalum ākiya talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
“iruvēmāy oruvēmākiya namakku nīṅkāk kātalōṭu uyir oru cēra nīṅkuka!” (kuṟun.57) eṉavum, ‘nī añcal!’ eṉṟu yāṉ colliya eṉ col poykkum eṉa añcaṟka! yāṉ ivvulakamē niṉṉip pirivatāl kiṭṭumāyiṉum ataṉi vēṇṭēṉ” (kuṟun. 300) eṉṟāṟ pōlavum kūṟutal. 	(101 nac.)
talivaṉ, “yām maṟintu ceṉṟu ivaṉ kaṇṇip putittāl, taliniṉṟoḻukum parattiyar peyar kūṟuvāṉ!” eṉṟu uṭkoṇṭu kāmakkiḻattiyātal taliviyātal ceṉṟu kaṇ putittuḻi, kūṟutal -
“kāntaḷkuli pōṉṟa aḻakiya kikaḷāl eṉ kaṇṇip pottiya eṉ tuṇiyākiya āṇmayil pōlum cāyali yuṭiya maṭantiyē!niṉṉit tavira eṉ neñcattu iṭaṅkoṇṭār yār?” (aiṅ. 293) eṉṟāṟpōlat talivaṉ maṟumoḻi kūṟal.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘paṇṇami pakuti’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ oru kūṟṟu	(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉ vaṇṭiṉi muṉṉiliyākkic colvaḻippaṭuttu naṉṉayamurittal -
muṉṉiliyākāta vaṇṭiṉit taṉmuṉṉē aḻittu, vārtti collum iyalpiṟṟu allāta ataṉi maṟumoḻi taruvatākak kaṟpittu, ataṉiṭam tāṉ talivi yiṭattuk koṇṭa mikka kātalit terivippatu.
“naṟumaṇattit tērvati vāḻkkit toḻilāka uṭiya aḻakiya ciṟakukaḷiyuṭiya tumpiyē! nī eṉ nilattu vaṇṭu ākiyāl eṉakkākak kūṟātu uṇmiyāka nī kaṇṭa taṉik kūṟu. eṉ taliviyiṉ kūntaliviṭa naṟumaṇam mikkirukkum malarkaḷ uḷavō?” (kuṟun. 2) eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟumāṟu.	(to. po. 101 nac.)
iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ muṉnikaḻvatu eṉṟa karuttum uṇṭu. 	(98 iḷa.)
talivaṉ (vaṇṭuṟi tārōṉ) vantu etirppaṭutal -
tāṅkāta vēṭkiyuṭaṉ kāttirunta talivaṉ taliviyi etirppaṭṭu muyaṅki makiḻtal.
“mēkaliyi oliyāmal paṟṟikkoṇṭu, cilampukaḷi aciyātapaṭi mēlē kaṭukki, añcattakka ariyāmattil īṇṭu vantu taṅkum tirumakaḷ pōṉṟa nīvir virumpuvatu yātō?” (kōvi. 164) eṉpatu pōlak kūṟikkoṇṭu talivaṉ taliviyinōkki varutal.
itu ‘taḷarvu akaṉṟu urittal’ (kōvi. 164) eṉpa taṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ iravukkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 158)
talivaṉ, ‘varital vēṇṭit tōḻi ceppiya puritīr kiḷavi pulliya etir’ viṉkaṇ kūṟal -
“talivaṉ taliviyi virivil varital vēṇṭum” eṉṉum karuttāṉ tōḻiyāṉ collappaṭṭa kuṟṟam tīrnta kūṟṟukku maṟumoḻiyākap piṉṉum kaḷavoḻuk kattit toṭartal vēṇṭum karuttoṭu talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“neñcē! nalla urikaḷ nīṅkip payaṉaṟṟa urikaḷ pēcap paṭṭu, maḻi nīri ēṟṟa pacumaṭpāṇṭam pōla, āci veḷḷattil nīnti nī atikamāka ācippaṭukiṟāy. peṇ kuraṅku taṉ kuṭṭiyāṉ taḻuvappaṭṭu makiḻvatu pōla, uṉ vārttikaḷic cevimaṭuttu uṉakku utavuvār iruppiṉ uṉ āci niṟivēṟum. aḵtu illāta niliyil nī yātu ceyya iyalum?” (kuṟun. 29) eṉṟu talivaṉ taṉmaṉat tiṟkuk kūṟutal.	(to. po. 105 iḷa.)
ittuṟiyit talivi kūṟṟākkuvār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
varital viruppiṉāṉ tōḻi talivaṟku varivu kaṭāvik kūṟiya uyarcci nīṅkiya iyaṟpaḻittali uṭkoṇṭu avviyaṟpaḻittali maṟuttāḷ pōlat talivi kūṟum iyaṟpaṭa moḻiyum ceytikaḷ.
tōḻi talivaṉit tāṉ uṟṟa  cūḷ pēṇāṉ poyttāṉ eṉṟu iyaṟpaḻittavaḻit talivi “kuṉṟakal naṉṉāṭaṉ vāymiyil poy tōṉṟutal eṉpatu cantiraṉil tīttōṉṟuvatu pōṉṟa nikaḻā nikaḻcci. ‘añcal vēṇṭā’ eṉṟu taṉṉāṉ kūṟappaṭṭavari avaṉ poyt tataṟku uriyaṉō?” (kali.41) eṉa iyaṟpaṭa moḻital pōlvaṉa.	(107 nac.)
talivaṉ variviṭi vittu vēntaṟku uṟṟuḻip pirintuḻit taliviyiṉ varuttamikuti kaṇṭār kūṟiyatu -
“katiravaṉ ivvulakattē toṉmiyāṉa taṉ iyalpiṉāl katirūṉṟip pakal ceytu. ‘pirintiruntāri yāṉ varuttu taṟku nī maṟika’ eṉṟu kāmaṉ kūṟiya kāriyattit taṉ tali yilē ēṟaṭṭukkoṇṭu pōvāṉ pōla attakiriyi aṭintu, kaṇ taṉ payaṉ keṭumāṟu maṟiyavē, tīviṉi yip pōkkupavaṉatu aruḷ koṇṭamukampōla matiyam tōṉṟi iruḷi aḻikkaluṟa, vaṟiyavaratu illaṟam pōlak kaḻimalarkaḷ kūmpa, tēykiṉṟa eṉṉuyirp puṟattē vantu viṭṭa māliyē! nī aṟiviṉi mayakkum māliyāy iruntāy.
“mālāy! kaṇavarōṭu iṉpuṟṟa makaḷirkku nēyamāy avarkaḷ tammiṟ kūṭum niliyiyē muṉpu mikac ceytu pōntāy. itupōtu, kātalar tām aḻumāṟu nītta at tuṉpattuḷ kalakka muṟṟa makaḷiri varuttam ceytal niṉakkut takkataṉṟē!
“mālāy! tammuṭ kūṭiṉartam kāmatti makaḷirkkut tuṇiyāka āṭavar maṉattiṉiṉṟu nīṅkātu kaṉaṉṟu eriyum taṉmiyi muṉpu mikac ceytupōntāy. itupōtu, makaḷirtam nalam kavarntukoṇṭu nalkāta āṭavar pirinta am mikka taṉimikkaṇ, av alanta makaḷirkkut tuṇi yākāmal varuttamātal niṉakkut takkataṉṟē!
“mālāy! em kēḷvaṉit tarutalum ceytili; ātaliṉ emakku nī tuṇi alli. tam kēḷvaṉip pirinta makaḷirkku oru nōyāki, kūṭiṉavarkku iṉpattiṟkoru teppamāki ivvāṟu naṉmiyākāta ceyalkaḷic ceyta lallatu naṉmiyāka nī ceyyum kāriyam illiyō?” - eṉṟivvāṟu talivi varuntiṉāḷ. avaḷatu avalam nīṅka, ñāyiṟu iruṭ parappiṉi nīkkiyavāṟu pōla, pakivartam nilattiṉik kavarntu tam ceyviṉi muṭitta kātalar avaḷ pāl mīṇṭār. ivvāṟu kaṇṭōr kūṟiṉar.	(kali. 148)
talivaṉ, varivuṭaṉpaṭutaṟkaṇ tōḻiyiṭam kūṟal -
virivil varital vēṇṭum eṉṟu tōḻi ceppiya puri tīr kiḷaviyik kēṭṭut talivaṉ ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭutaṟkaṇ kūṟṟu nikaḻttutal.
“tōḻi!nam kaḷavoḻukkattāl taliviyiṉ kaṟpukku ētam varumō eṉṟu añci nī āvēcamuṟṟavaḷ pōla naṭuṅku vataṟku, nāṉ ciṟitu ciṟitāka iraṅki varunti, virivil taliviyi varintukōṭaṟku āvaṉa ceyval” (kuṟun. 52) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 105 iḷa.)
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar itaṉit talivikūṟṟākkuvār. (107 nac.)		(ain. eḻu. 3. kuṟun. 34)
talivaṉ varivu vēṇṭiviṭat tamar maṟuttuḻi, avar kēṭkumāṟṟāl talimakaṭkuc colluvāḷāyt tōḻi aṟattoṭu niṉṟatu -
“tōḻi! nam ūril variciyāka viḷaṅki vīḻum aruviyi uṭiya nam malināṭaṉām talivaṉ taṉṉic cērntā riṭam iṭiyaṟāta naṭpuṭiyavaṉ eṉiṉum, nam tanti taṉṉiyari irantāyiṉum niṉṉi maṉiviyākak koḷḷātu, aṅṅaṉam irattal taṉ perumikku iḻukku eṉṟu viṭuttāṉāyiṉ, nām uyirvāḻtal yāṅkaṉam iyalum?” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.	(aiṅ. 228)
talivaṉ vaṟuṅkaḷam nāṭi maṟukal -
talivi illāmiyāl vaṟumiyuṟṟu veṟuppaḷitta kuṟi yiṭatti aṭintu talivaṉ varunti maṉam puṇṇuṟutal.
“mayilkāḷ!kāṉavar (uṟaviṉar) taliviyi, ‘illiṟkuc cel’ eṉṟapōtu, avaḷ kālkaḷ illam nōkki evvāṟu naṭantuceṉṟaṉavō?” (kappaṟ. 141) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pakaṟkuṟi iṭiyīṭu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 156)
talivaṉ vaṟumpuṉam kaṇṭu vāṭik kūṟal -
talivaṉ, tiṉi koyyappaṭṭut taliviyatu kāval illātu pāḻttuk kiṭanta tiṉippuṉattik kaṇṭu varunti urittal.
“pāṇṭiyaṉatu maturinakar pōṉṟa iyaṟkiyaḻakuṭiya taliviyi avaḷ viḷiyāṭum iṭamākiya ittiṉip puṉattilum kāṇum vāyppuk kiṭṭavilli. avaḷ tali mayir muṭiyumāṟu kūṭiyati aṟintu avaḷukkup paruvam vantuviṭṭatu eṉṟu avaḷ uṟaviṉar avaḷi iṟceṟittu viṭṭaṉarpōlum!” (tiṇimāli.4) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘parivuṟṟu meliyiṉum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.	(to. po. 103 nac.)
talivaṉ (peruntaki) viruntu iṟi virumpal -
pāṅkiyiṉ vēṇṭukōṭku iṇaṅkiya talivaṉ viruntiṉa ṉāka vantu taṅkutali virumputal.
iṟi - iṟuttal - taṅkutal
“mayilaṉiyīr!num kiyāl toṭappaṭṭa poruḷ etuvāyiṉum eṉakku amutamākum. katiravaṉ maṟiyum nērattil ippoḻil taṅkutaṟkum iṉitāka uḷḷatu. uṅkaḷ maṉiyum kāṉavar kāvalō tēvar kāvalō uṭiyataṉṟu” (ampikā. 163) eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 149)
talivaṉ, vēntaṉ viṉimuṭippāṉ pōṉṟu muṭiyātavaḻip pākaṉiṭam nontu kūṟal -
“vēntaṉ tiṟikoṇṭu mīḷvāṉ; nāmum ūr mīḷalām” eṉṟu talivaṉ niṉitta nērattil, avvēntaṉ tiṟip poruḷip peṟātu mīṇṭum pōrit toṭarntāṉāka, atu kaṇṭu vēntaṟku uṟṟuḻi utavac ceṉṟa talivaṉ, “aracaṉ pōriṉi muṭittiruntāl, mēṭukaḷit tūḷākkik koṇṭu cellum uruḷikaḷ kōkkappaṭṭa tērilē, tammik kaṭṭivittirukkum iṭaṅkaḷi veṟuttu virintu celaṟku āyattamāka niṟkum kutirikaḷ pūṭṭiyuḷḷa nili yil, tēri nērē ūrukkut tiruppi virivil taliviyik kāṇalām. āṉāl ataṟkuriya vāyppu iṉṟu illiyē!” (aiṅ. 449) eṉṟāṟpōṉṟu pākaṉiṭam varuntik kūṟal.			(to. po. 41 nac.)
talivaṉ ‘ vēḷāṇ etirum viruppiṉkaṇ’ kūṟal -
talivi talivaṉukku upakāram ceyyum viruppiṉāṉ iravukkuṟiyiṉ ētatti viḷakki iravukkuṟiyi vilakkiya vaḻit talivaṉ kūṟutalum, talivi  ceyta upakāratti maṉam koṇṭu kuṟiyiṭattu avaḷip pukaḻntu kūṟutalum.
pakaṟkuṟiyiṭattut talivi taṉṉuṭiya kaṇkaḷip putitta vaḻi, “kāntaṭ kuli pōṉṟa aḻakiya kikaḷāl eṉ kaṇ kaḷip pottiya mayil pōṉṟa cāyalāy! paṭukkiyil eṉakkut tuṇiyāka utavum uṉṉiyaṉṟi eṉ neñcat tiruppār piṟar uḷarō?” (aiṅ. 293) eṉṟal pōlat taṉ kātaṉmi veḷippaṭat talivaṉ avaḷiṭam kūṟutalum.			(to. po. 105 iḷa.)
talivi vēḷāṇmi ceyya etirkoḷḷak karututal kāraṇattāṉ, tōḻi avaṉi viruntākat taṅkumāṟu kūṟutaṟkaṇ talivi kūṟṟu tōḻiyiṭam kuṟippāka nikaḻtal. ‘viruntiṉ kaṇṇum’ eṉpatu pāṭam. 	(107 nac.)
māliyil talivi talivaṉ pirivu karutik kaṇṇīrviṭṭuk kuṟippāṉ avaṉ pōvataṉit taṭi ceyyumāṟu tōḻikku aṟivuṟutta, tōḻi talivaṉiṭam avaṉ pirivāl talivikku ētam nikaḻtalum kūṭum eṉṟu kūṟi, iravil tam mūril tamar viruntāka etir ēṟṟukkoḷḷa iḷiyaroṭum puraviyoṭum tam ūril taṅkic cellu māṟu vēṇṭutal (akanā. 300) itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭām.	(nac.)
talivaṉ ‘vēṟṟu nāṭṭakalvayiṉ viḻumattiṉ kaṇ’ kūṟal -
talivaṉ, kaṟpuk kālattil vēṟṟu nāṭṭukkuc cellat taliviyip piriyum kālattu, pirivu oruppaṭṭa piṉ, “pōvōmā,  tavirvōmā?” eṉṟu taṉakku nikaḻum maṉa nikaḻcci paṟṟiya tuṉpattiṉkaṇ kūṟutal.
“maṉamē!kaṭalnīr pōla nī alintu koṇṭirukkiṉ ṟāyē aṉṟi, uṟutiyāṉa kōṭpāṭuṭiyāy alli. it taliviyip pirintu vaṟaṇṭa pālivaḻiyē ceṉṟu poruḷīṭṭi varavum añcukiṟāy; it taliviyoṭu maṉikkaṇṇēyē makiḻvōṭu iruppō meṉiṉum vaṟumikku añcukiṟāy. nī aḻitarumāṟu uṭivāyāka!” (akanā. 123) eṉat talivaṉ maṉattiṟkuk kūṟutal.			(to. po. 144 iḷa.)
aṅṅaṉam vēṟṟunāṭṭiṉ piriyuṅ kālattut tāṉ uṟum iṭumpiyiṭattut talivaṉ kūṟṟunikaḻum.
viḻumamāvaṉa: piriyak karutiyavaṉ, paḷḷiyiṭattuk kaṉaviṟ “pōvōmō, tavirvōmō” eṉa varuntik kūṟuvaṉavum, “ivaḷ nalaṉ tiriyum” eṉṟalum, “piriyuṅkol?” eṉṟu aiyuṟṟa taliviyi aiyan tīrak kūṟalum, neñciṟkuc colli aḻuṅkutalum, piṟavumām.
talivaṉ taliviyi aḷavukku mīṟip pārāṭṭa, ap pārāṭṭiṉāl talivi makiḻāmal talivaṉ piriyak karutu vati aṟintu maṉattuḷ varuntit tuyilātirukka, paṭukki yil avaḷoṭu kūṭi avaḷtōḷiyē aṇiyākak koṇṭu uṟaṅkiya talivaṉ, taṉ kaṉavil, “pāli nilattik kaṭantu ceṉṟu iru peruvēntariyum cantu ceyvittu yāṉ mīṇṭu varumvari eṉ talivi ceyalaṟavu tōṉ ṟāmal āṟṟiyiruntu illaṟatti naṭattum āṟṟal uṭiya vaḷō?” eṉṟu araṟṟiyati avaḷ kēṭṭut tōḻikkuc colliyatu.	(kali. 24)
‘uṇṇāmiyiṉ’ (akanā.123) eṉṟa pāṭal “pōvōmā tavirvōmā” eṉṟatu.
“maṉamē! puli koṉṟu uṇṭu eñciya kaḷiṟṟiṉ uṭali yāḷi iḻuttuc cellum koṭiya kāṭṭuvaḻiyē, ivaḷi illattil viṭuttup poruḷ tēṭak karutic celliṉ, ivaḷ māmiaḻaku iṉṟoṭu pōyviṭum eṉpati niṉittuppār” (naṟ. 205) eṉṟu talivaṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟic celavaḻuṅkal.
“talivi! yāṉ poruḷ tēṭap puṟappaṭṭup pōtaṟkuriya tēriṉ celavit taṭukkum vakiyil vāṉavilli vaḷittu ikkār kālattil mēkam maḻi peyyat toṭaṅki viṭṭatu. araca kāriyam oṉṟaṉit tavira, maṟṟa etaṟkum pirital karutātu niṉṉip purattalit toṭaṅki viṭṭēṉ. eṉ poruḷvayiṉ  celavit tavirttu viṭṭēṉ” (aiṅ. 428) eṉṟu, talivi “pirivārō” eṉṟu koṇṭa aiyattit talivaṉ nīkkiyatu.
“neñcē! vaṟiyārkku ītal, tāmum nukartal eṉṉum iraṇṭum celvamillātārkku illi karuti, poruḷ tēṭac cūḻkiṟāy. nī pirintu poruḷ tēṭac cellumpōtu taliviyum uṭaṉ varuvāḷā? eṉṉi māttiram aṉuppap pōkiṟāyā?” (kuṟun. 63) eṉṟu (taliviyi ‘varukiṉṟā ḷaṉṟē’ eṉṟu) kūṟic celavaḻuṅkiyatu.(to. po. 146 nac.)
talivaṉukku, ‘nōkkuva ellām aviyē pōṟalik’ kaṇṭōr kūṟuvatu -
kaḷavuk kālattil vēṭki mikutiyāl talivaṉ nāṇat tiṉ elliyik kaṭantu, kāṇum poruḷkaḷil ellām taliviyiṉ toṭarpi uṭkoṇṭu, koṭiyit taliviyiṉ iṭi eṉṟu karutit taḻuvutaliyum, kāntaṭ pūvi avaḷ kikaḷākak koṇṭu makiḻtaliyum, karuviḷam pūkkaḷi avaḷ kaṇkaḷākak karutip paṟittuk koḷḷu taliyum, māntaḷiri avaḷ mēṉiyākak koṇṭu taṭavutaliyum, poḻilkaḷil ceṉṟa āṇmayilkaḷiṉ (kaṇṇukku iṉimi tōṟṟum) meṉmiyi avaḷ mēṉiyiṉ cāyalākak koṇṭu mayilkaḷiṉ piṉ toṭar taliyum, mūṅkilkaḷit taliviyiṉ tōḷkaḷākak koṇṭu tivarutaliyum, aṟal paṭṭa karumaṇali avaḷ kūntalākak koṇṭu ataṉ mītu puraḷutaliyum kaṇṭa varkaḷ talivaṉatu vēṭki mikuti kuṟittu viyantu kūṟutal.		(97 iḷa.)
talivaṉum taliviyum ōr ūriṉarātalik kaṇṭōr kūṟutal -
“cila yāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ivaṉ ivaḷuṭiya kūntali īrttup pūcaliṭuvāṉ. ivaḷum ivaṉ talimayiri vaḷittiḻuttu ōṭuvāḷ. tam cevilittāyar taṭuppavum viṭātu kāraṇamiṉṟic cila pūcalkaḷ viḷittuk koḷvar. ippoḻutu malarip piṇitta iraṭṭi māliyip pōṉṟu kāṭci vaḻaṅki maṇam purintu makiḻkiṟārkaḷ. it taku naṉmiyi uṇṭākkiya ūḻviṉi vāḻka!” (kuṟun. 229) eṉṟu talivaṉ taliviyarik kaṇṭōr kūṟum kūṟṟil avarkaḷ ōrūriṉarātal peṟappaṭum.			(to. po. 93 nac.)
talivaṉit tōḻi cēṭpaṭuttavaḻi, avaḷ kuṟippaṟintu avaṉ taṉṉuḷḷē colliyatu -
taṉ kuṟiyi ēṟkāmal ētō collik kālam tāḻttiya tōḻiyiṉ kuṟippaṟinta talivaṉ taṉ maṉattiṟkuḷ kūṟik koḷḷutal.
“taliviyiṉ tōḻi yārō ayalār kūṟuvatu pōla eṉ kūṟṟukku maṟumoḻi kūṟiṉum, accoṟkaḷil kaṭumi illi; ākavē eṉkuṟi nērappaṭum naṉmiyē teri kiṟatu; payaṉ virivil kāṇappaṭum” eṉṟu talivaṉ niṉittukkōṭal.	(kuṟaḷ 1096)
talivi -
‘tolkāppiyam kuṟippiṭum talivi kāṇka.
talivi, accam nīṭiyavaḻik kūṟal -
kaḷavuk kālattil talivikku varum accam mūvakippaṭum. aviyāvaṉa:1. “talivaṉ coṉṉa uṟutimoḻiyi niṟivēṟṟātatāl teyvam avaṉi varuttumō?” eṉṟu añcum accam, 2. “ikkaḷavoḻukkiṉit tanti taṉṉi yar aṟikiṉṟārō?” eṉṟu añcum accam. (3) kūṭṭa muṇmi uṇarnta tōḻikkum uṇmi kūṟutaṟku añciya accam eṉpaṉa. ivvaccam, talivaṉ varukiṉṟataṟku iṭiyīṭāka amiyum.
1. talivaṉ cūḷpiḻittataṉāl avaṉit teyvam varuttum eṉṟu karutiya talivi, “maṉṟattiṉkaṇ aminta marāmarattiluḷḷa accam tarum teyvam koṭiyavarkaḷi oṟukkum eṉpar. em talivar attakiya koṭiyar allar. avar eṉakkuc ciṟitum koṭumi ceyyavilli. avarāl eṉakkut tīṅku oṉṟum viḷintilatu. nāṉ avari virumpiyatāl eṉ neṟṟi pacantatu; eṉ maṉam avariṭam nekiḻntatāl eṉ tōḷ melintaṉa” (kuṟun. 87) eṉṟāṟ pōlat taṉṉuḷ pulamputal.
2. ciṉṉāḷ iṭiyīṭiṭṭu vanta talivaṉi nōkki, “paṉṟi tiṉiyi uṇṭu niṟivoṭu malippakkattil uṟaṅkum nāṭaṉē! ‘eṉ tantiyār nam kaḷavoḻukkatti aṟi vārō’ eṉṟu añcip pōlum ciṉṉāḷ kuṟivayiṉ vārāmal viṭuttāy?” (aiṅ. 261) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟutal.
“‘ivvūrār alar tuṟṟumāṟu nī attakiya oḻukkatti uṭiyiyō?’ eṉṟu viṉavum tōḻiyē!yāṉ kūṟuva taṉik kēḷ.
“eṉ cevviralkaḷil civappu mikumāṟu nāṉ piñcāyk kōri paṟikka vēṇṭā eṉṟu ataṉi vilakkit talivaṉ tāṉē ak kōriyip paṟittu eṉakkup pāvi ceytu koṭuttāṉ.
“kuḷattiluḷḷa pūkkaḷ nīril cēymiyil iruntatāl nāṉ paṟikka muṭiyātu tirumpiyapōtu, akkuḷappūkkaḷi avaṉ paṟittu eṉakkut toṭuttuk koṭuttāṉ.
“eṉakku meyyil kōlam ceytu koḷḷat teriyātu eṉṟu kūṟi, eṉ kiyippiṭittu amarttit tōḷkaḷil karumpu eḻutiyataṉ mēlum toyyilum eḻutiṉāṉ.
“ivaṟṟit tavira nikaḻntatu vēṟoṉṟum iṉṟu. ataṟku ivvūravar tam kaṟpaṉiyik kalantu paḻi tuṟṟukiṉṟaṉar. iṉimēl  ūravariṭam uṇmi kūṟi avarkaḷ tam maṉattic cemmippaṭuttal iyalātu. vatuviyiṉ mūlam eṉṉi namar talivaṉukku maṇam muṭittu vippataṉ muṉṉarē, ūravar tam paḻiccol mūlam maṇam muṭittu viṭṭaṉar. nāṉ eṉ ceyya iyalum?” (kali.76) eṉṟal pōlat tōḻi yiṭamum uṇmi kūṟutalukku añcit talivi kaṟpaṉi yākak kūṟutal.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi (iṟimakaḷ), ataṉi (-vampu eṉṟali) maṟuttal -
poruḷvayiṉ pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ vārātatāl, kārpparuvam kaṇṭu kalaṅkiya taliviyit tōḻi atu vampu (aḵtāvatu mayil akavuvatu pōṉṟa nikaḻcci mayakkattāl nērntatu) eṉṟu kūṟi āṟṟuvikkumiṭattē, talivi ak kūṟṟiṉi maṟuttu, aḵtu uṇmiyākavē kārpparuvam eṉṟu kūṟutal.
“tōḻi! itu kārkālam aṉṟu eṉkiṟāy. kārkālattil pūkkavalla malliki mulli ceruntip pūkkaḷi viṟpār kūṟum paṇṭamāṟṟu oliyum poyyā?” (kappaṟ. 243) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 170)
talivi, arumi ceytu ayarttaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
talivaṉ aṇimiyākap piritalēyaṉṟit taṉṉi ariya ṉākac ceytukoṇṭu maṟantāṉ pōl kāṭṭiyiṭattut talivi, “talivaṉ neytal pakutiyil kuṟi ceyta iṭat tiṟku muṉpu pōlat tavaṟātu varuvatilli. pala nāḷkaḷ pakaṟkuṟi poyttuviṭṭatu. cāṉṟavar naṭpum ivvāṟu viyappit tarum vakiyil nekiḻntu pōkumō!” (tiṇimoḻi 41) eṉavum,
“cāṉṟōr, piṟar ceyyum tīmikaḷik kaṇṇāl kaṇṭa piṉṉarum ‘iṉimēl avvāṟu ceyyār’ eṉṟu at tīmikaḷip poṟuttiruppār. talivaṉ eṉṉik kiviṭṭamiyāl, maliyiluḷḷa palāppaḻam piḷappiṭat til viḻuntu payaṉpaṭātu aḻintavāṟu pōla, avaṉ toṭarpu pala nāḷukku muṉṉarē nīṅki oḻintatu. ataṉi aṟiyātu ūrppeṇṭir iṉṉum eṉṉip paḻi tūṟṟik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. piṟar ceyyum tīmikaḷiyum poṟukkum cālpuṭiya talivar nammit tuṟanta piṉ, nām ariyōmākiyatu paṟṟit tāmum ariyarāyiṉār pōlum!” (naṟ. 116) eṉavum,
taṉvayiṉ urimiyum talivaṉvayiṉ ayaṉmiyum tōṉṟat tōḻikkuk kūṟal.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi avaṇ ūṟu añcutal -
talivi, iravuk kuṟiyiṭattu iruḷil koṭiya vaḻiyil kāvalarkku añcātu varum talivaṟku ētēṉum iṭiyūṟu nikaḻumō eṉṟu añcutal.	(to. po. 210 nac.)
talivi, avaṉ aḷi ciṟantavaḻik kūṟal -
talivikkuk kāmam mikkami pōlat talivaṟkum kāmam mika, avaṉ vaḻiyatu arumiyi nōkkātu taliviyik kūṭutaṟkaṇ ceyyum aṉpuc ceyalkaḷ mikkavaḻit talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
“talivaṉuṭiya cantaṉam pūciya mārpiṉi niṉikkumtō ṟum uḷḷattil kāmanōy mikukiṟatu. āṉāl avaṉit taḻuviya aḷavil nōy aṭiyōṭu maṟintuviṭukiṟatē! itaṉ kāraṇam eṉṉa?” (kuṟun. 150) eṉavum,
“maṉṟattiluḷḷa palāmarattiṉ paḻaccuḷikaḷi uṇṭu vanta manti kāṭṭuppacuviṉ maṭikkāmpukaḷit taṭaviya aḷavil taṉ kaṉṟu varuṭuvatākak karuti appacu pāliṉic curakkum aṇimali nāṭaṉākiya talivaṉi nāmāka orupōtum pirintirukka māṭṭōm. pirivu eṉpatu avaṉāl varuva tākattāṉ irukkalām” (ain. eḻu. 4) eṉavum (iḷa.).,
“miṉṉaloṭu maḻi peyta naḷḷiravil karaṭikaḷ iyaṅkum vaḻiyum koṭitu. kāṭṭāṟukaḷum vēkamākak kari puraṇṭu ōṭukiṉṟaṉa. vaḻiyil pāmpiṉ maṇi oḷiyil puli tāṉ koṉṟa yāṉiyiṉ tacikaḷit taṉ piṇavukkāka iḻuttuc cellum attakiya naṭukkam tarum kaṟkaḷ niṟinta kuṟukiya vaḻiyil eṉakku iṉpam taruvataṟkākavē vēl oṉṟaṉiyē tuṇiyākak koṇṭu talivaṉ varukiṟāṉ” (aka.nā. 72) eṉavum,
talivaṉ ceyyum karuṇiyi niṉintu palavāṟu 
kūṟutal.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi, ’avaṉ aṟivu āṟṟa aṟiyumākaliṉ ēṟṟaṟkaṇ’ kūṟal -
talivaṉatu niṉivit talivimika aṟiyumātaliṉ avaṉi uyarttik kūṟutaṟkaṇ kūṟutal.
“nilipeṟṟa colliyuṭiyavar; eppoḻutum eṉ tōḷip piriyātavar ātaliṉ eṉakku iṉiyavar. vaṇṭukaḷ tāmarit tātiṉiyum cantaṉattātiṉiyum ūtic cantaṉamarattilē amitta tēṉpōla avarnaṭpu mika uyarntatu” (naṟ. 1) eṉṟu talivi talivaṉi uyarttik kūṟal.	(to. po. 145. iḷa; 147 nac.)
talivaṉ taliviyic ciṟappittal -
illaṟat talimi koṭuttal.	(195 kuḻa.)
talivi ‘avaṉvayiṉ piri’ttaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
taṉṉoṭu mintaṉiṭi uṟavu nīkki avaṉit taliva ṉoṭu cārttutaṟkaṇ. talivi kūṟutal.
taṉ makaṉoṭu pēci makiḻkiyil, talivaṉ varavē, tāṉ evvaḷavu taṭuttum nillātu, malimēl ciṅkam pāyvatu pōla aṟaṉillā aṉpili peṟṟa makaṉtāṉum tāyākiya taṉṉiṭattu aṉpilaṉāyt tantiyiṉ paranta mārpiṉi nōkkip pāyntu taṉ kippiṭiyiṉiṉṟu vilakit tanti yiṭam ceṉṟu viṭṭāṉ” (kali. 86) eṉṟa talivikūṟṟu.			(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi ‘aṟiyāḷ pōṉṟu kuṟiyāḷ kūṟal’ -
talivi talivaṉ toṭarpu paṟṟit taṉṉi viṉaviya tōḻikku nāṇattāl veḷippaṭiyāka viṭi kūṟāmal kuṟippākak kūṟutal.
taliviyiṭam tōḻi talivaṉip paṟṟiyum avaṉ kuṟi paṟṟiyum viṉaviyapōtu, talivi, “mucukkali tīṇṭiyata ṉāl kiḻinta tēṉ kūṭṭiṉiṉṟu perukkeṭutta tēṉ āṟu pōla ōṭip pākku nākam mutaliya maraṅkaḷi aṭittuk koṇṭu maliyiṉiṉṟu ūrntu varukiṟatu” (tañci. kō. 118) eṉṟu kūṟutaṟkaṇ, mēmpōkkāka nōkkiṉ itu tōḻi oṉṟu viṉavat talivi vēṟoṉṟu kūṟiyatu pōlat tōṉṟum; āyiṉ ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, “talivaṉ tīṇṭiyatāl, taliviyiṭam aṭikkappaṭṭirunta kāma veḷḷam veḷippaṭavē, ataṉ perukkil avaḷuṭiya nāṇam maṭam ākiya paṇpukaḷ aṭittuc cellappaṭṭaṉa” eṉṟa kuṟippupporuḷ pulappaṭṭavāṟu.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘aṟiyāḷ pōṟal’ eṉṉum (85).
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 147)
talivi aṟivu avaṉ urittal -
talivaṉ taliviyiṉ mūtaṟivuṭimi moḻital.	(cāmi. 94)
talivi, āṭiṭam nōkki aḻital -
talivi tāṉ muṉpu viḷiyāṭiya iṭattip pārttu varuntutal.
talivaṉik kāṇa vaḻiyiṉṟit tuyaruṟum talivi, tāṉ viḷiyāṭi makiḻnta iṭattip  pārttum, aṅkut taliva ṉuṭaṉ kūṭi makiḻntati niṉittum taṉakku iṉpamūṭṭip pirintu pōyviṭṭa talivaṉatu vaṉkaṇmiyi niṉit tum varuntutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pakaṟkuṟi iṭiyīṭu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 156)
talivi, āṭi kaḻuvuvāḷi vāyil eṉṟal -
“āṭi kaḻuvum taṉ toḻili viṭuttu niṉtūtākat tiri yum vaṇṇātti, parattiyarukkut teppamāka iruntu avaroṭu nīrāṭi avarkaḷ ūṟṟiya cātiliṅkak kuḻampu citiyāmal vantu anta aḻaki eṉakkuk kāṭṭac coṉ ṉāḷō?” (kali.72) eṉṟu talivi talivaṉiṭam pulit tiyik kūṟi ūṭutal.
itu ‘vāyiliṉ varūum vaki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷap paṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷil oṉṟu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, āyattiṭit talivaṉik kaṇṭavāṟum avaṉik kūṟiyaṉavum kūṟi, tōḻiyit talivaṉi varivukaṭāvi yāykku aṟattoṭu niṟka vēṇṭum eṉṟatu -
“tōḻi! talivaṉ, yām viḷiyāṭum iṭattu vantu ‘uṉakkuc ciṟṟil amittut tarukiṟēṉ’ eṉṟāṉ. yāṉ  ‘eṉṉi maṇantu ōrillam kaṭṭi vāḻa aṟiyātu ciṟṟil iḻikka varukiṟāyē’ eṉṟēṉ. talivaṉ, ‘kaṭṭiṉa māliyi uṉ kūntalil cūṭṭukiṟēṉ’ eṉṟāṉ. yāṉ, ‘nī eṉṉi maṇantukoṇṭu eṉakkup pūccūṭṭutali viṭuttu, emar kaṭṭiya māliyik cūṭṭa varukiṟāyē’ eṉṟēṉ. talivaṉ, ‘uṉakkut toyyil eḻutukiṟēṉ’ eṉṟāṉ. yāṉ, ‘nī virivil maṇantu palarum kāṇa eṉakku alaṅkāram ceytalē aḻakitu’ eṉṟēṉ. ivvāṟu avaṉ kūṟiyavaṟṟuk kellām yāṉ māṟāy viṭi kūṟa, avaṉ iṉik kaḷaviṉit toṭara muṭiyātu eṉṟu varuntip pōṉāṉ. nī avaṉukku varintu koḷḷum muṟiyi aṟivittut tāykku aṟattoṭu niṉṟu eṉ nōyip pōkkuvāyāka!” eṉṟu talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu.			(kali. 111)
talivi (piṇiviḻi) āṟṟal -
poruḷvayiṉ pirintu ceṉṟuḷḷa talivaṉ virivil mīḷvāṉ eṉṟu kūṟiya tōḻiyiṉ coṟ kēṭṭut taliviāṟṟiyiruttal.
“mēkam maḻi poḻital kaṇṭu varuntum neñcē! kāṭukaḷil mayilkaḷ āṭumāṟu mēkam maḻi poḻitaliṉ, talivar mīṇṭu varuvatākak kūṟiya kālam vantuviṭṭatu. avar virivil vantu viṭuvār” (ampikā. 318) eṉṟu pirital tuyari talivi poṟuttukkoḷḷutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 170)
talivi, āṟṟāmi vāyilākac ceṉṟāṟkuk kūṟal -
talivaṉ avā mītūrntu āṟṟāmiyē vāyilākat taṉṉit taḻuva varuvōṉukkut talivi kūṟutal.
“taliva! eṉ vaḷi nekiḻumāṟu pirintu eṉṉi varuttiya niṉṉip pullavum ceyyēṉ; ataṉāl niṉṉi veṟut tēṉum allēṉ. ayalām taṉmiyēṉ ātaliṉ, eṉṉit tīṇṭaṟka” (naṟ. 340) eṉṟu talivi nontu kūṟutal.
‘vāyiliṉ varūum vaki’yāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ oru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi (perumakaḷ) āṟṟiṉatu arumi niṉintu iraṅkal -
talivaṉ iravil naṭantu kaṭantu vanta ētam mikka vaḻiyi niṉittut talivi varuntutal.
“veṟpa! vāṉamum kaṟuttuḷḷatu. tariyilum maḻinīr ōṭukiṟatu. iruḷō mikuntuḷḷatu. palarum uṟaṅkum iv viruḷil nī evvāṟu vantu em mūri aṭintāy? nī paṭṭuḷḷa tuṉpatti niṉintu yāṉ varuntukiṟēṉ” (kuṟun. 355) eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivi kūṟutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 158)
talivi, āṟṟuval eṉak kūṟiyatu -
tāṉ poliviḻappiṉum, talivaṉatu piriviṉi āṟṟa vallaḷ ātalit talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu.
“tōḻi! talivar eṉ arukil iruppiṉ, tiruviḻā naṭakkum ūriṉip pōlap polivōṭu iruppēṉ; makkaḷ nīṅkiya pāḻpaṭṭa ūriṉkaṇ aṇilkaḷ viḷi yāṭum pāḻillattu muṉṉiṭaṅkaḷip pōla, avar akaṉṟañāṉṟu poliviḻantu kāṇappaṭuvēṉ” (kuṟun.41) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘palvēṟu niliyiṉum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, āṟṟoṭu pulampal -
“kaḷavil eṉṉi maṇantu piṉ pirintu eṉ uyiri vāṅkum talivaṉuṭiya koṭumiyi eṉakkuk kūṟutaṟkuk kūci maṉam iraṅki uṭali malarāṭiyāl maṟittukkoṇṭu ūrukku veḷiyil ōṭum natiyē! eṉ tīviṉiyāl talivaṉ eṉṉip pirintāṉāka, nī yātu ceyyaviyalum?” (ampikā. 291) eṉṟu talivi āṟṟoṭu maṉam nontu pēcutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘oruvaḻittaṇattal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu;uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.			(i. vi. 525 uri)
talivi, iṭṭup pirivu iraṅkik kūṟal -
talivaṉ cēṇiṭiyiṉṟi aṇimiyiluḷḷa taṉ ūrukkuc ceṉṟu virivil mīṇṭu varuvatākak kūṟip pirintaviṭattu, appiriviṉit tāṅka iyalāta varuttattāl talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
“yāṉ uṉṉōṭu illattil uḷḷēṉ. eṉ aḻakumikunta varuttattoṭu kaṭaṟkaric cōliyiṭattatu. talivaṉō, taṉṉūrkkuc ceṉṟuviṭṭāṉ. ūrār tuṟṟum alarō ūrppotuviṭam variyil paraviviṭṭatu” (kuṟun. 97) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
“talivaṉ nammit tuṟantāl iṉi viṭuvataṟku nampāl uyir tavira vēṟoṉṟum illi” (kuṟun. 334) eṉṟa kūṟṟum nikaḻvatu uṇṭu.
itu kaḷaviṉuḷ ‘pulavip pōli’ (ūṭalum uṇarttalum iṉṟit tōṉṟuvatu) eṉappaṭum.
iṭṭuppirivu - iṭṭuvittup pirital eṉavum poruḷ kūṟuvar.	(109. iḷa.)
talivi, iyaṟpaṭa moḻital -
pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ kuṟitta kālam kaṭappavum vārāmi yāl varuntiya taliviyatu nili kaṇṭa tōḻi talivaṉ mītu kuṟṟam cāṟṟi iyaṟpaḻittaviṭattu, talivi ataṉi maṟuttu avaṉ paṇpuk kuṟivu aṟṟavaṉ eṉavum, virivil vantu aruḷuvāṉ eṉṟum avaṉik kuṇamuṭiyaṉākac ciṟappittal.
“tōḻi! nam talivar koṭiyarāyiṉum, avarē eṉ uyirt tuṇivar. avarik kuṟikūṟutal namakku aṭukkumō?” (aiṅ. mikip.) eṉṟal pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu variviyaluḷ ‘aṟattoṭu niṟṟal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 176)
‘varital vēṭki’ eṉṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇum ittuṟi nikaḻum. ‘iṟiyōṉ taṉṉi nērntu iyaṟpaṭa moḻital’ eṉpatu atu. ‘talivi talivaṉi nērntu iyaṟpaṭa moḻital’ kāṇka.
talivi (pūṅkuḻi) iraṅkal -
poruḷvayiṉ pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ taṉṉiṭam atu paṟṟik kūṟāmi karutit talivi tōḻiyiṭam varuntik kūṟal.
“tōḻi! muṉpu eṉ nalamuṇṭu iṉpuṟṟa pōtu, ‘uṉṉip piriyēṉ, piriyiṉ tariyēṉ’ eṉat tām urittatu poyk kum eṉa añciyē pōlum avar itaṉi eṉṉiṭam collāmal ceṉṟuḷḷār?” (tañci. kō. 264) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itaṉi ‘neñcoṭu varuntal’ eṉṉum tirukkōviyār (272).
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 170)
talivi, iravukkuṟi nayantu kūṟutal-
pakaṟkuṟikku vāyppuk kuṟinta kālattil talivaṉ niṉi vāl piṇi mikuntu tōḷ vāṭiya talivi, talivaṉi iravukkuṟi nayappittal vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇattoṭu tōḻiyiṭam  taṉatu iravukkuṟi viruppattiṉip pulappaṭut tal.
“vēṅkikaḷ malara vaṇṭukaḷ ārkkum mālik kālattē eṉ kāmanōy tīravum, tōḷkaḷ melivu tīrntu vīṅkavum vāypput tarum vakiyil talivaṉ varuvāṉō?” (tiṇi moḻi aim. 9) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘aṉṉavum  uḷa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ oru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi, iravukkuṟi vanta talivaṉiṭam kūṟal -
kaṭiya maḻiyiyum koṭiya iruḷiyum poruṭpaṭuttātu taṉṉi makiḻvippataṟkākavē iravukkuṟiyiṭattu vanta talivaṉi nōkkit talivi, “maḻi kaṭumiyākap peyva tāl vāṉattiyum nōkka muṭiyavilli; maḻinīr peruk keṭuttu ōṭuvatāl tariyiyum kāṇamuṭiyavilli; katiravaṉ maṟintuviṭṭamiyāl iruḷum mikap paravi viṭṭatu. ivviravil ūravar perumpālōrum uṟaṅki viṭṭaṉar; ataṉāl ūril amiti nilavutaliṉ accam nikaḻkiṟatu. ittakiya iravil iṅku varavēṇṭumā? em iruppiṭamākiya ivvūri, iṅku malarntuḷḷa vēṅkip pūkkaḷiṉ naṟumaṇattāl aṭiyāḷam kaṇṭu vantāyā?” (kuṟun. 355) eṉṟāṟ pōla, avaṉ ceyyum taṇṇaḷiyi viñciya taṉ kavaliyi veḷip paṭuttiyatu.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘aṉṉavum uḷa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ oru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi, iravu nīṭṭippukku varuntik kūṟal -
“pēriruḷ eṅkum kavintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu. iravō mikak koṭiyatāka yukamāy nīḷkiṉṟatu. eṉ maṇāḷaṉāṉa tirumālō mukaṅkāṭṭavilli. iṉi eṉṉuyirik kāppār yārō?” eṉṉum talivi kūṟṟu.	(tiruvāy. 5-4-1)
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉpāṟpaṭum.
talivi, ‘iruvakik kuṟi(yum) piḻippākiya iṭattu’t taṉṉuḷ kiyāṟu eytik kūṟal -
pakaṟkuṟiyum iravukkuṟiyum tavaṟiyaviṭattut talivi taṉakkuḷ ceyalaṟṟuk kūṟutal.
“pēyum uṟaṅkum naḷḷiravil neytaṟpūvil amarntu varuntum aṉṟilē! eṉ talivaṉip pōla niṉ cēvalum kuṟiyiṭattuniṉṉik kāṇpataṟku varat tavaṟiyatō?” eṉavum, “tēṉmikka neytalē! pirintār varuntum mālik kālattil taṉimiyāl varuntumeṉkaṇkaḷ uṟakkatti maṟuttaṉa. avi pōla aṉṟi iṉitāka uṟaṅkum neytalē! nī uṟakkattil kaṉavu kaṇṭiruppāyē! akkaṉa vil kal neñciṉarākiya eṉ talivar kaṭaṟkaric cōlikku vantatākak kaṇṭāyāyiṉ eṉakkut teri vippāy (cilap. kāṉal. 33) eṉavum varum talivi kūṟṟu.
ikkūṟṟil taṉvayiṉ urimiyum, talivaṉvayiṉayaṉmi yum peṟappaṭum.	(to. po. 107 nac.)
talivi, ‘iruvakik kuṟi(yum) piḻippākiya viṭattu’t tōḻiyiṭam kūṟal -
pakaṟkuṟiyō iravukkuṟiyō nikaḻāmal taṭippaṭṭa viṭattut talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
“tōḻi! paṉimarattil kaṭṭiya kūṭṭil uḷḷa āṇ aṉṟil mutaṟcūlāl ēṟpaṭṭa vayānōyiṉiyuṭiya taṉ peṭi aṉṟili ataṉ vāyalaku nekiḻnta aḷavil kūvi aḻikkum naḷḷiravil, talivaṉatu tēr, nam iravuk kuṟiyi nōkki vārātāyiṉum, varuvatu pōṉṟa eṇṇat tāl eṉ ceviyil attērmaṇikaḷ olippatu pōṉṟa mayakkam ēṟpaṭṭataṉāl iravu muḻutum yāṉ uṟaṅka villi” (kuṟun. 301) eṉavum,
“nam maṉiyiṉ arukil uḷḷa ēḻil eṉṟa kuṉṟiṉ mēluḷḷa noccimarattiṉ pūkkaḷ utirum oliyik kēṭṭuk koṇṭē, ipperiya ūriluḷḷār yāvarum uṟaṅkiya irākkālattil, yāṉ uṟaṅkātu talivaṉ varaviṉi etirnōkkiyiruntēṉ” (kuṟun. 138) eṉavum,
“tōḻi! talivaṉik kūṭutaṟkaṇ uḷḷa viruppattāl, pakaṟkuṟiyit taḷḷiyatu tavaṟu eṉṟu niṉittu iravil noccimalar vīḻum ōciyik kēṭṭavāṟu avaṉ varukiyi yāṉ etirnōkki iruppavum, iṭikaḷ olikkum naḷḷiravil tāṉ iravukkuṟiyiṭi vantum yāṉ taṉṉik kāṇavilliyē eṉṟu uṉṉiṭam talivaṉ kuṟipiḻit tati eṉpiḻiyākak kūṟuvāṉ” (kali. 46) eṉavum, palavāka varum talivi kūṟṟu.
ikkūṟṟil taṉvayiṉ urimiyum, talivaṉvayiṉ ayaṉmiyum pulappaṭum.	(to. po. 107 nac.)
talivi ‘iṉpamum iṭumpiyum ākiya iṭattu’k kūṟutal -
talivikkuk kaṟpuk kālattil talivaṉ pirivāl alamaral perukiyavaḻit talivaṉik kaṇṭapōtu iṉpamum, taṉippaṭṭapōtu tuṉpamum uṇṭākumiṭattuk kūṟṟu nikaḻum.
talivikku iṉpamum tuṉpamum oruṅku nikaḻumvaḻiyum kūṟṟu nikaḻum.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
“taḻiyum kaṇṇiyum koṇṭu piṉṉiṉṟapōtu avar nōkku māttiramum puṇarcci kuṟittamiyāl iṉpam uṭittāy irukkum; iṉṟu appuṇarcci nikaḻavum’ atu “pirivar talivar” eṉṟu añcum accattiṉi uṭittā yiṟṟu. avar aṉpiṉ nili iḵtu” (kuṟaḷ 1152) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(nac.)
“kōḻi kural kāṭṭiya aḷavil talivaṉit taṉṉiṭatti ṉiṉṟu pirikkum koṭiya poḻutu vantuviṭṭatē eṉṟu eṉ neñcam tiṭukkiṭukiṟatu” (kuṟun. 157) eṉṟu iraviṭi iṉpam nukarnta talivi pakaliṭi annukarcci kūṭāmikku iraṅkik kūṟutal.	(145 iḷa.)
“tōḻi! naṉmali nāṭaṉ eṉṉoṭu kūṭumpōtu eṉṉi ṭattē aḻakum vantu cērkiṟatu; piriyiṉ, eṉ māmi aḻakip pacali aḻittuviṭukiṟatu. acuṇamāvi akap paṭuppār kipōlat talivaṉ mārpu eṉakkuk kūṭiyavaḻi iṉpamum, pirintavaḻit tuṉpamum taruvatāyuḷḷatu” (naṟ. 304) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(nac.)
talivi ‘iṉṉāt tolcūḷ eṭuttaṟkaṇ’ kūṟal -
iṉṉāmiyip payakkum poyccūḷuṟaviṉit talivaṉ kūṟumiṭattut talivi kūṟutal.
talivaṉ ‘vanta kuṟṟam vaḻikeṭa’ oḻukik kaḷavil cūḷuṟa vāl vanta ētam nīkki,  kaṟpākiya ikkālattuk kaṭavuḷari yum putalvaṉiyum paṟṟic cūḷuṟutaliṉ, ‘iṉṉāta cūḷ’ eṉṟār. kaṟpuk kālattum kaḷavu pōlac cūḷuṟutaliṉ, ‘tol cūḷ’ eṉṟār.
“taliva! nī kūṭiya parattiyar muṭiyiṟ cūṭiya pūkkaḷil tātu niṉ tōḷkaḷil paṭiya iṅku vantuḷḷāy; eṉṉit tīṇṭaṟka” eṉṟu talivi colliyavaḻit talivaṉ, “eṉṉip paṟṟit tavaṟākak karutaṟka; eṉmēl tavaṟilli eṉpataṉic cūḷurittu uṟuti ceykiṟēṉ” eṉṟāṉ; eṉṟavaḻi, talivi, “nī ivvāṟu tuṇinturikkum poyc cūḷ uṉakkut tuṉpam tarumāyiṉ, aḵtu eṅkaḷit tākkumēyaṉṟi niṉ parattiyarit tākkātu” eṉṟami (kali. 88)	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, “iṉiyum tuyaruṟṟu nī pacantu vāṭa vēṇṭā; āṟṟiyiru; avar vantuviṭṭār” eṉṟa tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu -
“eṉ kaṇṇāra eṉ kaṇavaṉik kāṇpēṉāka!aṅṅaṉam kaṇṭapiṉ eṉ tōḷkaḷiṉ vāṭṭamum pacappum tāmē nīṅkum!” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(kuṟaḷ 1265)
talivi, uṇarntu talivaṉoṭu pulattal -
parattiyiṟpirivil talivaṉ taliviyiṉ makappēṟṟiṉi aṟintu makiḻcciyuṭaṉ maṉikkaṇ vantu, talivi ney yāṭiyatiyum aṟinta piṉṉar, avaḷik kāṇa vantuḷḷa miyit talivikkut tōḻi coṉṉapōtu, avaḷ avaṉōṭu ūṭal.
“parattiyarit tōynta talivaṉ eṉṉit taḻuviṉāl, eṉ mārpiṉ tīmpāl paṭutalāl avarkaḷ ceyta alaṅ kāraṅkaḷ citintu viṭum.ataṉāl avarkaḷ pulattal kūṭum. ātaliṉ talivar iṅku varutal vēṇṭā. yāṉ putalvaṉik koṇṭē poḻutu pōkkuvēṉ.” (ampikā. 471) eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivi pulantu kūṟutal.
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ ‘parattiyar pirivu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 206)
talivi, ‘uyir cela, vēṟṟu varivu variṉ atu māṟṟutaṟkaṇ’ kūṟiyatu -
taṉakku iṟantupāṭu tōṉṟum vakiyil taṉṉi ayalār maṇam pēcat toṭaṅkiyatu aṟinta talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutalum, avaḷ vāyilākac ceviliyiṭam kūṟuvittalum.
“palā marattiluḷḷa kuraṅku taṉ uṇavākiya palāp paḻattit tiṉṉa iyalātu kāṉavaṉ toṭukkum ampiṟku añci mūṅkiṟputaril pāyum malippakkattiyuṭiya talivaṉ, nammiṭam mutal nāḷ cantittapōtu koṇṭa aṉpu caṟṟum kuṟiyāmal iruntuvarukiṟāṉ. āyiṉ, ivvūr eṉ maṇam kuṟittup putiyavarkaḷ peyarkaḷik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.” (kuṟun. 385) eṉat tōḻiyiṭam talivi kūṟutal.
“aṉṉāy! puṉṉi malarum tuṟivaṉi nāṅkaḷ eṅkaḷ talivar eṉṟu koṇṭirukkiṟōm. āyiṉ ivvūrār vēṟākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. viti eppaṭi nikaḻttappōkiṟatō?” (aiṅ. 110) eṉat tōḻi vāyilākat talivi ceviliyiṭam kūṟuvittal.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi, ‘uyirāk kālattu uyirttal’ -
talivaṉoṭu taṉ tiṟattu oruvarum oṉṟum uriyāta vaḻit taṉatu āṟṟāmiyāl talivi taṉṉoṭum ava ṉoṭum poruntiya ceytikaḷ cilavaṟṟit tāṉē kūṟutal.
“icciṟukuṭi, talivaṉuṭiya koṭumiyi niṉittalāl nīṅkāta tuyarattoṭu varuntip pāti iravilum uṟaṅkātu taṭumāṟuvārik kaṇṭu “uṟaṅkāmikkuk kāraṇam yātu?’ eṉṟu viṉavāta makkaḷiyum nīṇṭa nēramākiya irāk kālattiyum uṭiyatu ātaliṉ, eṉ pōlvār taṅki yiruttaṟku ēṟṟataṉṟu” (kuṟun. 145) eṉavum,
“eṉ uṭal vaṉappu mikuntatu kaṇṭu nāṉ paruvamaṅkiyāki viṭṭatākak koṇṭu eṉ uṟaviṉar eṉṉi iṟceṟittu viṭṭaṉar. veḷiyē viḷiyāṭikkoṇṭirunta nāṉ iṟceṟittu viṭṭamiyāl ettakiya tuṉpatti aṭintu koṇṭiruppēṉ eṉṟu eṉṉik kavaliyoṭu viṉavāta kavaliyaṟṟa makkaḷē ivvūril uḷḷaṉar” (kuṟun. 159) eṉavum talivi taṉṉuḷ kūṟik koḷḷutal.			(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi, uyirāk kālattut tōḻiyoṭu uyirttal -
talivaṉoṭu taṉ tiṟattu oruvarum oṉṟum uriyāta vaḻit talivi taṉatu āṟṟāmiyiṉ talivaṉoṭum taṉṉoṭum poruntiya ceytikaḷ cilavaṟṟit tōḻi yiṭam kūṟutal.
“tōḻi! ‘nampāl virumpiyoḻukum talivaṉ ippo ḻutu avvāṟu virumpi oḻukavilli’ eṉṟu yāṉ niṉakkuc colla varuvatu eṉakkē nāṇāka uḷḷatu. uṉakku eṉ niliyi maṟippatu māṉak kēṭākum. muṉpellām nam kaḷavoḻukkam aṉṉikkut terintuviṭumō eṉṟu yāṉ añcuvataṉi aṟintum, talivaṉ eṉṉiviṭṭu nīṅkamāṭṭāṉ. ippoḻutō, ‘nam kaḷavoḻukkam tōḻiyarukkut terintuviṭumō’ eṉṟu añcuvatākakkūṟi eṉṉi virivil pirintu celkiṟāṉ. ippaṭiyē avaṉ naṭpuc ciṟitu ciṟitākak kuṟintu illiyāy viṭumō eṉṟu nāṉ añcukiṟēṉ” (naṟ. 72) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi, ‘urimi koṭutta kiḻavōṉ pāṅkiṉ, perumiyiṉ tiriyā aṉpiṉkaṇ’ kūṟiyatu -
taṉakku illaṟak kiḻami koṭutta allatu illaṟa oḻukkattip pakirntu koṭutta talivaṉiṭattut taṉ kulattiṟkēṟṟa perumiyiṉiṉṟu māṟupaṭātu aṉpu ceyyumiṭattut talivi colliyatu.
“nāṭaṉ naṭpu nilattiṉum akalattatāy, vāṉiṉum uyarc ciyatāy, nīriṉum āḻattatāy niṟinta aḷaviṉiyuṭi yatu” (kuṟun. 3) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, uvaki āṟṟātu uḷattoṭu kiḷattal -
taṉṉi maṇam pēca vanta talivaṉuṭiya uṟaviṉarit tam uṟaviṉar varavēṟṟu maṇam icinta ceytiyik kēṭṭa talivi, makiḻcci tāṅkāmal taṉ maṉattuḷḷēyē kūṟik koḷḷutal.
“neñcē! nam illattil tirumaṇat toṭarpāṉa vātti yaṅkaḷ muḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ātaliṉ, kuṟikkaṇ varum tīyavaḻi yum, kuṟi piḻittalum, veṟiyāṭṭum, piṟar eṉṉi variyak karutalum, ivi kāraṇamāka nī paṭum varuttamum iṉi nikaḻtaṟku vāyppilli. nī makiḻvōṭu iruppā yāka!” (ampikā. 333) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 174)
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘varivu malital’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
talivi, uvattal paṟṟik kūṟal -
uvattal- talivaṉi uvattal. atu paṟṟit talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟuvatu.
“tōḻi! talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta kāṭci kaṇṭa aḷavil atu neñciṉic cuṭṭatu. avaṉiṭam ūṭal koḷḷalām. ūṭal maṟṟavariṭattuc ciṟantu payaṉ taruka! yāṉ ūṭutalit tavirntu, avaṉatu eyiṟṟu nīriyuṇṭu avaṉ eṉmārpil porunti uṟaṅkak kiṭṭiya ivvariya vāyppiṉi avaṉit taḻuvutaṟkaṇ payaṉpaṭuttiṉēṉ” eṉavum,
“talivaṉik kāṇātaviṭattu avaṉip paṟṟiya tavaṟu kaḷit tavirap piṟitu oṉṟaṉiyum karuti aṟiyāta yāṉ, avaṉik kāṇumpōtu avaṉ tavaṟṟi niṉikkum āṟṟal ilēṉāyuḷḷēṉ” (kuṟaḷ. 1286) eṉavum, tāṉ talivaṉatu toṭarpāl uvattal paṟṟit talivi kūṟiyavāṟu.	(to. po. 145 iḷa.)
talivi, uvantu kūṟal -
“koṟukkit taṭṭi tīṇṭuvatāl, vaṭukkaḷi uṭiya māmarattiṉ taḷirkaḷ aciyum tuṟikaḷiyuṭiya tali vaṉatu mārpu iṉiya uṟakkattik koṭukkum meṉmi yuṭiyatu ātaliṉ, avaṉatu parattami karuti ūṭātu, avaṉi ēṟṟut taḻuvukiṟēṉ” (aiṅ. 14) eṉṟu talivi tōḻikku uvantu kūṟiyatu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, ‘ēmam cāṉṟa uvakikkaṇ’ kūṟal -
iyaṟkip puṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam, pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam eṉṉum kaḷavuk kālap puṇarcci nāṉkaṉkaṇṇum iṭiyīṭu ēṟpaṭāmal talivaṉ vantu kūṭum vāyppup peṟṟamiyāl iṉpattiṟkup pātukāval aminta makiḻcciyit talivi eytumpōtu tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
“tōḻi! kāmanōyiṉāl varunti maṉavalimi kuṉṟiya kālattil, āṇmakkaḷ tam maṉattiluḷḷa kāmatti veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟutalum kūṭum. kaṭaliluḷḷa pulāl nāṟṟattip puṉṉimalar pōkkum nam kaṭaṟkarit talivaṉ, taṉṉiṭattē ārvamuṭiyārit taṉ mārpāl taḻuvi nāḷum nāḷum avariṭattil ārvam mikumpaṭi ceykiṟāṉ. avvāṟu mikum ārvatti eṉ peṇmi yiṉāl nāṉ veḷiyiṭa āṟṟēṉāy, naṉku kaḻuvappaṭāta paciya muttam taṉatu mikka oḷiyi maṟittuk kāṭṭumāṟu pōla, talivaṉatu puṇarcciyāl ēṟpaṭum mikka nalaṉi aritākat tāṅki niṟkiṟēṉ. ippaṭic ceyyum nam talivaṉ eṉṉa makaṉ!” (naṟ. 94) eṉṟu, iravil talivaṉ cūṭṭum pū mutaliyavaṟṟi vikaṟiyil kaḷintu taliyil eṇṇey nīvit taṉ  tōṟṟap polivi maṟittu oḻukum talivi (kuṟun. 312) tōḻiyiṭam kūṟal.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi, oruppaṭṭu eḻutal -
tōḻi kūṟiyataṉi ēṟṟuk koṇṭu talivi talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōtaṟku icital
“ūravar kūṟum alariyum, aṉṉimār ciṉantu nōkkuta liyum poṟuttukkoṇṭu iṅkiruppatu eṉṉāl iyalātatē. nāṉ eṉ nāyakaṉuṭaṉ pōvēṉ. inta ūr iṉṉum mikutiyāṉa alar pēcit tūṟṟuka!” (tañci. kō. 312) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu variviyaluḷ, kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭil, ‘uṭaṉ pōkku’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 182)
talivikkut talivaṉkuṟippu aṟintavaḻi nikaḻvaṉa -
talivaṉ kuṟitta puṇarcci vēṭkiyiyē taliviyum uḷḷattuk koḷvāḷāyiṉ oruvari oruvar cantitta lākiya purimukam purinta piṉṉar, (1) poṟinutal viyarttal, (2) naku nayam maṟittal. (3) citivu piṟarkkiṉmi, (4) kūḻi virittal, (5) kātoṉṟu kaḷital, (6) ūḻaṇi tivaral, (7) uṭi peyart tuṭuttal, (8) alkul tivaral, (9) aṇintavi tiruttal, (10) ilvaliyuṟuttal, (11) irukiyum eṭuttal eṉṉum patiṉoru meyppāṭukaḷum talivikku muṟiyē nikaḻum.	(to. po. 97 nac.)
talivaṉatu kuṟippaṟintu ataṟku uṭaṉpāṭṭit taṉ kaṇṇiṉāl terivitta piṉṉarum, talivi peṇmiyāl kūṟṟu nikaḻātu. avaḷ taṉ vēṭkiyik kaṇṇālēyē kuṟippiṭuvāḷ.	(94 iḷa.)
talivi, kaṭaloṭu pulampal - 
talivi kaṭali viḷittut taṉ tuyaraniliyik kūṟutal.
“oli kaṭalē! tam perumpukaḻāl ulakiyē pōrttiruk kum nam talivar tirumāliṉ avatāram āvār. avar irāmaṉāy, aṉalampu eytu uṉṉit tuṉpuṟuttiṉār eṉavum, kuraṅkuppaṭi koṇṭu uṉmītu aṇikaṭṭi uṉṉik kaṭantār eṉavum ciṉantu avar tēr ceṉṟa vaḻiyi uṉ alikaḷāl aḻittiyō?” (ampikā. 290) eṉpatu pōṉṟa kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘oruvaḻit taṇattal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu, uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.			(i.vi. 525 uri)
talivi, kaluḻtaṟ kāraṇam kūṟal -
kaḷavil taṉṉik kūṭip pirintu pōṉa talivaṉ vārā miyē taṉatu mikka tuyarattiṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṟu talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟutal.
“tōḻi! eṉ kaṇkaḷiṭattu aṉpu pūṇṭu avaṟṟiṟku eṉ kikaḷ mi tīṭṭiṉa. talivaṉik kaṇkaḷ kaṇṭa kāraṇat tāl avaṟṟiṟku aḻaku ceyvitta kikaḷ toṭi cōra vāṭum nili ēṟpaṭṭu viṭṭatu. tamatu tavaṟu nōkkik kaṇkaḷ varuntukiṉṟaṉa” (ampikā. 338) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.

itu variviyaluḷ, ‘aṟattoṭu niṟṟal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 176)
talivi kaḷam cuṭṭal -
talivaṉ kūṟiya kūṟṟit taṉakkum uṭaṉpāṭāka ēṟṟuc ceyaṟpaṭutalē talivikku aṟam ātaliṉ, talivaṉ pakaṟ kuṟiyum iravukkuṟiyum vēṇṭiyavaḻi, avaṉi maṟukkāmal, kuṟikaḷukku ēṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷākat tāṉ aṟintavaṟṟit talivi nēriṭiyākat talivaṉiṭam kūṟāmal, kuṟippākavō, talivaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākavō, tōḻi vāyilākavō aṟivittal. talivaṉ tāṉē pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟik kaḷaṉkaḷit tērnteṭuttuk kūṟiṉ, akkaḷavoḻukkam piṟarkkup pulappaṭṭuk kuṭippiṟappu mutaliyavaṟṟukkut takātatāka muṭiyumātaliṉ, taliviyē kuṟiyiṭaṅkaḷic cuṭṭuvāḷ eṉpatu.	(to. po. 120 nac.)
talivi, kaḷaviṉkaṇ taṉakkuc cākkāṭum nikaḻalām eṉṟu añcit tōḻiyiṭam kūṟal -
kaḷavuk kālattil talivaṉ ōriru nāḷē pirintu celluta liyum poṟuttukkoḷḷa iyalātavaḷākiya talivi, mālik kālattil kāmavēṭki mītūrntu tōḻiyi nōkkit taṉakkuc cākkāṭu nērtal kūṭum eṉṟu kūṟutal.
“vaṇṭukaḷ cuṉippūkkaḷi viṭuttuc ciṉippūkkaḷi nōkkic cella, pakal veppam kuṟiya, pirintavar ceyalaṟṟu varuntat tōṉṟiya immālikkālam, muṉpē tuṉpuṟuvār neñcil vēl pāyccuvārip pōla eṉṉi varuttu vatāl, kaṇṇāṭiyil ūtappaṭṭa āvi ciṟitu ciṟitākat tēyntu maṟivatu pōla, eṉ valimiyum ciṟitu ciṟitākak kuṟintu varukiṟatu. puyal kāṟṟil cuḻalum maraṅkaḷil taṅkiya paṟavikaḷ maraṅkaḷi viṭuttuc celvatu pōla, eṉ uyirum eṉ uṭali viṭuttuc cellum pōlum!” (akanā. 71) eṉṟu tōḻiyi nōkkit talivi kūṟutal.			(to. po. 100 nac.)
talivi, kaḷavu aṟivuṟṟavaḻik kūṟal -
taliviyum talivaṉum oḻukum kaḷavoḻukkam puṟattārk kup pulaṉākum vakiyil talivaṉ naṭantukoṇṭaviṭattē, talivi talivaṉatu niṉivākavē yiruntu taṉṉi maṟantu, taṉ kaḷavoḻukkattit tāy aṟiyak kūṟutal.
tiṉippuṉam eytiya tāy taliviyi nōkki, “tiṉiyil kiḷiyi uṇṇaviṭuttu yāṅkuc ceṉṟāy?’ eṉṟu vekuṇṭa aḷavil, talivaṉatu niṉivu māṟāta niliyil talivi, tāṉ tāykkuk kūṟa vēṇṭiya viṭiyi uṇarntu kūṟāmal, “aruvi olikkum periya malināṭaṉi nāṉ pārttatumilli; avaṉi eṉakkut teriyavum teriyātu. avaṉoṭu nāṉ pūppaṟikkac cellavum illi; aruvi yāṭac cellavum illi” eṉṟu kūṟiyatu (naṟ. 147).			(to. po. 109 iḷa.)
talivaṉ pirintu ceṉṟāṉāka, avaṉuṭaṉ taṅkiyirunta iṭatti nōkkiya aḷavil talivikkut taṉṉiyum mīṟik kaṇkaḷ nīr niṟiya, ataṉik kaṇṭa aṉṉi, “makaḷē! uṉakku uṭalnalam illiyā? iṅkē vā, uṉakku muttam koṭukkiṟēṉ” eṉṟu iṉiya coṟkaḷāl aḻitta aḷavil, uyiriṉum ciṟanta nāṇattiyē maṟantu, taṉakku vanta nōy talivaṉ mārpiṉāl viḷintatu eṉṟu aṉṉikkuk kūṟat toṭaṅki ataṉit taṭuttuk koṇṭa ceytiyit talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu. (naṟ. 17, nac. 111) ivi pōṉṟa kūṟṟukkaḷ koḷḷappaṭum.
talivi, “kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭatu” eṉṟu añcit tōḻikkuc colla, avaḷ, “namar niṉṉi avaṟkē koṭukkac  cūḻntār” eṉac colli accam nīkkiyatu -
“tōḻi nēṟṟiravu talivaṉ cūṭi vanta kaṇṇiyi eṉ kūntaluḷ cerukiyiruntēṉ. iṉṟu tāy eṉ talikku eṇṇey nīvak kūntali aviḻttapōtu naṟṟāy kāṇac cevilimuṉ appū viḻa, aṉṉi viṉavalō ciṉavalō ceyyātu neruppit toṭṭavarip pōlak kiyi utaṟikkoṇṭu appuṟam pōyiṉāḷ;yāṉ iṉi eṉṉa ceyvatu?” eṉṟu talivi kūṟat tōḻi, “avaṉ kaṇṇiyi nī puṉintāy. namarum avaṉukkē uṉṉi maṇam muṭittu vikka muṭivu ceytuviṭṭaṉar. virivil tirumaṇam nikaḻum” eṉṟu taliviyatu accattip pōkkiyatu. 			(kali. 115)
talivi, ‘kāṇāvakiyiṉ poḻutu naṉi ikappiṉ’ kaṇ kūṟiyatu -
iravukkuṟiyiṭattu varum talivaṉi iṉṟu iravu muḻutum kāṇṭal aritu eṉṟu talivi karutumāṟu, avaḷ palappala iṭiyūṟukaḷāl kuṟiyiṭatti nōkkic cella muṭiyāmal kālam vīṇāṉa niliyil kūṟiyatu.
“tōḻi! maṟṟa ūrkaḷilellām tiruviḻākkālaṅkaḷil makkaḷ iravil neṭunēram variyil uṟaṅkāmal viḻit tiruttal iyalpu. āyiṉ ivvūriluḷḷārō tiruviḻā illāta nāḷkaḷilum iravil neṭunēram uṟaṅkāmal viḻittuḷḷaṉar. ūril uḷḷār uṟaṅkiya piṉṉum, kaṭitteruviluḷḷa kaṭikaḷellām aṭikkappaṭṭa piṉṉum, nammēl kaṭuñcol collum aṉṉi uṟaṅkātu irukkiṟāḷ. nammi iṟceṟittu vittuk kākkum aṉṉi  uṟaṅkiyapiṉṉar ūrkkāvalar uṟakka miṉṟip paṟi koṭṭi ūric cuṟṟivaruvar. kāvalar oli aṭaṅkiṉ nāy kurikkat toṭaṅkukiṟatu. nāyiṉ kurip poli aṭaṅkiyatē eṉṟu nōkkiṉ, vāṉattil nilā pakal pōlap pēroḷiyuṭaṉ tikaḻkiṟatu. naḷḷiravil nilā maṟintāl, maṉiyiluḷḷa elikaḷi uṇavākak koḷḷum koṭiya vāyiyuṭiya kōṭṭāṉ pēykaḷ vaḻaṅkum naḷḷiravil kuḻaṟukiṟatu. kōṭṭāṉ oli aṭaṅ kiya ciṟitu nērattil maṉivāḻ kōḻic cēval vikaṟip poḻutiṉ varukiyik kāṭṭak kūvat toṭaṅkukiṟatu. ittakiya iṭiyūṟukaḷ illāta orunāḷ vāyttālum, uṟutiyāṉa maṉattip peṟṟirāta nam talivar iṅku varumāṟu illi. ivaṟṟi nōkkat tittaṉ eṉṟa aracaṉuṭiya uṟiyūriṉ kāvaṟkāṭṭik kaṭappataṟkuḷḷa iṭiyūṟukaḷip pōla nam kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻvataṟku iṭiyūṟu mikutiyākavuḷḷatu” (akanā. 122) eṉavum,
“neṭiya veḷḷiya nilavē! vēṅkippūkkaḷ viḻunta pāṟi pōlap pulikkuṭṭi kāṭci vaḻaṅkum iraviṭit talivaṉ kaḷavu oḻukkattiṟku niṉ oḷi iṭiyūṟāka vuḷḷatu. nī nallialli!”  (kuṟun. 47)
“eṉ talivaṉ vēṅkikaḷ vaḻaṅkum kuṟukiya vaḻiyē iravukkuṟikku varumpōtu nī veḷippaṭṭu oḷi vaḻaṅkā tiruppiṉ, niṉṉi viḻuṅkum pāmpiṉ paṟkaḷukku nī iriyākāmal iruppāyāka eṉṟu niṉṉi vāḻttu kiṟēṉ” (yā. ka. 87 mēṟ.) eṉavum varum talivi kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 107 nac.)
talivi, ‘ kāmakkiḻatti taṉmakat taḻīi, ēmuṟu viḷiyāṭ ṭiṟutikkaṇ’ kūṟal -
talivaṉāl maṉiyaṟattukku uriyaḷāka varintu koḷ ḷappaṭṭa kāmakkiḻatti taliviputalvaṉ maṉippuṟattu viḷiyāṭukiṉṟavaṉit taḻuvikkoṇṭu tāṉ ēmuṟu taṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa viḷiyāṭṭiṉ iṟutikkaṇ talivi kūṟutal.
avaḷ, “emmip pātukāppīrō!” eṉṟu viṉaviyavaḻi, avaṉum ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭāṉ pōlak kūṟuvaṉavum uḷa ātaliṉ, ‘ēmuṟu viḷiyāṭṭu’ eṉṟār. ‘iṟuti’ eṉṟār, viḷiyāṭṭu muṭiyum tuṇiyum tāṉ (talivi) maṟiya niṉṟu piṉṉark kūṟutaliṉ.
“taliva! teruvil putalvaṉ viḷiyāṭa, niṉ kāmakkiḻatti nāṉ maṟiyaniṉṟu nōkkuvati aṟiyātu, avaṉit taḻuvi avaṉoṭu cuvipaṭap pēcikkoṇṭiruntapōtu, nāṉ avaḷi aṇuki, “ēṉ pētuṟṟāy? nīyum ivaṉukkut tāytāṉē!’eṉṟu kūṟa, kaḷavu koṇṭa poruḷōṭu akappaṭṭa kaḷvarip pōla nāṇi nilam kiḷittu niṉṟa avaḷum niṉmakaṉukkut tāyātal ēṟṟatē eṉṟu nāṉ virumpi ēṟṟuk koṇṭēṉ!” eṉṟu talivi kūṟutal (akanā. 16)	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, kāmam ciṟattaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
talivaṉiṭam kāmam ciṟantu tōṉṟiyavaḻi, avaṉ pirivi yum variyātu pōtaliyum āṟṟāta talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
“ūril oli aṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. ūravar uṟaṅkiviṭṭaṉar. peṇṇi mītuḷḷa aṉṟilkaḷ tuṇi piriyiṉ varuntik kūppiṭum kuralik kēṭṭāl, talivi nammi niṉintu varuntuvāḷ eṉṟu nam talivaṉ taṉ neñcattē niṉippatum uṇṭō?” (naṟ. 303) eṉavum,
“vāṉattil katiravaṉ maṟiyum kālattē paṟavikaḷum māmarattil uḷḷa tam pārppukkaḷukku iriyi ūṭṭu taṟku virintu cellukiṉṟaṉa. attakiya kaṭamiyuṇarcci nam talivaṉpāl illiyē!”  eṉavum.
“naṇṭē! immāliyil eṉ talivaṉ eṉṉip pirintu tēr ēṟip pōkiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ tēr ceṉṟa cuvaṭṭik kaṇṭāvatu nāṉ āṟutal uṟuvēṉ. ātaliṉ maṇalil tēr ceṉṟa cuvaṭṭik kalikkātē!” (ain, aim. 42) eṉavum,
“nāriyē! talivaṉtēr nī irukkum pakkam vantāl, ‘taliviyi virivil maṇantu illaṟam naṭatta viḻiyātu, iṅṅaṉam kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻttit tēril vantu pōtal  kūṭātu’ eṉpataṉi avaṉtēri niṟuttik kūṟu” eṉavum kūṟutal pōlvaṉa. 	(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi, kāytal paṟṟik kūṟal -
kāytal - talivaṉik kāytal. talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟuvatu.
“tōḻi! eṉ aḻaku toliya makiḻvu keṭa uyirē pōvatāka iruppiṉum, avarip paṟṟik kuṟi kūṟātē. namakkut tantiyum tāyum avarē. avarukku nammiṭam aṉpu illāmaṟ pōṉaviṭattu avari veṟuppatāl payaṉ eṉ?” (kuṟun. 93) eṉṟu talivaṉik kāyntu talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟutal.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, kār tōṉṟiyavaḻi vampu eṉṟatu -
“tōḻi! talivar kārkālat toṭakkattil mīṇṭuvaruva tākak kūṟic ceṉṟār. koṉṟi kārkālattē alarum pū. pala poṉ aṇikaḷiyum aṇinta makaḷir kūntal pōlap pūkkaḷ alarnta koṉṟi maraṅkaḷi uṭiya kāṭu kār kālam vantuviṭṭa ceytiyik koṉṟimalarkaḷik koṇṭu terivittapōtilum, talivar poy kūṟārāka lāṉ, avar mīṇṭu vārāta variyil kār kālam vantu viṭṭatāka yāṉ koḷḷēṉ” (kuṟun. 21) eṉṟu talivi kūṟutal. (vampu - paruva mallāk kālattut tōṉṟum putumi.)
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘kāytalum uvattalum pirittalum peṭṭalum, āvayiṉ varūum niliyiṉum’ eṉṟataṉkaṇ, tōḻiyip pirittalākiya niliyiṉkaṇ kūṟiyatu. “kāṉam kāreṉak kūṟukiṟatu;talivar vārār” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟiṟku māṟāka, “yāṉ atu koḷḷēṉ (‘yāṉō tēṟēṉ’)” eṉṟu, tōḻiyip pirittuk kūṟiyavāṟu.			(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, kiḻavōṉ ceyviṉikku añcutal -
“talivaṉ pirintu ceṉṟaviṭattuk karupporuḷkaḷiṉ aṉput toḻilkaḷik kaṇṭu, eṭutta karumam muṭiyātu mīṇṭuviṭuvāṉō?” eṉṟu talivi añcutal
“pulamputaru kuralōṭu āṇpuṟāvip peṇpuṟā aḻikkum varuttam kaṇṭu cīṟūril taṅki viṉimuṭiyāmal talivar mīṇṭu viṭuvārō?” (kuṟun. 79) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 148 nac.)
talivi, kuḻal kēṭṭut tōḻikkuk kūṟal -
“ippiṟappil piṟarkkut tīṅku ceyiṉ immiyilēyē ataṉ payaṉi nukartal vēṇṭum. ‘maṟumiyiltāṉ ataṉi nukarvar’ eṉpavar uṇmi uṇarātār. talivaṉ pirinta kālattil nammi eḷiyār eṉṟu poruṭpaṭut tāmal taṉ oliyāl tuṉpuṟuttiya vēyṅkuḻal iṉamē cuṭṭut tuḷiyiṭappaṭṭavāṟu kāṇka!” (tiṇimāli. 123) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘palvēṟu niliyiṉum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, ‘kuṟiyiṉ oppumi maruṭaṟkaṇ’ kūṟiyatu -
iravukkuṟiyiṭattu varum talivaṉ ceyyum kuṟi piṟitu oṉṟaṉāṉ nikaḻntu akkuṟiyi ottavaḻi, ataṉit talivaṉ ceyta kuṟiyākavē talivi mayaṅkiviṭattut tōḻiyiṭam kūṟiyatu.
“tōḻi! nam talivaṉuṭiya tēril pūṭṭappaṭum kutiri yiṉ maṇiyōci kēṭṭāṟ pōlat tōṉṟa, eḻuntu kuṟiyiṉkaṇ ceṉṟu vēṅki marattilirunta paṟavikaḷ olitta olieṉṟu uṇarntu varuttattāl maṉam uruki mīṇṭēṉ” (ain. aim. 50) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
“āṇkuraṅku oṉṟu tāṉ tāvip pāyntāl kiḷi tāṅkumā tāṅkātā eṉpati niṉiyāmal oru kiḷimēl pāyntu ataṉi muṟittuviṭṭatu. (ataṉāl kiḷi muṟin tamikkuk  kāraṇam kuraṅkiṉ ceyalē; kuraṅku kiḷiyiṉ vaṉmi yaṟintu pāyntiruppiṉ atu muṟiya vēṇṭiya nili viḷintirātu) kuraṅkiṉ ceyalāl kiḷi muṟintatu pōlat talivaṉ ceyalāl eṉ tōḷkaḷ pacantaṉa” (kuṟun. 121) eṉṟu tāṉ kuṟi peṟuṅ kālattu vārātu kālam tāḻttu vantu kuṟi piḻikkac ceyta talivaṉ ceyali uṭkoṇṭu talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟiyatu.	(to. po. 111)
talivi, kūṭal iḻittal -
talivaṉ varukiyi aṟiyat talivi kūṭalcuḻi iḻittup pārttal.
“tāḻiyiṉ niḻalilē talivi iruntu kūṭalcuḻiyi vaḷippāḷ; cuḻippāḷ, piṉ vaḷikkikaḷāl aḻip pāḷ. avaḷ peṟṟi itu!” (ampikā. 295) eṉṟu tōḻi, oruvaḻit taṇanta talivaṉ pirivāl  talivi ceyyum ceyalkaḷi nōkkik kūṟuvatu.
iḵtu ‘oruvaḻit taṇattal’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.	(i. vi. 525 uri)
talivi, ‘kūṟiya vāyil koḷḷāk kāli’ kūṟal-
tōḻi talivaṟkuk kuṟinērntu vāyilākit talivi kuṟi nayappak kūṟiyavaḻiyum piṟavaḻiyum avaḷ kūṟṟiṉi ēṟṟuk koḷḷātu talivi kūṟutal.
“talivaṉ pōrkkaḷiṟu pōṉṟa taṉ perumitam kuṉṟi uṉ niṉivāl uruki niṟkiṉṟāṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiya tōḻiyi nōkkit talivi, “teruvil oru kāraṇamum iṉṟik kalaṅkuvōriṭattil karuṇi koṇṭu avar tuyariyum niṉ tuyarākak karuti nī varuntuvatu eṟṟukku?” (kali. 60) eṉpatu pōṉṟu maṟumoḻi kūṟutalum,
“tōḻi! ‘tōḷvaḷi nekiḻa, kaṇkaḷ oḷiyiḻakka, neṟṟiyil pacali pāya, ippolivuaḻivukaḷik kaṇṭu ūrmakaḷir paḻi tūṟṟumāṟu nam kātalar namakkut tuṉpam taruvarallar ātaliṉ, virivil varukuvar’ eṉṟu nī collum inta aṉpuccol, kollaṉ uliyil teḷitta nīr avvuli neruppic ciṟitu avikkumāṟu pōla, eṉ nōyic ciṟitu taṇittup pātukāvalāka irukkiṟatu. āyiṉ eṉ nōy niliyākat taṇikkappaṭ ṭilatu” (naṟ. 133) eṉpatu pōlak kūṟutalum ām.			(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi kūṟum uḷḷuṟi uvamam -
talivi, marutam neytal eṉṉum īriṭattum mikutiyum uḷḷuṟi uvamam kūṟuvāḷ. aṅṅaṉam kūṟumpōtu payaṉpaṭuttum karupporuḷkaḷum avaḷāl aṟiyappaṭṭa ṉavāy avaḷ irukkum illatti aṭuttaṉavāka iruttal vēṇṭum.
kuṟiñcikkaṇ talivi kūṟum uḷḷuṟi uvamam peruvara viṟṟaṉṟu. ēṉiya tiṇikaḷil mikamika arukiyē uḷḷuṟi uvamam kūṟuvāḷ.	(to. po. 301, 304 pērā.)
talivi, kipaṭṭuk kalaṅkik kūṟal -
talivi etirpārāta vakiyil avaḷic cantitta talivaṉ oruvaḻi avaḷi akappaṭuttavaḻi, avaṉik kāṇṭalit tāṉ virumpiyavaḷ eṉiṉum, avvetirpārāta cantippil tāṉ kalaṅkiya tiṟatti avaḷ tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
“etirpārāvaki eṉṉi etirppaṭṭa talivaṉ, taṉakku aruḷ ceyyāmal iruttal eṉakku ēṟṟataṉṟu eṉṟu kūṟi, narantampūviṉ maṇam kamaḻum eṉ kūntali añcātu paṟṟi, atil cūṭiyirunta māliyit tāṉ mōntu makiḻntu, naṟavampūp pōṉṟa eṉ viralkaḷit taṉ kaṇkaḷil oṟṟik koṇṭu, veytākap perumūcceṟintu, tāṉ toyyil eḻutiya eṉ iḷiya koṅkikaḷi iṉimi yākat taṭavip piṉ mēṉi muḻutiyum iṉitākat tivantamiyāl, yāṉ kippaṭṭu varuntiya varuttattik kaḷintēṉ” (kali. 54) eṉṟal pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivaṉ kiyakappaṭṭa piṉṉar ”eṉ ceytēmāyiṉēm” eṉṟu kalakkamuṟutal eṉṟu urippārum uḷar.	109 iḷa.
talivi kiyaṟu kiḷavi -
talivi ceyalaṟṟu varuntik kūṟal. “maṇaṟkuṉṟē! aṉṉaṅ kaḷē! nārikāḷ! tam tēril ūrkkuc ceṉṟu aṉṟē mīṇṭu varuvatākak kūṟiya talivar colli nampi varunti yum āṟṟiyiruntēṉ. ‘eṉakkum avarukkum uyir oṉṟu; kūrntu nōkkiṉ uṭalum oṉṟu’ eṉṟiruntēṉ. iṉiyavar naṭpu naṉṟu!” (ampikā. 294) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟu talivi pulamputal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, “oruvaḻit taṇattal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu;uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.			(i. vi. 525 uri)
talivi, kiyaṟu tōḻi kaṇṇīr tuṭippuḻik kūṟal -
talivi talivaṉip pirinta tuṉpattāl varuntumpōtu, avaḷ āṟṟāmiyik kaṇṭa ceyalaṟṟa tōḻi avaḷ kaṇṇīrit tuṭittaviṭattē, avaḷi nōkkit talivi kūṟutal.
“tōḻi! yāṉ eṉ kāmanōyip poṟuttukkoṇṭu tāṉ uḷḷēṉ. āyiṉ, kuṉṟanāṭaṉākiya nam talivaṉik kaṇṭa kaṇkaḷ tamakku avaṉōṭu uḷḷa uṟavu kāraṇamākak kalaṅkik kaṇṇīr ukukkiṉṟaṉa!” (kuṟun. 241) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi (iṟivi) kiyuṟi ēṟṟami pāṅki iṟivaṟku uṇarttal -
“aiya! uṉatu kiyuṟiyi em talivi ēṟṟu mikavum makiḻntāḷ. ataṉit taliyiṉum mārpiṉum vittut taḻuviṉāḷ. avaḷ attaḻiyiyum kaṇṇiyiyum nīyākavē koṇṭāḷpōlum!” (tañci. kō. 129) eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itaṉit ‘taḻi viruppu urittal’ eṉṉum tiruk kōviyār.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 149)
talivi (pāṅki taṉkik) kiyuṟi ēṟṟal -
talivi talivaṉ tanta kiyuṟiyi ēṟṟukkoḷḷutal.
“tōḻi!tītākil eṉ? naṉṟākil eṉ? ulakam yātu kūṟiṉ eṉ? cilampaṉ, niṉṉiṭattut tanta taḻiyum kaṇṇiyum yāṉ iṉṟu peṟṟukkoḷkiṟēṉ” (ampikā. 147) eṉṟu kūṟit talivi kiyuṟi ēṟṟal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 148)
talivi, ‘koṭiyōr koṭumi cuṭumeṉa oṭiyātu, nallici nayantōr colloṭu tokiip, pakutiyiṉ nīṅkiya takutikkaṇ’ kūṟal -
“koṭiyāratu koṭumi cuṭukiṉṟatu” eṉap puṇarcciyit tavirkkāmal, pukaḻi virumpiṉōr collōṭē orup paṭṭu vēṟupaṭutaliṉ nīṅkiya takutikkaṇ talivi kūṟutal.			(145 iḷa.)
koṭiyōrākiya pāṇar kūttar viṟaliyar antaṇar mutaliyōr talivaṉ pukaḻik kūṟutaṟku virumpiṉō rākip parattiyarkku vāyilāka vantu kūṟiya collōṭē, tāṉum avariṭattē cērntu,  “kāvaṟpāṅkiṉ pakkamum āṅkōr pakkamum ākiya pakuti kāttaṟkup pirivatākak kūṟi ataṉiṉiṉṟum nīṅkiya, parattiyarik kūṭiya koṭumi neñcic cuṭum” eṉṟu kūṟi, avaṉ tavaṟṟik kūṟutalit tavirāmal kūṟutaṟkut takka takutiyiṭattut talivi kūṟutal		(147 nac.)
“pakutikaḷik kāttaṟkup pirivēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟip pirintu, pāṇar mutaliyōr putitil kūṭṭiya parattiyariṭattē oḻukiya mey vēṟupāṭṭōṭē vantavaṉik kaṇṭu ap pakutikaḷip parattiyarākkik kūṟuvāḷāyiṟṟu.
“taliva! niṉ parattiyar ukirāl ceyta vaṭukkaḷiyum, avarkaḷ eyiṟu aḻuntiya itaḻiyum niṉpāṇaṉ eṉakkuk kāṭṭuvataṟkāka niṉṉi iṅkē aṉuppiyuḷḷāṉō?”
“taliva! niṉ parattiyar puṉalāṭutaṟkut teppamāṉa mārpilē ūṭiya apparattiyar ūṟṟiya cātiliṅkatti eṉakkuk kāṭṭu eṉṟu ūril tuṇikaḷit tuvikka vāṅkikkoṇṭu tuvikkāmal uṉakkākat  tiriyum vaṇṇātti aṉuppiyuḷḷāḷō?
“taliva! niṉ parattiyar mayirmuṭikaḷi nī vakirntu ki ceykiyiṉālē avarkaḷ cūṭiya pūkkaḷil makarantam paṭinta niṉ mēṉiyi eṉakkuk kāṭṭu eṉṟu niṉṉi vīṇē pukaḻntu tiriyum antaṇaṉ aṉuppiyuḷḷāṉō?” 	(kali. 72)
eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟukkaḷāl pāṇaṉ, pulitti, antaṇaṉ eṉṉumivar vāyil āyiṉavāṟu pulappaṭum.
talivaṉ kālam tāḻttic cōrvuṭaṉum kacaṅkiya āṭi yuṭaṉum cantaṉam aḻinta vērviyuṭaṉum kulinta muṭi māliyuṭaṉum vantu, tāṉ cāṉṟōrik kaṇṭatākavum (kali. 93), kutiri vaḻaṅki vantatākavum (95), putiya yāṉiyik kaṇṭu vantatākavum (96), putuppuṉalāṭi vantatākavum (97), kuṟumpūḻp pōr kaṇṭu vantatākavum (98) kūṟiyavaḻi, avaṟṟi uṇmiyeṉṟu koḷḷātu, parattiyarit kaṭavuḷarākavum kutiriyākavum yāṉi yākavum putuppuṉalākavum kuṟumpūḻākavum uruvakittut talivi ūṭal koṇṭavāṟu.	(147. nac)
talivi, ‘koṭumi oḻukkam kōṭal vēṇṭi, aṭimēl  vīḻnta kiḻavaṉi neruṅkik, kātal eṅkiyar kāṇiṉ naṉṟeṉa, mātar cāṉṟa vakiyiṉkaṇ’ kūṟal -
talivaṉ taṉ kaṭamikaḷi viṭuttup parattiyarpāl ceṉṟu taṅkik koṭumi ceyta oḻukkattit talivi poṟuttal vēṇṭi avaḷ aṭiyiṉmēl viḻuntu vaṇaṅka, avaṉik kōpattāl iṭitturittu, “nī iṅṅaṉam paṇintu oḻukutalip parattiyar kaṇṭāl naṉṟāyirukkum!” eṉṟu collip piṉ avaṉiṭam ūṭal nīṅkik kātal amintu māṟiya vēṟupāṭṭiṉkaṇ avaḷ kūṟutal.
talivi talivaṉatu puṟattoḻukkattic cāṉṟukaḷōṭu uṟutippaṭuttiyavaḻit talivaṉ, “yāṉ ceyta tavaṟukaḷi eṉmēl ākki eṉ poykaḷi veḷippaṭutti eṉ tavaṟu kaḷik kiyoṭu piṭittukkoṇṭāy, yāṉ ceytatu tavaṟutāṉ; eṉakku aruḷuka” eṉṟu kūṟa, talivi, “niṉakku aruḷceyya nāṉ yār? nī aḷittuk kiviṭṭa kuṟum pūḻkaḷ varuntātapaṭi niṉ pāṇaṉāl aḻittu vantu, niṉ aruḷiṉāl aḷittu āṭuvāyāka” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟil  (kali. 95), “eṉakku aruḷuka!” eṉṟu talivaṉ paṇintami yum, “aruḷutaṟku nāṉ yār?” eṉavē, talivi kātalum, “parattiyē niṉ paṇivi ēṟpavaḷ” eṉṟu kuṟittamiyum uṇarappaṭum.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi kōlam ceyyāmi paṟṟic cevili tōḻiyi viṉavutal -
kaḷavuk kālattil talivaṉ iṭṭuppirintum arumi ceytu ayarttum taliviyi nāḷtōṟum tavaṟātu vantu kāṇum niliyiṉiṉṟu tavaṟiyavaḻi, talivi, taṉṉiṭattut talivaṉ aṉpiṉṟit taṉṉip puṟakkaṇittāṉ eṉṟa tuyarāl, nāḷtōṟum ceytukoḷḷum oppaṉikaḷic ceytukoḷḷāmal nōyuṟṟavaḷip pōla vāḷā irunta viṭattu, avaḷnili kaṇṭa cevili avaḷatu vēṟupāṭṭuk kuriya kāraṇatti, eppoḻutum avaḷip piriyātu uṭaṉuṟiyum tōḻiyi viṉaval.
“aṉṉavi piṟavum” (to. po. 113 iḷa.) eṉpataṉāṉ ikkūṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭum.
talivi, ‘ciṟanta ceyki avvaḻit tōṉṟi, aṟampuri uḷḷamoṭu taṉ varavu aṟiyāmip, puṟam ceytu peyarttal vēṇṭu iṭattu’k kūṟal -
kāmakkiḻattiyatu ēmuṟu viḷiyāṭṭup pōlātu talivi taṉ putalvaṉit taḻīi viḷiyāṭṭiyuṭiya av viṭattē talivaṉ tōṉṟi, avviḷiyāṭṭu makiḻcci yākiya maṉiyaṟattiṉik kāṇa virumpiya neñcōṭē taṉṉit talivi aṟiyāmal avaḷpiṉṉē niṟṟalic ceytu taliviyatu tuṉiyip pōkkutal vēṇṭiya iṭattiṉkaṇ avaḷ kūṟutal.
taṉ varavu aṟiyāmi eṉṟatu, taṉṉik kaṇṭavarākiya taliviyarukē niṉṟavarkaḷ taṉakkuc ceyyum ācāraṅ kaḷiyum avarkaḷ ceyyāmal kikavittut taṉvaravu aṟiyāmal (talivaṉ) niṟṟal.
tāy makaṉi aḻittuvantu “nī kaṟṟa coṟkaḷil cilavaṟṟi yāṉ makiḻumāṟu niṉ maḻalimoḻiyil kūṟu” eṉṟa aḷavil, tāyiṉ piṉpuṟamāka varum taṉ tantiyip pārtta ciṟuvaṉ, “appā appā!” eṉṟu colla, talivaṉ vantami aṟiyāta talivi, “nām evvaḷavu aṉpu pārāṭ ṭiṉum ivaṉukkut tanti niṉipputtāṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiya aḷavil talivaṉ arukil vantu niṟka, talivi eḷḷi urikka, talivaṉ tāṉ tavaṟu ētum ceyyavilli eṉṟu kūṟa, talivi parattiyar cūṭiya pūkkaḷiṉ tātu utirntu poliviḻanta avaṉ uṭiyiṉic cuṭṭi avaṉuṭiya parattimiyi meyppikka, talivaṉ, “yāṉ kuṟṟa muṭiyēṉallēṉ eṉṟu evvaḷavu kūṟiyum, uṉ kōpam taṇiyavilliyāyiṉ, kaṉṟiṉiṭattu aṉpu koṇṭu varum pacuppōla eṉ putalvaṉit taḻuvikkoḷkiṟēṉ” eṉṟu taliviyiṭam iruntu putalvaṉi vāṅkavē, talivi tuṉi tīrntavāṟu (kali. 81).	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, ciṉṉāḷ kaḷavukkūṭṭam niṉintu varuntik kūṟal -
cilanāḷkaḷ talivaṉik kaḷavil kūṭiya iṉpatti niṉittu avaṉ pirintu pōṉatāl atu taṭippaṭṭatic cuṭṭit talivi varuntik kūṟutal.
“tōḻi! vēṅki aḻaku ceyyum cōliyil talivaṉ eṉṉoṭu kaḷavil cilanāḷkaḷ iṭiyīṭiṉṟik kūṭi makiḻvuṟuttiṉāṉ. taṭiyētumiṉṟi makiḻcci tanta akkaḷavukkūṭṭam ippoḻutu avaṉ pirintu ceṉṟatāl taṭiyuṟṟu varuttam ceykiṟatē!” (ain. aim. 11) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘piṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷap paṭṭavaṟṟuḷ oru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 173 nac.)
talivicuṟṟattār talivaṟku varivu maṟuttavaḻit tōḻi aṟattoṭu  niliyāl kūṟutal -
‘avaṉ varivu maṟuppiṉkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal’ kāṇka. 			(to. po. 114 nac. uri)
talivi, cūḷuṟavu poy eṉṟal -
talivaṉ, “niṉṉiṟ piriyēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiya uṟutimoḻi yit talivi poy eṉṟu karututal.
“talivaṉ vaṇaṅkum kaṭalteyvamum naṭuvunili tavaṟiyatu tāṉō? eṉ maṉattiṭi nāṇattit tuṟantu nāṉ uyir cōrvatu ēṉ? aṉṟu tām ceyta cūḷuriyi maṟanta talivari viṭuttu atteyvam eṉṉi vāṭṭuvatu ēṉ?” (ampikā. 297) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘oruvaḻit taṇattal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.			(i. vi. 525 uri)
talivi, ‘cellākkāli celkeṉa viṭuttaṟ’kaṇ kūṟal -
talivaṉ illatti viṭuttuc cellāṉ eṉpataṉi iṭamum kālamum paṟṟit talivi aṟintakālattu, ūṭal uḷḷattāṉ uṭaṉē avaṉik kūṭappeṟātu, “paratti yariṭamē celka” eṉṟu kūṟi viṭuttaṟkaṇ kūṟutal.
“em putalvaṉi em tiruptikkākap palavāṟu pārāṭṭik koṇṭu iṅku ēṉ irukkiṟāy? nī puṉinta kōlaṅ kaḷiyellām aḻikkum emputalvaṉi emmiṭam tantu viṭṭu apparattiyariṭamē celka!” (kali. 79) eṉavum,
“ūra! uṉṉiṭam kiḷakka emakku yātu urimi uḷḷatu? nēṟṟukkūṭa oruttiyi nam illattiṟku aḻittu vantu maṇamceytu koṇṭaṉi eṉṟu ūravar pēcukiṉṟaṉar. yām atu paṟṟi oṉṟum collavilli. uṉ pirivāṉ eṉ uṭal vāṭa, ataṉāl eṉ toṭi nekiḻntālum nekiḻka. peruma! iṉi niṉ viruppam pōlac ceyaṟpaṭuka! uṉṉi, uṉ ceyal tavaṟukaḷi eṭuttuc collit taṭuppavar yār?” (akanā. 46) eṉavum talivi kūṟutal.
	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, celavuk kuṟippaṟintu tōḻikkuk kūṟal -
“talivar aḻakiya māmi niṟattil tēmal paṭarnta eṉ mārpiṉit tam kaṇṇoṭu toṭuttu vittāṟpōlap palakālum kūrntu nōkkiyum tiruptiyaṭiyātu, puṇarc ciyāl eṉ neṟṟiyil tōṉṟiya viyarviyit tuṭippa tākiya aḷavumikka ceyalkaḷ, avar eṉṉip piritaṟku eṇṇukiṟār eṉpatik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa” (kali. 4) eṉṟu talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
iccūttirattuḷ ‘palvēṟu nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koḷḷap paṭṭa kūṟṟu itu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, cevili taṉṉi uṟṟu nōkki vēṟupāṭṭiṟkuk kāraṇam viṉaviyatākak kūṟal -
“tuṟivaṉākiya talivaṉ pirintamiyālē, attuṟiyiṉ kaṇ iruntu aḻutamiyāl varuttamuṟṟa eṉatu mukatti nōkki aṉṉi eṉ tuyarattit taṉakku urikkumāṟu viṉaviṉāḷāka, nāṉ ataṉi maṟittuk ‘kōrip pullāṟ ceyyappeṟṟa eṉ pāviyik kikkoṇṭu yāṉ viḷiyāṭ ṭayarnta ciṟṟiliyum kaṭal citittu viṭṭatu’ eṉṟu kūṟi uyntēṉ” (naṟ. 17) eṉṟu talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟum ikkūṟṟiṉkaṇ, cevili taṉ vēṟupāṭu kaṇṭu kāraṇam viṉaviyami koṇṭu kūṟṟāka vantatu.
‘neṟipaṭu nāṭṭattu nikaḻntavi maṟittaṟkaṇ’ talivi cevilikku maṟittatu ittuṟi.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi cōrtal -
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattil talivaṉ taliviyit tōḻiyar iruppiṭam cellumāṟu viṭuttu akaṉṟa piṉṉar, talivaṉ pirintamikkut talivi varuntutal.
“ik kuruntamaraṅkaḷiṭiyē aṉpoṭu kūṭiyum vāṭiyum eṉ aḻakik kikkoṇṭu ceṉṟa talivaṉatu tēruṭaṉ eṉ uḷḷamum ceṉṟuviṭṭatu. talivaṉ pirivip poṟuttal āṟṟātu eṉṉuyir pulampi varunta yāṉ uyiraṟ ṟavaḷ pōla uḷḷēṉ” (ampikā. 157) eṉṟu talivi cōrntu urittal. ‘neñcu mikkatu vāy cōrtal’ eṉpatu ikkōviyil nikaḻum kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu. (‘pakaṟ kuṟi’ eṉum tokutikkum iḵtu uriyatu.)	(i. vi. 509 uri)
talivi, ‘taṅkiya oḻukkattuk kiḻavaṉi vaṇaṅki, eṅkiyarkku uri eṉa irattaṟkaṇ’ kūṟiyatu -
parattiyarmāṭṭut taṅkiya cevviyi maṟiyāta oḻukkat toṭu vanta talivaṉi, “nī kūṟukiṉṟa paṇinta moḻikaḷi eṅkiyarkku vaṇaṅkik kūṟu” eṉa irantukōṭaṟkaṇ talivi kūṟiyatu.
“parattiyar mārpiṭi aṇinta pūkkaḷiṉ tātu utirntu aḻakiḻanta niṉmuṭimāli uṉakku uṭpakiyāy vantu uṉ parattimiyi veḷippaṭuttāviṭiṉ, uṉ parattimi paṟṟi veṟuttal illāta eṉṉiṭam, ‘yāṉ ettavaṟum ceyyēṉ’ eṉṟu teḷivittaṟku varuvāy. evvaḷavu āṟṟunīr vantu vīḻiṉum niṟiyāta kaṭal pōla, ettaṉi makaḷirit tōyntālum tiruptiyaṭiyāta uṉṉiṭam kōpikkum takutiyuṭiya peṇkaḷiṉ kōpattit tīrkkac cel. yāṅkaḷ uṉ poyyi meyyāka nampi eppoḻu tum uṉakkut tōṟṟiruppavarē!” (kali. 73) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
“taliva! uṉ mārpiṉi virumpiya paratti viḻinta ceyalkaḷic ceytukoṇṭu, emakku ētum karuṇi ceyyāmal, avarkaḷ iruppiṭattilēyē taṅkiyiruppatu uṉakkumāttiramaṉṟi eṅkaṭkum iṉitu!” (aiṅ. 49) eṉṟal pōlvaṉa.	(145 iḷa.)
talivi, ‘tantiyar oppar makkaḷ eṉpataṉāṉ, antamil ciṟappiṉ makappaḻittu neruṅkaliṉ’ kaṇ kūṟal -
viḷiyāṭukiṉṟa kālattu, ‘makkaḷ tantiyi oppar’ eṉṉum vētaviti paṟṟi, muṭivillāta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya makaṉip paḻittu vekuḷutaṟkaṇ talivi kūṟutal.
makaṉukkum itu varum eṉṟu karutik kūṟaliṉ, talivaṉip paḻittu eṉṉātu, ‘makappaḻittu’ eṉṟār.
“makaṉē! aḻakil uṉ tantiyi ottuḷḷāy; āyiṉ kuṇattil muḻutum uṉ tantiyik koḷḷaṟka. pakivari veṉṟu veṟṟi koḷḷum vīrattil uṉ tantiyip piṉpaṟṟuti; āyiṉ uṉ tantiyip pōla, tammi nampiya makaḷirik kiviṭṭuviṭum paṇpik koḷḷaṟka” eṉṟu talivi taṉ makaṉiṭam pēcikkoṇṭiruntapōtu piṉpuṟa māka vanta talivaṉik kaṇṭa putalvaṉ cirikka, taliviyum tirumpip pārkka, talivaṉ makaṉi aḻikkavē, malimītu ciṅkam pāyntatu pōla makaṉ tanti mārpil pāyntatu kaṇṭu, “aṟaṉillā aṉpili peṟṟa makaṉ, nāṉ taṭukkavum 
tantimārpiṉi aṇintuviṭṭāṉē!” eṉṟu avaḷ kūṟutal. (kali. 86).	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, ‘tamar taṟkātta kāraṇamaruṅkiṉ’ kūṟiyatu -
talivi talivaṉoṭu koṇṭa kaḷavoḻukkam ōraḷavu veḷippaṭṭa piṉṉar, ūravar kūṟum alariyum talivi yiṉ tōṟṟap poliviyum avaḷ aṭikkaṭi varuntutal mutalākiya kāraṇappakutiyiyum nōkki, avaḷtamar avaḷip puṟam pōkāvaṇṇam iṟceṟittavaḻi avaḷ kūṟutal.
“viḷiyāṭum makaḷirkku añci naṇṭu kaṭalil ōṭi oḷiyum tuṟiyiyuṭiya talivaṉatu meyyit tōynta naṭpu, avaṉoṭu maṟṟorunāḷ cirittu viḷiyāṭuvatikkūṭa nīkkiviṭṭatē!” (kuṟun. 401) eṉavum,
“mīṉuṇaṅkalip paṟavikaḷ kavarātapaṭi ōṭṭi, puṉṉi niḻalil taṅki, naṇṭu vaḷikaḷik kōliṭṭu alittu, ñāḻalmarattil tāḻikkayiṟṟāl amikkappaṭṭa ūñca lil āṭi, maṇalil kuravi āṭi, māliyil kaṭalāṭik kaṭaṟkariccōliyil taṅki nām varuvatip poṟukkāta ivvūrmakkaḷ nammēl poyppaḻi cumattiviṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ ‘pakalilum iravilum nām irukkum kaṭaṟkaric cōlippakkam oru tēr vantu pōkiṟatu’ eṉṟu kūṟiyati uṇmiyākak koṇṭu, tāy eṉṉi iṟceṟittu viṭṭāḷē!” (akanā. 20) eṉavum talivi tōḻiyiṭam varuntik kūṟiyavāṟu.
“koṅkikaḷ tōṟṟap polivu koṇṭuviṭṭaṉa. muṉ pōṉṟu viḻunteḻunta paṟkaḷ niralē amintuviṭṭaṉa. talimayirum kūṭṭi muṭiyum aḷavu vaḷarntuviṭṭatu. ippoḻutu taḻiyuṭi aṇiyum paruvam aṭintu viṭṭāy. āyattārōṭu iṉi viḷiyāṭap pōkak kūṭātu. tīya teyvaṅkaḷ niṉṉit tīṇṭiviṭum. iṉip pēti alli; nī petumpi!” (akanā. 7) eṉṟu tōṟṟap polivu kaṇṭu, tāy taṉṉi iṟceṟittatit tōḻi yiṭam talivi kūṟiyavāṟu.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi, talivaṟkut taṉ tamar varivuṭaṉ paṭṭataṉi virumputal -
“cōliyil kāntaḷ malara ataṉkaṇ vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum maliyi uṭiya talivaṉum tirumaṇam muṭittaṟku vantuviṭṭāṉ. iṉi, aṉṉi eṉṉi varuttum varutta mum nīṅkiviṭṭatu.” (ain. e. 3) eṉavum, ‘vaṇṭāḻaṅkurukiṉ tokuti, pakivarik koṉṟa vīraruṭiya āravāra ōci kēṭṭu añcum kuṭṭuvaṉuṭiya mānti eṉṟa ūr pōṉṟa iyaṟki vaṉappuṭiya eṉatu aḻakiya neṟṟikku urimi uṭiya talivaṉum avaṉē. iṉit tāyar varuttuvatum, tōḻiyar amitippaṭuttuvatum, teḷivu peṟātu talivaṉip pirintu taṉittu uṟaṅkuvatum ilavākum. talivaṉ eṉṉi maṇakkappōkum ceyti kēṭṭu ivvūrār makiḻcci aṭika!” (kuṟun. 34) eṉavum talivi kūṟutal pōlvaṉa.	(to. po. 107 nac.)
talivi, talivaṉ ikantami iyampal -
“niṉṉiṟ piriyēṉ” eṉṟu teyvattik kariyākki uritta talivaṉ taṉṉip pirintuviṭṭamiyit talivi tōḻi yiṭam kūṟutal.
‘talivaṉ eṉṉik kūṭiyapōtu yārum avaṉit tavirac cāṉṟu āvār ilar. mīṉ iri tēṭi niṉṟa kuruku oṉṟu tāṉ iruntatu. kolittoḻilil muṉintirunta atuvum namakkuc cāṉṟu āmāṟu illi! iṉṟu talivaṉ taṉ uṟutimoḻikku māṟākap pirintu ceṉṟuḷḷāṉ” (kuṟun. 25) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘aṟattoṭu niṟṟal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatorukūṟṟu. (talivaṉatu ippirivu oruvaḻit taṇantatum variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirintatum ām)	(na. a. 176)
talivi, talivaṉ iraviṭik kalantami tōḻikkuk kūṟal -
talivaṉatu pirivāṟṟāmiyāl tāṉ varuntiyatik kaṇṭu kalaṅkiya tōḻiyiṭam talivi, “talivaṉ iraviṉkaṇvantu eṉṉoṭu kūṭiṉāṉ” eṉpatit teṉṟalmēl vittuk kūṟutal.
“tōḻi! nāṉ pirivāṟṟāmiyāl varuntuvatāka nī niṉittal vēṇṭā. avaruṭiya aruḷi iravil peṟṟu nāṉ iṉpuṟṟatu uṇṭu. ataṉi arukiliruntu yārum aṟintilar! teṉṟal oṉṟu avaratu tiruttuḻāyiṉ maṇat tuṭaṉ vīci eṉ pulaṉkaḷiyum kalaṉkaḷiyum toṭṭut taṭavic ceṟṟatu” eṉṟa taliviyiṉ kūṟṟu. talivaṉ vantu kūṭiyati veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟa nāṇit teṉṟalmēl vittuk kuṟippāl kūṟiyavāṟu.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭamiyiṉ puṟat tiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvi. 56)
talivi, talivaṉ uruvu veḷippaṭa iraṅkal -
talivi āṭiṭattiṉiṉṟu illam ceṉṟapiṉ talivaṉatu uruvatti uruveḷiyāka eṅkum kāṇum nili aṭintu avaḷ varuntutal.
“puṉattil eṉṉit tēṭiya talivar eṉṉik kāṇāmal iṅkē kaṇṇīr corintu kalaṅkum eṉṉit tēṭi eṉ ūrkkē vantuviṭṭārā? eṅkup pārttālum eṉ uyiraṉiya talivariṉ aḻaku vaṭivam terikiṉṟatē!” (ampikā. 186) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pakaṟkuṟi iṭiyīṭu eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(i. vi. 515 uri)
talivi, talivaṉ (talimakaṉ) ūrkkuc celavu oruppaṭutal -
talivi talivaṉuṭiya ūrukkuc cella niṉittal.
“tōḻi! iṟceṟikkappaṭṭa nām pakalil palarum kāṇa nam nāṇatti viṭuttut talivaṉ ūriṉi viṉavi aṟintu aṅkuc ceṉṟu talivaṉi nēriṟ kaṇṭu, ‘nī cāṉṟavaṉākac ceyaṟpaṭavilli’ eṉṟu kūṟi varutaṟkup puṟappaṭuvōm; eḻuvāyāka” (naṟ. 365) eṉṟu talivi kūṟutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘varital vēṭki’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 164)
talivi, talivaṉ etir niṉṟu kūṟal -
cila pōtukaḷil parattiyar tām talivaṉāl puṟak kaṇikkappaṭṭatit taliviyiṭam kūṟa varuvatuṇṭu. avarkaḷ kūṟṟu uṇmitāṉā veṉṟu talivi ārāyntu muṭivu ceyvāḷ. aḵtu uṇmiyāyiruppiṉ, talivaṉ avarkaḷi varuttiyatu paṟṟit talivi makiḻtalum uṇṭu; talivaṉiṭam parattiyar urimi koṇṭāṭuvatu paṟṟit talivaṉiṭam ūṭuvatumuṇṭu. parattiyar taṉṉiṭam pēcik koṇṭirukkumpōtu talivaṉ illiṉkaṇ iruntāl talivi oṉṟum pēcāḷ; parattiyar illāta pōtu talivaṉ etir niṉṟu, avaṉ puṟattoḻukkam paṟṟik kuṟi kūṟiviṭuvāḷ.	(to. po. 213 kuḻa.)
talivi, talivaṉkaṇ nikaḻntatu niṉittut tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu -
talivi, muṉṉart talivaṉiṭattu nikaḻnta ceytiyi niṉittup piṉṉark kūṟiyatu.
“tōḻi! nīr vaṟṟiya kāṭṭu vaḻiyil cennāy paciyāṉ varuntum taṉ piṇavōṭu uḻiñcil maraniḻalil aminta naṭukalliṉ niḻalil paciyōṭu paṭuttirukkum iṭaṅkaḷik kaṭantu, ‘vaṟiyavarkaḷukku ītaṟkup poruḷ vēṇṭum’ eṉṟa kuṟikkōḷoṭu poruḷ tēṭutaṟkup paṇṭu pirintu ceṉṟa talivar poruṭkātal uṭiyavarē aṉṟi nammiṭattu aruṭkātal uṭiyavar allar. ātaliṉ iṉiyum nammi nīttu avar poruḷvayiṉ piritalum kūṭum” (akanā. 53) eṉṟu talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.	(to.po. 43  nac.)
talivi, talivaṉ kaṉaviṉkaṇ araṟṟiyati niṉintu tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu -
“tōḻi! ‘neñcu naṭuṅkumpaṭi palakālum kēṭṭum aiyuṟṟum añciya poruḷ orunāḷ uṇmiyākavē nikaḻntu varut tattit tarum’ eṉṟa paḻamoḻi eṉṉaḷavil uṇmiyāki viṭṭatu. eṉ talivar eṉṉic cila nāḷkaḷākap palapaṭiyākap pārāṭṭuvataṉik kaṇṭu ivar eṉṉi viṭṭup pirintu pōkap pōkiṟārō eṉṟa aiyam eṉṉuḷ ḷattil iruntatu. nēṟṟup paṭukkiyil eṉṉik kūṭi eṉ tōḷilē tuyil koṇṭa talivar tām kaṇṭa kaṉavil, ‘yāṉi, nīr vēṭkiyāl pēyttēri nōkki ōṭum koṭiya pāli vaḻiyik kaṭantu, ‘pōriṭattuṭittuk koṇṭirukkum iru peru vēntariyum cantu ceyvittu yāṉ mīḷum vari eṉ talivi maṉam aḻintu viṭāmal illaṟatti naṉmuṟiyil taṉittu naṭatta vallami yuṭiyaḷō?’ eṉṟu araṟṟiṉār. itaṉāl avar eṉṉip pirintu cellappōvatu uṟutiyākiviṭṭatu.” (kali. 24) eṉṟu talivaṉiṭam nikaḻntataṉi niṉittut talivi muṭivu ceytu tōḻiyiṭam kūṟiyatu. ituvum pālit tiṇiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(to. po. 43 nac.)
talivi, talivaṉ kuṟiñcinilattu vēṟṟu maliyiṉaṉ eṉpatik kuṟippāṟ kūṟutal -
pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟi iṭiyīṭukaḷāṉ taliviyik kūṭa muṭiyāta niliyilum talivaṉ virivil variyātu kālam tāḻttiya aḷavil, talivi tōḻiyiṭam talivaṉi iyaṟ paḻittuk kūṟiyapōtu, “nam talivaṉō, ‘niṉṉiṟ piriyēṉ. piriyiṉ, āṟṟēṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟiya uṟutimoḻiyip poyyākkiyavaṉ. aṅṅaṉam poyttavaṟku iyaṟki utaviyāka irātu eṉpatu vaḻakku. āyiṉ, poytta talivaṉatu mali maḻi tavaṟāmal peyvatāl viḷaṅkum aruvikaḷi uṭiyatāyirukkiṟatē! iḵtu eṉṉa viyappu!” (kali. 41) eṉṟu kūṟukiyil uṟutimoḻiyip poytta talivaṉ mali tam maliyiṉ vēṟāyatu eṉpatip peṟappaṭa vittal.	(to. po. 93 nac.)
talivi, talivaṉ ceṉṟa nāṭṭil ‘ivi ilakol?’ eṉṟu kūṟal -
“tōḻi! maṉi uṟi kuruvi pakalil iri tēṭi uṇṭu, poḻutu cāyum pōtu maṉi iṟappil taṉ pārppukka ḷoṭu taṅka vantu cērum mālip poḻutum, ataṉ nikaḻccikaḷum talivaṉ pirintu ceṉṟu taṅkiyirukkum nāṭṭil illi pōlum!” (kuṟun. 46) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
iccūttirattuḷ ‘palvēṟu nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koṇṭa kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, talivaṉ tavaṟṟit talivi veḷippaṭuttal kūṭātu eṉṟu kaḻaṟiya pāṅkikkuk kūṟiyatu -
