talivaṉ parattiyar toṭarpu koṇṭuḷḷāṉ eṉṟata ṉāṉ, avaṉ taṉ illattiṟku vantu taṉṉoṭu makiḻvākap paḻakutaṟku vāyppaḷikka maṟutta taliviyiṭam, “talivaṉ tavaṟē ceytiruppiṉum atu puṟattārkkup pulaṉām vakiyil naṭantu koḷvatu kulamakaḷirkku ēṟṟa ceya laṉṟu” eṉṟu kūṟiya pāṅkiyi nōkki avaḷ, “maṉikkaṇ uḷḷa vayalikkoṭi maṉikku veḷiyē uḷḷa koṟuk kantaṭṭi mītu tāvic ceṉṟu ataṉic cuṟṟip paṭarum ūraṉ talivaṉ ātaliṉ, avaṉ nam illattu iruppiṉum avaṉ uḷḷam eppoḻutum parattiyarmītē tāvic ceṉṟu avaric cuṟṟit tiriyum eṉpataṉi naṉkaṟintum, avaṉi ūravar paḻi tūṟṟutaṟku iṭaṉ taralākātu eṉṉum eṇṇattāṉ avaṉi nallavaṉ eṉṟē yāṅkaḷ kūṟikkoṇṭirukkiṟōm. āyiṉ eṉ mūṅkil pōṉṟa meṉtōḷkaḷ avaṉuṭiya puṟattu oḻukkattāṉ mika melintu avaṉik koṭiyaṉ eṉṟu paḻitūṟṟu kiṉṟaṉavē! itaṟku yāṉ yātu ceytal iyalum?” (aiṅ. 11) eṉpatu pōṉṟa kūṟṟu.
ippāṭaṟkaṇ, mutal karu uripporuḷ eṉṟa mūṉṟum varutaliṉ nāṭakavaḻakkum, talivaṉit talivi koṭumi kūṟutaliṉ ulakiyal vaḻakkum oruṅkē vantaṉa.			(to. po. 53 nac.)
talivi, ‘talivaṉ tavaṟilaṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟal -
kākki uppaṅkaḻikaḷiluḷḷa irikaḷit tērntuṇṭu cōlikaḷil taṅkum neytal nilattalivaṉoṭu nām ceytu koṇṭa naṭpu orukālum pirittal iyalātu. attakiya naṭpiṉaṉ nammip pirintiruppatu nam ūḻ viṉiyē aṉṟi avaṉ tavaṟaṉṟu” (kuṟun. 313) eṉṟu talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘palvēṟunili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koṇṭa kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, “talivaṉ eṉṉip pirintuṟiyum vaṉmi yāṅkup peṟṟāṉ?” eṉat tōḻiyi viṉāvutal -
“pēyttēr viḷaṅkum naṇpakaṟpoḻutil peṇpuṟā kaḷḷimarakkiḷiyil amarntu taṉ āṇpuṟāvit taṉimit tuyarōṭu aḻikkum kaṭattaṟkariya vaḻikaḷi uṭiya pālinilattil, talivar eṉṉip pirintu taṉittut taṅkum vaṉmiyi evvāṟu peṟṟār?” (kuṟun. 154) eṉṟu talivi tōḻiyi viṉavutal.
itu ‘koṭumi oḻukkattu... āvayiṉ varūum niliyiṉum’ eṉṟataṉkaṇ aṭaṅkuvatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, talivaṉ tūtu kaṇṭu kūṟal -
“tōḻi! talivar viṭuttuḷḷa tūtuc ceyti vantuviṭṭatu. avar virivil mīṇṭu varuvār. nāmum ney peyta tīppōla maṉaviḷakkattōṭu avar tūtiṉi ēṟṟuk koṇṭu, nam talināḷ aṉpiṟ kuṟiviṉṟi avaratu varaviṉi etir nōkkiyuḷḷōm eṉṟa ceytiyit tūtuvaṉ vāyilāka avarkku viṭuppōm” (kuṟun. 106) eṉṟu talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟiyatu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘pal vēṟu nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koṇṭa kūṟṟu.
talivi, talivaṉ tūtu varak kāṇātu kūṟiyatu -
“tammāl peritum virumpappaṭupavariṭamiruntu oru colliyum peṟāmal pirivāṟṟi vāḻum peṇṭirip pōṉṟa kalneñcam uṭiyār ulakil vēṟu yārum ilar.
“eṉṉāl peritum virumpappaṭṭa eṉ kātalar eṉṉiṭam aṉpilarāyp pirintāreṉiṉum, avarip paṟṟi avariṭa miruntu vanta yātēṉum oru collē eṉ cevikku mikka iṉimiyit tarum; ataṟku vāyppu illiyē” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu. (kuṟaḷ 1198, 1199)
talivi, talivaṉtūtu varappeṟātu, tāṉ tūtu viṭak karuti neñcoṭu kūṟiyatu -
“neñcē! nammi virumpāta talivarkku nam tuṉpattit terivittu avaratu aruḷip peṟuvativiṭa, namakkut taṉoliyāl tuṉpam tarum ikkaṭalit tūrppatu eḷiya tum payaṉuṭiyatumākiya ceyalākum” eṉṟu talivaṉatu karuṇiyiṉmi karuti veṟuttut talivi taṉatu neñciṭam kūṟiyatu.	(kuṟaḷ 1200)
talivi, talivaṉ tūtu viṭāmi nōkkit tōḻiyoṭu pulantu kūṟiyatu -
“tammāl virumpappaṭṭavarkkē ivvaḷavu tuṉpam tarum talivar pakivarkkuc ceyyum tuṉpam aḷaviṭaṟku ariyatu pōlum!” eṉṟu talivi tōḻikku muṉṉilip puṟamoḻi pōṉṟu kūṟutal. 	(kuṟaḷ 1165)
talivi, talivaṉ teyvam kāṭṭit teḷippat teḷintami kūṟal -
talivaṉ teyvattiṉ peyarāl uṟutimoḻi kūṟit tāṉ eṉṟum piriyāta ceytiyit teḷivittamiyit talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟutal.
“mutal nāḷ kūṭṭattiṉpōtu, talivaṉ eṉṉiṭam kaṭal teyvatti muṉ kāṭṭic collāta uṟutimoḻi oṉṟum illi. ataṉāl talivaṉ orunāḷum eṉṉip piriyāṉ eṉṟu eṉ neñcam uṭkoṇṭatu. āyiṉ, iṉṟu eṉviḻikaḷ kaṇṇīr ukuttaṟkuk kāraṇam pulappaṭa villi” (ampikā. 339) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘aṟattoṭu niṟṟal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 176)
talivi, talivaṉ neṭuntolivil pōvataṟku muṉṉē taṉ varuttatti avaṟkuk kūṟumāṟu tōḻiyi vēṇṭal -
“tōḻi! kaṭalalikaḷ tokutta nuṇṇiya veṇmaṇa lilē avar tēr pōṉa cuvaṭṭi iṉṉum kāṟṟu vīci maṇalip peytu aḻikkavilli. ippoḻutu ‘aiyō!’ eṉṟu nam varuttatti melliya kuralil kūṟiṉum, orukāl avar kēṭṭu nammi nōkki mīṇṭu varutalum kūṭum” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
iccūttirattu ‘variviṭi vitta kālattu varuntutal’ eṉpataṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭa varum kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ iḵtu oṉṟu.			(to. po. 112 nac.)
talivi, talivaṉpāl nikaḻntatu ētuvāy niṉittal -
muṉṉāḷkaḷil nikaḻttiyatu pōlātu orunāḷ talivaṉ taliviyiṉ muttumāli kiṭantu aciyum koṅkikaḷi iṭiyīṭupaṭāmal taḻuviyum vēṭki taṇiyātu, taṉ kūṭṭattāṉ kulinta taliviyiṉ kūntalik kōti aḻaku ceytāṉ; taliviyi atarapāṉam paṇṇi avaḷatu ūṟiya muḷ eyiṟṟu nīr amiḻtiṉum iṉitu eṉṟu kūṟiyum vēṭki tavirātu, puṇarcciyāl nilikulinta avaḷ aṇikaḷip paṇṭupōl kiṭakkumāṟu tiruttiṉāṉ; tēmal paraviya avaḷ mārpiṉi imiyāmal pārttuk koṇṭiruntum vēṭki nīṅkātu, puṇarcciyāl avaḷ neṟṟiyil arumpiya viyarviyit tuṭittāṉ. iṉṉō raṉṉa talivaṉuṭiya ceyalkaḷi nōkkit talivi avaṉ virivil taṉṉip pirintu cellat tiṭṭamiṭu kiṟāṉ eṉṟu niṉittal (kali. 4). itu pālit tiṇiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(to. po. 43 nac.)
talivi, talivaṉ pirintamikku iraṅkal -
talivaṉatu parattiyiṟ pirivu kuṟittut talivi varuntutal.
“tōḻi! ippoḻutu talivaṉ tēr iravilum nammaṉip pakkam varutalilli. nam talivaṉ nammiṭam koṇṭuḷḷa viruppam naṉṟu!” (ampikā. 450) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ, ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uṇartta uṇarum ūṭal; uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.	(i. vi. 554 uri)
talivi, talivaṉ ‘pirintavaḻik kalaṅka’ṟkaṇ kūṟal -
kaḷavu alarātal kūṭātu eṉpatu karutit tāṉ pirintāl talivi kalaṅkuvāḷ eṉṟu añcit talivaṉ piriyātu uṟitaliṉ, pirivu paṟṟiyē kavalāta talivi, piṉ talivaṉ pirintavaḻi uḷḷam kalaṅkumiṭattē kūṟutal.
“kaṭaltivali taṉ tērc cakkaraṅkaḷi naṉippat talivaṉ eppoḻutu pirivāṉ eṉṟu kāttiruntatu pōlap pacali avaṉ pirinta uṭaṉēyē eṉ neṟṟiyil paraviviṭṭatu” (kuṟun. 205) eṉavum,
“kuravampū naṟumaṇam kamaḻum nērattil nammip pirintu tuya rattil āḻttiya talivaṉukku eṉ tuyaric collutaṟ kākap piṉceṉṟa eṉ neñcam avaṉoṭu cērntu eṉṉik kāṇavaralām eṉṟu kālam tāḻtti aṅku niṟkiṟatō? aṉṟi, avaṉ mukamkoṭuttup pēcāmiyāṉ varunti ivviṭattukku vantu paḻiya aḻaku keṭṭuk kāṇappaṭum eṉṉi aṭiyāḷam kaṇṭukoḷḷa muṭiyāmal ivaḷ vēṟu oruttipōlum eṉṟu karutit tuyarōṭu eṉṉit tēṭap pōyviṭṭatō? aṟiyēṉ” (naṟ. 56) eṉavum,
“talivaṉoṭu ceṉṟa eṉmaṉam mīṇṭuvarap pōkiṟatō? aṉṟi avaṉiṭattilēyē taṅki iruttali virumpi aṅkēyē taṅkiviṭṭatō?” (aiṅ. 295) eṉavum,
“puṉṉimaram nirampiya maṇaṟkuṉṟiṉmēl ēṟit taṅkik kūṭaṟcuḻi iḻittut talivaṉuṭiya mārpu namakku virivil kiṭṭumō eṉpataṉik kāṇpōm” (ain. aim. 43) eṉavum varum talivi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi, talivaṉ (kiḻavōṉ) pirintuḻi māliyampoḻutu kaṇṭiraṅkal -
pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṟkup piṉ talivaṉ pirintu ceṉṟa pōtu talivi mālinēram vantatu kaṇṭu varuntutal.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘poḻutu kaṇṭu mayaṅkal’ eṉṉum (188).
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘orucār pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 154)
talivi, talivaṉ piriyappeṟṟut taṉ āṟṟāmi kūṟi neñcoṭu kalāyttal -
kalāyttal - ciṉantu kūṟal.
“eṉ uṭampukku aḻakūṭṭum māmi aḻikiṟatu; virivil neruṅki ūrum pacaliyum uṭampu muḻutum paravukiṉṟatu; iravu nēraṅkaḷ yukaṅkaḷip pōla nīṇṭu kolkiṉṟaṉa; ippaṭi nāṉ peṟṟuḷḷa vaḷamellām, emperumā ṉuṭiya tuḻāymāliyi niṉintu ēṅkum eṉ neñci ṉār (-maṉam) tantu pōṉaviyē” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvi. 12)
talivi “talivaṉ pirivaṉ” eṉak karutiya kuṟippuṇarntu, talivaṉ kūṟal -
“niṉ tāyum eṉ tāyum, niṉ tantiyum eṉ tantiyum iccantippukku muṉṉar uṟaviṉar allar; yāṉum nīyum oruvari oruvar aṟiyātavarāka itaṟku muṉṉar irun tuḷḷōm. cemmaṇ nilattil peyta maḻinīr nilattiṉ cemmiyum nīriṉ taṉmiyum pirikka muṭiyāta aḷavil peṟṟiruppatu pōla, pirikka muṭiyāta niliyil iruvar neñcamum kalantu oṉṟāṉapiṟaku yāṉ niṉṉip pirintu maṟantu viṭuvēṉ eṉṟa kavaliyē niṉakku vēṇṭā” (kuṟun. 40) eṉṟu talivaṉ talivi kuṟippuṇarntu vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟal.
iccūttirattuḷ ‘teḷivakappaṭuttal’ eṉpataṉkaṇ cārum kūṟṟu itu.	(to. po. 101 nac.)
talivi, talivaṉ putalvaṉpāl koṇṭa aṉpiṉik kūṟal -
“talivaṉ parattimi uṭiyaṉāyiṉum orutaliyāka namakkup paṟṟukkōṭāka iruppāṉ eṉpatu uṟuti. avaṉ oruttiyi maṇakka ēṟpāṭu ceyyak kāliyiliruntu avvillattil muracu olikkavum, nam maṉikkaṇ vantu putalvaṉip pārtta aḷavil avaṉoṭu viḷiyāṭuvatil poḻutu kaḻittut tāṉ maṇam nikaḻtta amitta ēṟpāṭṭiṉiyum maṟantuviṭṭāṉ” (akanā. 66) eṉṟu talivaṉ putalvaṉiṭam koṇṭa aṉpumikutiyiṉit talivi viyantu kūṟiyavāṟu.	(to. po. 170 iḷa.)
talivi, talivaṉ putalvaṉi nīṅkiyavaḻik kūṟal -
“taliva! eṉakkuk kōpam ciṟitum ēṟpaṭṭilatu. poy yātu col. mukkāṟ ciṟutēr uruṭṭi viḷiyāṭum putalva ṉatu viḷiyāṭṭik kāṇavāvatu ivvilliṟku nī eppoḻutāvatu varuvāyē! atuvum niṉivillātavāṟu niṉ uḷḷattik kavarnta appeṇ yāreṉṟu kūṟu” (aiṅ. 66) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
ic cūttirattuḷ ‘palvēṟunili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koṇṭa kūṟṟu itu.
talivi, talivaṉ perumiyum peyarum kūṟiya kiḷiyit toḻutal -
“nāṉ vaḷartta maṭakkiḷiyē! eṉ nāyakaṉāṉa araṅka ṉuṭiya perumikaḷiyum peyarkaḷiyum nāṉ kēṭṭu ukantu uvakkum vaṇṇam pāṭiṉāy. mikavum makiḻntēṉ; niṉṉi vaḷarttatāl ākiya naṟpayaṉiyum peṟṟēṉ. iṅkē vā. niṉṉit toḻukiṟēṉ” eṉṟurittut taṉmakaḷ kiḷiyik kikūppi vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ eṉṟa tāy  kūṟṟu.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(tiruneṭun. 14)
talivi talivaṉ ‘vantavaḻi eḷḷik’ kūṟal -
peritākiya iṭiyīṭṭuk kālattil talivaṉ eṉṟō orunāḷ aritāka vantāṉāka, talivaṉip pala nāḷkaḷum peṟāmiyāṉ ēṟpaṭṭa tuṉpa mikutiyāṉ, peṟṟataṉiyum kaṉavu pōlak koṇṭu ikaḻntu talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
“tōḻi! aṭumpiṉ pūvi alarttic ciṟumiyar viḷi yāṭum kaṭaṟkarikku urimiyuṭiya nam talivaṉi iṉi niṉiyēṉ. eṉ kaṇkaḷ tuyilvaṉavākuka!” (kuṟun. 243) eṉṟal pōlat talivaṉ nērāka vanta varukiyiyum kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭa kāṭciyākak kūṟi eḷḷit talivi taṉvayiṉ urimiyum avaṉvayiṉ ayaṉmiyum tōṉṟak kūṟutal.			(to. po. 111 nac.)
iṉi, talivaṉ vantaviṭattu avaṉ varuki alarākum eṉṟu añci avaṉi ikaḻtal eṉṟu poruḷ koḷvar iḷampūraṇar. (109).
“muttukkaḷi vaḻaṅkum kaṭalil kappalkaḷ vantu pōkum paṭiyāṉa tuṟivaṉit tāḻikaḷ cūḻnta kaṭaṟkaric cōliyil kaṇṭa nāṉ itaṟku muṉ, ‘ivaṉik kāṇpatu namakkup pēriṉpamākum’ eṉṟu karutivanta eṇṇam māṟi, ‘ivaṉtoṭarpu ippoḻutu namakkut tuṉpam taruvatā kum’ eṉṟu teḷintuviṭṭēṉ” (aiṅ. eḻu. 61) eṉṟal pōlat talivi talivaṉ varukiyi eḷḷik kūṟutal. talivaṉ piṉṉum toṭarntu varuvaṉ eṉṟu karutiya miyē avaḷ appoḻutu eḷḷiyataṟkuk kāraṇamām eṉpatu.	(nac.)
talivi, talivaṉ varavu kūṟiya tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu -
“eṉ kaṇ pōṉṟa kātalar varuvārāyiṉ, varavu nīṭṭitta mikkāka avarik kōpittuk koḷvēṉō, allatu eṉ āṟṟāmi tīrat taḻuvikkoḷvēṉō, allatu iraṇṭiyum kalantē ceyvēṉō? nāṉ yātu ceyyak kaṭavēṉ eṉpatu pōtaravilliyē?” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.		(kuṟaḷ 1267)
talivi, talivaṉ varavi virumpik kūṟutal -
“pāṇa! talivaṉ mīṇṭuvarutali nī kaṇṇāl kaṇṭāyā? allatu yārāvatu collak kēṭṭāyā? uṇmi nikaḻcciyi eṅkaṭkut teḷivākac col. innaṟcey tiyik kūṟiya nī poṉ mikunta pāṭalipurattiyē paricākap peṟuvāyāka!” (kuṟun. 75) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘palvēṟu nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koṇṭa kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, “talivaṉ varukiṉṟāṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiya uḻiyarkkuk kūṟal -
“talivaṉ varukiyi eṉakku uṇarttiya pāṇa! talivaṉ varuvati nī nērākak kaṇṭāyā? allatu piṟar kūṟak kēṭṭu vantu urikkiṉṟāyā? emakku uṇmi nikaḻcciyic col? nī poṉmikka pāṭalipurattiyē paricākap peṟuvāyāka” (kuṟun. 75) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘vāyiliṉ varūum vaki’ eṉṟaṉāṉ koṇṭa kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 145 iḷa.)
talivi, “talivaṉ varuṅkol” eṉa niṉittal -
talivi talivaṉi etirpārttuk kuṟiyiṭattē kāttu niṟkumpōtu taṉṉi muṉpōlavē taḻuvi iṉpuṟuttat taṉ uyiraṉiya kātalar varuvārō eṉṟu ēṅki niṉittal.		(ampikā. 25)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’  eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uriyil koḷḷappaṭṭatu.			(i. vi. 505 uri)
talivi, talivaṉ varuntoḻiṟku arumi cāṟṟal -
“talivaṉ iravil vantu kūṭutaṟku iṭiyūṟukaḷ palavum nērkiṉṟaṉa. nāy uṟaṅkāmi mēlum tāyum ūrkkāva larum iravil uṟaṅkāmal irukkiṉṟaṉar.” (akanā. 122) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘varital vēṭki’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 164)
talivi, talivaṉatu uruveḷippāṭu kaṇṭu tōḻikkuk kūṟutal -
uruveḷippāṭāvatu, oru poruḷiṭattu iṭiviṭātu karuttic celuttic cintippatāl, accintaṉi ūṟṟam peṟṟu, apporuḷ kaṇṇukku etirē tōṉṟuvatu pōlak kāṇutal.
“empirāṉuṭiya kaṇkaḷiṉ aḻakip peṟṟu, nīlamali mītum piṟa pacumip parappiṭiyum eṉ kaṇṇukkut tōṉṟum tāmarit taṭākaṅkaḷ yāvum eṉṉi mikavum makiḻvikkiṉṟaṉa” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉpāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvi. 39)
talivi, talivaṉatu tārmaṇam koṇṭuvarum teṉṟal vara makiḻnturittal -
talivaṉatu toṭarpu koṇṭa epporuḷiyum talivi taṉ makiḻccikkuk kāraṇamākak koḷvatu ivviṭac ceyti.
“tōḻi! iṅku vantu ulavum teṉṟal, emperumāṉuṭiya tiruttuḻāymāliyiṉ naṟumaṇam kalantu vīci eṉ pirivāṟṟāmiyic caṟṟē taṇivippatuṭaṉ, eṉṉi makiḻvikkiṟatu” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itaṉit tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟiya āṟutal moḻiyāka vum koḷvatuṇṭu.	(tiruvi. 74)
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.
talivi, talivaṉatu nōkkil īṭupaṭṭu viyanturittal -
nōkku - kaṇṇaḻaku
“eṉ talivaṉākiya tirumāliṉ perumikaḷi aṟintu avaṉi nāṉ paṇintēṉ. appoḻutu eṉṉik kuḷira orukkaṇittuk kaṭikkaṇṇāl nōkkiya avaṉuṭiya eḻil mikka kaṇkaḷ, valiya kāṟṟu vīciyatāl oru pakkamākac cāyntu carintirukkum tāmarip pūkkaḷip pōlap polivuṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉa” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvi. 42)
talivi, talivaṉatu ‘varuntoḻiṟku arumi vāyil kūṟu’ṅkāl kūṟal -
talivaṉ pakaṟkuṟiyilum iravukkuṟiyilum iṭiviṭātu varutaṟku iṭiyūṟukaḷ palavuḷḷaṉa eṉṟa ceytiyit tōḻi kūṟum iṭattē talivi kūṟal. vāyil - īṇṭut tōḻi.
“yāmam iruḷ ceṟintuviṭṭatu; makkaḷum col aṭaṅkip paṭuttuviṭṭaṉar; ūrum uṟaṅkiviṭṭatu. yāṉ oruttiyē uṟaṅkāmal viḻittirukkiṟēṉ!” (kuṟun. 6) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
“aṉṉiyiṉ kāvalik kaṭantu illatti nīṅkit talivaṉ ūr ceṉṟu pakalnērattil palarum kāṇa avaṉi eyti ‘nī cāṉṟōy alli’ eṉṟu kūṟalāmā?” (naṟ. 365) eṉṟal pōla pirivāṟṟāmi mikutiyāl talivi nili kaṭantu moḻitalum uṇṭu.	(109 iḷa.)
talivi, talivaṉiṭam, “nī kāmam cālā iḷamiyōḷik kaḷavil maṇantami aṟintēṉ” eṉak kūṟal -
“makiḻna! niṉ putiya maṉiviyik kāṇum vāyppup peṟṟēṉ. palarum paṭiyum nīrttuṟiyilē malarik kariyil koṇṭu cērtta nīrali, tāṉ viḷiyāṭiya maṇalciṟṟilik karittuviṭṭatu eṉṟu kaṇkaḷ civappa avaḷ aḻutu koṇṭu niṉṟāḷ” (aiṅ. 69) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘palvēṟunili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ koṇṭa kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, talivaṉiṭam vēṭki pulappaṭuttal -
vēṟuvēṟākat tammil kātalceytu oḻukum aṟivillā tārip pōlat talivaṉ aṟiyumpaṭiyākat talivi taṉakkuṟṟa vēṭkiyit talivaṉmuṉ veḷippaṭiyākac kūṟāḷ. avaḷ uḷḷattu vēṭkiyit talivaṉ taṉ aṟiviṉāṉ ārāyumpōtu, putiya maṭkalattup peyta nīr puṟattup pocintu kāṭṭumāṟu pōla, avaṉ aṟiyak kuṟippāṉ veḷippaṭum.	(to. po. 118 nac.)
talivi, “talivaṉi iyaṟpaḻittaṟku añci avaṉ aruḷiṉmiyi maṟitta nī, kaṟpuṭi makaḷirāl naṉku matikkappaṭukiṟāy” eṉṟu coṉṉa tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu -
“kaṇavaṉ aruḷiṉmiyit tam niṟiyāl maṟittoḻu kum peṇṭirit kaṟpuṭiya makaḷir naṉku matittup pārāṭṭuvatu perumiyē. āyiṉ attakiya peṇṭir tām mika virumpum kātalarāl virumpappaṭāmaṟpōvatum avarkaḷ tīviṉiyē. kaṇavarāl virumpappaṭāta niliyil ūravaratu naṉmatippāl payaṉ oṉṟumilli” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(kuṟaḷ 1194)
talivi, talivaṉi iravukkuṟi vilakkal -
iravuk kuṟi vilakkal - iravukkuṟikkaṇ vāraṟka eṉa vēṇṭutal.
‘iṟimakaḷ iṟivaṉik kuṟivilakkal’ eṉpatu it tokutik kiḷavi.
“taliva! eṉ poruṭṭāka, puli mutaliya vaḻaṅkum mūṅkil vaḷarntuḷḷa piḷavupaṭṭa vaḻikaḷiṉūṭē, katiravaṉum nuḻikallā iruḷ mikka nammalic cāralil, nī vāraṟka” (tañci. kō. 182) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ iravukkuṟikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.			(na. a. 158)
talivi, (mutirā meṉmuli), talivaṉi etirkoṇṭu paṇital -
parattiyiṟ piriviṉ pirintirunta talivaṉ, pāṅkiyatu cevvaṇi kaṇṭu, parattiyāl viṭukkappaṭṭut taṉatu illam nōkki virintu vantapōtu, nīrāṭiya talivi avaṉi etirkoṇṭu aḻittup paṇital.
“talivaṉpirivu pacaliyāl nammi urukkukiṟatu. in niliyil avaṉ muṉ avaṉōṭu ūṭutal namakku evvāṟu iyiyum?” (tañci. kō. 386) eṉṟu tōḻikkut talivi kūṟiyavāṟē, avaṉi etirkoṇṭu paṇintavāṟu.
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ, ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uṇartta uṇarum ūṭal.	(na. a. 205)
talivi, “talivaṉi ēṉ pulavāy?” eṉṟa tōḻikkuk kūṟal -
“talivaṉ tavaṟu ceyyiṉum avaṉip pulavātu ēṟṟuk kōṭaltāṉ niṉ iyalpō?” eṉṟu viṉaviya tōḻiyi nōkki, “talivar yāṉ pulattaṟku vāypput tārātu illattiṟku viruntoṭu varukiṟār” (naṟ. 280) eṉṟu talivi cepputal.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘vāyiliṉ varūum vaki’ eṉṟata ṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭatoru kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, talivaṉik kāṇa virital -
pirivāṟṟāta niliyil, talivi evvāṟṟāṉāyiṉum tali vaṉik kaṇṭētīra vēṇṭum eṉa virumpi muyalutal.
“emperumāṉē! aḻakaṉāṉa uṉṉi aḻaku mikka makaḷir tiraṇṭuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilēyō, aṟivu mikkōrum ñāṉi kaḷum īṇṭiyuḷḷa kūṭṭaṅkaḷilēyō kāṇap peritum viḻikiṉṟēṉ” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvi. 84)
talivi, talivaṉik kāyntu tōḻiyiṭam kūṟal -
kāytal - ciṉattal.
“tōḻi! kiḷiyil vikum paṟavikkūṭṭaṅkaḷ tām āṇum peṇṇumākak kūṭiṉamiyiṉ, pirintiruppārtam tuyar paṟṟik kavalippaṭāmal iṉiya kuralālē tammuḷ aḷavaḷāvum oliyik kēṭṭuvittum, nām tuyaruṟutal aṟiyātu akaṉṟa talivar iṉi vantāl, ‘eṉ kūntalil pūkkaḷic cūṭṭutalum vēṇṭā; eṉṉit tīṇṭavum vēṇṭā’ eṉṟu yāṉ kūṟappōvataṉik kāṇpāyāka!” (kuṟun. 191) eṉṟu talivi kūṟutal.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, talivaṉi niṟuttaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
talivaṉ paṇpiṉit tōḻi kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ talivi tāṉ niṟuttuk kūṟutaṟkaṇ avaḷ kūṟṟu nikaḻum.
“tōḻi! ‘talivaṉ uyar kuṭippiṟappiṉaṉ, taṉṉuṭaṉ kūṭumavarkaḷip piriyātavaṉ; nāvāl koṭumoḻikaḷik kūṟātavaṉ, aṉpuṭiyavaṉ eṉṟellām paṇṭu nī avaṉip paṟṟik kūṟiya ciṟappukkaḷ nāḷum mēmpaṭum iṉpam tarukiṉṟaṉa” (akanā. 352) eṉṟu talivaṉ paṇpu kaḷit talivi uṟutippaṭuttik kūṟal.
	(to. po. 145 iḷa.)
“karuṅkōl kuṟiñciyiṉ pūkkoṇṭu peruntēṉ iḻik kum nāṭaṉākiya talivaṉuṭaṉ yāṉ ceytukoṇṭa naṭpāṉatu nilattiṉum akalattatāyum, vāṉiṉum uyarn tatāyum, kaṭaliṉum āḻntatāyum uḷḷatu” (kuṟun. 3) eṉṟu talivaṉi niṟuttaṟkaṇ talivi kūṟiyavāṟu.			(147 nac., po. 195 kuḻa.)
niṟuttal eṉpataṟku, taliviyit talivaṉ nalloḻuk kattil niṟuttal, talivaṉit talivi nalloḻukkattil niṟuttal, talivaṉiṭam koṇṭa aṉpiṉit talivi niliniṟuttal eṉṟu uri ceyvār kuḻanti. (po. 145)
talivi, talivaṉi niṉintu varuntit tōḻikkuc colliyatu -
“tam neñcattil yām vara iyalāmal taṭiceytuviṭṭa nam kātalar iṭiviṭātu em neñcattil māttiram varuki ṟārē! itaṟku avar nāṇamuṟṟilar pōlum!” (kuṟaḷ 1205) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
“eṉakkut tummal varuvatupōl arumpip piṉ niṉṟatu. itaṉāl eṉ kātalar eṉṉi niṉippavar pōliruntu piṉ niṉiyātavarāki yiruttal vēṇṭumeṉa niṉik kiṟēṉ” (kuṟaḷ 1203) eṉpatum atu.
(eṅkēyō vikum uṟṟavarāl niṉikkappaṭṭārkkut tummal varum eṉpatoru vaḻakkup paṟṟi ivvāṟu kūṟiṉāḷ).
talivi, talivaṉi niṉivūṭṭum poruḷkaḷāl cōrvu koḷḷal -
“nāṉ emperumāṉip pirintu aṭinta āṟṟāmi tīra, avaṉi otta pūviyum piṟavaṟṟiyum pārttāvatu āṟutal peṟalām eṉṟu cōlikku vantēṉ; āyiṉ iṅkuḷḷa pūkkaḷum piṟavum avaṉ niṉivi mikutiyākki eṉṉi varuttukiṉṟaṉa. kuyilē! pūviyē! kiḷiyē! nīṅkaḷ ivvāṟu kūvik kūvik eṉṉi nōkac ceytal vēṇṭā; itō eṉṉuyir irukkiṟatu. itik koṇṭupōy nīṅkaḷ emperumāṉ kaṇṇaṉiṭam koṭuttu viṭuṅkaḷ!” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvāy. 9 - 5 - 1)
talivi, talivaṉi (iṟiyōṉ taṉṉi) nērntu iyaṟpaṭa moḻital -
iravukkuṟikkup piṉ vantu kūṭāta talivaṉip pāṅki paḻittu urittami kēṭṭa talivi, “tavaṟu avaruṭiya taṉṟu” eṉat talivaṉukkuc ciṟappu nērak kūṟutal. ciṟappāvatu kuṟṟamiṉmi. iyaṟpaṭamoḻital - ciṟappu ēṟpaṭumāṟu kūṟutal.
“nammuṭiya tuṉpattiyum, uṭal vāṭṭamuṟṟu niṟam peyarvatiyum aṟiyāmal talivar nammi maṟantiruttal namatu ūḻviṉip payaṉē. avari nontu yātu payaṉ?” (tañci. kō. 214) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu ‘varital vēṭki’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 164)
talivi, talivaṉip pirintuḻip pāṅkiyi nōkki iruḷ viyanturittal -
“tōḻi! avaruṭaṉ kūṭiyirukkumpōtu palappala nāḷkaḷ iṉpuṟṟālum, oru nāḻikiyip pala kūṟukaḷ ākkiya mikac ciṟiya pōtāyk kaḻintu viṭukiṉṟaṉa, avviravu nēraṅkaḷ. āyiṉ, avarip pirintirukkum pōtu, iraviṉ oru nāḻikiyip pala kūṟukaḷ ākkiya mikac ciṟiya kālamum palappala ūḻikaḷ ākiviṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉ tuṉpamum mikukiṟatu. ivviruḷiṉ cūḻcci mikka vañcaṉitāṉ eṉṉē! amma! itu vāḻi!” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(tiruvi. 16)
talivi, talivaṉip pirintuḻip pōli kaṇṭu makiḻtal -
pōli - talivaṉi niṉivupaṭuttum niṟappōlikaḷ.
“eṉ talivaṉāṉa tirumāliṉ vaṭivam pōl nīlaniṟam peṟṟut tikaḻum ikkaruniṟa neytaṟpūkkaḷ, tamakkuriya kaṭamiyellām īṭēṟṟip piṉ uṭaliyum varuttit taṇṇīril pukku niṉṟu ceyta tavattāl tāṉ avvāṟu avaṉōṭu oppumi peṟṟaṉavō!” eṉṟu talivi kūṟi makiḻtal.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉpāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvi. 38)
talivi, talivaṉip pirintuḻi vāṭikku varunti iraṅkal -
“em perumāṉē! uṉ tiruttuḻāy kiṭikkāta kāraṇat tāl (uṉatu piriviṉāl) melintu yām emmuṭiya vaḷikaḷi iḻappōm; iḵtu iyalpē. āyiṉ uṉatu āṇikkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭa ivvāṭikkāṟṟu vantu, uṉ toṭarpu peṟṟu varuntum eṉṉi uṭal muḻutum tuḻāvi, eṉ māmi aḻakik koḷḷikoṇṭu pōka muyalvatu eṉpaṟṟi? nī tāṉ itaṉit taṭuttaruḷal vēṇṭum” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷippāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvi. 28)
talivi, talivaṉip pukaḻtal -
talivi talivaṉatu aṉpiṉi uyarttik kūṟal.
“tōḻi! talivaṉ muṉpu nammāṭṭuk koṇṭa aṉpiṉi iṉṉum maṟantilaṉ. ātaliṉ, ‘kaṇavaṉip pōlac ciṟanta uṟaviṉar vēṟu yāvarum irār’ eṉṟu cāṉṟōr coṉṉa mētakka moḻiyiṉ poruḷ pulaṉāyiṟṟu” (tañci. kō. 406) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ, ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uṇartta uṇarā ūṭaliṉ oḻipu.			(na. a. 207)
talivi, talivaṉōṭu avaṉūr cēṟali virumpik kūṟal -
“tōḻi! ‘ūravar alar tūṟṟat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉar; ap paḻic coṟkaḷ tāyiṉ cevikaḷi eṭṭiviṭṭāl iv villattil nāṉ amitiyuṟa iruttal iyalātu’ eṉṟu talivariṭam terivittāl avar tammūrukku nammiyum aḻittuc celvārā?” (naṟ. 4) eṉṟu āṟṟāmiyāṉ tōḻiyiṭam talivi kūṟiyavāṟu.	(to. po. 113 nac.)
talivi taṟkāṭṭuṟutaṟkaṇ kūṟiyatu -
kaḷavuk kālattut talivaṉ taṉṉik kāṇāvakiyil nāṇāṉ maṟintu oḻukiṉum, taṉ polivaḻiviṉit talivaṟkuk kāṭṭal vēṇṭum eṉṟu talivi virumpum pōtu avaḷ tōḻiyi nōkkik kūṟutal. taṟkāṭṭuṟutal - taṉ (polivaḻivi)ṉit talivaṟkuk kāṭṭa virumputal.
“tōḻi! nammaṉik kolliyil putarilē vaḷarntuḷḷa pīrkkam pūkkaḷ cilavaṟṟi eṭuttuc ceṉṟu talivaṉi aṇukit ‘taliviyiṉ neṟṟi ippīrkkampūp pōlap pacali pāyntu viṭṭatu’ eṉṟu eṉ niliyiṉi avaṉiṭam kūṟuvār uḷarāyiṉ, aḵtu eṉakku naṉmi payakkum” (kuṟun. 98) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.(to. po. 111 nac.)
taṟkāṭṭuṟutal - taṉṉi avaṉ kāṇumāṟu niṟṟal eṉṟu urippārum uḷar. inniliyil kūṟṟu nikaḻātu eṉpa.			(104 iḷa.)
taṉ neṟṟi pacali pāyntatu eṉpatu taṉvayiṉ urimi; talivaṉ ataṉi aṟiyavilli eṉpatu avaṉvayiṉ ayaṉmi.	(nac.)
talivi, ‘taṉkuṟi taḷḷiya teruḷākkāli, vantaṉaṉ peyarnta vaṟuṅkaḷam nōkkit, taṉ piḻippākat taḻīit tēṟi’k kūṟal -
talivi taṉṉāṉ ceyyappaṭṭa kuṟiyiṭaṅkaḷ iṟceṟippu mutalāya kāraṇaṅkaḷāṉ ikaḻappaṭum eṉpataṉi muṉṉarē uṇarā niliyil, muṟkūṟiya kuṟiyiṭamē iṭamāka vantu talivaṉ kūṭātu peyartalāṉ, tamakkup payaṉpaṭāta vaṟuṅkaḷatti niṉittu, talivaṉukkut tam niliyi muṉṉarēyē uṇarvittu vēṟoru kuṟiyit tammaṉikku aṇmittākak kuṟippiṭāta tavaṟṟiṉit taṉmēlum tōḻimēlum ēṟṟikkoṇṭu talivaṉmītu tavaṟu yātum illi eṉpataṉi uṭkoṇṭu kūṟutal.
“talivaṉ namkuṟiyiṭattu varuvāṉē! vantāl, payaṉiṉṟi acōkamarattil toṅkum ūcaliyum, nām nīrāṭātatāl kalaṅkātu teḷintu kāṇappaṭum cuṉiyiyum, tiṉi ariyappaṭṭatāl nam kāval illāta tiṉikkolli yiyum nōkki, nām kuṟi peyarttu iṭāta tavaṟṟip peritāka maṉattuk koṇṭu varuttattoṭu celvāṉē! nam ūr kūppiṭu tūrattiltāṉ uḷḷatu eṉpataṉic colla nāṉ maṟantuviṭṭēṉē!” (akanā. 38) eṉṟu talivi varuntik kūṟutal.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivi, taṉcelavu īṉṟāṭku uṇartti viṭuttal -
uṭaṉpōkkuc cellum talivi, vaḻiyil kāṇum antaṇar mutaliyōr vāyilākat taṉ naṟṟāyiṭam tāṉ talivaṉuṭaṉ celvatic colli aṉupputal.
“antaṇīr! eṉṉip peṟṟa tāyiṭam ceṉṟu mullip pantalukkuk kāl naṭumpaṭiyākavum, māṉukku nā vaṟaḷātu nīr ūṭṭumpaṭiyākavum, eṉ pūvippaṟavi uṟaṅkumaḷavum ataṉ toṭṭili āṭṭum paṭiyākavum yāṉ vēṇṭiya tākac colvīrāka” (nellik.48) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘uṭaṉ pōkku iṭiyīṭu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 198)
talivi, taṉtuyar talimakaṟku urittal vēṇṭal -
iravukkuṟiyil kūṭi makiḻnta talivaṉ mīṇṭum vārātatāl tuyaruṟṟuk kalaṅkum talivi, taṉ tuṉpaniliyit taliva ṉukku aṟivittal vēṇṭum eṉṟu tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
“mārik kālattil pūkkum pīrkkampūkkaḷik koṇṭu ceṉṟu, ‘taliviyiṉ neṟṟi ivaṟṟiṉ niṟam koṇṭu pacali pāyntu viṭṭatu’ eṉṟu talivaṉiṭam kūṟuvār uḷarāyiṉ naṉṟu” (kuṟun. 98) eṉṟāṟ pōla varum talivi kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘varital vēṭki’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 164)
talivi, taṉmakaṉip pārāṭṭip pāl kūṟiṭṭu ūṭṭukiṉṟavaḻic ciṟippuṟamākap pukka talivaṉik kaṇṭu taṉṉuḷḷē pulantu, pulaviyoṭu piṉṉum pārāṭṭiyatu -
“cemmāl! iṅkēvā. uṉakkup paruka vēṇṭivit tirukkum pālip paruku. ippakuti uṉ tantiyāriṉ pāl; ippakuti uṉ tāyāriṉ pāl” eṉṟu pakutippaṭuttic ciṟuvaṉukkup pāli uṇpitta iṭattē, talivaṉ aṅku vantu cēravē, ataṉik kaṇṭu piṉṉar makaṉi nōkki, “eñciyiruppatu uṉ tāyiṉ pakutiyākiya pāl. itaṉi māttiram parukiviṭu, pōtum” eṉṉavum, makaṉ appāli māttiram parukāmal appāl pōy viḷiyāṭa, avaṉ viḷiyāṭṭik kaṇṭu avaḷ pārāṭṭiyatu.	(kali. 85)
talivi, ‘taṉvayiṉ ciṟappiṉum avaṉvayiṉ pirippiṉum, iṉṉāt tolcūḷ eṭuttaṟkaṇ’ kūṟiyatu -
taṉmāṭṭu niṉṟa mikutiyāṉum avaṉ (talivaṉ)māṭṭu niṉṟa vēṟupāṭṭāṉum iṉṉāta paḻiya cūḷuriyit talivi eṭuttaṟkaṇ kūṟiyatu.
“taliva! nī tēril aḻittuvanta parattiyaruṭiya kūṭṭat tāṉ ēṟpaṭṭa cuvaṭukaḷ niṉ parattimiyip pulappaṭut taṟku añci nī poyccūḷ urittāl, ataṉāl ēṟpaṭum tuṉpam emmiyē tākkum eṉpati aṟiyāy” (kali. 88) eṉṟa kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 145 iḷa.)
iṉi nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ‘taṉvayiṉ ciṟippiṉum’ eṉap pāṭam koṇṭu urikkumāṟu;	(to. po. 147)
taṉvayiṉ ciṟippiṉum -
talivaṉiviṭat tāṉ putalvaṟkuc ciṟantavaḷāki, at talivaṉmāṭṭum avaṉ kātalitta parattiyarmāṭṭum cellāmal, putalvaṉit taṉpāl ciṟiceytaṟkaṇ talivi kūṟṟu nikaḻum.
“taliva! parattiyarik kāṇac ceytuḷḷa oppaṉi yoṭu vantu eṉ putalvaṉit tūkkātē. avaṉ eccil niṉ mārpiṉi naṉittu alaṅkāraṅkaḷik keṭuttuviṭum. nī aṇintuḷḷa muttumāliyi avaṉ aṟuttuviṭuvāṉ. nī aṇintuḷḷa pūṅkottukkaḷip paṟṟic citittuviṭu vāṉ. iccitivukaḷik kaṇṭu niṉ paratti tavaṟākak kaṇakkiṭṭu niṉṉi vekuḷuvāḷ. uṉ aḻaki aḻikkiṉṟa eṉ putalvaṉi eṉṉiṭam koṭuttuviṭṭu, nī virumpum parattiyariṭamē cel” (kali. 79) eṉṟu talivaṉiṭam kūṟum kūṟṟil, talivi putalvaṉit taṉṉiṭamē koḷḷa virumputal aṟiyappaṭum.
putalvaṉ putupparattiyatu illattiṟkuc ceṉṟatu kuṟit tut talivi vekuṇṭu, avaṉukku, avaḷ illattiṟkup pōkak kūṭātu eṉṟu eccarittut taṉṉip pōṉṟu talivaṉpirivu niṉintu varuntum muṉṉip parattiyaratu (kātaṟparattiyar) illamē cella icivu tantami kali. 82il kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
avaṉvayiṉ pirippiṉum - taṉṉoṭu mintaṉiṭi uṟavu nīkki avaṉit talivaṉoṭu cārttutaṟkaṇ talivi kūṟṟu nikaḻum.
talivi taṉ makaṉoṭu pēci makiḻkiyil talivaṉ varavē, tāṉ evvaḷavu taṭuttum nillātu malimēl ciṅkam pāyvatu pōla, ‘aṟaṉ illā aṉpili peṟṟa makaṉ’ tāṉum tāyiṭattu aṉpilaṉāyt tantiyiṉ paranta mārpiṉi nōkkip pāyntu taṉ kippiṭiyiṉiṉṟum vilakit tantiyiṭam ceṉṟuviṭṭāṉ eṉṟu kūṟum avaḷ kūṟṟu (kali. 86).
iṉṉāt tol cūḷ eṭuttaṟkaṇṇum - iṉṉāṅku payakkum cūḷuṟaviṉit talivaṉ ‘cūḷuṟuvēṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟumiṭattut talivi kūṟṟu nikaḻum.
talivaṉ vanta kuṟṟam vaḻikeṭa oḻukik kaḷavil cūḷuṟavāṉ vanta ētam nīkki, ikkālattuk kaṭavuḷariyum putalvaṉiyum paṟṟic cūḷuṟutaliṉ, ‘iṉṉāta cūḷ’ eṉṟār.
“taliva! nī kūṭiya parattiyar muṭiyiṟ cūṭiya pūkkaḷil tātu niṉ tōḷkaḷil paṭiya iṅku vantuḷḷāy, eṉṉit tīṇṭātē” eṉṟu talivi colliyavaḻi, talivaṉ, “eṉṉippaṟṟit tavaṟākak karutātē. eṉmēl tavaṟu illi eṉpataṉic cūḷurittu uṟuti ceykiṉṟēṉ” eṉṟāṉāka, atu kēṭṭa talivi, “nī ivvāṟu tuṇin turikkum poyccūḷ uṉakkut tuṉpam tarumāyiṉ aḵtu eṅkaḷit tākkumē yaṉṟi niṉ parattiyarit tākkātu” eṉṟu kūṟiyami (kali. 88)
talivi, taṉvayiṉ putalvaṉic ciṟippaṭuttaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
talivi, ‘taṉ vayiṉ ciṟippiṉum... tolcūḷ eṭuttaṟkaṇ’ kūṟiyatu kāṇka.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi taṉṉuḷḷē collutal -
“eṉṉik kūṭip pirinta talivaṉ mārpit tōyātatāl viḷintuḷḷa uṭal veppam avaṉ malippakkattu mūlikikaḷi aṭittukkoṇṭu varum iṉiya nīril kuḷittāl iṉṟē nīṅkiviṭum!” (ampikā. 299) eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivi taṉakkuḷ kūṟikkoḷḷutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘oruvaḻit taṇattal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uriyiṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.			(i. vi. 525 uri)
talivi taṉṉi aḻital -
talivaṉ iravukkālattuk kaṭumiyāṉa vaḻiyilē kāvalik kūṭap poruṭpaṭuttāmal iravukkuṟikku varattāṉ kāraṇam āyiṉami paṟṟit talivi taṉṉi nontu koḷḷutal. (akanā. 72)	(to. po. 210 nac.)
talivi, taṉittuḻit tuṉittu aḻutu iraṅkal -
talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintu ceṉṟamiyāl, talivi taṉittiruntu mikavum kōpam koṇṭum, tuyaruṟṟu aḻutum varuntutal.
“eṉ talivar eṉṉi maṟantu vēṟu eṅkō tam kātalit tantu makiḻkiṟār. nāṉ iṅkē aḻutukoṇṭē, eṉ kaṇṇīrum ikkoṭiya iravum eppoḻutu pularum eṉṟu etirpārttu ēṅkip pulampukiṟēṉ” (tañci. kō. 378) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ, ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘potuppaṭak kūṟi vāṭi aḻuṅkal’ eṉṉum kūṟṟiṉuḷ (354) aṭakkum.	(na. a. 205)
talivi, ‘tāyar kaṇṇiya nallaṇip putalvaṉi, māyap paratti uḷḷiya vaḻik’ kūṟal -
tāyarik kiṭṭi avarkaḷukku makiḻcci ceyvitta nalla aṇiyiyuṭiya (taliviyiṉ) putalvaṉi, vañcaṉiyi yuṭiya paratti cantittamiyik kēṭṭavaḻit talivi kūṟutal.
putalvaṉip parattimi kuṟittalāvatu, talivaṉ putup peṇṭirmāṭṭup pōkiyavaḻi vekuḷumāṟu pōlap putalvaṉiyum avariṭic ceṉṟavaḻi vekuḷutal. ‘māyam’ parattiyarukkup paṇpāki, iṉam cuṭṭātu niṉṟatu. ‘nallaṇi’ eṉavē, avar koṭutta nallaṇi eṉpatu peṟutum.	(to. po. 145 iḷa.)
parattiyar karuti aḷitta naṉṟākiya aṇikaḷiyuṭiya putalvaṉit talivaṉuṭiya māyap parattimiyik kuṟittiṭattut talivi kūṟutal.	(147 nac.)
avaruḷ tuṉiyālē varuntiya parattiyar tam varuttattiṉi uṇarttiyum, taliniṉṟoḻukum parattiyar tam ciṟap puṇarttiyum aṇivar eṉṟataṟkuk ‘kaṇṇiya’ eṉṟār. parattiyar cēri ceṉṟu aṇi aṇintataṟku vekuṇṭu kūṟaliṉ, ‘poyyākiya parattimi’ eṉṟār.	(nac.)
putalvaṉik kōyilukku aḻittuc ceṉṟu mīṭaṟku neṭunēram kālam kaṭattiyami paṟṟit talivi tōḻiyi viṉavat tōḻi, “talivaṉuṭiya parattiyar palarum putal vaṉik kaṇṭa aḷavil cūḻntukoṇṭu ovvoruvarum ovvōraṇiyi aṇivittaṉar” eṉṟu kūṟavē, piṟar koṭuttatip peṟṟataṟkut talivi taṉ putalvaṉi vekuṇṭu, putupparatti iṭṭa mīṉ poṟitta mōtiram kaṇṭu, maṉmataṉkoṭi eḻutiya mōtiratti avaḷ aṇivitta kuṟippu ivaṉtantiyit tāṉ eṉṟum akap paṭuttikkoḷḷum kuṟippip pulappaṭuttukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟi, piṉ, tanti kiyil aṇiyum toṭiyi orutti putal vaṉukku aṇivittatik kaṇṭu talivaṉatu parattimikku varunti, “tavaṟu piṟarmēlatu aṉṟu; uṉ āparaṇaṅkaḷ paṟṟi viṉaviya yāṉē tavaṟuṭiyēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟutal (kali. 84)			(nac.)
talivi, tār peṟṟu makiḻtal -
talivi taṉ āṟṟāmi tīrat talivaṉatu māliyi aṇintu iṉpuṟutal. tār - āṭavar mārpil aṇiyum māli.
“yāṉ eṉ talivaṉatu tāriṉip peṟātamuṉ, ovvōr iravilum aṟi kūvikkoṇṭē vantu kaṉali vīciya avvāṭikkāṟṟē, yāṉ tār peṟṟu makiḻum iṉṟu ettuṇik kuḷircciyit tarukiṉṟatu! ‘iṟivaṉ aruḷē nammik kākkavallatu; ataṉāl pakivarum naṇpar āvar’ eṉṟu ulakam kūṟuvatu mikka niliyāṉa uṇmiyē” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvi. 27)
talivi ‘tāvil naṉmoḻi kiḷa’ttal -
tā - valimi, varuttam. ‘uṭampum uyirum vāṭiyakkālum, eṉ uṟṟaṉakol ivi eṉiṉ allatu, kiḻavōṟ cērtal kiḻattik killi’ (to. po. 203 nac.) eṉṟa naṭimuṟi variyaṟiyik kaṭippiṭikkum maṉavalimi illāmal, “talivaṉ irukkum ūrukkuc celvōm” eṉṟa naṉmoḻiyiṉit talivi tāṉē kūṟutal.
“‘karaṭikaḷum pāmpukaḷum paṉṟikaḷum pulikaḷum yāṉi kaḷum naṭamāṭum ikkāṭṭil ivviruḷil immaḻi peyyum nērattil taṉittu varum numakku ippakutiyil naṭantu cellum vaḻikaḷil muṉpē paḻakkamuṇṭō?’ eṉṟāṟpōla naṭuvaḻiyil nammi vicārikkak kūṭiya vaḻippōkkar cilarāvatu iruppiṉ, talivaṉūr nōkkic cellutal namakku aritaṉṟu” (akanā. 8) eṉat talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
‘tā’ eṉṟa coṟku valimi eṉṟa poruḷil nacciṉārk kiṉiyar tarum viḷakkam itu.	(to. po. 113 nac.)
iṉi ‘tā’ eṉṟa coṟku varuttam eṉap poruḷkoṇṭu, varuttam illāta collit talivi kūṟutal eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.	(to. po. 111)
talivaṉ tāṉ coṉṉa collik kāvāmiyāṉ avaṉit teyvam varuttum eṉṟu karuti, avaṉukku varuttam yāvatum nikaḻāta vakiyil, talivi, “ūrp potuviṭattuḷḷa marāmarattil poruntiya añcattakka teyvam koṭiyavarkaḷi oṟukkum eṉṟu kūṟuvar. em talivar koṭiyarallar. emakku avar tīṅkoṉṟum ceytilar. avari yāṉ virumpiyatāltāṉ neṟṟi pacali pāyntatu; avari niṉittu eṉ maṉam nekiḻntatāl tōḷkaḷ melintaṉa” (kuṟun. 87) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(iḷa.)
talivi, ‘tāṉ akam pukāaṉ peyartal iṉmiyiṉ, kāṭci āciyiṉ kaḷam pukkuk kalaṅki, vēṭkiyiṉ mayaṅkiya kiyaṟu poḻutiṉ’ kaṇ kūṟutal -
talivaṉik kāṇātavakiyil poḻutu kaṭakkat talivi kuṟi tappiyavaḻiyum talivaṉ kuṟiyiṭattukku vārāmal irāṉ eṉpataṉi avaḷ aṟivāḷ ātaliṉ, kuṟiyiṭat tukku vanta talivaṉ tāṉ vantataṟku aṟikuṟiyāka vit tiruppatik kāṇum viruppāl talivi maṟunāḷ viṭiyalil iravukkuṟiyiṭattuc ceṉṟu mayaṅkiya ceyalaṟṟa poḻu tiṉkaṇ tōḻiyi nōkkik kūṟutal.
“kāntaḷ viralāy! ikkāntaḷ moṭṭiṉmēl iruppatu vaṇṭaṉṟu. itaṟkup poruttamāka aṇivikkappaṭ ṭirukkum im mōtiram yāruṭiyatu eṉpatu namakku muṉṉarē teriyum!” eṉṟu kalaṅki, avaṉik kāṇum vēṭkiyoṭu ceyvataṟiyātu mayaṅkik kiyaṟṟuk kūṟutal.	(to. po. 107 nac.)
talivi, tāṉ kaṇṭa kaṉavit tōḻikku urittal -
eṉ nāyakaṉāṉa nārāyaṇaṉ, nāḷi eṉṉi varintu vatuvi ceytukoḷḷa vēṇṭi, āyiram yāṉikaḷ cūḻa ūrvalam varuvataṟkāka eṉ ūr muḻutum tōraṇam kaṭṭip pūraṇakumpam vittu varavēṟpu nikaḻttuvatāyt kaṉavu kaṇṭēṉ, tōḻi!” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(nācciyār. 6 - 1)
talivi, tāṉ kātalaṉmāṭṭup parattimi karutiyum pulavāmikkuk kāraṇam kūṟutal -
“nam kātalar nampāl vantu nam maṉattiṟkukanta ceyalkaḷi nām virumpiyavāṟē ceytu iṉpuṟuttumpōtu, parattiyar tōḷ tōynta avarmārpiṉi nām taḻuvalākātu eṉṟa nāṇatti nām aṟiyamuṭiyavilliyē! inniliyil avaroṭu pulattal evvāṟu?” eṉṟu talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.	(kuṟaḷ 1257)
talivi, tāṉ ‘vantavaḻi eḷḷiya’ tākat talivaṉ karutuvāṉō eṉak kūṟal -
talivaṉ iravukkuṟiyiṭattut taṉṉik kāṇa muṭiyāmal mīṇṭami aṟinta talivi, tāṉ vantapōtu taṉṉi nām poruṭpaṭuttāmal cantikkātu iruntuviṭṭōm eṉṟu talivaṉ niṉippāṉō eṉṟu tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
“talivaṉik kūṭutaṟkaṇ uḷḷa vēṭkiyāl pakaṟkuṟiyi nīkkiyami tavaṟu eṉṟu maṉamkoṇṭu, noccippūkkaḷ vīḻum oliyik kēṭṭavāṟu iravu muḻutum yāṉ varunti avaṉ kuṟiceytali etirpārttu uṟaṅkātiruppavum, iṭiyoli mikka iruḷ ceṟinta irāk kālattut talivaṉ vantu nāṉ iravukkuṟikkaṇ vārātu tavaṟiviṭ ṭēṉ eṉṟu eṉmēl piḻi ēṟṟuvāṉō?” (kali.46) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 109 iḷa.)
talivi, tāṉē kūṟum kālam -
taliviyit tāṉ maṇantu koḷḷutaṟkuc celaviṭa vēṇṭum poruḷit tiraṭṭi varuvataṟkut talivaṉ poruḷtēṭa avaḷip pirintu ceṉṟa kālattu, taṉakkup piritaltuṉpattāl varuttam mikka iṭattu, talivi tāṉē kūṟuvāḷ.
maṇam ceytu koḷḷāmal kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻttivarum talivaṉi etirpārātavakiyil tōḻiyarō cevilit tāyō cantittu avaṉ nikaḻttum kaḷavoḻukkam paṟṟi aṟitaṟku vāyppu nikaḻumiṭattum, talivaṉ poruḷvayiṉ pirintaviṭattu ayalavar taliviyi maṇam pēcuvataṟku ēṟpāṭu nikaḻumiṭattum, tōḻi viṉavāmalēyē talivi tāṉē tōḻiyi aṟattoṭu niṟkumāṟu vēṇṭuvāḷ.			(to. po. 112 nac.)
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉpiṉ tōḻiyiṉ kūṭṭattukku muyalātu talivaṉ virivil taliviyi maṇantu koḷva tākak kūṟip pirintu piṉṉarc cila nāḷkaḷ vari taliviyic cantikka vārāta iṭattum, kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻttum talivaṉ etirpārātavakiyil cevili mutalāyiṉāric cantittiṭattum, “ivvoḻukkat tiṉi niṉ tōḻikku uri” eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟiya iṭattum, talivi tāṉē tōḻikku aṟattoṭu niṟpāḷ.	(to. po. 110 iḷa.)
talivi, tūtuviṭak karutik kūṟal -
“pāṇaṉē! nī palarum pukaḻum nam talivaṉik kāṇṭal vēṇṭi avaṉ irukkum iṭattiṟkuc celvāyāyiṉ, emakku ippaṭip pirital tuṉpattic ceytuḷḷa poyyil valla talivaṉip pōla, eṅkaḷi maṟantuviṭātē!” (aiṅ. 473) eṉṟu talivi pāṇaṉit tūtuviṭak karutik kūṟal. (aiṅ. 474) pāṇaṉit tūtuviṭṭut talivi kūṟuvaṉavum uḷa.
iḵtu iccūttirattuḷ ‘palvēṟu niliyiṉum’ eṉṟaṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
talivi, “teyvam poṟikoḷac celkuvam” eṉṟal -
“tōḻi! ‘niṉṉiṟ piriyēṉ’ eṉṟu teyvattiṉmēl cūḷuritta eṉ talivaṉ pirintu ceṉṟāṉ. cūḷ poytta avaṉit teyvam koṭumi ceytu oṟukkāmal poṟuk kumāṟu vēṇṭi vaḻipaṭac celvōm” (tañci. kō. 294) eṉṟu talivi kūṟutal.
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘aṟattoṭu niṟṟal’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (talivaṉatu ippirivu oruvaḻit taṇantatum, variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirintatum ākum.)	(na. a. 176)
 
